Roadside scenes are globally structured yet locally evolving, requiring comprehensive scene-level context while precisely modeling sparse localized motion. However, existing forecasting paradigms struggle to tackle both requirements. To address this limitation, we propose InfraWorld, a world model formulating roadside occupancy forecasting as selective state evolution. InfraWorld maintains a dense BEV state for the complete scene and persistent motion queries for moving regions, assigning complementary states to global context and local dynamics. Specifically, we propose Dynamic Query Initiation (DQI) to initialize queries in regions supported by motion in the observed history. During rollout, the dense state is updated through Long--Short Temporal Fusion (LST), which combines short-term adjacent-frame continuity with long-term motion trends to preserve stable context while capturing scene evolution. Motion-Aware Query Injection (MAQI) then refines the queries and injects confidence-weighted query information into corresponding BEV tokens, enabling targeted dynamic updates without perturbing global context. Together, InfraWorld concentrates predictive capacity on evolving traffic regions. Extensive experiments on the InfraOcc and Occ3D--nus datasets demonstrate that InfraWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance for roadside 4D occupancy forecasting while retaining its effectiveness under ego-centric settings. Code will be released.

</details>


### [34] [Adversarial Gradient Forcing for Robust Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14553)
*Chenxi Xia,Yixuan Xu,Shan Liu,Jiapeng Wang,Maohua Li,Hanlin Tang,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Weiyi Lu,Xinggao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AGF框架，通过对自生成历史施加对抗扰动并联合训练，提升自回归视频生成在长时程上的鲁棒性，在60秒与4分钟生成上达SOTA，同时仅需5秒回放训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成在推理时以自身历史为条件，尽管自强制(Self Forcing)类方法缓解了暴露偏差，但长时程仍会因早期小误差被放大而逐步崩坏。核心瓶颈是模型对本地历史误差高度敏感（局部历史鲁棒性缺口）。

Method: 提出Adversarial Gradient Forcing (AGF)：利用来自未来块预测的分布匹配梯度，选择对自生成历史的最具破坏性的对抗扰动；随后在原始与扰动历史上联合训练生成器，使其对本地扰动与误差累积更鲁棒。仅需短滚动（5秒）即可训练针对长时程的稳健性。

Result: 在大规模实验中，AGF在60秒视频生成的VBench Total上最高；在4分钟视频生成中获得最佳视觉质量，同时维持稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过梯度引导的对抗历史扰动与联合训练，AGF弥补了自回归视频生成的局部历史鲁棒性缺口，实现长时程稳健生成，并以短滚动训练达到优异性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation models generate videos sequentially, with each new block conditioned on previously generated blocks. By training on the model's own rollouts, Self Forcing and its follow-up methods substantially mitigate exposure bias caused by the mismatch between training- and inference-time history distributions. However, these models still suffer from error accumulation during long-horizon generation. We identify a key underlying limitation: the model remains highly sensitive to errors in its generated history, allowing small deviations to be amplified through subsequent generations. We refer to this underexplored problem as the local history robustness gap. To address this gap, we introduce Adversarial Gradient Forcing (AGF), a framework designed to improve the robustness of few-step autoregressive video models to perturbations and accumulated errors in their own generated histories. AGF uses distribution matching gradients from future block predictions to select adversarial perturbations of self-generated histories. It then jointly trains the generator on the original and perturbed histories to improve the robustness of future generation. Extensive experiments show that AGF achieves the highest VBench Total score on 60-second video generation and the best visual quality on four-minute video generation. Notably, AGF achieves such long-horizon robustness while requiring only 5-second rollouts for training.

</details>


### [35] [FrameFold: Response-Guided KV Cache Consolidation for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14568)
*Muyu Liu,Mingming Liu,Hong Gu,Yuyao Zhang,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameFold是一种无需再训练的KV缓存合并方法，通过比较合并前后对相同历史查询的注意力响应来指导代表构建与合并选择，在固定缓存预算下提升长视频自回归生成的一致性与画质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成需要保留大量历史KV状态以维持角色、背景与运动一致性，但这导致内存和每步注意力成本随视频长度线性增长。固定缓存预算虽能抑制成本，却引入“保留什么、如何尽量不损失有用历史信息”的关键难题。现有历史选择或合并策略难以兼顾空间依赖差异与画质稳定性。

Method: 提出FrameFold：利用相邻历史条目间的时域冗余，将其KV状态合并为共享代表，并保留完整空间网格。核心做法是对“相同的最近历史查询”在合并前后得到的条件注意力响应进行匹配：闭式求解value合并系数以最小化响应差异，并在候选中选择剩余响应失真最低的合并。方法训练无关、以响应失真为准则指导代表构建与合并选择。

Result: 在相同KV缓存预算下，于Self Forcing与Causal Forcing场景（30/60秒）以及LongLive（120秒）上取得更高一致性与更佳视觉质量；在连续8分钟Self Forcing滚动生成中，相比基线呈现更缓慢的质量退化。

Conclusion: 在固定缓存预算情形下，通过基于条件注意力响应的指导性合并，FrameFold能有效压缩历史KV而尽量保持对空间查询有用的信息，从而提升长视频自回归生成的稳定性与画质，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: In autoregressive video generation, retaining all historical key-value (KV) states increases memory usage and per-step attention costs as videos grow longer. A fixed cache budget bounds these costs but raises the challenge of preserving historical content that supports consistent, high-quality generation. Existing methods control cache size through history selection or consolidation, yet deciding what to retain and how to limit the loss of useful content remains a shared challenge. Our analysis shows that spatial queries depend on different historical content and that higher current attention coverage does not necessarily yield better long-video quality. We introduce FrameFold, a training-free KV cache consolidation method that compares the content retrieved by the same recent historical queries before and after merging to guide both representative construction and merge selection. Exploiting temporal redundancy between adjacent historical entries, FrameFold merges their KV states into shared representatives while retaining complete spatial grids. It fits value-merging coefficients in closed form by matching conditional attention responses, then selects the merge with the lowest remaining response distortion. Under matched KV cache budgets, experiments on Self Forcing and Causal Forcing at 30 and 60 seconds and on LongLive at 120 seconds demonstrate improved consistency and visual quality. Continuous eight-minute Self Forcing rollouts further show less quality degradation than the baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Attribute-Centric Vision-Language Alignment for Fine-Grained Gait Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14589)
*Saihui Hou,Chenye Wang,Wenpeng Lang,Qiannan Guo,Zhengxiang Lan,Chao Fan,Shiqi Yu,Yongzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RA-GAR大规模步态属性基准与Gait-VL视觉-语言对齐框架，实现细粒度步态属性识别并迁移提升身份识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态属性基准标注稀缺且粗糙；主流方法以分类器权重隐式表示类别，难以直接将视觉证据与可解释的文本属性对应，限制了细粒度理解与可迁移性。

Method: 1) 基准：构建RA-GAR，涵盖1001名受试者、242,729段行走序列，标注15个静态与动态步态属性。2) 模型：提出Gait-VL框架。视觉侧以属性特定的可学习query跨时空聚合多帧RGB特征，得到逐属性视觉嵌入；语言侧借助大语言模型扩写多样化描述，并结合相关身体部位上下文，构建文本类别原型；通过视觉-文本匹配实现属性识别。3) 迁移：以属性预训练的视觉编码器替换通用上游视觉模型，注入到以身份识别为目标的步态管线中，无需身份分类或度量学习目标。

Result: 在RA-GAR上获得最先进的属性识别性能；在多种下游身份识别管线与基准上持续提升性能，显示属性中心的表示学习具备良好可迁移性。

Conclusion: 丰富标注的RA-GAR与Gait-VL将可解释的视觉证据与文本原型对齐，显著提升步态属性识别并可无监督于身份标签地增强下游身份识别，验证了以属性为核心的步态表示学习的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Walking sequences reveal human-interpretable attributes related to body structure, posture, and motion. Gait attribute recognition provides a concrete route to fine-grained gait understanding by predicting these attributes. However, existing benchmarks provide limited fine-grained annotations, while classifier-based gait attribute recognition methods typically represent candidate classes through learned output weights rather than directly matching visual evidence with textual class representations. To address the annotation gap, we present RA-GAR, a large-scale, richly annotated multimodal benchmark for gait attribute recognition, containing 1,001 subjects, 242,729 walking sequences, and annotations for 15 static and motion gait attributes. We further propose Gait-VL, an attribute-centric vision-language alignment framework that recognizes gait attributes by matching attribute-specific multi-frame visual evidence with textual class prototypes. On the visual side, attribute-specific learnable queries aggregate RGB features across spatial locations and frames into per-attribute visual embeddings. On the language side, diverse descriptions expanded by a large language model are combined with context about relevant body regions to construct textual class prototypes. Beyond attribute recognition, we evaluate the transferability of the attribute-pretrained visual encoder by using it to replace generic upstream large vision models in identityoriented gait recognition pipelines; attribute pre-training uses no identity classification or metric-learning objectives. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art attribute recognition performance on RA-GAR and consistent improvements in downstream identity recognition across multiple pipelines and benchmarks, supporting the effectiveness and transferability of attribute-centric gait representation learning.

</details>


### [37] [Bidirectional Adaptive Transformers for Multimodal Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14598)
*Yuxin Jiang,Yunkang Cao,Weiming Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BAT框架，通过双向自适应Transformer在RGB与点云间实现语义对齐与互补，结合轻量适配器微调预训练编码器，在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies上取得SOTA的多模态异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态异常检测多采用静态融合与冻结编码器，只做空间对齐而缺乏潜在语义对应，导致跨模态干扰与异常可分性下降。

Method: 1) 提出Bidirectional Adaptive Transformers (BAT)。2) 设计双向特征对齐（BFA）模块，用互预测机制在2D纹理与3D几何间双向交互，强化点云描述子的纹理细粒度与RGB特征的空间上下文。3) 采用参数高效适配架构，在图像与点云分支加入轻量适配器，对预训练模型进行专门微调，避免冻结带来的信息损失。

Result: 在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies数据集上显著优于现有MAD方法，达到SOTA性能（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 通过双向语义对齐与参数高效适配，BAT有效缓解跨模态干扰，构建信息密度更高、语义一致的多模态表示，从而显著提升异常检测效果。

Abstract: The effective integration of features derived from RGB images and 3D point clouds presents a substantial challenge in the field of multimodal anomaly detection (MAD). Existing MAD methods typically rely on static fusion paradigms built upon frozen pre-trained encoders. While these frameworks facilitate spatial alignment, they lack the mechanism to establish latent semantic correspondence, thereby inducing cross-modal interference that degrades anomaly discriminability.
  To mitigate these limitations, this study proposes a Bidirectional Adaptive Transformers (BAT) framework, which constructs comprehensive and semantically aligned multimodal representations by leveraging complementary attributes across modalities.
  The BAT framework introduces a Bidirectional Feature Alignment (BFA) module, which employs a mutual predictive mechanism to bridge the gap between 2D textural patterns and 3D geometric structures. This bidirectional interaction explicitly enhances the textural granularity of point cloud descriptors while augmenting the spatial contextual awareness of RGB features.
  To ensure that the latent representations possess sufficient informational density for alignment, BAT incorporates a parameter-efficient adaptation architecture. By utilizing lightweight image and point cloud adapters, the framework facilitates specialized fine-tuning of pre-trained models, mitigating the information loss inherent in frozen encoders.
  Extensive experiments conducted on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies confirm that BAT achieves state-of-the-art anomaly detection performance, significantly outperforming existing MAD methods.

</details>


### [38] [EviScene: Beyond "Is It AI?" for Agentic Image Factuality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14609)
*ZiAn Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Zhengbo Zhang,Yi Han,Jialiang Shen,Ruoxin Chen,Manni Cui,Ximo Zhu,Jin Jiang,Mingzhe Liu,Linhao Wang,Wenhao Wang,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出图像事实性评估任务与基准EviLens，并用多模态ReAct代理EviScene实现，在无显式文本主张下通过迭代提问、检索与证据对齐给出支持/反驳判定与证据解释；在1,527张测试图上达79.05%均衡准确率、52.26%严格证据充分率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有“AI生成检测”无法判断图像所描绘情境是否与事实一致；缺乏能在无给定文本主张下，自动提出可验证问题、检索外部证据并对齐到具体图像情境的系统与评测基准。

Method: 1) 定义任务：给定图像与通用核查指令，输出支持/反驳及基于证据的解释；需逐步细化问题并对齐证据。2) 构建EviLens数据集：10,174张受控构造与网络收集图像，涵盖12类事实错误，评估判定准确率与证据充分性。3) 设计EviScene：多模态ReAct代理，交替进行视觉检查、外部检索和证据分析，用新发现迭代提问并回看图像细节。4) 训练：先用经专家复核的教师轨迹进行SFT，再用“特权在策略自蒸馏”，从代理自身调查的经验证决策中蒸馏，得到紧凑策略（基于Qwen3.5-9B）。

Result: 在1,527张测试集上，Qwen3.5-9B代理获得79.05%均衡准确率，严格证据充分率（SESR）52.26%，相对同骨干的基线代理分别提升6.71与9.37个百分点；在被评测代理中SESR最高。

Conclusion: 图像事实性评估可通过多模态ReAct代理结合外部检索与迭代问题精化有效实现；EviLens为系统化评测提供基准，EviScene在准确率与证据充分性上均显著优于基线，显示该范式与训练策略的有效性；代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Determining whether an image is AI-generated does not establish whether the situation it depicts is factually supported. We formulate Image Factuality Assessment: given an image and a generic verification instruction, a system returns a supported or refuted verdict with an evidence-grounded explanation. Without a supplied textual claim, the system must progressively refine factual questions during investigation and align external evidence with the specific situation depicted. We introduce EviLens, an expert-reviewed benchmark of 10,174 supported and refuted images from controlled construction and web collection, covering twelve factual error types and evaluating both verdict accuracy and evidence sufficiency. We develop EviScene, a multimodal ReAct agent that interleaves visual inspection, external retrieval, and evidence analysis, using new findings to refine its questions and revisit relevant image details. We train a compact policy with supervised fine-tuning on reviewed teacher trajectories, followed by privileged on-policy self-distillation applied to verified decisions from the policy's own investigations. On the 1,527-image test set, the trained Qwen3.5-9B agent achieves 79.05% balanced accuracy and a Strict Evidence Sufficiency Rate (SESR) of 52.26%, improving over the same-backbone base agent by 6.71 and 9.37 percentage points, respectively. Its SESR is the highest among the evaluated agents.Code, models, and data will be released at https://github.com/wangzian828/EVISCENE and https://huggingface.co/ZiAnwang/EviScene.

</details>


### [39] [EarthAlign: Learning Language-Aligned Representations of Earth System Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14624)
*Fadillah Adamsyah Maani,Numan Saeed,Jiaqi Ma,Dat Tien Nguyen,Youssef Wehbe,Duncan Watson-Parris,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EarthAlign数据集与MeteoLIP模型，用多源灾害与常态天气数据对齐气象-语言表征，在跨模态检索、天气分类与物理量估计上优于CLIP/SigLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态天气系统多偏向特定任务（预测、生成、问答），缺乏广泛且贴合气象条件的语言监督，导致通用的气象-语言对齐不足，限制了跨任务与跨区域的理解与应用。

Method: 构建EarthAlign：整合2000-2024年、覆盖广域的10个专家整理的自然事件/灾害来源，并加入基于气候学的常态天气样本；每个样本含时间、地理信息、对齐至ERA5的63变量，以及四种风格的地面化文本描述。提出MeteoLIP：双编码器结构，融入MetTok，将地理与时间条件注入，对变量特定patch嵌入进行聚合，生成紧凑的“气象tokens”，从而学习气象-语言对齐表征。评估三类下游任务：跨模态检索、天气状况分类、物理变量估计。

Result: 在三类任务上均超过相应的CLIP与SigLIP基线；消融显示气象输入、时空条件以及多风格监督对对齐效果均至关重要。

Conclusion: EarthAlign与MeteoLIP为学习与评估地球系统数据的语言对齐表征提供了基础设施与方法论，提升跨任务泛化与可解释性，资源（代码/数据/权重）已公开。

Abstract: Interpreting Earth system conditions is central to understanding environmental risks and informing decisions across weather- and climate-sensitive sectors. Existing multimodal weather systems primarily target task-specific prediction, generation, or question answering, while general-purpose alignment between Earth system data and language remains underexplored due to the limited availability of diverse language supervision grounded in meteorological conditions. To fill this gap, we introduce EarthAlign, a large-scale dataset with broad geographic coverage spanning 2000-2024 that combines 10 expert-curated natural event and disaster data sources with climatology-derived normal-weather samples. Each sample includes temporal and geospatial information and four grounded caption styles aligned with 63-variable ERA5 meteorological data. Building on EarthAlign, we introduce MeteoLIP, a dual-encoder framework for learning aligned meteorology-language representations. MeteoLIP incorporates MetTok, which uses a geolocation- and time-conditioned query to aggregate variable-specific patch embeddings into a compact sequence of meteorology tokens. To comprehensively assess Earth system multimodal representations, we introduce three downstream tasks: cross-modal retrieval, weather-condition classification, and physical variable estimation. Our experiments show that MeteoLIP improves upon the corresponding CLIP and SigLIP baselines across these tasks. Further analyses highlight the importance of meteorological input, temporal and geospatial conditioning, and multi-style supervision for meteorology-language alignment. Thus, EarthAlign and MeteoLIP provide a foundation for learning and evaluating language-aligned representations of Earth system data. Our code, dataset, and weights are publicly available at https://github.com/mbzuai-oryx/EarthAlign.

</details>


### [40] [DuSRFlow: Dual-Lens Super-Resolution via Parallax-Aware Flow Estimation and Complementary Reference Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14630)
*Fangpu Zhang,Huanjing Yue,Zifan Cui,Shaoxiong Tu,Qirui Yang,Jingyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuSRFlow，用专用双摄光流DuFlowNet与互补参考变形（CRW）提升双镜头超分中的参考利用，并发布更难数据集DuSR-RealV2；在多基准上达SOTA的重建、感知质量与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双镜头超分依赖单目光流作为几何先验，但双摄存在大视差、遮挡与跨相机成像差异，破坏光度一致与平滑运动假设，导致对齐不稳、参考利用差，影响重建质量与泛化。

Method: 1) DuFlowNet：面向双镜头的专用光流估计器；通过“真实-合成融合”训练策略，在真实双摄对上叠加合成前景，获得可控大视差且保留跨相机成像特性的无GT训练样本。2) 互补参考变形（Complementary Reference Warping, CRW）：依据局部参考可靠性，协调块状（patch-based）变形与无核引导的可变形变形（kernel-free-guided deformable warping），在LR-Ref融合前生成更可靠的参考表征。3) 构建困难真实基准DuSR-RealV2，涵盖复杂景深变化、大视差与遮挡。

Result: 在多个DuSR基准上取得SOTA：提升重建质量（如PSNR/SSIM）、感知质量（如LPIPS）与跨数据集泛化；对大视差、遮挡和跨相机差异更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 面向双镜头场景，通用光流不适用。专用的DuFlowNet与基于可靠性的互补参考变形有效提升参考对齐与利用，并在真实复杂场景中验证了优越性能与泛化，DuSRFlow为双镜头超分提供了更稳健的参考驱动方案。

Abstract: Dual-lens super-resolution (DuSR) aims to enhance a wide-angle low-resolution (LR) image by exploiting a telephoto reference (Ref) image. A central challenge in DuSR is reliable reference utilization under large parallax, occlusion, and cross-camera imaging discrepancies. Existing methods often employ off-the-shelf monocular optical flow models to provide geometric priors. However, their assumptions of photometric consistency and smooth motion are violated in dual-lens scenarios, leading to unreliable alignment and degraded reference-based reconstruction. To address these issues, we propose DuSRFlow, a dual-lens super-resolution framework that improves reference utilization from both correspondence learning and reference aggregation. First, we introduce DuFlowNet, a dedicated dual-lens flow estimator for robust cross-view alignment. To train DuFlowNet without ground-truth flow annotations, we develop a real--synthetic data fusion strategy that composites synthetic foreground objects onto real dual-lens image pairs, generating controllable large-parallax samples while preserving realistic cross-camera imaging characteristics. Second, we observe that reference reliability is highly spatially uneven in DuSR, where unreliable local reference responses may suppress the utilization of reliable cues. We therefore propose a Complementary Reference Warping strategy that coordinates patch-based warping and kernel-free-guided deformable warping according to local reference reliability, producing a more reliable reference representation before LR--Ref fusion. In addition, we construct DuSR-RealV2, a challenging real-world benchmark with complex depth variations, large parallax, and occlusion. Extensive experiments on multiple DuSR benchmarks demonstrate that DuSRFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance in reconstruction quality, perceptual quality, and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [41] [Automatic assembly modeling through attention-based assembly feature mate prediction and assembly feature pattern recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14633)
*Ziheng Dai,Wanbin Pan,Zhihong Tang,Shuming Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的装配特征配合预测框架，从未装配B-Rep零件自动构建可编辑约束；通过自动注释构建大规模数据集，训练注意力网络并融合工程兼容性，利用特征模式与后处理搜索生成并求解约束，达到约64–75%准确率（提供真值面标签时提升到81–81%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有装配建模仍依赖交互式、经验驱动流程；数据驱动方法要么输出不可直接转为可编辑B-Rep装配约束的姿态，要么需要预设零件对或预定义装配面，限制了自动化与通用性。需要一种既不依赖预定义装配面的候选缩减策略，又能直接生成B-Rep约束的自动装配方法。

Method: 1) 提出以装配特征（将局部配合界面的几何实体成组）为核心的配合预测框架，显著缩小候选空间；2) 通过自动标注流水线构建大规模带装配特征注释的数据集；3) 训练注意力式网络预测装配特征配合，提出工程兼容性融合（ECF）将显式工程兼容准则融入匹配评分；4) 以装配特征模式（AFP）引导匹配以提升鲁棒性；5) 通过后处理搜索选择配合与AFP候选，生成并求解相应B-Rep装配约束以完成建模。

Result: 在6个装配的对比集上装配约束准确率74.79%；在包含该对比集的16装配扩展测试集上为64.09%。若提供真值面标签作为额外输入，准确率分别提升至81.78%与80.93%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需预定义装配面即可从未装配B-Rep零件自动生成可编辑的装配约束，并通过特征分组、ECF与AFP显著提升预测与建模精度；提供更精确的面级先验时性能进一步提高，显示方法可与人工或检索先验互补。

Abstract: Assembly modeling plays an important role in constructing product models, yet it is still mainly performed via interactive operations that are experience-dependent and labor-intensive. Data-driven strategies hold promise for automating this process; however, existing approaches either predict poses that cannot directly generate editable B-Rep assembly constraints or require prescribed part pairs or predefined assembly faces.
  To reduce the large candidate space of constraint prediction without predefined assembly faces, we propose a data-driven framework based on assembly feature mate prediction. The framework performs automatic assembly modeling from unassembled B-Rep parts, using assembly features to group the geometric entities of local mating interfaces and restrict candidate formation. Given the lack of suitable assembly feature annotations in existing datasets, we construct a large-scale dataset through an automatic annotation pipeline. Using the annotated dataset, we train an attention-based network to predict assembly feature mates, with Engineering Compatibility Fusion (ECF) incorporating explicit engineering compatibility into learned matching scores. To improve the accuracy of automatic assembly modeling, we use assembly feature patterns (AFPs) to guide the matching process. Finally, a post-processing search selects assembly feature mate and AFP mate candidates, whose associated constraints are generated and solved to complete assembly modeling.
  Experimental results show that the framework achieves assembly constraint accuracies of 74.79% on the six-assembly comparison set and 64.09% on the extended test set of 16 assemblies, which includes the comparison set. With ground-truth face labels supplied as additional input, the corresponding accuracies increase to 81.78% and 80.93%, respectively.

</details>


### [42] [Can Synthetic UAV Benchmarks Be Trusted? Real-Synthetic Validation for UAV Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14647)
*Yuyi Zhi,Min Du,Bill Shi,Guodong Yuan,Junheng Liang,Peifan Liao,Xin Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文评估合成无人机(UAV)图像能否在真实数据稀缺时可靠用于模型选择，结论是“有条件可信”：在某些特定任务与协议下可保留模型排序，但在其他情境下会失真，需逐案验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实UAV任务常缺少充足、标注一致的真实数据；虽然合成数据在视觉合理性与语义标签对齐方面可满足表面需求，但尚不清楚它是否能在模型选择阶段复现真实数据所给出的结论，从而指导系统选型与部署。

Method: 在固定评测协议下，对比“合成(Synthetic)”与“真实(Real)”数据的模型选择一致性：1) 需求特定的道路碎片(road-debris)基准上，测四个语言条件化感知系统的F1与排序一致性，并用bootstrap与等规模重采样检验是否出现排序反转；2) 在一个独立构建的商业语料上做封闭集压力测试，使用四个与VisDrone兼容的检测器、八个经验支持的类别，比较点估计的Top-1与成对偏好方向；3) 对五个UAV商业场景做互补审计，量化类分布、目标密度与尺度差异。

Result: 案例1：合成数据上绝对F1更低，但模型点估计排序与Top-1保持，与bootstrap与等规模重采样一致，无显著成对反转。案例2：商业压力测试中，合成数据改变了Top-1检测器，并在六个成对比较中有三个方向反转。审计发现真实与合成在类构成、目标密度、目标尺度上显著不匹配，且小目标系统性欠代表。

Conclusion: 合成UAV基准的可信度取决于具体任务、运行条件与模型集合；仅具语义覆盖不足以保证有效性。需以经验方式验证合成评测能否复现实测的模型选择结论，特别关注小目标与分布差异。

Abstract: Synthetic UAV imagery can expand pre-deployment test evidence when task-specific Real data are scarce, but visual plausibility or semantic label alignment alone does not establish validity for model selection. We study when Synthetic evaluation preserves the model-selection conclusions obtained from Real UAV data through two complementary cases under fixed evaluation protocols. In a requirement-specific road-debris benchmark, four language-conditioned perception systems obtain lower absolute F1 on Synthetic data, yet the point-estimate ordering and top-ranked model are preserved; bootstrap analysis yields no supported pairwise reversal, and size-matched resampling preserves the top-model decision. In contrast, a closed-set stress test on a separately materialized business corpus, using four VisDrone-compatible detectors over eight empirically supported classes, changes the point-estimate top-ranked detector and reverses three of six pairwise point-estimate preference directions. A complementary audit of five UAV business scenarios further reveals substantial Real-Synthetic mismatch in class composition, object density, and target scale, including systematic under-representation of small targets. These results show that Synthetic UAV benchmark trust is conditional: semantic coverage is insufficient, and validity must be established empirically for the task, operating regime, and model panel of interest.

</details>


### [43] [TAG: Calibrating SAM3 for Universal Image Segmentation without Dense Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14651)
*Hao Zhu,Heyu Sun,Shuo Jin,Jiayu Xiao,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现SAM3在下游分割失灵的关键瓶颈是“概念识别”而非掩码质量，并提出TAG通过低秩适配与无密集标注的校准训练，有效重定位文本锚并显著提升分割表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3把分割从空间提示扩展到概念级，但在实际任务中常无法稳定识别哪些类别存在，导致整体性能受限。需要一种无需昂贵像素级标注、能在目标域上纠正概念识别的办法。

Method: 提出TAG（Text Anchor re-Grounding）：在SAM3上增设Anchor Residual Pathway（图像条件、低秩adapter）以在目标分布中重定位文本锚并调制后续文本条件交互；仅用图像级类别标签联合优化二元交叉熵与Soft Boundary Ranking以校准存在置信度并区分易混负例；进一步用Confidence-Weighted Distillation将多模态大模型的语义上下文与SAM3的空间先验结合，在无人工像素标注下进行有效蒸馏。参数开销约3.1M。

Result: 在多种场景上显著提升SAM3的下游分割性能，解锁其潜力；在若干指标上甚至超过主流mask监督方法。

Conclusion: 瓶颈在于概念识别而非掩码质量。通过TAG的文本锚重定位与置信度加权蒸馏，可用极少可训练参数、仅靠图像级标签实现对SAM3的有效校准与强性能提升。

Abstract: The recent Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) extends powerful promptable segmentation from spatial cues to concepts, raising the prospect of a single foundation model for universal image segmentation. However, this promise often fails to translate into reliable performance on downstream segmentation tasks. In this work, we empirically identify concept recognition, rather than mask quality, as the primary bottleneck: SAM3 accurately delineates the objects it recognizes yet struggles to robustly determine which concepts are present. Driven by this, we present \textit{\underline{T}ext \underline{A}nchor re-\underline{G}rounding (\TAG{})}, a simple yet effective framework that rectifies the concept recognition of SAM3 on the target domain without dense annotations. Specifically, \TAG{} introduces an \textit{Anchor Residual Pathway} comprising image-conditioned, low-rank adapters to re-ground text anchors in the target distribution and modulate subsequent text-conditioned interactions. Guided solely by image-level category labels, \TAG{} jointly optimizes binary cross-entropy with \textit{Soft Boundary Ranking} objectives to calibrate presence confidence and distinguish present concepts from confusable negatives. Building upon this, we devise \textit{Confidence-Weighted Distillation} to couple rich semantic context of a multimodal large language model with fine-grained spatial priors of SAM3, thereby achieving effective calibration in the absence of manual annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \TAG{} unlocks SAM3's segmentation potential with only 3.1M trainable parameters, achieving impressive performance across diverse scenarios, and notably, even surpassing leading mask-supervised methods on several metrics. \href{https://github.com/MiSsU-HH/TAG_SAM3}{Our code will be publicly available at GitHub \faGithub}.

</details>


### [44] [Prospective Forcing: Learning to Look Ahead for Autoregressive Long Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14654)
*Yifan Shen,Yewei Xia,Gongxu Luo,Peiyuan Zhu,Boyang Sun,Zhenhao Chen,Guangyi Liu,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“前瞻强制”（Prospective Forcing）的多头预测框架，在自回归视频生成中同时预测多个未来时间偏移的片段，提升长时序一致性并加速采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型通常只学习“下一片段”预测，滚动生成时误差累积、长程依赖难以维护，导致长视频质量下降与时序不一致。作者希望在训练中引入更远期的监督信号，增强历史表示对长时预测的有用信息保持，同时在推理端提升效率。

Method: 在滚动窗口式生成器上添加多个偏移特定（offset-specific）的轻量多头，每个头共享相同条件与历史表示，独立预测不同未来时间偏移的片段；通过扩展监督到“下一片段以外”的未来，强制共享表示编码长程信息。推理时：利用可接受的未来片段预测跳过部分窗口的主干执行以加速；为下一片段头加入可学习嵌入以生成多个候选；再用已学习的验证器选择最优候选，若全部不合格则回退到标准自回归生成。

Result: 多头远期监督促使共享历史表示更具长程预测能力，实现更强的时间一致性；同时利用被接受的未来预测可减少主干前向次数，实现采样加速；候选-验证机制在保证质量的同时避免错误放大。

Conclusion: 通过在训练期注入远期监督与在推理期引入候选选择和跳步执行，Prospective Forcing在不显著增加参数的情况下提升了长视频生成的稳定性与效率，可作为通用插件用于自回归视频生成框架。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generators synthesize long videos through chunk-wise rollouts, yet they are typically trained to predict only the immediate future. This limited prediction horizon makes it challenging to maintain video quality over extended rollouts. To address this problem, we introduce Prospective Forcing, a multi-head prediction framework that injects long-range predictive signals into autoregressive video representations. Built on a rolling-window generator, Prospective Forcing attaches lightweight, offset-specific heads that share the same conditioning information and independently predict chunks at different future temporal offsets. By extending supervision beyond the next chunk, these heads encourage the shared history representation to retain information useful for longer-horizon prediction, supporting temporally consistent generation. At inference time, we accelerate sampling by using accepted future-chunk predictions to skip full-window backbone executions. We equip the head with learnable embeddings to generate multiple candidates for the next chunk, and a learned verifier either selects a candidate or rejects all and falls back to standard autoregressive generation.

</details>


### [45] [TGuide: Faster Autoregressive Video Diffusion via Historical Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14657)
*Zhuo Chen,Bin Zou,Fanyue Wei,Lixin Duan,Angela Yao,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TGuide，用历史采样轨迹引导的轻量ATC（Anchor-Transport-Correct）预测器，在仅剩4步去噪的自回归视频扩散中加速，保持质量并达至最高1.89×提速，并提升长视频泛化与VBench分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当扩散只剩很少步数（如4步）时，相邻时间步特征变化大，传统特征缓存与轻量预测难以复用或准确外推；但作者观察到：跨视频块在相同去噪步的特征仍高度相关，可作为新型指导信号。

Method: 提出TGuide框架与ATC（Anchor-Transport-Correct）预测器：
- Anchor：用当前块的上一步特征保持空间结构。
- Transport：用前一块在同一去噪步的特征提供目标步信息，通过空间检索进行对齐/迁移。
- Correct：自适应校正融合两路参考以提升速度（velocity）预测精度；
- 训练时进行自回归rollout，让预测器适应自身误差分布并抑制跨步与跨块误差累积；
- 可选的置信度感知调度器在质量-效率间自适应取舍。

Result: 在Self Forcing、Causal Forcing、HY-WorldPlay数据上，相比原始4步生成器，实现最高1.89×去噪加速且基本保持生成质量；在Self Forcing上从训练的81帧泛化到生成333帧（约4倍时长），并超过原始模型的VBench分数。

Conclusion: 利用跨块同步步特征相关性作为补充指导，通过ATC的锚定-迁移-校正与自回归式训练，能在极少步去噪的自回归视频扩散中显著加速而不牺牲质量，并提升长时序泛化能力。

Abstract: Can autoregressive video diffusion be accelerated further when only four denoising steps remain? Existing feature caching approaches struggle in this regime, as large feature changes between denoising timesteps undermine both direct reuse and lightweight predictors. We observe that features across video chunks remain strongly correlated at matching denoising timesteps, offering a complementary source of guidance. Building on this insight, we introduce TGuide, a framework that accelerates autoregressive video diffusion through a lightweight Anchor-Transport-Correct (ATC) predictor guided by historical sampling trajectories. Our ATC combines two complementary references: the current chunk's preceding-step features preserve its spatial structure, while the previous chunk's same-timestep features provide information at the target denoising stage. By integrating these references through spatial retrieval and adaptive correction, ATC improves velocity prediction accuracy despite the large gaps between denoising steps. To mitigate error accumulation across both denoising steps and video chunks, we train the predictor on its own autoregressive rollouts, allowing it to adapt to states shaped by its own earlier predictions. An optional confidence-aware scheduler further adjusts the quality-efficiency trade-off. Experiments on Self Forcing, Causal Forcing, and HY-WorldPlay show that TGuide preserves generation quality while achieving denoising speedups of up to $1.89\times$ over the original 4-step generators. On Self Forcing, TGuide generalizes from 81-frame training videos to 333-frame generation, approximately four times the training length, while exceeding the original model's VBench score.

</details>


### [46] [TETRIS-4DGS: Efficient Post-Training Compression of 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14663)
*Chengzhen Wu,Kelei Liu,Sicheng Li,Lu Yu,Yiyi Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种针对4D高斯溅射（4DGS）的通用后训练压缩框架，基于重要性自适应量化与运动/重要性感知打包，将属性映射为适合现有图像/视频编解码器的2D图，实现秒级、40×以上压缩且保持高质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4DGS能实时高质量渲染动态场景，但存储开销巨大。现有压缩多为逐场景优化，通用性差、延迟高，不利于实际部署。需要一种能快速、通用、与现有编解码生态兼容的压缩方案。

Method: 1) 重要性驱动的自适应量化：依据高斯的重要性分配不同量化步长，细化码率分配并与现成编解码器兼容。2) 运动与重要性感知的属性打包：利用4DGS统计特性，将时空与重要性关联的属性重排为“编解码友好”的2D贴图，交由现有图像/视频编码器压缩。

Result: 在不牺牲显著画质的前提下，实现超过40倍压缩，并能在数秒内完成压缩过程。

Conclusion: 该通用后训练压缩框架显著降低4DGS存储成本、具备低时延与强通用性，便于在实际场景中快速部署动态场景表示。

Abstract: 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has emerged as a powerful dynamic scene representation, supporting real-time high quality rendering but suffers from prohibitive storage overhead. Existing methods typically attempt to solve this by treating compression as a per-scene optimization problem, limiting generalizability and applicability where low-latency compression is required. In this work, we leverage existing image and video codecs and propose a generalized post-training compression framework for 4DGS to address these issues. Specifically, we apply importance-based adaptive quantization to Gaussians according to their importance levels, effectively refining bitrate allocation while remaining fully compatible with existing codecs. To further improve compression efficiency, we utilize the statistical properties of 4DGS and propose motion- and importance-aware packing to reorganize attributes into codec-friendly 2D maps. Our approach can achieve over 40x compression rate in seconds while maintaining high quality, facilitating the practical deployment of 4DGS.

</details>


### [47] [SPARE: Shared Perception and Adaptive Retrieval of Evidence for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14671)
*Senbin Xu,Zijun Zhang,Xinhua Wu,Li He,Jubo Feng,Li Qiao,Zhen Gao,Yan Zhang,Jixiang Luo,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARE：在视频流问答中，先判断“当前片段是否足够回答”，不足则自适应检索历史片段；检索与理解共享视觉编码器，通过LoRA指令微调与蒸馏对齐，多模态联合推理并在证据不足时选择放弃作答。在OVO‑Bench与StreamingBench显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频问答既需关注最新帧，又常依赖较早事件；引入过多历史会干扰当前判断，但缺少历史又会漏答。历史何时介入、选哪段、以及仅“相关”并不等于“足够证据”是关键痛点。此外，独立的检索与理解分支造成视觉参数冗余。

Method: 提出SPARE：1) 轻量门控模块判断当前观察是否足以回答；2) 若不足，用多模态嵌入模型从历史中自适应检索相关片段补充上下文；3) 通过LoRA指令微调训练理解模型学会联合使用当前与历史，并在证据不足时选择弃答；4) 检索与理解共享同一视觉编码器，但使用独立语言骨干；5) 四个线性适配器将共享视觉特征与检索模型对齐，并在视觉token和最终检索嵌入两层级进行蒸馏。

Result: 纯视觉输入下：在OVO‑Bench（实时与回溯任务组）得分77.78%；在StreamingBench得分73.31%。相较Qwen3‑VL‑8B‑Instruct（2 fps评测），在OVO‑Bench提升20.68个百分点、在StreamingBench提升14.15个百分点。

Conclusion: 自适应历史检索+共享视觉编码的设计，在保证当前帧决策不被历史噪声干扰的同时，有效利用必要历史证据，并降低参数冗余，显著提升流式视频问答性能。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires answering questions from the observed video, with evidence drawn from recent observations or earlier events. Historical context can improve recall but may interfere with judgments based on recent frames. Its usefulness depends on both when it is introduced and which clips are selected, while relevance alone does not guarantee sufficient evidence for an answer. We propose SPARE, a framework that combines adaptive historical retrieval with visual encoder sharing. A lightweight gate predicts whether recent observations provide sufficient evidence to answer the question. When additional evidence is predicted to be necessary, a multimodal embedding model retrieves relevant historical clips to supplement the current context. LoRA instruction tuning trains the understanding model to use recent observations and retrieved history jointly and abstain when the evidence remains insufficient. To reduce redundant visual parameters, the understanding and retrieval branches share a visual encoder while retaining separate language backbones. Four linear adapters align the shared features with the retrieval model through distillation at the visual token and final retrieval embedding levels. With visual-only inputs, SPARE achieves 77.78% on the evaluated Real-time and Backward task groups of OVO-Bench and 73.31% on StreamingBench. Compared with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct evaluated at 2 fps, SPARE improves accuracy by 20.68 percentage points on OVO-Bench and 14.15 percentage points on StreamingBench.

</details>


### [48] [Competition Domains Matter: Selective Head Globalization for Training-Free Sparse Attention in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14672)
*Tiantian Liu,Meng Wan,Zhiqiang Liang,Jue Wang,Ningming Nie,Yangang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EG-Sparse 在训练免调的稀疏注意力中，根据注意头熵自适应地改变竞争域：低熵头共享跨头池，高熵头保留行内本地选择，从而在不新增训练与额外分数计算的前提下，提升视频质量并降低注意力密度与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免调的稀疏注意力多在每个注意头内固定查询行的竞争域，只关注在该固定域内如何估计token重要性，忽略了不同注意头之间异质性可能需要不同的竞争域，导致低效或次优的选择。

Method: 提出EG-Sparse：利用稀疏管线已产生的分数，基于注意头的熵来决定竞争域路由——低熵头路由到跨头分组池（共享候选竞争域），高熵头保留逐行的本地选择；不需训练。通过受控诊断，仅改变竞争规则而固定排序分数以分离变量影响。

Result: 在诊断实验中，跨头分组选择使低熵头重建误差降低9.8%；而将高熵头强制进全局跨头池会使误差上升7.9%。在四种配置下，相比强力的训练免调稀疏注意力基线，EG-Sparse同时提升视频质量并降低注意力密度与GPU去噪延迟。

Conclusion: 竞争域自适应是训练免调、质心引导的稀疏注意力中的独立关键设计维度。选择性域适应优于统一、随机或固定的域分配策略，能在不训练的前提下更好地平衡质量与效率。

Abstract: Training-free sparse attention is a practical route to efficient video diffusion transformer inference, yet existing methods typically fix the competition domain at each query row for every attention head. Prior work primarily addresses how to estimate token-level importance within this fixed domain; whether the domain itself should adapt to head heterogeneity remains under-specified. We introduce EG-Sparse, a training-free framework that routes low-entropy heads to a grouped cross-head pool while retaining per-row local selection for high-entropy heads, using only scores already materialized by the sparse pipeline. A controlled diagnostic fixes the ranking score and varies only the competition rule: grouped cross-head selection reduces low-entropy-head reconstruction error by 9.8%, whereas forcing high-entropy heads into an all-head global pool increases it by 7.9%. Across four configurations, EG-Sparse achieves higher video quality while reducing attention density and GPU denoising latency compared with strong training-free sparse-attention baselines. Mechanism ablations further show that selective domain adaptation outperforms uniform, random, and fixed domain-assignment policies. These results support competition-domain adaptation as a distinct design axis within centroid-guided training-free sparse attention.

</details>


### [49] [CAST-ASD: Causal Attention over Speakers and Time for Online Active Speaker Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14676)
*Pratham Gupta,Haotian Qi,Tushar Sandhan,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAST-ASD，一种结合跨说话人场景注意力与因果时间注意力的低延迟联合ASD模型，实现零前瞻的在线检测，并在多说话人场景显著降低误报与参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优ASD多为离线、需整段视频才能标注；而低延迟因果模型通常独立处理每张人脸，无法共享说话人间信息，易在多说话人场景产生“竞争说话人”误报。需要一种既因果低延迟又能跨人脸联合推理的模型。

Method: 提出CAST-ASD：在当前帧对所有可见人脸做场景级（跨人脸）注意力，同时对每张人脸沿时间轴做因果（仅用过去）注意力，实现零lookahead的信息融合；参数量与场景内人脸数无关。

Result: 在MSDWild数据集上，相比其扩展的因果逐脸基线，竞争说话人误报下降32%，且场景人物越多收益越大；在两个验证划分上均优于所有因果基线；相较三块固定名册卷积模型，仅用约1/10的跨说话人参数。

Conclusion: CAST-ASD实现了联合但因果的ASD推理，在多说话人复杂场景中以更少参数获得更低误报与更好整体性能，适合低延迟在线应用。

Abstract: We present CAST-ASD (Causal Attention over Speakers and Time), a causal joint model for active speaker detection (ASD). Recent ASD models improve performance by jointly processing all faces in a scene, but operate offline, with the full clip available before any frame is labelled. Low-latency causal detectors instead process each face independently. CAST-ASD combines joint face reasoning with fully causal inference. It applies scene-level attention across faces within the current frame and causal temporal attention over each face's history, enabling information sharing across speakers with zero lookahead. Its parameter count does not grow with the number of faces. On MSDWild, where most negative face-frames contain a visible competing speaker, CAST-ASD reduces competing-speaker false alarms by 32% relative to the causal per-face detector it extends, with larger gains as scene occupancy increases. It outperforms all causal baselines on both validation splits while using only one-tenth the inter-speaker parameters of a three-block fixed-roster convolution. Code and models are available at https://github.com/prathamg007/CAST-ASD.

</details>


### [50] [HumanPhys: A Human-Centric Physical-World Perception Benchmark for Real-World Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14679)
*Yiming Zhang,Enzhi Zhou,Junan Lin,Dulan Zhou,Weiqiang Wang,Yuge Huang,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HumanPhys 提出一个以人为中心的物理感知基准与配套训练语料，覆盖深度、距离、注视角、位移/速度/轨迹、眨眼、接触-形变、光照与相机自运动等，多来源于传感器/渲染器元数据的程序化标签。对六大维度进行系统评测，现有开闭源 MLLM 在各维度均表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型缺乏对人与场景中物理量的系统性监督与评测，现有基准多依赖人工标注或第三方估计器，难以提供精确一致的物理真值，且很少联合覆盖人相关的空间、运动、事件、形变、光照与相机姿态等关键维度。

Method: 构建一个生成与标注统一的流水线：通过传感器校准、源数据元信息与渲染器状态，直接生成问题—答案对及精确物理标签；问题围绕人与相机的物理量（深度、距离、注视角、位移/速度/朝向-步态轨迹、眨眼计数、接触-形变强度、光照推断、相机自运动），并能在有无人物情况下测试光照与自运动推断；据此形成HumanPhys基准与训练语料，对多种开闭源MLLM进行系统评测。

Result: 六个能力维度在受测的多种MLLM上均表现困难，维度间只部分可分离，显示当前模型对以人为中心的物理感知与推理仍存在系统性短板。

Conclusion: HumanPhys提供了一个高保真、程序化标注的人类中心物理感知基准与语料，填补联合监督多物理维度的空白，可作为推动多模态系统理解真实世界中人的物理属性与动态的重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce HumanPhys, a human-centric benchmark for real-world physical perception, and a training corpus emitted by the same pipeline. Each question targets a physically grounded quantity related to people, including depth, inter-person distance, gaze angle, displacement, velocity, heading-step trajectory, blink count, and contact-deformation strength. The rendered probes additionally test illumination inference from body and face cues and camera ego-motion, both with and without a person in the frame. All labels are derived programmatically from sensor calibration, source metadata, or renderer state, rather than written by annotators or produced by estimators on the benchmark frames. HumanPhys systematically probes six dimensions of human-centric physical perception: spatial state, kinematics, blink events, deformation, illumination, and camera pose. To the best of our knowledge, no existing MLLM benchmark-and-training corpus jointly supervises this set of human-centered physical quantities under direct metadata- or renderer-derived labels. The six dimensions appear only partially dissociable across the tested MLLMs. We benchmark a range of open- and closed-source MLLMs on HumanPhys and find that all six capability dimensions remain challenging for current models. We hope HumanPhys will support future research on multimodal systems that perceive and reason about people in the physical world.

</details>


### [51] [Demiurge: Forging Navigable 4D Worlds via Autoregressive Video Rerendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14685)
*Hanxin Zhu,Jianqi Ma,Cong Wang,Zhihao Hu,Junyao Gao,Tianyu He,Junyan Lin,Jiayin Xiao,Jiateng Shou,Sen Liang,Weidong Zhang,Zhibo Chen,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Demiurge：基于动态4D点云记忆缓存的自回归视频重渲流程，实现跨轨迹一致的新视角视频生成，并在大视差和极端相机运动下达到SOTA，产物可直接优化为高质量3DGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视频新视角视频扩散模型已能生成多视角视频，但不同相机轨迹独立处理，导致跨轨迹不一致，难以构建可导航、连贯的4D世界；还存在尺度歧义、遮挡处理与几何不连续等问题。

Method: 构建动态4D点云记忆缓存为全局世界表征，采用自回归重渲流程并包含四个关键组件：1) 外观视频扩散模型，条件依赖4D记忆以合成写实新视角视频；2) 记忆一致的视频深度补全，使已知区域深度与缓存几何对齐并补全缺失，缓解尺度歧义与不连续；3) 记忆感知的双流变形/重投影，为生成提供高保真视觉引导与遮挡线索；4) 几何感知的视频细化，提升感知质量。

Result: 在大量实验中达成SOTA，并展现三项能力：a) 在密集多视角轨迹上构建连贯的时空格点（spatiotemporal lattice）；b) 在大视差与极端运动（含180°无重叠）下稳定重渲；c) 输出几何准确，可直接优化为高质量3D Gaussian Splatting，伪影极少。

Conclusion: 以4D点云记忆为核心的自回归视频重渲能实现跨轨迹一致、几何忠实的4D世界建模，并在复杂相机运动下保持高保真与可重建性，为世界模型与机器人仿真提供可靠基座。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion enable novel-view video synthesis from a single input video, supporting world modeling, embodied intelligence, and robotic simulation. However, existing methods process camera trajectories independently, lacking the cross-trajectory consistency needed for coherent, navigable 4D worlds. We introduce Demiurge, an autoregressive video rerendering pipeline built on a dynamic 4D point-cloud memory cache. It promotes cross-trajectory consistency through four components: (1) an appearance video diffusion model that synthesizes photorealistic novel-view videos conditioned on the 4D memory; (2) a memory-consistent video depth completion model that aligns known-region depths with cached geometry and completes missing regions, mitigating scale ambiguity and discontinuities during memory updates; (3) memory-aware dual-stream warping that provides high-fidelity visual guidance and occlusion cues for novel-view synthesis; and (4) geometry-aware video refinement that enhances perceptual quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and three key capabilities: coherent spatiotemporal lattice construction over dense multi-view trajectories; large-parallax video rerendering under challenging camera motions, including 180-degree rotations with no input-view overlap; and geometry-faithful outputs that can be directly optimized into high-quality 3D Gaussian Splatting representations with minimal artifacts. Project page: https://hanxinzhu-lab.github.io/demiurge_project_page/.

</details>


### [52] [PURE: Refining Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction with Unlabeled Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14698)
*Yaolong Zhu,Peng Li,Jiahao Lu,Chengfeng Zhao,Mengfei Li,Chao Wang,Wenyue Chen,Zekai Gu,Chengwei Ren,Ronggang Wang,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PURE：一种无需标注的后训练精炼框架，将2D强先验蒸馏到4D前馈重建模型，以在动态视频中稳定并提升深度与相机位姿估计。通过相对深度约束、仿射不变几何蒸馏和基于特征对应的位姿优化，在多架构与数据集上带来一致增益并缓解域偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建模型在大规模训练下表现出色，但在包含非刚体运动、遮挡与光照变化的野外动态视频上易失稳；且依赖昂贵的深度与位姿标注，难以扩展与泛化。需要一种无需标注、能在动态场景中稳健提升几何预测的方法。

Method: 提出PURE作为无监督后训练（post-training）精炼：1）利用SOTA 2D先验进行知识蒸馏，将稳健的结构线索转移到4D前馈模型；2）相对深度约束以避免尺度/偏移依赖；3）仿射不变几何蒸馏提高在视角与光照变化下的一致性；4）基于特征对应的位姿细化以稳定相机估计。整体无需GT深度或相机标注。

Result: 在合成与真实动态基准上，跨多种SOTA架构取得一致性提升；在具有强域偏移的野外动态场景中显著改善深度与相机位姿稳定性与精度。

Conclusion: 通过无标注的2D先验蒸馏与几何一致性约束，PURE有效解锁前馈4D重建模型在动态场景中的潜力，缓解域偏移并提升泛化，无需昂贵标注。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models have achieved remarkable progress through large-scale training and strong learned priors. However, they remain fragile when applied to dynamic in-the-wild videos, where non-rigid motion, occlusions, and illumination changes are prevalent. A key limitation lies in their reliance on expensive depth and camera pose annotations, which hinders scaling to diverse real-world dynamic data and restricts generalization. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{PURE}, an unlabeled post-training refinement framework that improves depth and camera estimation without requiring ground-truth supervision. Instead of relying on annotations, PURE elegantly distills robust structural knowledge from state-of-the-art 2D priors into 4D feed-forward architectures. PURE leverages relative depth constraints, affine-invariant geometry distillation, and pose refinement based on feature correspondences to stabilize geometric predictions under challenging dynamic conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world dynamic benchmarks demonstrate that PURE delivers consistent improvements across multiple state-of-the-art architectures. Furthermore, our data-efficient paradigm effectively mitigates domain shifts, successfully unlocking the architectural limits of these base models in challenging, in-the-wild dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [53] [PixARG: Pixel-Asynchronous Retrieval-Augmented Diffusion for Fast and Realistic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14713)
*Zewei Xin,Qinya Li,Chaoyue Niu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixARG 将外部图像检索融入扩散生成：用文本–检索图像的像素级亲和图来做异质噪声初始化，并配套像素异步调度与时空去噪器及亲和自适应目标，在多场景下更快收敛、更逼真、更贴合语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型计算昂贵、易产生不可信伪影，且因静态集中训练难以适配动态/私有数据。需要一种能利用实时外部知识且提升效率与真实感的方法。

Method: 1) 在线检索与文本相关的真实图像；2) 计算文本与检索图像的像素级亲和图；3) 用亲和图进行空间非均匀的异质噪声初始化：相关区域降噪声、非相关区域加噪声；4) 设计像素级异步去噪调度器，使不同区域以不同步速去噪；5) 以亲和图为条件的时空去噪网络；6) 提出亲和自适应训练目标，在像素层面在确定性复原与随机再生之间权衡。

Result: 在 LAION、COYO 的离线、在线与用户特定场景实验中，PixARG 相比基线显著减少扩散步数，加速收敛，同时提高图像真实感与文本—图像语义对齐度，伪影更少。

Conclusion: 将检索增强与像素级亲和引导融入扩散流程，可在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升效率与可适配性；像素异步调度与自适应目标是关键。

Abstract: While diffusion models achieve impressive image generation capabilities, they suffer from high computational costs, implausible artifacts, and limited adaptability to dynamic or private data due to static, centralized training. To address these, we propose PixARG, a novel retrieval-augmented generative framework that integrates dynamic real-world image retrieval into the diffusion pipeline to simultaneously enhance efficiency and visual realism. Specifically, PixARG constructs a pixel-level affinity map between the user's text prompt and the retrieved image to guide a heterogeneous noise initialization. By modulating noise intensity across spatial regions, this mechanism preserves semantically relevant structural priors while perturbing less relevant areas, thereby significantly accelerating convergence. To accommodate this spatially non-uniform noise and ensure synchronized generation, we introduce a pixel-wise asynchronous denoising scheduler and a spatiotemporal denoiser conditioned on the affinity map. Furthermore, we derive an affinity-adaptive training objective that elegantly balances deterministic restoration with stochastic regeneration across pixels. Experiments on LAION and COYO datasets across offline, online, and user-specific settings show PixARG enhances realism and semantic alignment while drastically reducing diffusion steps.

</details>


### [54] [LightVAE: Towards Compact and Efficient Video Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14726)
*Shiqiao Gu,Yang Yong,Fengjun Zhong,Ruihao Gong,Chengtao Lv,Xiangyu Fan,Lei Yang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightVAE通过对因果Conv3D进行可精确重参数化，并仅对“时间校正”部分做低秩压缩，同时结合宽度保持的块剪枝与教师引导对齐，实现视频VAE（解码/编码）在多GPU、多骨干上的显著加速与画质保持，超过现有高效解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VAE推理昂贵；已有轻量化多在特定阶段施加统一/过度的时空压缩，或直接去除时间建模，忽视预训练Conv3D在空间与时间维度、以及网络深度上的不同压缩敏感性，导致性能或质量受损。

Method: 1) 对可用的因果Conv3D层进行精确重参数化：将其分解为“聚合的空间响应”(dense)与“差分驱动的时间校正”(temporal correction)。2) 时间低秩压缩(TLR)：仅对时间校正核做分层/逐层低秩近似，空间核保持致密；并给出代数等价的折叠实现，将算术节省转化为真实加速。3) 宽度保持的块剪枝以减少网络深度。4) 探针引导的特征对齐：利用冻结教师后缀反馈，指导压缩模型恢复质量。5) 同步扩展到视频编码器。

Result: 在三种视频VAE骨干与两类GPU上均加速解码：对Wan VAE家族，在NVIDIA H100与RTX 5090上分别达到2.24×与1.11×的吞吐提升，同时获得更高DAVIS PSNR与更低LPIPS；在MiniMax-H3上，相对原模型解码吞吐提升至2.97×，VBench-2.0总体得分保持99.51%。

Conclusion: 区别对待空间与时间压缩敏感度并结合网络深度剪裁与教师对齐，可在不牺牲（甚至提升）视频质量的前提下显著加速视频VAE的编解码；LightVAE在多平台、多骨干上稳定有效，优于现有高效VAE解码器。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models enable high-quality video generation, but video VAE encoding and decoding remain computationally expensive. Existing methods use stage-specific lightweight operators, yet either constrain spatial and temporal computation jointly or remove temporal computation altogether, without distinguishing their compression sensitivities in pretrained Conv3D. To address these limitations, we present LightVAE, a compression framework that exploits redundancy in temporal modeling and network depth. LightVAE exactly reparameterizes eligible causal Conv3D layers into an aggregate spatial response and a difference-driven temporal correction. Based on their distinct compression sensitivities, temporal low-rank compression (TLR) keeps the aggregate spatial kernel dense and applies layer-specific low-rank approximation only to the temporal correction. An algebraically equivalent folded implementation converts the arithmetic savings into practical acceleration. LightVAE further uses width-preserving block pruning to reduce depth and probe-guided feature alignment, guided by feedback from a frozen teacher suffix, to recover quality after compression. The framework also extends to video encoders. Across three video VAE backbones and two GPU platforms, LightVAE consistently accelerates decoding. For the Wan VAE family, it achieves up to $2.24\times$ and $1.11\times$ the decoding throughput of state-of-the-art efficient VAE decoders on NVIDIA H100 and RTX~5090, respectively, with higher DAVIS PSNR and lower LPIPS. On MiniMax-H3, it achieves up to $2.97\times$ the original decoding throughput while retaining 99.51\% of the original model's overall VBench-2.0 score. Our code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightVAE.

</details>


### [55] [Align-VAE: Aligning Attention and Geometry in VecSet VAEs for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14734)
*Huaizhi Qu,Mingxin Yang,Xintong Han,Fangneng Zhan,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Align-VAE，通过对 VecSet VAE 的注意力、查询特征与法向监督进行四项改进，使潜变量与几何对齐并显著提升 3D 形状重建精度（ABO 上 Chamfer 距离下降 26.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VecSet VAE 用集合潜变量表示 3D 形状，但潜变量与真实几何是否对齐不明，且存在注意力吸附、解码器周期性响应、编解码器法向信息不一致、以及 SDF 与法向关系缺乏监督等问题，限制了重建质量与可解释性。

Method: 提出 Align-VAE，对应四个问题做四项设计：1) 引入共享可学习解码器 token 银行，作为全局计算工作区以缓解注意力“吸附”token；2) 在跨注意力中加入距离偏置，引导检索靠近预测的空间锚点，减少远程误检索与周期性伪影；3) 编码器查询不再使用法向信息，但在键和值中保留法向，使编码器与解码器对法向访问一致；4) 基于筛选泊松（screened Poisson）的正则，同时监督 SDF 的表面取值与法向方向导数，使场与法向内在一致。

Result: 在 ABO 数据集上相较标准 VecSet VAE，Chamfer 距离降低 26.6%，且在不增加每形状码长度的情况下实现对齐与精度提升。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的注意力与几何一致性正则，Align-VAE 将潜变量检索与场预测与几何对齐，提升 3D 重建质量且保持编码紧凑，验证了对 VecSet VAE 的有效改进。

Abstract: Set-based variational autoencoders (VecSet VAEs) represent 3D shapes as compact sets of latent tokens. Although each token originates from a query at a sampled surface location, whether these latents truly align with geometry remains unclear. We investigate this alignment and identify four issues: (i) attention-sink-like tokens that attract queries from across the shape; (ii) spatially periodic repetitions in decoder attention responses; (iii) mismatched normal information in encoder and decoder query features; and (iv) missing supervision of the intrinsic relation between signed distance fields and surface normals. To address these issues, we introduce Align-VAE with four corresponding modifications: (i) a shared bank of learnable decoder tokens provides a workspace for global computation; (ii) distance-biased cross-attention guides retrieval toward nearby predicted spatial anchors; (iii) normal-free encoder queries align the two stages' access to normal information while retaining normals in the encoder keys and values; and (iv) a screened Poisson-inspired regularizer supervises surface values and normal-direction derivatives. These changes align latent retrieval and field prediction with geometry without enlarging the per-shape code. On the ABO dataset, Align-VAE reduces Chamfer distance by 26.6% compared to the standard VecSet VAE.

</details>


### [56] [Japanese Handwriting Generation Based on Human Preference Feedback Using Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14743)
*Wakana Kuwata,Ryota Mibayashi,Masanori Tani,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合人类偏好反馈（DPO）的日文手写体生成框架，在无需大规模数据的情况下提升生成质量；对简单到中等复杂风格有效，但在高复杂度（如毛笔）风格上难以捕捉风格特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字体生成可稳定输出一致风格，但手写体天然存在非确定性与形变多样性，导致训练困难、数据采集成本高；需要一种利用人类感知偏好、降低数据需求、同时对齐结构与风格偏好的方法。

Method: 在手写体生成模型的训练环中引入人类成对偏好评估，采用无需强化学习的Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）直接对齐生成分布与人类偏好；以四种日文手写风格为对象，比较常规训练与DPO训练的差异。

Result: DPO训练在四种风格上整体提升了字形结构准确性；但对于变异度高、结构复杂的风格（如毛笔字），模型未能充分学到风格特征。

Conclusion: 人类偏好驱动的DPO能在有限数据下改进日文手写体生成的结构质量，但对高复杂度、高变异风格存在局限，提示需发展能更好捕捉风格多样性的偏好学习或模型结构。

Abstract: We propose a Japanese handwriting generation framework that integrates human preference feedback using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to align generative models with human cognitive and stylistic preferences. While font generation technologies leveraging Generative Adversarial Networks and diffusion models have advanced significantly, applying these approaches to handwriting generation presents unique challenges. Font generation is a deterministic process yielding consistent styles. Handwriting generation, on the other hand, is inherently non-deterministic. In handwriting, glyph shapes never match exactly even when the same writer writes the same character. We define this shape difference as handwriting variability. Although this variability is essential for conveying the natural quality of human writing, it complicates model training and substantially increases data collection costs to capture diverse distribution patterns. To address these limitations, we incorporate human perceptual feedback into the training loop of handwriting generation. While collecting extensive handwritten data is costly, evaluating pairwise preferences between two generated samples remains very easy for humans. Applying DPO, an efficient RL-free preference optimization method, to our generation model enables high-quality handwriting generation without relying on large-scale dataset collection. Experimental results across four distinct Japanese handwriting styles demonstrate that DPO-based training enhances overall structural accuracy. However, in highly complex styles with significant variability, such as brush pen writing, the model fails to capture style-specific features. These findings highlight a critical challenge in extending DPO to complex handwriting styles.

</details>


### [57] [YOUDrive: Driving Style as a Composable Direction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14802)
*Guantian Zheng,Lidong Yu,Tianyu Gao,Jiaxing Chen,Jiashu Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YOUDrive：一种视觉-语言-动作规划器，通过“任务向量”实现可连续、可组合的驾驶风格控制，并用流匹配解码器生成多模态、运动学可行的轨迹；在NAVSIM上可单系数单调调节“果断度”，在保证安全/任务达成的前提下PDMS≥0.90（最高0.954），并提出风格对齐评分SAS衡量风格对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶虽性能强，但缺乏对加速、制动、车距等“驾驶风格”的可控性；现有方法在推理时难以根据偏好调风格。作者希望无需重训骨干、可在推理时连续调节并可组合多种风格，同时用可解释的指标量化风格对齐。

Method: 1) 将不同“教师”策略的行为蒸馏为任务向量：对规划器参数的可学习更新，形成风格方向；推理时可缩放与线性组合，无需重训骨干。2) 在给定风格方向条件下，用流匹配（flow-matching）解码器建模多模态轨迹分布，生成运动学可行的轨迹候选。3) 提出SAS（Style Alignment Score），在通过安全与任务门控后，用人类参照的运动学统计衡量风格匹配度。4) 在NAVSIM navtest上用六种教师派生的人设（persona）进行评测，并考察单系数调参与多风格混合。

Result: - 单一系数可在六种人设间单调控制“果断度”。- 在个体与混合配置下，PDMS保持>0.90，最高达0.954。- 控制场景语境后，仍观察到系统性的策略特定运动学差异；离散风格标签的额外解释力有限。- 代码、权重与指标将开源。

Conclusion: YOUDrive实现了无需重训即可连续、可组合地控制端到端驾驶风格，并通过流匹配解码器产生多模态且可行的轨迹；SAS提供了人参照的可量化风格对齐度。实验显示在保持高任务成功的同时可稳健调节果断度，且风格方向具备可组合性与可迁移性。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies can achieve strong task performance while offering limited control over their driving style. Driving style describes how a vehicle accelerates, brakes, and maintains distance. Policies trained without explicit preference conditioning lack a direct mechanism for adjusting these behaviors at inference. We propose YOUDrive, a vision--language--action planner for continuous and composable driving-style control. Each teacher's behavior is distilled into a task vector, a learned parameter update that induces a style direction in the planner's representation. These directions can be scaled and combined at inference without retraining the backbone. Conditioned on these directions, a flow-matching decoder models a multimodal trajectory distribution and generates kinematically feasible motion alternatives. We introduce the Style Alignment Score (SAS) to describe human-referenced kinematic behavior after safety and task gating. On NAVSIM navtest, a single coefficient monotonically controls assertiveness across six teacher-derived personas, while PDMS remains above 0.90 across the tested individual and blended configurations, reaching 0.954. Further analyses reveal systematic policy-specific kinematics after controlling for scene context, whereas the evaluated discrete style labels provide limited additional explanatory power. Code, checkpoints, and the metric will be released.

</details>


### [58] [[Re] Boosting the Visual Interpretability of CLIP via Adversarial Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14832)
*Anton Nuzhdin,Andrei Gruzitski,Balázs Egyed,Aleksandr Raudvee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 复现ICLR 2025方法：对CLIP进行无监督对抗微调并加入范数正则以提升可视化可解释性；作者验证其主张并提出基于显著图的Energy Pointing Game监管扩展，在小幅牺牲零样本精度/鲁棒性下显著提升定位指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验原论文关于：显著图质量提升、概念对齐增强、OOD可迁移性，以及与零样本精度的权衡等关键主张是否稳健；并探索显式显著图监督是否能进一步改进空间定位与可解释性，同时保持可接受的性能权衡；同时将代码结构化以便系统化实验。

Method: 1) 复现AFT：对CLIP图像编码器进行无监督对抗微调，并加入范数正则；评估显著图（Simple Gradient等）、概念对齐、OOD迁移与零样本精度权衡。2) 提出扩展：引入显著图引导的正则——Energy Pointing Game（EPG）损失，直接监督Simple Gradient与目标对象的空间对齐；在不同显著损失权重w_s∈{0.2,0.6等}上系统评测。3) 代码重构以支持可配置实验。

Result: - 复现层面：总体支持原主张（显著图质量与概念对齐提升、具备一定OOD迁移、与零样本精度存在权衡）。- 扩展层面：显式EPG监督显著提升定位指标；在w_s=0.2时仅带来很小的零样本精度与对抗鲁棒性下降；在w_s=0.6时下降中等。

Conclusion: AFT+范数正则的可解释性收益基本可复现；在此基础上加入EPG显著监督能在可控代价下显著提升定位性能。工程上提供了更可配置的代码框架，便于今后系统化研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a reproducibility study of ``Boosting the Visual Interpretability of CLIP via Adversarial Fine-Tuning'', published at ICLR 2025, which proposes an unsupervised adversarial fine-tuning (AFT) method with norm regularisation to enhance the visual interpretability of CLIP's image encoder. We attempt to reproduce the key claims regarding improved saliency map quality, increased concept alignment, transferability to out-of-distribution datasets, and the trade-off with zero-shot accuracy. Beyond reproduction, we propose a saliency-guided regularisation extension that introduces an Energy Pointing Game loss, directly supervising the spatial alignment of Simple Gradient saliency maps with target objects. We evaluate our extension across a range of saliency-loss weights and show that explicit saliency supervision \rev{substantially} improves localisation metrics at a modest cost: the reduction in zero-shot accuracy and adversarial robustness is small at the lowest saliency-loss weight ($w_s=0.2$) and moderate at the strongest setting ($w_s=0.6$). We restructure the codebase for configurable, systematic experimentation.

</details>


### [59] [Towards Privacy-Preserving Thermal Human Perception from Dataset to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14846)
*Kailai Sun,Yue Cui,Xinwei Wang,Zijie Gao,Baoshen Guo,Zhiqing Hong,Irfan Qaisar,Xu Yang,Yueting Li,Dian Fan,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Indoor RGB-T大规模室内成对RGB–热成像数据集与基线，验证在看不见房间中进行热图人体状态检测与占用估计的有效性，并在上千台商用睡眠监测设备上实测，揭示多项实际挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内人类感知对智慧家居与医疗关键；RGB相机有隐私问题，低分辨率热相机语义与空间信息有限，且缺乏大规模热成像数据与真实部署验证，需要兼顾隐私、鲁棒性的解决方案与标准基线。

Method: 构建覆盖20个室内房间的大规模配对RGB–热数据集（90,576张80×62热帧、87,016对时序匹配的RGB–热对、115,557个人体框）；建立并评测两类任务基线：热域的人员状态检测与占用估计；评估跨模态翻译（RGB↔热）并报告温度MAE与R²；将热感知模型部署到商用睡眠监测系统，进行100名参与者与约1,000台设备的实地测试。

Result: 在未见房间上，热域人员状态检测mAP=0.75，占用估计准确率=0.847；跨模态翻译达到温度MAE=0.654°C与R²=0.923；真实世界部署显示方案具备可行性。

Conclusion: Indoor RGB-T数据集与基线推动隐私友好的室内人感研究，在真实部署中可用；同时暴露出环境热干扰、感知距离与多人遮挡等挑战，指向未来改进方向；代码、模型与部分数据已开源。

Abstract: Indoor human perception is the foundation for smart homes and healthcare. However, RGB cameras raise privacy concerns in private indoor environments, while low-resolution thermal cameras provide limited spatial and semantic information. Large-scale indoor thermal datasets and real-world deployments remain limited. Here, we introduce Indoor RGB-T, a large-scale paired RGB--thermal dataset for indoor human perception. The dataset covers 20 indoor rooms and contains 90,576 80x62 thermal frames, 87,016 temporally matched RGB--thermal pairs, and 115,557 annotated human bounding boxes. We establish benchmarks for robust thermal occupant-state detection with 0.75 mAP and occupancy estimation with an accuracy of 0.847 in unseen rooms. We further evaluate RGB-to-thermal and thermal-to-RGB translation, achieving a temperature MAE of 0.654 C and an R2 of 0.923. Finally, we deploy the thermal perception model on a commercial sleep-monitoring system in the real world. Field testing with 100 participants and deployment of approximately 1,000 devices further demonstrate its practical feasibility. We identify practical challenges that motivate future research directions, including environmental thermal interference, sensing range and multi-person occlusion. The code, trained models and partial dataset are available at https://github.com/kailaisun/RGBT and https://huggingface.co/skl24/RGBT.

</details>


### [60] [Getting Space Right: Agentic Rewards for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14852)
*Xiaofeng Tan,Jiaxin Lin,Haobo Yuan,Tiancheng Shen,Zihan Su,Rui Chen,Hongsong Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向空间约束文本生成图像的强化学习框架，通过“空间评论员”奖励模型与课程学习的组合，显著提升开放域空间关系遵从性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间T2I的RL方法多假设奖励准确、在线rollout有效，但在开放域存在两大问题：1）空间奖励不可靠，导致域外退化；2）rollout缺少从基础到复杂的进阶结构，学习不稳定、泛化差。

Method: 提出两部分方法：
1）SpatialCritic 奖励模型：引入带“空间感知工具吊具(harness)”的代理式奖励评审，结合可复用的验证指引与专用视觉工具；通过对生成结果进行针对性的反事实扰动构建空间偏好数据集，并进行“代理-吊具对齐”，学习更优的工具选择与证据整合策略。
2）课程式RL：构建能力图（capability graph），组织基础空间能力与其组合依赖，用以指导精通式rollout、周期复习与定向补救，实现从基础到复杂的课程推进。

Result: 在SpaRW-Eval上，SpatialCritic达到95.1%验证准确率，以仅8B骨干超过320B GLM-5.3-Flash；在SD3.5-Medium与BAGEL两条生成基线中，平均分数分别由78.4→83.9与83.2→86.5。

Conclusion: 通过面向空间理解的奖励建模与课程化rollout，框架缓解了开放域奖励不稳与学习路线无序的问题，带来更强的空间一致性与跨域泛化，同时具备高效小模型奖励评估能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is promising for spatial text-to-image generation, but existing work largely focuses on policy optimization, assuming accurate rewards and effective online rollouts. We observe in-domain gains with out-of-domain degradation, exposing two limitations: unreliable spatial rewards in open-world settings and rollouts lacking basic-to-complex progression. We propose a curriculum-guided RL framework. First, we introduce SpatialCritic, an agentic reward model equipped with a spatial perception harness that combines reusable verification guidance with specialized vision tools. We then construct spatial preference datasets through targeted counterfactual perturbations and perform agent-harness alignment to improve tool selection and evidence integration efficiently. Second, curriculum learning organizes shared foundational capabilities and their compositional dependencies in a capability graph for mastery-guided rollouts, periodic review, and targeted remediation. On SpaRW-Eval, SpatialCritic achieves 95.1% verification accuracy, outperforming 320B GLM-5.3-Flash with a 40x smaller 8B backbone and raising average baseline scores from 78.4 to 83.9 on SD3.5-Medium and 83.2 to 86.5 on BAGEL. Project page: https://xiaofeng-tan.github.io/projects/spatialcritic/

</details>


### [61] [CAST: Real-Time Motion Capture for Any Skeleton Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14856)
*Pu Pang,Zhongfei Qing,Ling-Hao Chen,Songnan Li,Hua Huang,Bo Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAST：一种适应不同骨架拓扑的单目视频动捕框架，通过“学习的局部关节旋转+由全局骨骼方向引导的几何校正”组合，降低沿运动学层级传播的关节旋转误差；在TopoCap与Mixamo跨骨架评测中优于现有方法，并有实时因果版本（>51 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨骨架（目标骨架拓扑与视频中人物不同）动捕常出现耦合的关节旋转误差，且会沿运动学树传播；现有方法难以稳健约束这些误差并保证可泛化到未知拓扑。

Method: 提出CAST框架：1）可学习的局部旋转预测器；2）由预测的全局骨段方向（outgoing bone directions）驱动的几何校正；3）基于可观测性分析，识别哪些旋转分量可被骨段方向约束；4）GRACE校正策略：按父到子顺序、带可靠性门控对组合姿态做对齐，未受约束的分量交由学习模块处理；5）冻结图像编码器，运动预测与校正模块联合、以运动派生监督训练；6）提供因果版本CAST-B以实现实时。

Result: 在TopoCap协议下，对不同且含未见拓扑的目标骨架，运动恢复精度显著提升；在Mixamo策划基准上的参考式跨骨架评测与用户偏好研究进一步验证了跨结构迁移能力；CAST-B在单块A100上含编码与预测整体达>51 FPS。

Conclusion: 结合可观测性引导的方向约束与学习式旋转预测，可有效缓解跨骨架动捕中的层级旋转误差传播，具备对未见拓扑的泛化与实时性；代码已公开。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of recovering motion from monocular video, even when the target skeleton topology differs from the subject observed in the video. Although previous methods have made efforts to resolve this problem, they remain vulnerable to joint-rotation errors that are coupled across the skeleton and can propagate along its kinematic hierarchy. To mitigate these errors, we present CAST, a topology-aware motion capture framework that combines learned local rotations with geometric correction guided by predicted global bone directions. Our observability analysis characterizes which rotational components can be constrained by outgoing bone directions. Guided by this analysis, GRACE uses reliability-gated alignment to correct composed orientations in parent-to-child order, leaving unconstrained components to the learned rotation predictor. The motion predictor and correction module are trained jointly with motion-derived supervision, using a frozen image encoder. Experiments under the TopoCap evaluation protocol show that the proposed framework improves motion recovery across different target topologies, including unseen structures. Reference-based cross-skeleton evaluation on a curated Mixamo benchmark and a user preference study further assess transfer to different target structures. A causal CAST-B variant achieves over 51 FPS on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, with timing covering image encoding and motion prediction. Code repository: https://github.com/fankewen/CAST.

</details>


### [62] [ScalLoc: Continuous Oblique Thermal UAV Geo-Localization with a 2D Satellite Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14866)
*Xiaoran Zhang,Yu Liu,Jinyu Liang,Huaxin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScalLoc框架，实现仅凭2D卫星图进行连续斜视热红外UAV定位，将平均误差从112.69 m降至26.97 m（降幅76.07%），并发布Thermal-Oblique数据集与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热成像地理定位多依赖近天顶视角或地图侧高程/3D先验，难以在仅有2D卫星图条件下实现连续斜视（oblique）热成像定位；UAV在GNSS受限环境下需要鲁棒、轻量的定位方案。

Method: 提出ScalLoc的“重建—优化—锚定—传播”流水：1) 从热序列的局部窗口重建几何；2) 基于轨迹的位姿优化细化相机位姿；3) 自适应地将每个窗口与卫星图进行尺度对齐锚定；4) 传播经验证状态以恢复逐帧全局轨迹。配套构建Thermal-Oblique数据集（216条昼夜城郊航迹、28,112帧、帧级标注）用于系统评测。

Result: 与仅用VGGT配置相比，平均定位误差由112.69 m降至26.97 m，降低76.07%；在对比方法中取得最低的帧加权平均位置误差，验证了方法有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: ScalLoc在无DSM或预建3D地图的条件下，利用轻量2D卫星图即可实现连续斜视热红外UAV地理定位的可行性与优越性；数据集与代码公开，促进该方向研究。

Abstract: Cross-modal Thermal Geo-localization (TG) provides an illumination-robust positioning solution for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-denied environments. However, existing thermal geo-localization methods mainly focus on near-nadir observations or depend on map-side elevation priors, limiting their applicability to continuous oblique thermal localization with only geo-referenced 2D satellite maps. To address this problem, we propose ScalLoc, a scale-aligned local-to-map localization framework for continuous oblique thermal UAV geo-localization. ScalLoc follows a reconstruction--refinement--anchoring--propagation design: it reconstructs local window geometry from thermal sequences, refines camera poses using track-based pose optimization, adaptively anchors each window to the satellite map, and propagates validated states to recover frame-level global trajectories. To support systematic evaluation, we construct Thermal-Oblique, a real thermal UAV sequence dataset containing 216 urban and rural day--night trajectories and 28,112 thermal frames with frame-level annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScalLoc reduces the mean localization error from 112.69 m to 26.97 m compared with the VGGT-only configuration, corresponding to a 76.07% reduction, and achieves the lowest frame-weighted mean position error among the compared methods. These results demonstrate the feasibility of continuous oblique thermal geo-localization using only lightweight 2D satellite maps, without requiring digital surface models or preconstructed 3D maps. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/FloralHercules/ScalLoc.

</details>


### [63] [MORE: Learning Motion-Oriented Registers for Driving World Models and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14871)
*Yingdong Gu,Haoyu Wang,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Hao Han,Pengcheng Shi,Xianfei Li,Qinghua Tan,Wenlong Liao,Tao He,Jiayuan Li,Pai Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MORE：在视频预训练阶段直接学习面向运动的寄存器作为世界模型与规划的共享状态接口，以解决视觉密特征与下游运动建模不匹配及高计算成本问题；在NAVSIM-v2上提升EPDMS 1.6，推理延迟为DriveLaW的1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶多用视觉预训练的稠密特征作为世界状态接口，但：1) 稠密、信息泛化却未针对运动与规划优化，需依赖下游再适配；2) 未来预测在高分辨率下计算代价高。作者主张在视频预训练中直接学习适合世界建模与规划的紧凑状态表征。

Method: 提出V-JEPA框架MORE：1) 设计少量Motion-Oriented Registers（寄存器）作为唯一上下文；2) 将时间对齐的自车位姿/运动信息注入寄存器；3) 以寄存器为上下文执行掩码补丁预测，把稠密补丁监督“挤压”进紧凑瓶颈；4) 额外的分块式寄存器掩码目标塑造寄存器空间的预测结构；5) 下游阶段沿用同一寄存器空间作为世界模型预测的未来状态，并作为规划轨迹生成与评分的共享记忆。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v2数据集上，EPDMS指标较SOTA提升1.6；相较DriveLaW，推理延迟仅为其1/10，表现出更高效的未来预测与规划能力。

Conclusion: 在视频预训练阶段直接学习面向运动的紧凑状态接口（寄存器）能更好地服务下游世界建模与规划，兼顾精度与效率；证据显示MORE是优于“预训练稠密视觉特征+下游适配”的有效范式。

Abstract: By predicting future states, latent world models provide rich self-supervision for end-to-end driving, but often inherit dense features from pretrained visual encoders as their world-state interface. This creates a mismatch: visual pretraining produces information-rich, spatially dense features, but their adaptation for motion-conditioned world modeling and planning is deferred to downstream training. The dense grid also makes future prediction more computationally expensive as visual resolution increases. We therefore argue that video pretraining should directly learn state representations suited to downstream world modeling and planning, rather than relying on downstream training to adapt inherited visual features. We propose ***MORE***, a V-JEPA framework that learns **M**otion-**O**riented **RE**gisters during video pretraining as a shared state interface for driving world models and planning. MORE injects temporally aligned ego motion into a small set of registers and uses them as the sole context for masked patch prediction, channeling dense patch-level supervision through a compact state bottleneck. A complementary blockwise register-masking objective further shapes predictive structure in the register space. During downstream training, the same register space defines the future states predicted by the world model and provides shared scene memory for trajectory generation and scoring. On NAVSIM-v2, MORE outperforms the state of the art by 1.6 in EPDMS, while achieving superior efficiency with only 1/10 the inference latency of DriveLaW. These results support learning the state interface for world modeling and planning directly during video pretraining.

</details>


### [64] [Engram Neuron Inspired Adaptive Memorization for Lifelong Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14940)
*Suiyang Jiang,Bomin Wang,Hangqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种受生物记忆痕迹（engram）全周期启发的终身学习框架：用梯度表征构建结构化记忆，调控参数更新与记忆管理，在四个基准上实现稳定与适应兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习多聚焦缓解灾难性遗忘，但缺乏像生物系统那样可自适应更新与重组记忆的机制；需要一种既保持稳定又能灵活整合新旧知识的人工学习者。

Method: 将生物记忆的“编码-巩固-再激活-遗忘/重整”全生命周期映射到算法：从梯度表征构建结构化记忆（类似engram），以此作为外部/内在记忆索引与控制信号，调节参数更新策略并指导何时、如何存储、巩固与更新记忆，从而在任务序列中动态管理知识。

Result: 在四个终身学习基准上验证，方法在长任务序列中保持性能稳定，减轻遗忘并在遇到新任务时具备灵活适应能力，整体表现优于或具竞争力于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅带来性能提升，还提供了与生物记忆机制相呼应的计算视角，展示出稳健且灵活的终身学习潜力。

Abstract: Lifelong learning enables artificial systems to adapt to changing environments. To achieve this, an effective artificial learner should dynamically reorganize past and new experiences, maintaining stability while enabling flexible knowledge integration. However, existing efforts primarily mitigate catastrophic forgetting, lacking the ability to adaptively update memory akin to biological systems. Here, by integrating the full memory life cycle of engram neurons into artificial learners, we propose a framework that constructs a structured memory from gradient representations, which regulates parameter updates and guides memory management. Through comprehensive evaluations on four benchmarks, our approach shows promising lifelong learning capability, maintaining stability over long task sequences and supporting flexible adaptation. Beyond the performance gains, our approach offers a computational perspective on robust and flexible lifelong learning, echoing mechanisms observed in biological memory systems.

</details>


### [65] [Text-Guided Medical Image Segmentation via Residual Aggregation and Dense Alignment Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14944)
*Jie Gui,Hang Tu,Yunfeng Dou,Wen Sha,Xiuquan Du,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RADAR 是一种参数高效的文本引导医学图像分割框架，通过残差聚合与稠密对齐来更好地把自然语言语义落地到像素级提示，在低对比、边界模糊和解剖结构相似场景下提升分割鲁棒性；仅需约0.74M可训练参数即可在多数据集上取得有竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导分割难以将高层语义稳定映射为精确的空间提示，尤其在医学影像中常见的低对比病灶、模糊边界与相似解剖背景下表现不佳；需要一种既能可靠语义锚定又参数高效、易适配的方案。

Method: 提出 RADAR：1) Guided Residual Depth Aggregation 在语义引导下跨层聚合特征，缓解文本注入滞后与深层语义稀释；2) Structured Dense Representation Alignment 用冻结的 BiomedCLIP 构建裁剪级目标/边界/上下文锚点，提升局部判别性并增强自动提示可靠性；3) Multi-modal Prompt Generation 将跨模态语义响应转为 MedSAM2 可用的复合提示（文本、点、掩膜）；通过 LoRA 实现低成本任务自适应，并解耦 BiomedCLIP 语义锚定与 MedSAM2 掩膜解码能力。

Result: 在三个公共医学分割数据集上，RADAR 以约0.74M 可训练参数取得与现有方法有竞争力的性能，验证了其在参数效率、语义落地与稠密表示增强方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过残差聚合与稠密对齐的设计，并结合多模态提示生成与 LoRA 适配，RADAR 能更可靠地把自然语言语义转为像素级分割提示，在保持极低可训练参数量的同时获得强竞争力，是文本引导医学分割的高效可扩展方案。

Abstract: Specifying segmentation targets with natural language provides flexible semantic conditioning for medical image segmentation. However, existing methods still struggle to reliably ground high-level textual semantics into accurate pixel-level spatial prompts, especially under low-contrast lesions, ambiguous boundaries, and visually similar anatomical contexts. In this paper, we propose RADAR (Residual Aggregation and Dense Alignment Representations), a parameter-efficient framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. RADAR explicitly decouples and synergizes the medical semantic anchoring capability of BiomedCLIP with the prompt-driven mask decoding ability of MedSAM2, while enabling low-cost task adaptation via LoRA. Specifically, Guided Residual Depth Aggregation adaptively aggregates cross-layer features under semantic guidance to alleviate delayed textual injection and deep semantic dilution. Structured Dense Representation Alignment leverages a frozen BiomedCLIP to construct crop-level target, boundary, and context anchors, improving the local discriminability of dense features and the reliability of automatic prompts. Multi-modal Prompt Generation further converts cross-modal semantic responses into composite text, point, and mask prompts for MedSAM2 decoding. Experiments on three public medical image segmentation datasets show that RADAR achieves competitive performance with only 0.74M trainable parameters, demonstrating its effectiveness in parameter-efficient adaptation, semantic grounding, and dense representation enhancement. The source code is available at https://github.com/JGuiidev/RADAR.

</details>


### [66] [DSM-Free UAV Visual Localization via Latent-Depth Variable Projection and Structure-Adaptive Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14945)
*Yibin Ye,Xichao Teng,Shuo Chen,Xiaokai Song,Yuli Sun,Qifeng Yu,Zhang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LDVP与SAC，实现无需DSM的正射-透视几何定位：把射线尺度当作潜在深度并解析消元，将非线性估计降为仅旋转优化，从而直接恢复水平位姿；再用单目深度判断场景结构，在多平面用LDVP、单平面用Planar-PnP，显著提升鲁棒性与精度，逼近依赖DSM的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV在GNSS失效下常用图像与正射地图匹配+PnP，但依赖精确、最新的DSM；OPG虽可免DSM，但多通过中间矩阵估计与分解，间接且不稳。需要一种无需DSM、可直接、稳健地恢复位姿的方法，并适应不同场景结构。

Method: 1) Latent-Depth Variable Projection (LDVP)：将透视射线上未知尺度建模为潜在深度，通过变量投影解析消元；在给定旋转时闭式求解水平相机中心，将问题化为仅对旋转的优化，直接得到度量水平位置，无需平面假设。2) Structure-Adaptive Coupling (SAC)：利用单目深度估计得到相对结构指示器；若匹配点不被单一平面主导，选用LDVP；若近似单平面，选Planar-PnP。3) 可观性分析：揭示场景高程变化如何影响OPG几何约束，为自适应策略提供依据。

Result: 在多数据集与多样场景下，LDVP较现有OPG无DSM方法稳定提升定位精度；SAC进一步增强跨场景鲁棒性，显著缩小与基于DSM方法的性能差距。

Conclusion: 通过将未知深度潜变量化并解析消元，LDVP把定位问题简化为旋转优化，实现无需DSM的度量级水平定位；结合SAC按场景结构自适应选择模型，获得更高精度与鲁棒性，为GNSS拒止环境下UAV视觉定位提供有效方案。

Abstract: Visual localization enables unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to maintain absolute positioning in GNSS-denied environments. A common approach matches UAV images with geo-tagged orthographic maps and estimates camera pose using Perspective-n-Point (PnP) with elevations from Digital Surface Models (DSMs). However, accurate and up-to-date DSMs are often unavailable in practice.
