### [100] [EmoTrace-Bench: Diagnosing Emotional Expression in Text-to-Video Generation through Controlled Cues and Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15452)
*Yeheng Wang,Liudan Shi,Yi Zheng,Jingke Li,Shiyuan Liu,Xin Tao,Yuanxing Zhang,Guoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EmoTrace-Bench：一个细粒度的T2V情感生成诊断基准，控制情绪强度与情感线索，多层面评估并提供可解释诊断；用12个模型生成5376段视频并以经人校准的自动评估器衡量，发现角色反应式提示优于事件/场景式提示，增加线索或影视化语言并不必然增强情感生成；释放提示与评估规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V评测多为整体视频-文本对齐，无法定位失败源头：是目标情感未达成、强度控制不当，还是具体情感线索未实现；缺少可追踪、细粒度、与生成流水线解耦的诊断工具。

Method: 构建EmoTrace-Bench：448个受控提示（8种情绪、2档强度、多个线索条件），用12个T2V模型生成5376视频；设计自动化评估器，评估三层面：目标情感是否出现、感知强度、10个视听维度的情感表达；并以人工标注子集进行校准与验证；比较不同提示设计与模型表现差异。

Result: 不同模型与提示策略差异显著；“明确描述角色情绪反应”的提示更可靠；单纯增加情感线索数量或采用更显式的影视化视听语言不稳定，未必提升情感生成；总体对齐分数会掩盖目标情感、强度控制与线索实现的局部差异。

Conclusion: EmoTrace-Bench提供可追踪、可解释的情感视频生成诊断框架，无需改动现有生成流水线；公开提示与自动评估规则，促进模型在情感表达、强度控制与线索实现上的细粒度改进。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to generate videos with targeted emotional expressions, yet existing evaluation methods mainly rely on holistic video-text alignment and provide limited insight into whether failures arise from target-emotion expression, intensity control, or specific affective cues. We introduce EmoTrace-Bench, a fine-grained diagnostic benchmark that controls emotion intensity and selected affective cues in prompts and evaluates generated videos at multiple levels. EmoTrace-Bench contains 448 controlled prompts covering eight emotions, two intensity levels, and diverse cue conditions. Using 12 T2V models, we generate 5,376 videos and evaluate target-emotion presence, perceived intensity, and ten audiovisual dimensions of emotional expression with an automated evaluator calibrated and validated against human annotations on a subset of the data. Results reveal substantial differences across models and prompt designs. Prompts that explicitly describe a character's emotional reaction are followed more reliably than prompts that describe only events or scenes, whereas adding more affective cues or using more explicit cinematic audiovisual language does not consistently improve emotional generation. These results illustrate how aggregate alignment scores may overlook localized differences in target-emotion expression, intensity control, and cue realization. EmoTrace-Bench provides a traceable and interpretable framework for diagnosing emotional video generation without modifying existing generation pipelines, and we release the prompts and the automated evaluation rules.

</details>


### [101] [Semantic Saliency from Multi-Modal Large Language Model Scene Understanding Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15467)
*Ziqi Wen,Shravan Murlidaran,Jonathan Skaza,William Wang,Miguel P. Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AUTO-SUM：一种无需拟合人类数据、基于MLLM语义比较的自动语义显著性估计方法，可预测场景理解中关键物体与人类凝视热点，性能优于/可比多种显著性基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低层次显著性仅捕捉颜色、对比度等视觉特征，难以反映由高层语义与任务驱动的注意力与眼动分配。需要一种能够量化“哪个物体对场景理解最重要”的自动化方法，避免依赖大规模人类标注或参数拟合。

Method: 对每幅场景图像：1) 自动实例分割并逐一“移除”单个物体形成改图；2) 用多模态大语言模型分别生成原图与改图的文本描述；3) 基于文本嵌入度量两段描述的语义相似度，将相似度下降量作为该物体对场景理解的贡献（语义显著性）。汇聚得到整图的AUTO-SUM。

Result: AUTO-SUM能准确预测人类场景理解图（H-SUM）中判定的最关键物体；在自由观看与描述任务中，AUTO-SUM对人类最常凝视物体的预测优于GBVS与Itti-Koch、优于基于意义评分训练的Meaning Map，并与基于人类凝视训练的DeepGaze相当。

Conclusion: AUTO-SUM可作为不依赖人类拟合的语义显著性模型，补充低层显著性模型，在解释与预测人类注意与眼动方面表现出强竞争力；代码已开源。

Abstract: Image-computable low-level image saliency has shaped perceptual psychology and computer science, but is limited by its inability to capture human high-level cognitive factors that influence attention and eye movements. Here, we propose a new fully automated method to compute semantic saliency by quantifying each object's contribution to scene comprehension without fitting any parameters to human data: the Automated Scene Understanding Map (AUTO-SUM). AUTO-SUM automatically segments and removes individual objects from a scene, uses multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to describe the original and modified scenes, and measures the semantic similarity between these descriptions using text embeddings. We show that AUTO-SUM can predict which object is most important in a human-measured scene understanding map (H-SUM) estimated using human scene descriptions. We also show that AUTO-SUM can predict the object most fixated by human observers during free viewing and scene description tasks better than a low-level saliency model (GBVS, Itti-Koch), a saliency model trained based on human meaning ratings (Meaning Map), and comparable to DeepGaze, which is trained on human fixation data. We contend that AUTO-SUM can be used as a semantic saliency model that complements lower-level saliency models. Code are available at https://github.com/starsky77/AUTO-SUM-Source-Code .

</details>


### [102] [Structured Stateful Relational Scripts with a High-Quality Dataset for Multi-Shot Audio-Visual Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15512)
*Chen Zhao,Lehan Shen,Qilong Huang,Hongyu Li,Zhuoliang Kang,Xiaoming Wei,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SSRS脚本表示与MSAV-Script-100K数据集，以结构化监督提升多镜头视听生成中的跨镜头一致性、状态转换遵循与声画对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多镜头视听视频生成难以在不同镜头间保持身份连续、正确建模状态演化，并明确声音来源与跨镜头事件延续；密集字幕缺乏对持久实体/声音身份、状态转移和声源绑定的结构化标注。

Method: 提出Structured Stateful Relational Scripts（SSRS）：将持久实体与声音身份从镜头级视觉/听觉状态中解耦；显式编码声源绑定、时间关系，并以全局视听事件序列连接局部动作与声音，覆盖跨镜头延续事件。构建MSAV-Script-100K：大规模多镜头视听视频与SSRS配对数据集，用于训练模型学习身份持久性、状态转移与事件连续性。

Result: 在多项实验中，相比使用传统密集字幕的基线，SSRS与MSAV-Script-100K显著提升跨镜头一致性、对指定状态变化的遵循度，以及音画对齐质量。

Conclusion: 结构化的SSRS表示与相应大规模数据集为多镜头视听生成提供有效监督，能系统性改进身份一致性、状态演化建模与跨镜头事件与声音的连贯性。

Abstract: Coherent multi-shot audio-visual video generation requires maintaining consistent identities while depicting evolving states and coordinating sounds across shot boundaries. Although dense captions describe individual shots in detail, they rarely specify which entities persist, how their states change, or which entities and actions produce sounds. To address this limitation, we propose Structured Stateful Relational Scripts (SSRS), a textual supervision representation that separates persistent entity and voice identities from shot-specific visual and auditory states. SSRS explicitly encodes sound-source bindings and temporal relations, while a global audio-visual event sequence connects local actions and sounds, including events that continue across shot boundaries. We further introduce MSAV-Script-100K, a large-scale dataset of curated multi-shot audio-visual videos paired with detailed SSRS annotations, enabling models to learn identity persistence, state transitions, and event continuity from structured supervision. Experiments demonstrate improvements in cross-shot consistency, adherence to specified state changes, and audio-visual alignment, highlighting the effectiveness of our SSRS and MSAV-Script-100K for coherent multi-shot audio-visual generation. Our proposed dataset is available at \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/shenlehan/MSAV-Script-100K}{MSAV-Script-100K}.

</details>


### [103] [Semantically Steerable Image Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15515)
*Danilo de Goede,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,Yuki M. Asano,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GIST，一种可控“语义忠实度”的自适应图像分词器；在保持语义不变的同时可牺牲像素细节，相比仅像素控制方法在相同token数下显著提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应图像分词器依据像素重建误差分配token，但像素度量忽视空间与语义结构，导致在高压缩下会突然丢失关键内容。需要一种能按语义忠实度分配token的方法，以在低token下仍保留图像语义。

Method: 提出GIST：在训练/推理时以双目标误差共同控制token分配——像素重建误差和语义误差；语义误差在冻结的DINOv3特征空间中度量。通过设定语义目标（可至0）与像素目标，动态决定每张图所需token数，从而在压缩时优先保留语义相关token。

Result: 在ImageNet-100的控制对比下，GIST在任意token数上均优于仅像素控制的KARL：LPIPS最多降低约30%，DreamSim最多降低约37%。平均而言，降低语义目标每增加一个token带来的LPIPS改善速度约为降低像素重建目标的2倍，在低token区间可达5倍。

Conclusion: 将语义忠实度作为控制信号能有效引导自适应分词器分配token，优先保留“图像所表达的内容”。GIST实现了在牺牲像素细节的同时保持语义的能力，并在感知指标上全面优于像素导向方法。

Abstract: Adaptive image tokenizers compress an image into a variable number of tokens matched to its complexity, and recent work makes this allocation controllable: conditioned on a target reconstruction error, the tokenizer itself decides how many tokens an image needs. However, the fidelity measure guiding this allocation shapes which aspects of the image are preserved under compression. The pixel-wise error used in prior work is blind to the spatial structure that carries an image's meaning, and under heavy compression such tokenizers are prone to abruptly discarding tokens that encode essential content. We introduce GIST (Guided Image Semantic Tokenizer), an adaptive tokenizer that can be steered toward a requested semantic fidelity. Its token allocation is jointly controlled by pixel-level and semantic target errors, with semantic error measured in the feature space of a frozen DINOv3 encoder. This joint control enables semantic protection: with the semantic target fixed at zero, the reconstruction target can be relaxed to discard pixel detail while preserving what the image depicts. In a controlled comparison on ImageNet-100, GIST outperforms its pixel-controlled counterpart KARL at every token count, with up to $30\%$ lower LPIPS and $37\%$ lower DreamSim. On average, lowering its semantic target reduces LPIPS $2\times$ faster per token than lowering the reconstruction target, and up to $5\times$ in the low-token regime. Semantic fidelity is thus an effective control signal for adaptive tokenization: it lets a tokenizer see, and so preserve, what an image depicts.

</details>


### [104] [UMAS3D: Unified Mask-Free 3D Editing with Region-Adaptive Source Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15533)
*Xianlin Zeng,Xiaolong Shen,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UMAS3D 提出一个无需训练、无需反演、无需外部3D掩码的统一3D编辑框架，将多种条件转为统一的目标视图引导，并通过在线区域估计与源内容保持实现加、删、改、外观与姿态编辑的通用流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法依赖特定任务训练、源3D反演或外部掩码，难以在多种编辑条件与任务间统一，并且往往无法在编辑指定区域外可靠地保持原始几何与外观。需要一个在多模态条件下通用、灵活且源内容友好的编辑框架。

Method: 1) 将文本、边界框、参考图像及其组合统一转化为“目标视图引导”。2) 关键思想：联动的在线编辑区域估计与区域感知的源保持。具体包括：a) MARM（无掩码自适应区域建模）：从源-目标的视觉差异初始化“编辑/保留”先验，并随目标结构演化逐步细化编辑区域；b) SGPS（源引导的保持引导）：在“保留”区域对当前状态施加约束，使其保持源几何与外观，同时不过度抑制由目标引导触发的必要变化。3) 在统一的3D生成流程中共享上述机制，支持加、删、改、外观与姿态编辑。

Result: 在多种编辑条件与任务（加、删、改、外观、姿态）下，UMAS3D 实现了对未编辑源内容的有效保持，并展示了稳定的编辑质量与灵活性。

Conclusion: UMAS3D 实现了一个跨条件、跨任务的统一3D编辑框架，通过在线区域建模与源内容保持策略，无需额外训练、反演或3D掩码即可进行灵活且源友好的编辑。

Abstract: 3D editing aims to modify an existing 3D asset according to user intent while preserving geometry and appearance outside the intended edit region. Existing methods often rely on task-specific training, source-3D inversion, or external 3D masks, and lack a unified framework for diverse editing conditions and tasks. We propose UMAS3D, a unified 3D editing framework that is training-free, inversion-free, and free from external 3D masks. UMAS3D converts text, bounding boxes, reference images, and their combinations into common target-view guidance, after which addition, deletion, modification, appearance, and pose editing share the same downstream 3D generation process. Our key insight is to couple online edit-region estimation with region-aware source preservation. Mask-Free Adaptive Region Modeling (MARM) initializes an Edit/Keep prior from source-target visual differences and progressively refines it as the target structure evolves, while Source-Guided Preservation Steering (SGPS) constrains the current Keep-region states to retain source geometry and appearance without suppressing target-driven changes. Experiments show that UMAS3D supports addition, deletion, modification, appearance, and pose editing while effectively preserving unedited source content. These advances make UMAS3D a unified framework for flexible and source-preserving 3D editing across diverse editing conditions.

</details>


### [105] [ELECT: Settling Identity Before Rendering for Scalable Story Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15555)
*Mehrajul Abadin Miraj,Md. Mahmudul Hasan,Meherun Farzana,Aniket Joarder,Mahmudul Hasan,Abir Chakraborty Partha,Md. Ahasanul Alam,Redwan Ahmed Rizvee,Md. Fahim Arefin,Md Mahmudur Rahman,Md. Tanvir Alam,Md Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ELECT是一种无需训练、无参考图像的长篇故事可视化方法，通过在最终渲染前用语言引导的场景-实体映射与从模型草稿中选出的静态锚点，先行确定跨帧身份一致性，从而将最终逐帧渲染成本与故事长度解耦；在极长序列上显著降低VRAM与时间开销，同时保持或提升角色一致性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练故事可视化为保持跨帧一致性，常需让每帧依赖同一故事的其他帧，导致生成时间和显存随故事长度线性或更糟增长；需要一种能在长叙事下依然稳定一致、且成本不随长度上升的方法。

Method: 提出ELECT：1) 语言引导的场景-实体映射，先解析叙事中实体与场景关系；2) 从模型的初始草稿中“选举”出静态锚点（stationary anchors），用于锁定关键实体的跨帧身份；3) 最终渲染阶段对每帧独立生成，不再进行昂贵的跨帧联合注意力或多轮互相条件化，从而将每帧成本与故事长度解耦。

Result: - 资源效率：联合注意在32个场景需>96GB VRAM；ELECT在10到1000场景下保持<20GB VRAM，约8.3秒/帧。- 基准：Story50上，ELECT的aCCS=0.833，优于十轮refinement的0.826，代价仅为其1/7，但文本对齐略低（0.327 vs 0.340）。- 在1000场景：ELECT的aCCS比十轮refinement高0.017，比平坦成本基线高0.091；并在文本对齐上也优于refinement，生成时间仅为其1/24。

Conclusion: 通过在渲染前先行确定实体身份并以锚点稳定跨帧一致性，ELECT将长篇故事可视化的逐帧成本与叙事长度解耦，实现了在极长序列上的一致性与效率兼得，表明长时程一致性无需随故事增长而依赖日益昂贵的跨帧条件化。

Abstract: Training-free story visualization without reference images must preserve recurring entities across frames while scaling to long narratives. Existing methods typically maintain consistency by conditioning each frame on other frames of the same story, coupling generation time, VRAM, or both to story length. We introduce ELECT, which instead settles cross-frame identity before final rendering through a language-guided scene-entity mapping and stationary anchors elected from the model's own drafts. This makes the per-frame cost of the final rendering pass independent of story length. Joint attention exceeds 96 GB of VRAM at 32 scenes, whereas ELECT remains at 8.3 s per frame and under 20 GB VRAM from 10 to 1000 scenes. On the Story50 long-story benchmark, ELECT reaches 0.833 average Character Consistency Similarity (aCCS), compared with 0.826 for ten-round refinement, at one seventh of its generation cost, with lower text alignment (0.327 vs. 0.340). At 1000 scenes, to our knowledge, the first evaluation of training-free story visualization at this length, ELECT exceeds ten-round refinement by 0.017 aCCS and the nearest flat-cost baseline by 0.091, while also surpassing refinement in text alignment at one twenty-fourth of its generation time. These results show that long-horizon consistency need not require increasingly expensive inter-frame conditioning as stories grow.

</details>


### [106] [A Plausible Future Is Not a Causal Future: Can Video Models Generate How the World Unfolds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15562)
*Jialiang Li,Zhengning Xu,Zongyang Ma,Shaobo Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CausalCascade基准，用仿真器生成的事件链场景系统性评测视频生成模型在因果级联上的能力；结果显示模型在空间定位尚可，但时空演化、物理实现与对干预的选择性响应不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评测多偏重画质、文本一致性或粗粒度物理合理性，缺少检验模型能否从起始事件正确推动一连串因果相依互动直至终态（完整因果级联）的过程级能力评测。

Method: 构建CausalCascade：由50个富事件的Blender场景组成，具结构化事件图；提供图对齐的诊断式VQA；设定三种未来事件规格层级；并创建2,832个受控反事实变体。用其对9个图生视频模型进行评测，设计运动层面、事件层面与反事实层面的诊断指标。

Result: 跨模型观察到：生成的运动在空间局部化上较可靠，但正确的时空演化不足；更丰富的未来事件规格有助于事件回收（被实现）但仍难以实现符合物理的演化；反事实测试显示对物理干预的选择性响应较弱；多数失败源自基础问题，如场景一致性或关键事件未发生。

Conclusion: 因果级联生成可作为世界建模的过程级测试。要提升模型，需要同时：恢复意图中的未来事件、忠实实现其物理演化、对物理干预做出选择性响应，并维持跨时间的一致物理世界。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale video generation models are increasingly viewed as a promising path toward world models, yet their ability to capture causally coherent physical dynamics remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations largely emphasize visual quality, prompt adherence, or broad physical plausibility, leaving open whether a model can faithfully realize a complete causal cascade, in which an initiating event propagates through causally dependent interactions to a terminal outcome. We introduce CausalCascade, a simulator-grounded benchmark centered on 50 event-rich Blender scenes with structured event graphs, graph-grounded diagnostic VQA, three levels of future-event specification, and 2,832 controlled counterfactual variants, and use it to evaluate nine image-to-video models with complementary motion-, event-, and counterfactual-level diagnostics. Across the evaluated models, generated motion is more reliably localized spatially than realized with correct spatiotemporal evolution, while richer future-event specification improves event recovery but leaves physical realization limited. Counterfactual evaluation further reveals weak selective responsiveness to physical interventions, while most audited failures are foundational, arising at scene consistency or event occurrence. Together, these results position causal cascade generation as a process-level test of world modeling and highlight the need to jointly recover the intended future, faithfully realize its physical evolution, selectively respond to physical interventions, and maintain a coherent physical world over time. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SJTU-DeepDeltaLab/CausalCascade .

</details>


### [107] [Genesis: Visual Augmentation Primitives for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15565)
*Fan Yang,Lifeng Chen,Yipu Wang,Han Wang,Feilong Chen,Yidong Ma,Zhiyang Chen,Chaoyang Zhao,Lingxiang Wu,Hanqi Jiang,Yibo Chen,Xu Tang,Yao Hu,Ming Tang,Xiaojie Jin,Xiaolong Zheng,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VAP数据增强与多模态CoT强化学习，提升MLLM在复杂真实场景中的视觉推理，并发布UNREAL基准；4B超越8B，规模化至32B持续增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在标准STEM基准上接近人类，但在复杂真实场景中推理能力显著退化。根因在于训练阶段的链式思维多停留在文本空间，缺乏逐步以视觉证据为锚的多模态推理数据与监督。

Method: 1) 视觉增强原语VAP：基于最先进生成模型对种子图像施加操作（如几何、语义、视觉属性变换等）生成多样、写实的变体，并在生成过程中同步构建与每一步视觉变化一致的多模态推理链；2) 多模态CoT基于规则的强化学习：设计评分规约，从逻辑有效性、完整性、一致性对中间推理步的文本与视觉对齐进行奖励，促使模型在每步都“看图”并推理；3) 构建UNREAL基准：超写实场景、可控难度，用于系统评估视觉推理。

Result: 在该框架下训练的4B参数模型在视觉推理任务上超过8B基线；随参数扩展至32B性能持续提升，呈现明确的视觉推理规模律；在UNREAL等评测上表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过VAP生成多样且可对齐的视觉-文本推理数据，并结合多模态CoT强化学习，可显著提升MLLM在复杂真实场景中的逐步视觉推理能力；同时提供UNREAL作为标准化评测并验证了随规模提升的稳定增益。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models achieve promising results on visual reasoning tasks, approaching human-level performance on existing STEM benchmarks. However, these capabilities degrade under complex real-world scenes. We identify that chain-of-thought reasoning remains largely confined to the linguistic space, rarely anchoring each step in visual evidence. The main cause is the absence of such data during training. In this work, we introduce a training data augmentation pipeline, \textbf{Visual Augmentation Primitives} (VAP), which leverages state-of-the-art vision generative models to synthesize diverse photorealistic training samples and corresponding reasoning text. VAP introduces some basic operators and applies them to a seed image and gets a variant. It also constructs coherent multimodal reasoning chains throughout the generation process. Such training data force models to perceive visual content and perform logical reasoning according to them at every step. We further introduce multimodal chain-of-thought rubric-based reinforcement learning to enhance this. It rewards not only text reasoning but also visual reasoning by scoring intermediate reasoning steps for logical validity, completeness, and consistency. Moreover, we construct the UNREAL benchmark to systematically evaluate visual reasoning on hyperrealistic scenes across controllable difficulty levels. Experiments show that a 4B model built on this framework surpasses 8B baselines, with performance improving consistently as the model scales to 32B, validating the scaling law for visual reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [Ellipsoids from Tangents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15580)
*Efrat Dahan,Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于2D/3D/高维的多椭球检测与拟合算法：利用切线及接触点形成线性方程，少样本生成假设，通过参数空间聚类鲁棒恢复椭球；在2D超过现有方法，并可泛化到鲁棒求解一般线性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点采样的椭球拟合在多目标与噪声/外点情形需大量样本与迭代，效率低且不稳。切线信息比点更“强”，每条切线提供d个独立约束，可减少样本、降低迭代并提升对外点的鲁棒性。希望同时在任意维度检测多个椭球，并构建可推广的鲁棒线性求解框架。

Method: 以切线与接触点而非仅点来表征椭球。将拟合转化为线性方程组：每条切线贡献d个独立线性约束，构建最小样本假设；在参数空间中对线性解进行聚类以鲁棒估计多个椭球的参数；支持2D、3D及更高维。

Result: 算法在所有维度通用；在2D上超过当前最优方法（更高准确率/更少迭代/更强外点鲁棒性）；可在含多椭球与显著外点的数据中稳定恢复各椭球参数。

Conclusion: 基于切线与接触点的线性化与参数空间聚类，使多椭球拟合更高效鲁棒、样本需求更低；该思想可推广到一般线性方程的鲁棒求解，超越单一椭球拟合场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an algorithm for detecting and fitting multiple ellipsoids in 2D, 3D and higher-dimensional data. The representation is based on tangents to the ellipsoid together with their contact points, rather than on points alone. A tangent contributes d independent equations where a point contributes one, so each hypothesis needs fewer samples, which in turn reduces the number of iterations required to recover every ellipsoid. The problem is posed as sets of linear equations whose solutions -- the ellipsoid parameters -- are recovered robustly by clustering in parameter space. The algorithm works in all dimensions and outperforms the state of the art in 2D. The approach applies in general to the robust solution of linear equations, and so has utility beyond ellipsoid fitting.

</details>


### [109] [FireNav: Decision-Oriented Reconstruction Evidence Routing for Vision-Language Navigation in Indoor Fire Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15595)
*Hongbo Ding,Xiang Chen,Jiangxin Dong,Jinshan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FireNav：在室内火灾等退化视觉环境下，将图像恢复与VLN决策紧密对齐的“决策导向重建-证据路由”框架，并配套发布成对基准FireVLN-Bench；通过路由器按需引入重建潜特征并以“决策效用校准”监督选择，显著提升导航成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在室内火灾烟雾、遮挡、光照异常等导致视觉退化时，VLN的指令落地、航点与长程探索能力大幅下降。传统图像恢复方法以重建保真为目标，未必提升下游决策，甚至会抹除任务关键线索。因此需要让恢复过程服务于导航决策，而非仅追求像素级还原。

Method: 1) 构建FireVLN-Bench：提供干净参考、任务语义与安全监督一致、并与连续导航和机器人动力学匹配的成对火灾导航基准。2) FireNav框架：保留原生视觉tokens，把重建网络的潜在特征视为“辅助证据”；设计轻量路由器，基于视觉特征、任务语境与历史执行状态，动态决定是否接纳证据及其融合强度。3) 决策效用校准：在同一状态下比较“使用/不使用候选证据”的动作预测损失差异，据此对证据选择进行监督，面向航点、朝向与到达预测的效用学习。

Result: 在多种评测设定中，FireNav在导航成功率与路径效率上均稳定优于最先进基线，显示其在退化视觉环境中的强鲁棒与高效决策能力。

Conclusion: 将图像恢复从“重建保真”转向“决策效用”，通过证据路由与效用校准实现与VLN深度对齐；配套基准推动领域研究。FireNav在火灾场景下显著提升VLN表现，具备在真实机器人中的落地潜力。

Abstract: In real-world indoor fire environments, visual degradation severely impairs instruction grounding, waypoint prediction, and long-horizon exploration in vision-language navigation (VLN). Although image restoration offers a natural means to mitigate such visual degradation, existing methods primarily optimize reconstruction fidelity, which does not necessarily translate into better navigation decisions and may even corrupt task-critical cues when applied indiscriminately. In this paper, we present FireNav, a decision-oriented reconstruction evidence routing framework that aligns visual restoration with navigation utility in VLN. To advance this task, we first contribute FireVLN-Bench, a paired indoor fire navigation benchmark with clean references preserving task semantics, safety supervision, and matched settings for continuous navigation and robot execution with simulated dynamics. Our FireNav retains native visual tokens and treats latent reconstruction features as auxiliary evidence, where a lightweight router leverages visual features, task context, and prior execution states to determine whether such evidence should be admitted and how strongly it should contribute to the visual tokens. To learn when reconstruction is beneficial, we further introduce decision utility calibration, which compares action prediction losses with and without candidate evidence under the same state, thereby supervising evidence selection according to its contribution to waypoint, heading, and arrival prediction. Experiments demonstrate that FireNav consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both navigation success and path efficiency across evaluation settings.

</details>


### [110] [Squeeze-Aware-YOLO: Early Channel Recalibration and Multi-Scale Fusion for Real-Time Traffic Sign Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15604)
*Mizbaul Haque Maruf,Syed Samin Sadaf,Syed Tamzid Bakht,Md. Hasanul Kabir,Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级交通标志检测框架Squeeze-Aware-YOLO，引入多分支瓶颈+SE注意力，并结合改进的特征金字塔与重参数化多尺度模块，提升小目标与退化场景检测精度，同时保持实时性；在三大数据集上mAP@0.5分别达74.7/79.4/84.0，优于近期YOLO系方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实道路中光照变化、恶劣天气、运动模糊与遮挡会导致交通标志小目标与退化目标特征弱、空间信息丢失，现有实时检测器（含YOLO系）在此类复杂场景下精度下降，难以兼顾鲁棒性与推理效率，限制ADAS/自动驾驶落地。

Method: 1) 设计多分支瓶颈模块：并行不同卷积核尺寸以获得多尺度感受野，并与Squeeze-and-Excitation通道注意力耦合，进行自适应通道重标定；2) 引入多分支辅助FPN以加强多尺度特征表达与自顶向下/自底向上融合；3) 使用重参数化异构多尺度（Rep-HMS）模块，在训练期用多分支、推理期合并为等效单分支卷积，兼顾表达力与实时性；4) 以YOLO为基干结构，整体轻量化、端到端训练。

Result: 在BTSD、CCTSDB2021、GTSDB上mAP@0.5分别为74.7%、79.4%、84.0；相较近期YOLO系交通标志检测器在三数据集上均有一致领先，且保持实时推理效率（文中强调轻量与部署友好）。

Conclusion: 融合多尺度感受野、通道注意力与增强型特征融合，可在复杂退化条件下显著提升小目标交通标志检测的准确性与鲁棒性，同时维持实时计算开销，适合实际道路部署。

Abstract: Traffic sign detection plays a vital role in intelligent transportation systems, supporting the safe operation of advanced driver assistance systems and autonomous vehicles. Despite significant progress in real-time object detection, accurately identifying traffic signs under challenging conditions remains difficult. Variations in lighting, adverse weather, motion blur, and partial occlusion often reduce detection performance, especially for small or visually degraded signs where important spatial information can be lost. To address these challenges, we propose Squeeze-Aware-YOLO, a lightweight traffic sign detection framework that introduces a multi-branch bottleneck module combining convolutional kernels of different sizes with a Squeeze-and-Excitation mechanism to improve feature extraction and adaptive channel weighting. In addition, we integrate the Multi-Branch Auxiliary Feature Pyramid Network and Re-parameterized Heterogeneous Multi-Scale modules to strengthen multi-scale feature representation and feature fusion while preserving real-time inference efficiency for practical deployment. The proposed approach is evaluated on the Bangladeshi Traffic Sign Detection Dataset, the CSUST Chinese Traffic Sign Detection Benchmark 2021, and the German Traffic Sign Detection Benchmark, achieving mAP@0.5 scores of 74.7%, 79.4%, and 84.0%, respectively, while consistently outperforming recent state-of-the-art YOLO-based traffic sign detectors. These findings demonstrate that combining multi-scale receptive fields, channel attention, and enhanced feature fusion enables more accurate and robust traffic sign detection while maintaining the computational efficiency required for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [111] [Lesion-Aware Attention for Mammography Segmentation with Heterogeneous Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15612)
*Di Dai,Bo Liu,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在乳腺X线片中进行开放词汇病灶分割的混合监督方法：用有掩码的基础类别+仅图像级标签的更广类别共同训练，两分支架构并配合病灶注意分类器，在多数据集上提升基类与新类分割，逼近全监督效果，尤其对小、低对比度病灶定位更完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割数据集标注昂贵且类别有限，而大规模乳腺数据多只有图像级标签，导致模型无法泛化到稀有或弱标注病灶类别。需要一种能利用像素级与图像级混合标注，并在开放类别设定下提升分割泛化的方法。

Method: 提出两分支框架：分割分支用像素级掩码学习边界；分类分支用图像级标签提供语义监督。核心是“病灶级注意分类器”（Lesion-wise Attentive Classifier, LAC）：维护类别原型，生成病灶感知注意力图，对前景特征进行加权汇聚，抑制背景组织干扰，形成更紧致的病灶表示。共同训练实现从图像级到像素级的监督迁移，支持开放词汇分割。

Result: 在多个乳腺X线数据集上，相比开放词汇分割基线，在基础与新颖类别上均有一致提升，并缩小与需要密集掩码的全监督模型之间的性能差距；可视化显示对小体积、低对比、细微征象的定位更完整。

Conclusion: 混合监督+病灶注意机制能有效将图像级语义转移到病灶区域，提升开放词汇乳腺病灶分割的泛化与定位质量，为减少像素级标注依赖、覆盖更多病灶类别提供可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate lesion segmentation in mammography is important for computer-aided breast cancer diagnosis, but dense pixel-level annotations remain costly and limited. Existing public segmentation datasets usually cover only a small set of lesion types with manually delineated masks, whereas larger mammography datasets often provide only image-level diagnostic labels. This annotation mismatch makes it difficult to train segmentation models that can generalize to rare or weakly annotated lesion categories. To address this problem, we study open-vocabulary mammographic lesion segmentation under a mixed-supervision setting, where pixel-level masks are available for base lesion categories and image-level labels are available for a broader set of categories. We propose a two-branch framework that jointly learns from segmentation data and classification data. The segmentation branch learns lesion boundaries from annotated masks, while the classification branch introduces additional image-level semantic supervision. To better transfer image-level supervision to lesion-relevant regions, we further introduce a Lesion-wise Attentive Classifier that maintains category-specific feature prototypes and generates lesion-aware attention maps for foreground feature pooling. This design reduces the influence of background tissue and encourages more compact lesion representations. Experiments on multiple mammography datasets show that the proposed method consistently improves open-vocabulary segmentation baselines on both base and novel lesion categories, while reducing the gap to fully supervised models that require dense target-domain masks. Qualitative results further indicate that our method produces more complete lesion localization for small, low-contrast, and visually subtle mammographic findings.

</details>


### [112] [1Step-WAM: Your World Action Model but Faster using 1-Step Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15625)
*Rundong Su,Pengtao Chen,Haohan Geng,Sha Zhang,Zhihang Yuan,Yicheng Rong,Chong Yu,Hao Huang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出1Step-WAM：将WAM多步动作去噪压缩为单步推理，并与视觉预填并行执行，实现显著加速且几乎不损失任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在实际部署受限于两点：动作多步去噪带来的迭代开销，以及视觉预填与动作预测需串行执行的时延瓶颈。作者观察到动作去噪轨迹近似直线，首步速度已能很好估计最终动作方向，因而有机会以单步替代多步。

Method: 核心是把多步去噪转化为“一步前向”。1) 观察：去噪轨迹近似线性。2) 轻量校准器：直接修正首步速度，得到更准的一步动作。3) 受带宽约束的动作解码，进一步减少一步动作误差。4) 将一步动作生成与视觉预填并行，打破想象后再行动的串行范式。模型仅约13.4M可训练参数。

Result: 在RoboTwin与LIBERO上，1Step-WAM基本保持多步方法的任务成功率，同时推理提速：相较FastWAM 7.44×、ImageWAM 5.98×、Kairos 24.84×。在真实机器人（FastWAM骨干）上同样实现约7.44×加速，成功率与多步基线接近。

Conclusion: 利用去噪轨迹近似直线性，通过轻量校准与带宽受限解码，将WAM动作生成单步化并与视觉预填并行，显著提升推理速度且保持性能，适用于高效机器人部署。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) unify visual world modeling and action generation, yet their real-world deployment is heavily bottlenecked by iterative action denoising and the sequential execution of visual prefilling and action prediction. We demonstrate that the action-denoising trajectories in WAMs are approximately straight, rendering the first-step velocity a strong estimate of the final action direction. Building on this observation, we introduce 1Step-WAM, a framework that transforms multi-step action denoising into one single forward. Specifically, we employ a lightweight calibrator to directly correct the first step velocity, and use bandwidth-constrained action decoding to further reduce errors in the calibrated one-step actions. Compressing action generation into a single step enables it to execute in parallel with visual prefilling, completely breaking the sequential bottleneck of the traditional imagination-then-action paradigm. Evaluations on RoboTwin and LIBERO show that 1Step-WAM largely preserves multi-step task success with only about 13.4M trainable parameters, while achieving inference speedups of $7.44\times$, $5.98\times$, and $24.84\times$ for FastWAM, ImageWAM and Kairos, respectively. Furthermore, real-world robotic deployments using the FastWAM backbone confirm a consistent 7.44$\times$ speedup while maintaining close success rate to the multi-step baseline. Code is avaliable at https://github.com/changdong1687/1Step-WAM.

</details>


### [113] [Robust Detection of Directional Adversarial Attacks in Deep Neural Networks for Radiological Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15628)
*Tristan Malatyński,Joanna Jaworek-Korjakowska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于医学影像对抗样本检测的框架，通过对样本施加随机噪声并分析Sigmoid输出变化，区分正常与对抗图像；在三类医疗数据集和多种攻击强度下，检测准确率最高达99.8%，低误报，优于现有防御，具备临床落地潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在影像诊断中广泛应用，但对抗样本可用极小扰动诱导误判，可能导致漏诊或误诊；既有攻击成功率高（>90%），且医院AI渗透率升高，亟需鲁棒且可落地的对抗攻击检测方案，尤其是能判别攻击方向性的检测方法以保障系统可信度。

Method: 提出基于“后续随机噪声攻击”的检测思路：对输入图像叠加随机噪声并观察模型Sigmoid输出的变化模式；比较干净图像与被攻击图像的预测差异，以及不同对抗版本之间的差异，通过Sigmoid变化特征来判别是否存在对抗扰动并估计其方向性。

Result: 在Chest X-Ray、Retinal Fundus多病种与Brain Tumor三类医学数据集上，在包含随机噪声在内的多种攻击场景与强度下评测，检测对抗方向性攻击的准确率最高达99.8%，在各强度下均能稳定识别对抗样本，误报率低，显著优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过分析Sigmoid输出对随机噪声的敏感性，有效检测并区分对抗攻击方向，表现出高准确率与低误报，具有稳定性与临床应用前景；为医学影像AI系统的可靠部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning is now central to radiology, helping detect changes on X-rays, CTs, and MRIs. However, these systems are highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks - small, crafted perturbations that mislead models while appearing unchanged to humans. Such errors risk missed cancers or false positives. Studies show over 90% attack success on medical scans. Almost 30% of US hospitals use AI in imaging in 2023. Thus, an effective method of detecting such attacks and their directionality is crucial to ensure the credibility and reliability of medical systems based on DNNs. We propose a novel detection framework that leverages subsequent attacks using random noise to identify adversarial perturbations. Our approach uses the analysis of variations in the Sigmoid function to distinguish genuine medical images from adversarially manipulated ones. Specifically, we compare prediction differences between clean and attacked images, as well as between different adversarially altered versions, to improve detection accuracy. We evaluated our method on three popular medical datasets including 'Chest X-Ray Images for Classification', 'Retinal Fundus Multi-disease Image Dataset' and 'Brain Tumor Dataset' under various attack scenarios, including random noise. Our framework achieved up to 99.8% ACC in identifying adversarial directional attacks, significantly outperforming existing defense mechanisms. It consistently identified adversarial samples across varying attack strengths while keeping false positives low, showing strong reliability and potential for clinical use. Furthermore, our proposal highlights its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [114] [SurgCast: Action-Conditioned Future Skeletons for Controllable Surgical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15645)
*Wanhao Liu,Rulin Zhou,Liangjing Shao,Zhaocheng Lin,Dongyue Li,Jinsong Lin,Guoheng Ma,Hangheng Yang,Zhiqing Tang,Jing Chen,Panshuo Li,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgCast 先预测未来器械骨架，再据此引导手术视频生成；通过双通路注入几何与运动语义，显著提升视觉质量与器械一致性，优于直接以动作驱动的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人世界模型需要将“未来动作”准确映射为既视觉逼真又结构一致的器械运动。直接用动作控制视频生成常导致器械几何与运动不稳定，缺乏对关节化结构与其运动语义的显式建模。

Method: 提出 SurgCast：1) 预测模块以当前器械骨架、机器人状态与未来动作为输入，预测未来器械骨架；2) 将预测骨架用于视频生成控制，通过“双通路注入”：a) 几何残差通路注入骨架几何与残差信息；b) 条件调制通路注入从骨架派生的器械特定运动描述子；两通路与原生动作条件互补；3) 知识蒸馏为四步因果学生模型，保留双通路注入。

Result: 在 SutureBot 与 SRTH-Porcine-Cholei 数据集上，教师与学生模型在7个质量与器械一致性指标上均优于各自直接动作基线；教师模型 FVD 分别下降 37.6% 与 67.3%。消融显示：引入未来动作可提升骨架预测；在使用预测骨架时，双通路联合最佳，覆盖6项指标均最优。

Conclusion: 将可预测的关节化器械结构及其运动语义作为控制接口，能显著提升手术视频预测的真实性与结构一致性；双通路注入与因果学生模型提供高效、稳健的实现路径。

Abstract: Surgical world models must turn prospective robot actions into visually faithful, structurally consistent instrument motion. We introduce SurgCast, which predicts future instrument skeletons from current structure, robot state, and future actions, then uses the forecast to control video generation. Dual-path injection combines skeleton geometry with derived, instrument-specific motion descriptors through geometric residuals and conditioning modulation. Both paths complement native action conditioning and are retained in a four-step causal student. On SutureBot and SRTH-Porcine-Cholei, the teacher and student improve all seven reported quality and instrument-consistency metrics over their respective direct-action baselines; teacher FVD decreases by 37.6% and 67.3%. Ablations show that future actions improve skeleton prediction and that combining both injection paths performs best across all six metrics under predicted skeletons. These results support using predicted articulated structure and its motion semantics as a control interface for surgical video prediction. Project videos and examples are available at https://surgcast.netlify.app/.

</details>


### [115] [Sparse All-Layer Connector for Domain Generalised Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15660)
*Ruoyu Guo,Xin Kun Lin,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出用于多基础模型融合的稀疏全层连接器（SALC），突破传统仅在同深度层融合的限制，实现跨深度、可学习的稀疏选择与融合，并配合候选丢弃正则以提升鲁棒性；在多种模型组合与DGSS设置上优于深度对齐融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多基础模型（视觉-语言、纯视觉）预训练目标不同，导致层级表征结构和对域偏移的敏感度随深度不一致。现有做法只在同层融合，隐含假设“有用信息在同深度”，可能错失跨层互补；且深层可用层数增多，密集聚合易过拟合、损害泛化。

Method: 提出Sparse All-Layer Connector（SALC）：允许每一层从另一模型的所有先前层获取信息，并通过可学习的稀疏选择机制只保留少量最有信息的来源层；同时引入candidate dropout（候选丢弃）正则，在训练中随机丢弃部分候选层，强化稀疏性、鼓励探索多样层级，提升稳健选择。与四种基础模型组合集成，在DGSS场景进行训练与评测。

Result: 在三种DGSS评测设置与四种基础模型组合上，SALC在泛化性能上系统性优于传统的深度对齐融合方法，显示更强的跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨模型的最优信息并不必然对齐于同深度。通过SALC实现跨层、可学习且稀疏的连接，并配合候选丢弃，可更好地挖掘互补表征、抑制过聚合带来的过拟合，从而显著提升DGSS的域泛化能力。

Abstract: Domain generalised semantic segmentation (DGSS) has recently benefited from combining multiple foundation models, such as vision-language and vision foundation models, to leverage their diverse representations for generalisation. Existing multi-model approaches share a common design choice: cross-model fusion is restricted to depth-aligned layers, implicitly assuming that mutually beneficial information resides at the same depth across models. However, foundation models pretrained under different objectives may develop distinct representation hierarchies, making the optimality of depth-aligned fusion questionable. Moreover, we observe that the foundation models exhibit different depth-wise domain sensitivity. Motivated by these observations, we propose a Sparse All-Layer Connector (SALC) that enables each layer to access information from preceding layers of another model. As the number of accessible layers grows with depth, dense aggregation may hurt generalisation. SALC therefore learns to adaptively select a sparse subset of informative layers. We further introduce a candidate dropout regularisation that strengthens sparsity and encourages SALC to explore diverse layers, leading to more robust selection. Across four foundation model combinations and three DGSS evaluation settings, the proposed design demonstrates stronger generalisation than conventional depth-aligned fusion. The code is available at https://github.com/RuoyuGuo/SALC.

</details>


### [116] [A Persian Vision-Language-Action Dataset and Benchmark for Ambiguity- and Infeasibility-Aware Instruction-to-Plan Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15666)
*Abdollah Pourasghari,Saeedeh Sadat Sadidpour,Mohammad Roustaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个面向波斯语的“图像-语言-行动”数据集与评测框架；检验检索增强、CoT与代理式消歧的效用，并提出PGOS-FA新指标，显示检索增强显著有效、其他改动方向一致但未显著；PGOS-FA较LLM裁判更有判别力且与人类评判相关性更高；开放权重与闭源差距多源于生成质量；数据与代码可按需获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令到行动规划基准多局限于英语、仿真/视频场景，且假设指令明确可执行，缺少在真实场景、含歧义/不可行性的非英语（波斯语）基准与可靠评测方法。

Method: 构建258条、覆盖230张真实居家/办公照片的波斯语数据集，包含参考计划、歧义与不可行性标注、消歧QA、以及用于训练的自动生成推理链。设计消融：检索增强、链式思维提示与代理式消歧，并采用LLM裁判与新指标PGOS-FA评估；PGOS-FA在PG2S基础上加入步骤顺序敏感、过度生成惩罚与波斯语嵌入；进行成对统计检验与人类相关性实验；比较开放权重与闭源规划器并与外部基线对比。

Result: 跨36组比较，检索增强带来显著且稳健的大效应；CoT与代理式消歧方向上有益但在此规模下统计上不显著；LLM裁判在许多情形几乎无区分力，而PGOS-FA能成功区分并与人类评判在79个真实输出上呈良好相关，至少不逊于LLM裁判；开放权重与更强闭源规划器的性能差距主要来自开放模型的生成质量；在较弱骨干下，作者最佳配置仍显著优于复现的外部基线。

Conclusion: 波斯语真实场景行动规划基准与PGOS-FA评测为跨语言、含歧义/不可行性的规划研究提供了新资源；检索增强是当前最稳健的提升手段；仅靠LLM作为裁判存在显著局限，PGOS-FA更可靠；未来应扩大数据规模、提升开放模型的生成质量，并进一步验证消歧与推理策略的效益。

Abstract: Converting natural-language instructions into executable action plans conditioned on a scene image is a core capability for embodied human-robot interaction, but existing benchmarks target English, simulated or video-grounded environments, and largely assume instructions are clear and executable. We introduce the first Persian vision-language-action dataset for this task: 258 instances over 230 real household/office photographs, annotated with reference plans, deliberate ambiguity and infeasibility labels, disambiguating question-answer pairs, and an auto-generated chain-of-thought rationale for training. Using this resource, we ablate retrieval-augmentation, chain-of-thought prompting, and an agentic disambiguation step, evaluated with an LLM-as-judge protocol and a new metric, PGOS-FA, extending prior work (PG2S) with step-order sensitivity, an over-generation penalty, and Persian-adapted embeddings. Paired statistical testing across 36 comparisons shows retrieval-augmentation is a large, robustly significant effect, while finer-grained additions are directionally consistent but not statistically distinguishable from noise at this scale, and the LLM-judge metric has near-zero power where PGOS-FA succeeds. A blind human-correlation study on 79 real outputs shows PGOS-FA tracks human judgment at least as closely as the LLM-judge. Testing generalization beyond the ablation's single backbone, we find that most of the gap between an open-weight and a substantially stronger closed planner is attributable to open-weight model generation rather than to the closed model's capability specifically, and that our best configuration significantly outperforms an independently reproduced external baseline despite a weaker backbone. The dataset, PGOS-FA implementation, and evaluation code will be made available upon reasonable request.

</details>


### [117] [ProtoPur: Prototype-Purity Pseudo-Labeling for Weakly Supervised Instance Learning in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15669)
*Chen Shu,Chengyang Zhang,Boyu Fu,Ting Yin,Wenchuan Zhang,Jie Chen,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoPur是一种基于原型纯度的伪标签方法，在仅有包级弱标注的病理全图中，从基础模型特征构建原型区域，挑选高纯度、与目标类别强相关的局部区域打伪标签，从而提升实例级学习；并给出包含统一收敛项与伪标签噪声项的PAC式风险分解；在多数据集、多编码器、多个MIL骨干上提升实例级性能且袋级表现具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理全图的袋级弱标注易得，但实例级（patch/细胞）标注昂贵且含糊；现有弱监督MIL虽能做好袋级分类，但用注意力、预测、负包或空间细化得到的实例监督常混入非特异或共享形态，难以定位真正的类特异证据。

Method: 利用冻结的病理基础模型（foundation model）特征构建类原型与其覆盖的原型区域；估计每个原型区域的“类平衡局部纯度”，选择高纯度且与目标类强关联的区域赋予伪标签；以“纯度优先、覆盖为辅”的策略，确保伪标签既特异又足够多。理论上给出PAC式分析，将实例分类器的风险分解为统一收敛项与ProtoPur特有的伪标签噪声项（由选区不匹配与覆盖残差构成）。

Result: 在三个公开WSI数据集、两个病理基础编码器、多个MIL骨干上，ProtoPur稳定提升实例级指标，同时保持竞争性的袋级性能；在两个数据集上的伪标签质量评测显示其原型纯度选择提供更强的实例监督。

Conclusion: 基础模型特征蕴含可开采的实例级监督信号；ProtoPur通过基于原型纯度的选择机制，在仅有袋级弱标注的场景下有效揭示并利用这些信号，无需密集实例标注。

Abstract: Weak bag-level labels are widely available in computational pathology, whereas reliable instance-level annotations for whole-slide images remain expensive and ambiguous. Weakly supervised multiple instance learning (MIL) achieves strong bag-level classification, but instance-level supervision from attention scores, model predictions, negative bags, or spatial refinement may fail to identify class-specific local evidence. We propose ProtoPur, a prototype-purity pseudo-labeling method for weakly supervised instance-level learning in pathology whole-slide images. ProtoPur estimates class-balanced local purity over prototype regions constructed from frozen foundation-model features, and assigns pseudo-labels to high-purity regions strongly associated with a target class. This purity-first design separates class-specific evidence from negative or shared morphology, with coverage used only to ensure sufficient pseudo-label assignment. We provide a PAC-style analysis showing that the risk of an instance classifier trained with ProtoPur pseudo-labels can be decomposed into a standard uniform-convergence term and a ProtoPur-specific pseudo-label noise term, comprising selected-region mismatch and a coverage residual. Experiments on three public whole-slide image datasets with two pathology foundation encoders show that ProtoPur consistently improves instance-level performance across multiple MIL backbones while maintaining competitive bag-level performance. Pseudo-label quality comparisons on two public datasets further demonstrate that prototype-purity selection provides stronger instance supervision. These results suggest that the foundation-model features contain exploitable instance-level supervision signals, and ProtoPur is a way to reveal and use these signals from weak bag labels without requiring dense instance annotations.

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Human Intent-to-CAD: Parametric CAD Model Generation via Cooperative Multi-Task Alignment and Spatial-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15681)
*Qingwang Zhang,Jiahao Li,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiCAD：从手绘草图与文本等多层次人类意图直接生成可执行参数化CAD代码的统一方法，并构建首个对齐草图-文本-CAD代码的大规模数据集；通过多任务对齐与空间感知强化学习，显著提升几项任务表现与几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念设计阶段的人类意图常以不完整、非结构化的草图与文本呈现，现有从意图到CAD的流程依赖已有目标模型或分步转换，难以保证几何/拓扑一致性与可执行性，缺乏大规模对齐数据集。

Method: 1) 构建HiCAD数据集：对齐手绘草图、文本描述与可执行参数化CAD代码。2) 两阶段框架：a) Cooperative Multi-Task Alignment（协同多任务对齐）学习跨模态表征，缩小草图/文本与代码之间的语义鸿沟；b) Spatial-Aware Reinforcement Learning（空间感知强化学习）以几何与拓扑一致性为奖励，优化生成代码的可执行性与形状准确性。

Result: 在多项任务（从草图/文本到CAD代码与模型重建等）上显著优于现有基线，生成的代码更稳健、几何与拓扑一致性更强，重建质量与可执行率提升。

Conclusion: 统一从多层次人类意图到参数化CAD代码的方案可行且有效；对齐学习结合空间感知RL能保障高保真、可执行的CAD生成。数据集与框架为未来跨模态CAD研究提供基础。

Abstract: Parametric Computer-Aided-Design (CAD) modeling from human intent remains challenging, particularly during the conceptual design stage, where design goals are expressed through incomplete and unstructured modalities (e.g., hand-drawn sketches and textual descriptions). In this work, we rethink the human intent-to-CAD pipeline and propose a unified method that directly maps multi-level human intents to executable codes, without assuming the prior existence of target CAD models. To support our study, we construct HiCAD, the first large-scale dataset aligning hand-drawn sketches, textual descriptions, and parametric CAD codes. Based on this, we introduce HiCAD, a two-stage framework comprising Cooperative Multi-Task Alignment to bridge the representational gap between heterogeneous inputs, and Spatial-Aware Reinforcement Learning to enforce geometric and topological consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines across multiple tasks, validating its effectiveness and robustness in transforming heterogeneous human intents into high-fidelity parametric CAD models. Our project page: https://zqwlearning.github.io/HiCAD.

</details>


### [119] [PhySimCode: A Benchmark and Evaluation Method for Physics Video to Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15722)
*Souraja Kundu,Aditya Gupta,Joel Julin,Yizhou Zhao,Liuyue Xie,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhySimCode基准，要求从原始视频恢复物理规律与参数并生成可运行仿真代码；系统评测显示当前MLLM能说规律但难以从视频推断并复现实验，存在显著视频到仿真的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物理评测多关注合理性、属性估计或预测，但不能检验模型是否能把视觉动力学翻译成可运行的仿真代码。为推动将视频转为可执行模拟、为机器人提供结构化物理监督，需要一个能评估“从视频到可执行物理仿真”的能力的基准。

Method: 构建PhySimCode数据集与评测：160.6K程序化生成样本，覆盖162类物理现象，包含SciPy解析仿真与PyBullet渲染的刚体、关节体、流体与接触场景。每个样本打包仿真代码、物理参数、渲染视频及结构化链式推理（观测→定律→ODE→数值方法→校验）。评估10个前沿闭源与开源MLLM，指标涵盖物理定律正确性、仿真等价性、代码可执行性与参数恢复。

Result: 模型在“陈述物理定律”上得分高（4.27–4.93/5），但“从视频推断定律”仅1.51–2.47/5；最佳模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6）仅有7.7%的参数可在±20%误差内恢复。总体显示视频到仿真的性能较差。提供2430视频评测集与评测代码。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM虽擅长口头化物理与代码生成，但难以从视频中恢复可执行的物理仿真与精确参数，存在明显能力缺口。PhySimCode为该方向提供大规模数据与统一评测，有望推动面向机器人与科学计算的“视频→仿真”研究。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are good at video understanding and code generation, but it remains unclear whether they can recover the physical laws underlying videos, a capability essential for converting videos into executable simulations and for providing structured physical supervision in robotics. Captioning motion video does not imply that a model has inferred its governing dynamics, estimated quantitative parameters, or can regenerate the observed scene from code. Existing video-physics benchmarks evaluate plausibility, property estimation, or future-state prediction, but offer limited insight into whether models can translate visual dynamics into runnable simulation code. We introduce PhySimCode, a benchmark for physics video-to-code generation: given only a raw video, a model must infer the relevant physics, recover parameter values, and generate runnable Python that reproduces the input video. The dataset contains 160.6K procedurally generated samples spanning 162 physics phenomena across analytic SciPy simulations and PyBullet-rendered rigid-body, articulated, fluid, and contact-rich scenes; each sample bundles the simulation code, physical parameters, rendered video, and a structured chain-of-thought covering observations, laws, ODEs, numerical methods, and validation checks. We evaluate ten state-of-the-art closed- and open-source MLLMs along physical-law correctness, simulation equivalence, code executability, and parameter recovery. Models reliably state physics laws (4.27-4.93/5) yet score only 1.51-2.47/5 on inferring them from video, and the best system (Claude Sonnet 4.6) recovers just 7.7% of ground-truth parameters within $\pm 20\%$, exposing a substantial video-to-simulation gap. The 2,430-video evaluation set is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SourajaKundu123/PhySimCode and the evaluation code at https://github.com/SourajaKundu/PhySimCode

</details>


### [120] [Visually Legible but Adversarial: Watermark-Constrained Attacks on End-to-End VLMs for Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15728)
*Qingzhe Han,Fan Dang,Shengqi Chen,Xinyi Sun,Haoyu Xiong,Lingkun Li,Di Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种对白盒文档视觉语言模型的自动区域对抗水印（AR-AWM），仅优化水印区域像素即可显著破坏DocVQA与OmniDocBench等任务表现，且与问题无关、可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见水印常用于标识与保密，但其形状和位置也可能成为对抗攻击载体；现有针对VLM的对抗研究多依赖问题或语言侧信号，缺乏面向文档理解、可复用、与问题无关的水印式攻击。

Method: 设计小而分布式、贴合文本形状的“水印支持”区域，仅对白盒条件下这些像素做梯度优化，目标是扰乱视觉表征；无需输入问题或使用语言模型梯度/输出；同一被攻击页面可复用于不同下游查询；比较无优化水印、随机扰动、矩形布局等变体以剖析贡献。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，约7.98%像素的水印在报告阈值内将DocVQA EM从0.899降至0.150、ANLS从0.939降至0.212；无优化与匹配随机扰动ANLS仍为0.932/0.933，说明退化来自对抗优化；布局匹配矩形优化仅到0.276，表明文本形状带来增益；在OmniDocBench上多项文本/公式/阅读顺序/表格指标均下降；对白盒优化的InternVL2.5-8B将ANLS从0.922降至0.046；跨模型迁移弱，显示编码器特异性强。

Conclusion: 传统可见水印形状可成为文档VLM的对抗攻击面；在白盒设定下，问题无关、页面可复用的区域化对抗水印能显著破坏多种文档理解指标，且效果依赖模型编码器与文本形状几何。

Abstract: Visible watermarks convey document ownership, confidentiality, and status, but can also carry adversarial signals. We introduce Auto-Regional Adversarial Watermarking (AR-AWM), a model-aware white-box attack on end-to-end visual language models (VLMs) for document understanding. AR-AWM constructs a small, spatially distributed watermark-text support and optimizes only its pixels to disrupt visual representations. The attack is untargeted and question-agnostic: generation uses no questions, answers, parsing prompts, language-model gradients, or language-model outputs. Each attacked page is reused across downstream queries. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, a watermark support covering 7.98% of page pixels under the reporting threshold reduces DocVQA exact match from 0.899 to 0.150 and average normalized Levenshtein similarity (ANLS) from 0.939 to 0.212. Selected qualitative cases retain visually legible source fields. The same support without optimization and with matched random perturbations retains ANLS of 0.932 and 0.933, respectively, identifying adversarial optimization as the primary cause of degradation. Layout-matched optimized rectangles yield ANLS of 0.276, showing an incremental benefit from text-shaped geometry. AR-AWM also degrades all reported text, formula, reading-order, and table metrics on OmniDocBench. Separate white-box optimization on InternVL2.5-8B reduces ANLS from 0.922 to 0.046, while weak cross-model transfer reveals strong encoder specificity. These results identify conventional watermark-shaped supports as an adversarial surface for document VLMs in the evaluated white-box setting.luated white-box setting.

</details>


### [121] [ScenePlacer: Reformulating Scene-Level Object Pose Estimation as Visibility Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15734)
*Ken Deng,Jiarui Liu,Guibiao Liao,Kunming Luo,Mochu Xiang,Hongyu Yan,Yebin Liu,Shenghua Gao,Yizhou Yu,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScenePlacer将“生成物体”和“放到场景里”解耦：先用原生形状生成模型产出3D网格，再从部分观测点云学习可见性场并对齐，得到物体在场景中的姿态与位置，无需修改生成器或依赖场景级标注。实验在3D-FUTURE优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景生成/放置要么直接在场景坐标生成物体，破坏形状在规范坐标的先验；要么在生成阶段从完整形状回归位姿，遇到尺度不确定与部分观测导致的不适定问题。需要一种既保留形状先验、又能在部分观测下稳健估计位姿的方法。

Method: 提出ScenePlacer：将对齐重表述为条件可见性场学习。输入为重建得到的部分点云与生成的物体网格；网络在物体坐标中预测可见性场，随后将观测与网格“可见部分”对齐以求刚体变换。骨干使用预训练3D形状VAE，保持网格先验，同时通过独立编码的观测进行条件化。引入可见性感知、半径约束的监督；仅用物体级网格与渲染视图合成训练对，避免场景级位姿标注。

Result: 在3D-FUTURE数据集上，相比放置与位姿微调基线，在真值深度和单目估计深度下均显著提升。对生成物体，在估计深度下Chamfer 0.0077、F-score 92.40；在真值几何下Chamfer 0.0023、F-score 98.27。

Conclusion: 通过将位姿估计转化为可见性场学习并与生成器解耦，ScenePlacer在部分观测下实现稳健、无场景级标注的物体放置，优于现有方法，同时保留生成模型的规范空间先验。

Abstract: We present ScenePlacer, a framework for scene-level pose estimation of objects generated by state-of-the-art native shape generation models, placing generated 3D objects into scenes given a partial geometric observation without modifying the underlying generative pipeline. Existing scene generation approaches either generate objects directly in scene coordinates, compromising their canonical-space priors, or regress poses from complete generated shapes in the shape generation process - the latter being a poorly conditioned problem that is sensitive to scale ambiguity and partial observations. ScenePlacer decouples object generation from pose estimation by reformulating alignment as conditional visibility-field learning. Given an observed partial point cloud from reconstruction and a generated mesh, it predicts a visibility field defined in object space, then estimates the rigid transformation by aligning the partial observation to visible parts on the mesh. We propose a pretrained 3D shape VAE as our backbone, preserving its mesh prior while conditioning it on a separately encoded observation. We further introduce visibility-aware, radius-constrained supervision and synthesize training pairs using only object-level meshes and rendered views, eliminating the need for scene-level pose annotations. On 3D-FUTURE, ScenePlacer consistently outperforms existing placement and pose-refinement baselines under both ground-truth and monocular depth estimation. With generated objects, it achieves a Chamfer distance of 0.0077 and an F-score of 92.40 under estimated depth and improves to 0.0023 and 98.27, respectively, under ground-truth geometry.

</details>


### [122] [RSAJEV: Training-Free Abstaining Prototype Memory for Eliminating Silent Hallucination in JEV-Based Remote Sensing Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15742)
*Man Chen,Antai Chen,Shuai Xu,Chengjie Gu,Huaping Zhou,Kelei Sun,Tao Wu,Yongjie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将遥感场景分类用MLLM的“插槽-字母选项”单次前向推理框架改造成基于检索的原型读出，显著缓解跨数据集与开放集下的置信失配（“静默幻觉”）问题；在无需训练、每类少量参考样本下，实现强鲁棒性与可弃权能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用MLLM进行分类时，常把类别作为字母选项并用语言建模头打分。该“字母绑定”在训练词汇内表现很好，但跨数据集/词汇时行为异常：零适配准确率大幅波动，且在开放集里对未见类反而更自信（AUROC低至0.38）。需要一种无需再训练、能稳健泛化并提供可靠不确定性/弃权的读出方式。

Method: 保留MLLM的插槽表示，不再用字母token打分；提出RSAJEV：一种免训练、可弃权的原型记忆读出。做法：为每类缓存K个参考图像，在相同prompt下提取其插槽表示作为原型；推理时对比查询的插槽表示与各类原型做最近邻检索，并辅以收缩-马氏（shrinkage-Mahalanobis）几何校正；以检索相似度与ID-ness评估实现校准与弃权；类的增删通过离线重计缓存矩阵完成。

Result: 在UCM跨数据集门（gate）上，用每类16个参考、零梯度更新达89.43%准确率；在AID上达96.90%。开放集上ID-ness AUROC为0.91–0.99，全面超过符号化置信度（0.38–0.96）。同时支持零额外推理开销的类增删。

Conclusion: 将读出从“字母打分”改为基于检索的原型匹配，可在无需训练的条件下显著提升跨域与开放集鲁棒性，修复置信校准并提供可弃权通道；为MLLM插槽表示引入首个检索式读出，机制上类似RAG但用于判别决策，实用性与可扩展性强。

Abstract: Scene classification of remote sensing imagery with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is formulated as a single-forward-pass decision problem: candidate class names are lettered options in the prompt, a registered answer slot is conditioned on the prompt, and classification is read out by scoring letter tokens with the language-modeling head. We show that although this letter binding is highly effective within its training vocabulary (97.71% OA on UCM), it behaves erratically out of vocabulary: zero-adaptation accuracy across four cross-dataset directions fluctuates between 7.35% and 96.40%, and under out-of-vocabulary weights its confidence fails inversely on open-set evaluation, assigning higher confidence to unseen than to seen classes (AUROC 0.38), a failure we term silent hallucination. We retain the slot representation and replace the readout with RSAJEV, a training-free abstaining prototype memory that performs retrieval-based prototype matching: K reference images per class are cached as prototypes via their slot representations under the same prompt, and classification becomes nearest-neighbor retrieval over the prototypes, refined by a shrinkage-Mahalanobis geometric correction. This readout is mechanistically isomorphic to retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) but operates on discriminative decisions; it is the first retrieval-based readout for MLLM slot representations. The retrieval readout reaches 89.43% on the UCM cross-dataset gate with 16 references per class and zero gradient updates, and 96.90% on AID. Its ID-ness scores attain 0.91-0.99 AUROC on open-set evaluation over two datasets, exceeding symbolic confidence (0.38-0.96) in all four open-set settings, restoring a calibratable abstention channel and supporting class addition/removal at zero extra inference cost via offline rescoring of the cached matrix.

</details>


### [123] [SpikeOcc: Towards Resilient Multimodal 3D Occupancy Perception via Spiking Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15745)
*Yiding Sun,Zijie Xu,Hongqiang Lin,Jiajun Cao,Dongxu Zhang,Kai Tang,Jihua Zhu,Wenxuan Liu,Zhaofei Yu,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpikeOcc：用于自动驾驶的多模态3D占用预测框架，结合脉冲神经网络与两项机制（SMoE与SAL），在保持全传感器精度的同时显著提升传感器缺失场景下的鲁棒性与能效潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态3D占用方法聚焦精细融合以追求更高精度，但对“传感器缺失/失效”情况下的鲁棒性关注不足；同时探索类脑脉冲神经网络在多模态感知中的价值与可训练性挑战。

Method: 引入SpikeOcc，核心包括：1) Spiking Mixture-of-Experts（SMoE）：为RGB、LiDAR与其融合各设专门通路，利用脉冲神经元实现专家分工，通过有界残差保留原始多模态特征，实现全宽度的单模态表征与融合兼顾。2) Sensor Availability Learning（SAL）：分阶段课程式训练并加入显式路由监督，使模型在不同传感器可用性下学会合适路由与表征，提升在严重传感器缺失时的稳健性；该策略对传统（非SNN）模型也有效。

Result: 在多组实验中，SpikeOcc在全传感器条件下维持与主流方法有竞争力的精度；当出现传感器掉线/缺失时显著抬高性能下限（更高的最低表现），展现更好的鲁棒性。SAL在常规模型上同样带来显著增益。

Conclusion: 采用SNN与SMoE+SAL的设计能够构建更可靠、能效更高的自动驾驶多模态占用预测系统。SpikeOcc展示了类脑计算在提升多模态鲁棒性方面的潜力，并对未来面向失效感知的融合方法具有启示。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D occupancy prediction underpins safe autonomous driving through comprehensive scene understanding. However, existing methods have largely pursued higher accuracy through sophisticated fusion, leaving resilience to missing sensors underexplored. In this paper, we investigate spiking neural networks (SNNs) as a means of building more resilient multimodal perception. Still, the discrete nature of spiking neurons makes optimization challenging. To overcome this difficulty, SpikeOcc is introduced with two complementary components, a Spiking Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) and Sensor Availability Learning (SAL). Specifically, SMoE organizes RGB, LiDAR, and joint fusion into specialized pathways, enabling full-width unimodal representations while retaining the original multimodal features via a bounded residual. Beyond sensor dropout, SAL employs a staged curriculum with explicit routing supervision, progressively improving robustness under severe sensing conditions. Remarkably, SAL also yields notable gains for conventional models, highlighting its broader potential for resilient multimodal learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SpikeOcc maintains competitive full-sensor accuracy while markedly raising the performance floor under sensor outages. SpikeOcc illustrates the promise of neuromorphic computing, pointing toward more reliable and energy-efficient autonomous driving.

</details>


### [124] [Frozen History: When Temporal Losses Stop Accumulation in Learned Real-Time Supersampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15752)
*Hao Yu,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示：在实时学习超采样中，一旦时空配准足够精确，常用的“时序一致性损失”会意外抑制历史累积，导致网络倾向于直接拷贝已对齐的历史，从而出现“冻结”现象；减小或规范化时序项并采用严格配准，可显著提升PSNR与稳定性并降低抖动伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时超采样（如DLSS 类方法）依赖在低分辨率抖动采样与沿运动向量的时序累积来提升质量，通常引入时序一致性损失以减轻闪烁。但作者发现：当配准足够精确时，该损失会鼓励网络“偷懒”，几乎零代价复制历史，造成静态像素冻结与细节丢失。社区中还存在多种被训练流程掩盖的配准误差，使问题被相机抖动成本所“遮蔽”。需要系统分析配准与时序损失的相互作用，并给出稳健的训练与结构设计。

Method: 1) 提出“模型无关的oracle”以评估在完美配准下静态像素冻结成本；2) 系统剖析四类常见配准错误：携带抖动的运动向量、对两种分辨率格点复用同一运动场、抖动的深度、未注册的skip路径；3) 通过可控训练，分别在精确与不精确配准下扫描时序损失权重，观察对PSNR、像素方差与主观质量的影响；4) 采用小时序权重与归一化样本累加器，构建“严格注册”的朴素网络，并在多网络族、两种放大倍率与多数据集上评估。

Result: - 精确配准下，“冻结”代价骤降：静态像素两数量级更便宜，网络倾向复制历史。
- 时序权重效应反转：在精确配准下，增大权重降低方差但牺牲准确度与感知质量；静态帧PSNR在权重0.25时下降0.7–0.9 dB；该现象在三种网络族、两种尺度复现，且在无抖动STSS数据上亦降低精度。
- 性能与SOTA对比：采用小权重+归一化累加的严格注册网络，在QRISP上×2/×3/×4到1080p较SOTA提升0.34–0.62 dB；×2下静态像素标准差仅为DLSS 2的1/4；无需再训练，在Arm的抖动Bistro数据上较经典时序上采样至少高4.4 dB。
- 速度：最准确的×4模型在H200上处理1080p帧耗时2.03 ms。

Conclusion: 核心结论：配准精度决定了时序一致性损失的作用方向。精确配准会让较大的时序损失促使网络冻结历史、牺牲真实度；因此应弱化时序损失、使用归一化累加并确保端到端严格配准（含运动向量、深度与skip路径）。在该框架下，简单模型即可在PSNR与稳定性上超越既有方法，并保持极高实时性。

Abstract: Learned real-time supersampling accumulates jittered low-resolution samples along motion vectors. Many methods use a temporal consistency loss to reduce flicker. We show that with accurate registration, this loss can suppress accumulation: on static content, the network copies its warped history at almost no temporal cost. Camera jitter in motion vectors raises this cost and can hide the effect. We identify four registration errors that training can conceal: jitter-carrying motion vectors, one motion field for both resolution lattices, jittered depth, and an unregistered skip path. A model-free oracle shows that correct registration makes freezing two orders of magnitude cheaper on static pixels. Controlled training shows that registration reverses the temporal weight's effect on PSNR. Under exact registration, increasing the weight reduces pixel variance at the expense of accuracy and perceived quality, with a 0.7 to 0.9 dB loss on static frames at weight 0.25. This reversal recurs across three network families and two scales; the loss also reduces accuracy on unjittered STSS data. With a small temporal weight and a normalised sample accumulator, a plain registered network improves the best published QRISP PSNR by 0.34 to 0.62 dB at $\times$2, $\times$3 and $\times$4 upscaling to 1080p. At $\times$2, its static pixel standard deviation is one quarter that of DLSS 2. Without retraining, it outperforms classical temporal upsampling by at least 4.4 dB on Arm's jittered Bistro capture. Our most accurate $\times$4 model runs in 2.03 ms per 1080p frame on an H200. Code: https://github.com/CV-AC/Frozen_history

</details>


### [125] [SlotAVS: Object-Centric Audio-Visual Segmentation via Bi-Modal Slot Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15764)
*Arnav Sharma,Anand Mishra,Renu Rameshan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlotAVS：以竞争式双模态slot attention实现音视频分割，在多声源场景避免掩码混叠，配合匈牙利匹配与置信度融合，在AVSBench上S4 88.6% mIoU、MS3 71.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐导向的跨模态Transformer在多声源场景中易将不同声源绑定到共享视觉表示，导致掩码合并或实例不稳定，难以充分利用基础模型先验进行细粒度的声物体定位。

Method: 引入对象中心的SlotAVS框架：1）从时间域音频序列初始化离散“对象slot”；2）在视觉空间token上施加严格的slot级竞争式双模态slot attention，显式竞争绑定，减少声源混合；3）通过匈牙利匹配基于空间与边界重叠将slot与GT最优对齐，稳定实例学习；4）采用数值稳定、置信度感知的掩码融合机制聚合多源预测；并利用基础模型的语义先验作为辅助而非完全依赖全局融合。

Result: 在AVSBench上取得S4分割mIoU 88.6%、MS3 mIoU 71.7%，优于基于全局跨模态对齐的基线，在复杂多声源场景中显著减少掩码混叠与专化不稳定。

Conclusion: 显式的竞争式绑定对复杂音视频场景理解至关重要。对象中心slot机制与稳定匹配/融合策略，能在不完全依赖基础模型能力的前提下，有效分离并定位多声源对象，提升AVS性能。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) requires predicting pixel-wise masks of sounding objects by grounding acoustic signals in visual space. In multi-source scenes, concurrent sound sources often remain entangled within shared visual representations, leading to merged masks or unstable specialization when using standard alignment-focused transformer architectures. To address this, we present SlotAVS, an object-centric framework driven by competitive bi-modal slot attention. While robust foundation models provide rich semantic priors, SlotAVS shifts the paradigm from global cross-modal fusion to explicit competitive binding. By initializing discrete object slots from temporal audio sequences and enforcing strict slot-wise competition over spatial visual tokens, our method structurally mitigates the blending of distinct acoustic entities. Further, to stabilize instance-level learning, we introduce a Hungarian matching strategy that optimally aligns slots with ground-truth objects using spatial and boundary overlap. Coupled with a numerically stable, confidence-aware mask fusion mechanism, SlotAVS robustly aggregates multi-source predictions. Extensive experiments on the AVSBench dataset demonstrate the efficacy of our approach, achieving 88.6% mIoU on the Single-Source (S4) split and 71.7% mIoU on the Multi-Source (MS3) split. These results show that explicit competitive binding is critical for complex audio-visual scene understanding and effectively leveraging foundation-model priors without relying solely on their capacity.

</details>


### [126] [Data-Centric Recursive Self-Improvement for Navigation via Policy-Synchronized Language-Action Dual Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15782)
*Songze Li,Haoming Gu,Ziyang Gong,Zhitong Wang,Gengze Zhou,Zun Wang,Yuhan Zhu,Tianxiang Jiang,Juanzi Li,Qi Wu,Weinan Zhang,Yu Qiao,Wenwei Zhang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSI-Nav 提出一个递归自改进的数据中心框架，将指令跟随（语言→行动）与指令生成（视觉行动→语言）统一到同一模型中，并以小批分片循环：生成指令→执行评估→筛选样本→立即更新，使合成监督与策略同步，最终在R2R-CE达68.6% SR、RxR-CE达72.4% SR，并提升指令生成与空间理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据方法要么分离导航与指令生成模型、要么通过特定组件耦合，两者都缺乏统一性；且常用静态一次性标注导致监督滞后于策略迭代，影响导航性能与泛化。需要一种能统一两任务、并让监督与策略同步演化的机制，降低人工成本、提高数据质量与训练效率。

Method: 在监督微调打底后，对未标注轨迹按小分片迭代：当前共享模型为分片轨迹生成语言指令；再用这些指令执行导航，依据目标达成率与路径效率评估；筛选合格样本并立刻用其更新模型，然后处理下一分片，实现语言-行动双向自举与策略同步。并引入空间理解数据以强化空间推理。

Result: 在R2R-CE获得68.6% 成功率（SR），在RxR-CE获得72.4% SR；相较基线，指令生成质量更强，且在多轮自演化后空间理解有小幅提升。

Conclusion: 通过分片递归自改进与语言-行动双向自举，RSI-Nav将生成的监督与不断演进的策略保持一致，减少标注依赖并显著提升VLN性能，同时兼顾更好的指令生成与空间推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires agents to follow natural-language instructions to reach target locations, making high-quality language supervision essential. Since human annotation is costly, prior work has explored scalable data synthesis methods. However, existing methods typically either use separate models for navigation and instruction generation or couple the two tasks through task-specific components; most also annotate the full trajectory corpus with a fixed model before updating, which can cause generated supervision to lag behind the evolving policy. Therefore, we introduce RSI-Nav, a data-centric recursive self-improvement framework for navigation via policy-synchronized language-action dual bootstrapping, which unifies vision-language-to-action instruction following and vision-action-to-language instruction generation within a shared model. Specifically, after supervised fine-tuning establishes fundamental capabilities, RSI-Nav progressively processes unlabeled trajectories in small shards. The current model generates instructions for these trajectories, executes them to evaluate goal completion and path efficiency, retains qualified samples, and immediately updates itself before processing the next shard. This shard-wise update keeps newly generated supervision aligned with the evolving policy. We also incorporate spatial understanding data, since stronger spatial reasoning benefits navigation. Extensive experiments show that RSI-Nav reaches 68.6% SR on R2R-CE and 72.4% SR on RxR-CE, while providing stronger instruction-generation utility and modestly improved spatial understanding after recursive evolution.

</details>


### [127] [Reference-Efficient Multimodal Bitstream-Corrupted Video Recovery via Depth-Conditioned Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15806)
*Tianyi Liu,Jiaojiao Wang,Ye Lu,Kejun Wu,Chengtao Cai,Zhengguo Li,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种多模态比特流损坏视频恢复（MBSCVR）方法：用深度作为几何先验，并在每个恢复窗口仅选取一个非本地RGB参考，通过可靠性感知的多模态选择与深度条件化的参考自适应（DCRA）实现更好的质量-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖本地残差或多个非本地RGB参考，但比特流损坏会使两者不可靠；同时多参考会显著增加接收端的计算与内存。若在传输前获得的深度几何仍可用，就能在外观受损时提供稳健约束，从而在保证质量的同时降低资源消耗。

Method: 1) 多模态选择器：在编码参考前融合外观可靠性与深度兼容性，选出每个恢复窗口唯一的非本地RGB参考；2) 深度条件化参考自适应（DCRA）：将所选RGB证据注入特征补全与内容生成；3) 帧对齐的深度条件用于引导查询补全；整体框架在损坏视频上进行特征补全与生成，同时严格限制参考数量。

Result: 在带oracle掩码的完整BSCV基准上，仅用一个参考，YouTube-VOS达到32.30 dB，DAVIS达28.93 dB，分别超过全参考的B2SCVR 0.27和0.33 dB，并在两子集上提升SSIM；端到端实测在接收端延迟降低36.8%，峰值内存降低40.8%。

Conclusion: 借助紧凑的几何引导与对参考外观的自适应处理，框架在保证甚至提升恢复质量的同时显著节省计算与内存，实现更优的质量-资源权衡。

Abstract: Recovering visually faithful content from damaged video bitstreams is important for reliable multimedia communication and storage. Existing methods exploit local residuals and non-local RGB references of the input video sequence, but both sources can become unreliable after decoding, while processing additional references increases receiver computation and memory. This paper studies multimodal bitstream-corrupted video recovery (MBSCVR) using RGB and depth information, where pre-transmission depth-based geometry aids reconstruction when the received appearance is corrupted. We propose a novel MBSCVR framework that selects and effectively utilizes a single selected non-local RGB reference per recovery window to achieve higher performance. It integrates a reliability-aware multimodal selector that combines valid appearance with depth compatibility before reference encoding. Depth-Conditioned Reference Adaptation (DCRA) introduces selected RGB evidence into feature completion and content generation, while frame-aligned depth conditions completion queries. On the complete BSCV benchmark with oracle masks, MBSCVR uses one selected reference to achieve 32.30 dB on YouTube-VOS and 28.93 dB on DAVIS, surpassing full-reference B2SCVR by 0.27 and 0.33 dB and improving SSIM on both subsets. Matched receiver-forward measurements show 36.8\% lower latency and 40.8\% lower peak memory. These results demonstrate how compact geometric guidance and adapted appearance processing improve the quality--resource trade-off of multimedia recovery.

</details>


### [128] [Totem: Object-Centric 3D Memory for World-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15807)
*Zichen Wan,Tianyu Huang,Fan Li,Kai Wang,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Totem提出一种以对象为中心的3D记忆框架，使视频生成在大视角变化下保持物体一致性与场景连贯性。通过为每个目标物体建立持久3D记忆并与统一世界坐标对齐，结合双流3D条件与注意力约束，提升目标视角合成与时空一致性。实验显示在世界生成与对象基准上均优于现有方法，并支持多样应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成在大视角变化与长时序下易出现物体形变、身份漂移与空间关系不稳定，缺乏统一世界坐标的稳健表示。需要一种能跨时刻、跨视角维护物体状态并与场景几何一致的机制。

Method: 1) 对象级3D记忆：为选定物体建立与共享世界坐标对齐的持久3D表征，赋予独立状态；2) 双流3D条件架构：一条流利用对象记忆，另一条流融合场景几何用于目标视角合成；3) 3D结构约束注意力：用对象记忆提供的3D结构限制模型注意力，保留物体表面与相对空间布局，从而提升跨视角一致性。

Result: 在世界生成与对象中心基准上，Totem在大幅相机运动下能更好保持物体一致性与3D世界连贯性，目标视角渲染质量更高，并展现更稳定的表面/布局保持能力。

Conclusion: 以对象为中心的3D记忆结合双流条件与结构化注意力，可显著提升世界一致性视频生成，对多种应用场景具有通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Generating coherent videos grounded in a unified underlying world remains a major challenge, particularly under large viewpoint changes, where existing methods often struggle to maintain stable representations of individual objects. To this end, we introduce Totem, an object-centric 3D memory framework for world-consistent video generation. Totem first represents each selected object as a persistent 3D memory aligned to a shared world frame, assigning it a dedicated state. Based on these memories, we design a dual-stream 3D conditioning architecture that combines object memories with scene geometry for target-view synthesis. Moreover, these object memories constrain the model's attention with 3D structural information, thereby preserving object surfaces and relative spatial layouts. Extensive experiments on world-generation and object-centric benchmarks demonstrate that Totem generates videos depicting a more object-consistent 3D world under challenging camera trajectories, while supporting versatile applications.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Look, Flexible Thought: Learning How and How Long to Think in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15810)
*Quanlin Chen,Xiuwei Chen,Kun Xiang,Zehua Ma,Chunjin Ye,Boqiang Guo,Xianming Liu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出L2T框架，将视觉感知与文本推理统一到同一自回归空间，并通过模态感知路由奖励与难度自适应快慢推理策略，提升MLLM在通用与推理密集任务上的效率与性能，实现与显式工具调用相当性能但吞吐提升1.7–3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快慢推理主要调控文本思维链长度，把视觉计算（外部工具或视觉tokens）当作辅助，导致视觉感知与文本推理无法联合优化，限制了MLLM在复杂多模态任务上的效率与效果。

Method: 1）将外部工具生成的视觉状态离散化为视觉tokens，引入与文本共享的自回归表示空间；2）基于该统一表示，设计模态感知的路由奖励，自动选择合适的推理模式（看与想的配比）；3）提出难度感知的快-慢策略，按任务复杂度自适应控制推理深度（计算量）。

Result: 在通用感知与重度推理任务上验证有效性；在保持与显式工具调用方法相当/有竞争力性能的同时，实现1.7–3.6倍吞吐提升。

Conclusion: 统一视觉与文本推理表示并用模态感知与难度自适应机制，可在不牺牲精度下显著提高MLLM的计算效率与吞吐，优于将视觉计算当作外部、分离组件的传统范式。

Abstract: The introduction of long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and external tools has substantially improved the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on general and reasoning tasks. However, existing fast-slow reasoning paradigms mainly regulate textual reasoning length, while visual computation introduced by external tools or visual tokens is often treated as an auxiliary component. This separation prevents MLLMs from jointly optimizing visual perception and textual reasoning. To address this issue, we propose L2T (adaptive Look, flexible Thought), a unified reasoning framework that jointly optimizes the form and depth of computation. L2T first discretizes tool-produced visual states into visual tokens, bringing visual perception and textual reasoning into a shared autoregressive space. Based on this unified representation, L2T introduces a modality-aware routing reward to select appropriate reasoning modes and a difficulty-aware fast-slow strategy to adapt reasoning depth according to task complexity. Experiments on general perception and reasoning-intensive tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of L2T. Compared with explicit tool-calling approaches, L2T maintains competitive performance while achieving 1.7--3.6 times higher throughput.

</details>


### [130] [ChangeT$^3$: Evaluating Vision Test-Time Training for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15813)
*Sharukshan Niranjan,Oshadha Samarakoon,Nichula Wasalathilaka,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Vijitha Herath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChangeT^3：一种结合ViT^3-Tiny编码器与ChangeFormer解码器的Siamese双流变化检测模型，利用视觉测试时训练（内更新）在不显式时序注意的情况下提升F1至80.61±0.90（LEVIR-CD+），但零样本迁移差、推理速度较慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时相变化检测需要既保留空间细节又捕获跨时相上下文。现有显式注意或时序交互模块带来复杂度与耦合；视觉测试时训练（V-TTT）可按图像自适应小内模型，从而探索无需额外时序交互的改进路径与其效益。

Method: 采用“原生”Siamese架构：两条图像流共享ViT^3-Tiny分层编码器的可学习权重，但分别基于各自空间特征计算快速权重（fast weights），每个编码块保留全局简化SwiGLU分支与本地自适应深度卷积分支。解码端使用多尺度ChangeFormer：在四个分辨率对齐并学习融合双流特征，随后粗到细聚合并进行二分类预测。通过关闭/开启内更新对比，直接检验图像条件的内更新效应，而不添加额外时序交互模块。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD+上与ChangeFormer同等解码器、配方、预算、从零训练、三随机种子：ChangeT^3 F1=80.61±0.90，优于ChangeFormer的78.84±0.21；当禁用内更新时，同一编码器F1=78.63±0.20，显示内更新贡献。按该数据集常规协议在测试集上选取checkpoint。零样本迁移到SYSU-CD、DSIFN-CD、WHU-CD均失败。尽管理论参数与算子相近，小输入下ChangeT^3推理更慢。

Conclusion: 在不引入显式时序交互的前提下，利用测试时内更新的Siamese ViT^3+ChangeFormer能在LEVIR-CD+上带来统计显著的F1提升，但泛化与速度受限：零样本迁移差且小尺度推理较慢。未来应关注跨数据集泛化、加速内更新与探索更轻量的时序建模。

Abstract: Bitemporal remote sensing change detection requires representations that preserve spatial detail while capturing contextual information relevant to changes between acquisitions. Visual test-time training offers an alternative to explicit token-to-token attention by adapting a compact inner model to each image. This letter describes ChangeT$^3$, a vanilla Siamese configuration combining the existing hierarchical ViT$^3$-Tiny encoder with the multiscale ChangeFormer decoder. The two image streams share learned encoder parameters, while their fast weights are computed independently from their respective spatial features. Each encoder block retains the inherited global simplified SwiGLU and local adaptive depthwise-convolution branches. Temporal comparison is performed by the decoder through learned fusion of paired features at four resolutions, followed by coarse-to-fine aggregation and binary prediction. This separation provides a direct setting for examining image-conditioned inner updates without introducing an additional temporal interaction module. Trained from scratch on LEVIR-CD+ with the same decoder, recipe, and budget as a ChangeFormer control, ChangeT$^3$ reaches an F1 of 80.61$\pm$0.90 against 78.84$\pm$0.21 for ChangeFormer (three seeds each); as in the standard protocol for this dataset, checkpoints are selected on the test split. Trained with both inner updates disabled, the same encoder reaches 78.63$\pm$0.20. Neither model transfers zero-shot to SYSU-CD, DSIFN-CD, or WHU-CD, and ChangeT$^3$ is the slower of the two at small inputs despite similar parameter and operation counts.

</details>


### [131] [Mobility Helps Learning: Unsupervised Model Adaptation for Object Recognition via Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15846)
*Yanan Ma,Yihang Tao,Zhengru Fang,Senkang Hu,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCaFe 利用移动代理在不同视角/距离下对同一物体预测质量的差异，进行无监督测试时自适应；通过方差加权的跨视角伪标签融合与难样本选择，并可用公共数据进行有偏校正加权（p-MoCaFe），在自动驾驶与 embodied 数据集上显著优于现有方法（最高约20%精度提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA把测试样本当作独立静态快照，忽视了移动代理对同一目标的多视角/多距离观测所蕴含的监督信号——预测质量随视角/距离显著变化，这种运动诱导的方差信息可用于更可靠的自适应。

Method: 提出MoCaFe：1) 机动性校准的伪标签过滤——对同一物体跨视角预测进行逆方差加权融合，构造高置信伪标签；2) 利用这些伪标签进行难样本选择以提升学习效率与稳健性。扩展p-MoCaFe：利用公共数据为样本分配权重，形成带有有界偏差的洁净损失估计，从而抑制伪标签污染与确认偏差。方法模型无关、超参不敏感。

Result: 在nuScenes、KITTI及embodied-agent数据集上，相比SOTA TTA获得显著提升，最高约20%准确率增益；表现稳定且对超参不敏感。

Conclusion: 利用运动带来的预测方差进行跨视角融合和样本加权，能在无标注条件下有效地对移动代理的识别模型进行测试时自适应，显著超越现有TTA方法，并具备良好的泛化与稳健性。

Abstract: Movable agents, such as autonomous vehicles and robots, should be able to autonomously adapt their pre-trained object recognition models to new environments without human annotations. While existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods address label-free model adaptation, they typically treat test data as independent snapshots, without constructively exploiting a rich, unique source of supervision: the significant variance in prediction quality as an agent observes the same object from different distances and viewpoints. To capitalize on this, we propose \texttt{MoCaFe}, a model-agnostic and hyperparameter-insensitive paradigm that leverages motion-induced prediction variance for unsupervised adaptation. Specifically, we first develop mobility-calibrated filtering of pseudo-labels, which constructs reliable pseudo-labels by fusing cross-view predictions using inverse-variance weights. The filtered pseudo-labels then guide the selection of ``hard samples'' for effective learning. To further suppress pseudo-label corruption, we introduce \texttt{p-MoCaFe}, an optional extension that uses a public dataset to assign weighting factors to data samples, yielding an estimator of the clean loss with bounded bias. Extensive experiments on autonomous driving (nuScenes, KITTI) and embodied-agent datasets show that \texttt{MoCaFe} significantly outperforms state-of-the-art TTA schemes, achieving up to approximately 20% accuracy gain.

</details>


### [132] [Sharper Frames, Worse Rooms: Viewpoint Coverage Dominates Image Quality in Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15854)
*Alibek Dadajonov,Hikmatillo Umarhojiyev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在小视角预算下，挑最清晰的帧会严重伤害多视角室内重建；广覆盖视角比单帧清晰度更重要，且推理阶段的可用不确定性信号无法揭示该上游失误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态室内空间推理依赖多视角几何重建，但通常在固定视角/帧预算下选择输入帧。业界直觉偏好“更清晰的图像”，作者怀疑这种偏好在小预算下可能适得其反，并欲量化“视点覆盖 vs. 图像清晰度”的相对重要性，以及推理阶段的不确定性信号能否发现选择失误。

Method: - 数据：9段手持室内视频，每段约200候选帧。
- 选择策略比较：从200帧中选取固定预算（如16帧），比较“最清晰帧优先”“时间均匀采样”等多种策略。
- 诊断：分析清晰度与相机运动耦合（清晰帧多出现在慢速段，导致视角集中）。
- 2×2因子实验：独立操控“视点分布广度（spread）”与“单帧清晰度（quality）”，评估对房间尺寸误差的主效应与交互。
- 不确定性分析：检查重建模型的多视图一致性分数与误差的关系，以及VLM在相同帧上的信心与空间估计误差的关系。

Result: - 在9个房间扫描中，从200帧里选最清晰的16帧是最差策略：房间尺寸误差达48%，而时间均匀采样仅3%。
- 失败机制：清晰帧集中于慢速运动区，导致视角高度聚集，出现高达192°未观测（最差单元超300°）。
- 因子实验：视点覆盖的主效应约为清晰度的3倍；在9个场景中有8个是“模糊但分布广”优于“清晰但聚集”。随帧预算上升、角覆盖饱和，优势减弱。
- 检测性：重建模型的多视图一致性分数与误差正相关（越错一致性越高）；VLM的自信与其空间误差无关，均未能在推理时揭示上游帧选错。
