Method: 建立一个库：含多件衣物的规范（canonical）三维网格。给定单次局部点云观测，对每个候选网格用共享的、以图为条件的flow-matching生成模型对其可能的三维配置进行采样；然后依据与观测的匹配度对候选网格进行排序，以同时得到衣物身份与完整配置。模型对不同网格形状、顶点数与连通性保持适配，因此可直接加入新的规范网格而无需重训。持久化的顶点身份使得从配置到抓取目标点（如衣角、袖口）可直接映射。

Result: 在DexGarmentLab留出衣物上：从单次平铺观测中，221个候选中22/22均能将正确网格排在首位；从单次自遮挡观测中识别15/22（对比确定性图估计器8/22）。在真实衣物抓取定位上，平均误差由15.4 cm降至6.4 cm；在被遮挡目标上由17.0 cm降至8.3 cm，相较密集描述子匹配显著提升。

Conclusion: ClothAtlas能在部分观测下联合完成衣物识别与完整配置重建，具备跨拓扑与网格分辨率的泛化能力，可零重训纳入新衣物，并显著提升机器人抓取定位精度。项目资源见：https://clothatlas.github.io/

Abstract: Garment manipulation requires inferring both garment identity and complete configuration from partial, self-occluded observations. This creates a chicken-and-egg problem: completing a partial observation requires a garment-specific deformation prior, while identifying the garment requires a configuration that explains the observation. We introduce ClothAtlas, which jointly identifies garments and estimates their complete configurations from partial point clouds and a library of canonical meshes. A shared graph-conditioned flow-matching model samples configurations for each candidate mesh and ranks candidates by observation fit. The model accommodates different mesh shapes, vertex counts, and connectivities, allowing new garments to be added from canonical meshes alone without retraining. On held-out DexGarmentLab garments, ClothAtlas ranks the correct mesh first among 221 candidates for all 22 test garments from a single flat observation. From a single self-occluded observation, it identifies 15/22 garments, compared with 8/22 for a deterministic graph estimator. Persistent vertex identities support grasp-target localization on real garments, reducing mean error from 15.4 to 6.4 cm overall and from 17.0 to 8.3 cm on occluded targets compared with dense descriptor matching. Project page: https://clothatlas.github.io/

</details>


### [166] [Learning Temporal Evidence Selection for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16299)
*Hangil Park,Seungheon Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种语义条件化的Temporal Sampling Q-Former，用于从长视频中选择与异常事件解释相关的时域证据，以改进视频异常理解（VAU）的语言生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAU多依赖异常中心或预设采样，虽能抓住可疑时刻，但忽视上下文、对象交互和分散在时间轴上的证据，导致解释性不足。受长视频问答中可学习时域选择进展启发，将VAU的时域采样重构为与下游生成对齐的证据选择问题。

Method: 设计Semantic-conditioned Temporal Sampling Q-Former：在视觉token空间对帧级表示计算软时域权重，通过VAU生成目标端到端学习证据选择；利用从输入视频自动提取并经CLIP编码的对象标签作为条件，仅用于采样器，不注入LLM提示；合并重复选取后再生成回答。

Result: 在HIVAU-70K上优于对比的微调模型；消融显示：可学习时域采样优于固定的异常聚焦采样；引入CLIP对象标签条件优于仅用通用提示的条件。

Conclusion: VAU的时域采样应与解释导向的证据效用对齐，而非仅关注异常显著性；语义条件化、可学习的采样能更好支撑面向解释的生成。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) aims to generate language-level interpretations of anomalous events in long videos, requiring visual evidence that supports explanation beyond anomaly detection alone. Existing VAU pipelines commonly rely on anomaly-centric or predefined temporal sampling strategies, which efficiently emphasize suspicious moments but overlook contextual cues, object interactions, and temporally distributed evidence needed for reasoning. Motivated by recent progress in learnable temporal selection for long-video question answering, we revisit VAU temporal sampling as a downstream-aligned evidence selection problem. We introduce the semantic-conditioned Temporal Sampling Q-Former that learns to select temporal visual evidence through the VAU generation objective. Since generic VAU prompts provide weak video-specific guidance, we condition the sampler on CLIP-embedded object tags automatically extracted from the input video. These tags are used only for temporal evidence selection and are not appended to the LLM generation prompt, separating sampler conditioning from prompt augmentation. The proposed sampler operates in visual-token space, computes soft temporal selection weights from frame-level representations, and merges duplicated selections before response generation. Experiments on HIVAU-70K show that our model achieves the best performance among the compared fine-tuned models, while ablations demonstrate that learnable temporal sampling improves over fixed anomaly-focused sampling and that CLIP-embedded object-tag conditioning further improves over generic-prompt conditioning. Our results suggest that temporal sampling in VAU should be aligned with explanation-oriented evidence utility rather than anomaly saliency alone.

</details>


### [167] [RAR: Rate-Adaptive Rollout for Time-Consistent Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16302)
*Yaoyang Liu,Dongyu Yan,Yihua Du,Shengju Qian,Zeke Xie,Xin Wang,Ying-Cong Chen,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rate-Adaptive Rollout (RAR)，通过Stride-RoPE对齐不同采样步幅的潜在帧到同一物理时间轴，实现视频世界模型的可控、多速率、一致推理与在线速率切换，兼顾高频细节与长时程覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在固定时间率训练与推理，导致运行速率与学习速率不匹配时出现时间尺度漂移；且单一时间率在时间分辨率与物理时长之间存在固有限制，难以既捕捉细粒度动态又覆盖更长时间范围。

Method: 提出RAR框架：1) Stride-RoPE（跨步幅旋转位置编码）将以不同采样步幅得到的潜在帧映射到统一物理时间坐标，保证多速率一致性、对未见速率的泛化与在线速率切换无需丢弃历史；2) 采用多stride视频采样联合训练，使模型同时学习高频动态与长时程演化；并可迁移适配到已有预训练世界模型。

Result: 在包含动作控制与相机控制的环境中，RAR实现了时间一致的多速率rollout与在线速率切换；在维持高频生成质量的同时，在相同训练预算下延长了物理时域覆盖；并在迁移到预训练模型时保持感知质量。

Conclusion: RAR解决了时间率耦合问题，实现了可控的多速率推理与长时程建模，提升交互式世界模型的实用性与鲁棒性，同时具备对不同与未见速率的泛化与无缝切换能力。

Abstract: Video-based interactive world models aim to enable real-time, user-controllable environments. Yet most existing systems are trained and rolled out at a fixed temporal rate, limiting both runtime-rate adaptation and physical-horizon coverage -- a limitation we term temporal-rate coupling. Specifically, when runtime generation throughput differs from the learned temporal rate, the generated world evolves unintentionally more slowly or quickly. Moreover, using a single temporal rate imposes an inherent trade-off between temporal granularity and physical horizon: higher rates capture finer motion but cover a shorter duration under a fixed context window. To address both limitations, we introduce Rate-Adaptive Rollout (RAR), a framework that enables interactive world models to roll out at controllable temporal rates. At its core, Stride-RoPE aligns latent frames sampled at different rates on a shared physical-time coordinate, enabling consistent multi-rate rollout, unseen-rate generalization, and online rate switching without discarding history. Meanwhile, RAR uses multi-stride video sampling to enable joint learning of fine-grained dynamics and long-horizon evolution. Across action- and camera-controlled environments, RAR achieves temporally consistent multi-rate rollout and online rate switching while preserving high-rate generation quality and extending physical horizons under matched training budgets. RAR also transfers to pretrained world models while preserving perceptual quality.

</details>


### [168] [Building age estimation based on widely available multi-source1 geospatial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16320)
*Jialu Sui,Hongsheng Zhang,Rui Liu,Jing Ling,Ruisheng Wang,Hao Wu,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于多源地理空间数据的深度学习框架，在统一管线内进行像素级与实例级建筑年代估计，并通过时间序序约束提升年龄顺序一致性；并发布香港基准与代码数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖难以大规模获取的精细建筑信息（街景、功能、材料），或仅用光学影像与局部纹理，难以表征结构信息、区分不同时期建筑，且忽视建筑年龄类别的时间序关系。

Method: 构建多源遥感与派生产品+建筑轮廓为输入的统一框架：采用大规模预训练骨干+微调适配器提特征；像素级分支用金字塔解码器与余弦引导专家混合（CMoE）跨尺度融合多源栅格，刻画细粒度差异；实例级分支用注意力池化与属性条件自适应实例归一化（PCAdaIN）融合建筑属性形成实例表征；提出时间序序约束损失（TOCL）以强化年龄有序性；并含栅格派生与属性派生的融合模块，统一输出像素与实例估计。

Result: 在新构建的香港建筑年代基准上进行系统评测（摘要未给出具体数值），方法在区分不同时期建筑与顺序一致性方面优于依赖单一光学纹理的基线；支持像素级与实例级的多建筑年代估计。

Conclusion: 多源数据融合与有序约束可显著提升建筑年代估计的精度与时序一致性；统一管线兼顾像素与实例层级，具备实际城市治理与规划应用潜力；发布的基准与代码将促进后续研究。

Abstract: Building age is fundamental for urban infrastructure safety, operation, and planning, as it directly affects structural safety, and sustainable development. Existing methods mostly rely on detailed building information such as street view, function, or materials, which are harder to obtain than geospatial data like satellite imagery and Digital Surface Models (DSMs). However, existing methods based on those geospatial data mainly rely on optical imagery and local texture features, limiting their ability to capture structural information and distinguish buildings from different periods. Moreover, the inherent temporal ordering among building age categories is often overlooked. To address these limitations, we propose the first deep-learning framework for multi-building age estimation using widely available multi-source geospatial data, including remote sensing data and their derived products, as well as building footprint information that estimates age at both pixel and instance levels within a unified pipeline. Specifically, a large pretrained backbone with fine-tuning adapters extracts representative features, and raster-derived and property-derived fusion modules support pixel-wise and instance-wise predictions. Pixel-level estimation employs a pyramid decoder with Cosine-guided Mixture-of-Experts (CMoE) to fuse multi-source raster data across scales, capturing fine-grained building age differences, while instance-level prediction uses attention pooling and Property-Conditional Adaptive Instance Normalization (PCAdaIN) to extract instance-level representations and integrate property attributes. A Temporal Ordinal Constraint Loss (TOCL) enforces the sequential ordering of predicted ages. We also construct a building age benchmark for Hong Kong to enable comprehensive evaluation. The source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/littlebeen/RS-buildingAge.git.

</details>


### [169] [Anomaly-Guided Observation and Two-Level Semantic Feedback for Training-Free Video Anomaly Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16324)
*Hangil Park,Seungheon Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种训练免调权的语义反馈框架：用VAD引导的主动观测从视频中选帧，用冻结的VLM生成一次性的异常描述，并以视频级与局部级双重反馈回传，重整上游异常分数曲线，从而提升无训练的视频异常检测与视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言的训练免VAD方法虽利用更广的时序与语义上下文，但“泛化上下文”未必凸显异常，也未说明视频级语义应如何修正局部分数，导致异常定位与叙述不对齐。

Method: 1) 以上游异常分数曲线为起点；2) 迭代式、基于推理的帧选择：以高分区为种子，结合异常分数与VLM相关性评估扩展时域观测；3) 用冻结的视频-语言模型从所选帧生成一次性的开放式异常描述；4) 语义反馈一次性回传：- 视频级反馈决定对全局分数分布抑制/保持/锐化，并可选择性回看视觉证据；- 局部级反馈检索与事件相关的时间窗，仅在目标级视觉再验证后提升对应分数；5) 全流程冻结权重、无需再生成描述。

Result: 在UCF-Crime与XD-Violence的HIVAU视频摘要评测上，VAD引导的观测使GPT评分在合理性、细节与一致性上优于CLIP引导；在VAD上，语义反馈将重评后的上游检测器性能从UCF-Crime AUC 84.36%提升到86.25%，XD-Violence AP 68.07%提升到72.58%，最终AUC达91.74%。

Conclusion: 通过VAD引导的主动观测与一次性语义反馈（视频级+局部级）在不调权的前提下显著提升异常理解与检测精度，证明冻结的VLM/LLM可经语义反馈有效重整时序分数，实现更强的零训练VAD。

Abstract: Training-free language-based video anomaly detection (VAD) uses frozen vision--language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) to localize abnormal events without task-specific model-weight optimization. Existing approaches increasingly exploit broader temporal and semantic context, yet generic context does not necessarily foreground abnormal events or specify how video-level semantics should modify local anomaly scores. We introduce a training-free semantic feedback framework that uses VAD-guided active observation for video anomaly understanding (VAU) and returns the resulting anomaly-focused description to refine temporal detection. Starting from an upstream anomaly-score curve, we adapt reasoning-based iterative frame selection: high-score regions seed the observations, and anomaly scores together with VLM relevance assessments guide subsequent temporal exploration. A frozen video-language model generates a single open-ended anomaly description from the selected frames. The description is returned through two complementary feedback levels: video-level feedback determines whether the score distribution should be suppressed, preserved, or sharpened, with selective visual rechecking, while local feedback retrieves event-related windows and promotes scores only after target-local visual verification. All model weights remain frozen, and feedback is applied once without regenerating the description. On the HIVAU video-summary evaluations of UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, VAD-guided observation improves GPT-rated reasonability, detail, and consistency over the matched CLIP-guided variant. For VAD, semantic feedback improves the re-evaluated upstream detector from 84.36% to 86.25% AUC on UCF-Crime and from 68.07% to 72.58% AP on XD-Violence, where the final AUC is 91.74%.

</details>


### [170] [Explicitly Aligned Multi-View Breast Cancer Risk Prediction from Variable-Length Screening Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16329)
*Solveig Thrun,Zijun Sun,Suaiba A. Salahuddin,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Elisabeth Wetzer,Stine Hansen,Robert Jenssen,Michael Kampffmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlexLMV-Net：可变随访次数、跨CC/MLO多视角、显式时序对齐与融合的乳腺癌风险预测框架；并发布标准化评测基准BCRBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乳腺筛查风险预测未充分利用：1）纵向检查中同一区域的显式空间一致性；2）CC与MLO互补信息的联合建模；3）多于一次的历史检查。需要一个既能显式时序对齐、又能多视角融合、还能灵活处理不同随访数量的模型与统一评测基准。

Method: 提出FlexLMV-Net：以“乳房级”输入，联合建模CC与MLO视图和多个既往检查。核心包括：1）显式纵向特征图对齐（跨时间的空间匹配）；2）跨视图时序融合（整合CC/MLO在各时间点的信息）；3）双流注意力模块以增强互补特征交互；4）可处理可变数量的历史检查。另发布BCRBench，统一预处理、训练、推理与评估，用于公平可复现实验对比。

Result: 在EMBED与CSAW-CC两数据集上，方法在总体与各亚组（乳腺密度、癌症亚型、人口学子群）中均优于现有方法，显示显式对齐、多视角联合与灵活历史利用的增益。

Conclusion: 显式纵向对齐与跨视图融合对乳腺癌风险预测有效；灵活纳入多次既往检查可进一步提升性能。BCRBench为社区提供了标准化、公平可复现的比较平台。

Abstract: Accurate breast cancer risk prediction from screening mammography is essential for improved risk stratification and personalized screening. It can benefit from both temporal breast tissue changes across longitudinal examinations and complementary anatomical information from craniocaudal (CC) and mediolateral oblique (MLO) views. Leveraging longitudinal information requires identifying corresponding tissue regions across examinations, yet most approaches rely on implicit feature alignment without explicitly enforcing spatial consistency. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of explicit temporal alignment; however, existing methods model each view independently, preventing them from exploiting complementary CC and MLO information. Furthermore, explicit approaches have been limited to a single prior examination. To fill these gaps, we propose (github.com/sot176/FlexLMV-Net), a breast-wise longitudinal multi-view framework that jointly models CC and MLO views across screening examinations while accommodating a variable number of prior examinations. FlexLMV-Net explicitly aligns longitudinal feature maps and integrates complementary views through cross-view temporal fusion and a dual-stream attention block. Evaluated on EMBED and CSAW-CC, FlexLMV-Net consistently outperforms existing approaches across breast density, cancer subtypes, and demographic subgroups, highlighting the benefits of explicit longitudinal alignment, multi-view modeling, and flexible use of available screening history. We further introduce (github.com/sot176/BCRBench), an open-source benchmark that unifies state-of-the-art single- and multi-timepoint breast cancer risk prediction models under standardized preprocessing, training, inference, and evaluation protocols, enabling fair and reproducible comparison of existing methods.

</details>


### [171] [BAT3R: Robust Online 3D Reconstruction with Bayesian Adaptive State Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16330)
*Lijian Li,Yuanpeng He,Yudian Zheng,Chung-ju Huang,Linyu Li,Dongming Jin,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Chi-Man Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BAT3R：一种训练免调参的贝叶斯自适应框架，通过SIG和BASU在长序列流式3D重建中抑制漂移、降低误更新，显著优于SOTA，尤其在长视频与复杂场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式（在线）3D重建需顺序处理长视频并保持常量内存，但现有循环/递归架构在长序列上会渐进性劣化：无法区分观测可靠性与历史状态置信度，导致表示漂移与误差累积。需要一种既稳定又能选择性吸收新信息的更新机制。

Method: 提出BAT3R，无需再训练，包含两模块：1) State Innovation Gating (SIG)：作为分布正则/锚定器，将状态演化约束在其几何初始化附近，抑制长期推理中的表示漂移；2) Bayesian-guided Adaptive State Updating (BASU)：将状态更新重构为卡尔曼滤波式的递归贝叶斯推断，联合建模时间维的观测可靠性（以信息增益度量）与空间维的相关性（跨注意力图），实现不对称时空融合：巩固区域少更新，真正新内容强更新。

Result: 在7-Scenes、TUM、ScanNet、KITTI等基准上，BAT3R持续超过在线3D重建SOTA，长序列与复杂场景上优势最明显。

Conclusion: 通过SIG抑制漂移、BASU的贝叶斯自适应融合，BAT3R有效缓解流式重建的误差累积与退化，在无需训练的前提下实现稳健、可扩展的长序列3D重建。

Abstract: Feed-forward transformer models have driven remarkable progress in 3D vision,yet their quadratic complexity renders them impractical for long video sequences. Streaming 3D reconstruction addresses this bottleneck by processing frames sequen?tially with constant memory. However, existing recurrent architectures inevitably suffer from progressive degradation over long sequences, as they fail to account for the intrinsic reliability of observations and the accumulated confidence of the historical state, leading to severe error accumulation. To address these issues, we introduce BAT3R, a training-free Bayesian adaptation framework comprising two synergistic modules: State Innovation Gating (SIG) and Bayesian-guided Adaptive State Updating (BASU). SIG acts as a distributional regularizer that anchors state evolution to its geometric initialization, effectively arresting representational drift during long-term inference. Building on this, BASU reformulates state update as a recursive Bayesian inference process within a Kalman filtering framework. By jointly modeling the temporal reliability of observations via information-theoretic gain and spatial relevance via cross-attention maps, BASU achieves an asymmetric spatio-temporal state fusion: well-consolidated historical regions are protected from redundant updates, while genuinely novel scene content is aggressively incor?porated. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks (7-Scenes, TUM, ScanNet, KITTI, etc.) demonstrate that BAT3R consistently outperforms state-of-the-art online 3D reconstruction methods, with the most significant gains observed on long sequences and complex scenes.

</details>


### [172] [WorldRoPE: Probabilistic World-Centric Rotary Embedding for Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16337)
*Mijeong Kim,Kyujin Lim,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldRoPE 将RoPE扩展到多视角：把每个patch在世界坐标中表示为体素高斯混合，闭式积分旋转算子以处理不确定几何，从而稳健跨视角注意力，退化时等价于标准RoPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RoPE依赖2D图像内坐标，跨视角时同一3D点在不同视图坐标不同，导致难以建立稳定对应；多视角任务存在深度歧义与不确定性，需要将位置编码与3D几何不确定性结合。

Method: 提出WorldRoPE：在每层，把每个patch反投影到共享世界坐标系中的高斯体素混合（多种深度假设+不确定性+视锥范围）。将key的世界支持投影到query视图，诱导key在query中的概率分布；对RoPE旋转算子在该分布上做闭式积分：以混合权重加权中心旋转，并根据协方差进行衰减。该机制无需栅格化或特征扭曲；当支持退化为点时还原为确定性RoPE。跨层迭代：几何指导注意力，注意力更新的特征再反哺下一层几何。

Result: 在新视角合成、深度估计与三维重建等多视角Transformer任务上，WorldRoPE带来显著性能提升，验证了其在处理跨视角对应与不确定性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 面向多视角的世界中心化RoPE通过概率几何建模与闭式旋转积分，使注意力对关键点位置不确定性更鲁棒，兼容标准RoPE为特例，并能与分层几何—特征互促的框架结合提升多视任务表现。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is widely used to encode relative positions in Transformers. In multi-view settings, however, patch coordinates are defined independently in each image: the same 3D point can therefore have different coordinates across views, making them insufficient for establishing cross-view correspondences. We introduce WorldRoPE, a probabilistic world-centric RoPE for multi-view attention. At each layer, WorldRoPE back-projects each patch token into a set of volumetric Gaussian primitives in a shared world frame. These primitives represent multiple plausible 3D supports for a token, jointly capturing alternative depth hypotheses, depth uncertainty, and the finite spatial extent of its patch frustum. When a key token's support is projected into a query view, it induces a distribution over where that key may appear in the query image. WorldRoPE integrates the rotary operator over this distribution in closed form, combining rotations at the projected primitive centers according to their mixture weights with covariance-dependent attenuation. This makes attention less sensitive to uncertain key locations in the query view, without rasterizing or warping token features. The formulation reduces to deterministic RoPE when the support collapses to a point. Across layers, the predicted geometry guides cross-view attention, while the updated features refine the geometry used at the next layer. Experiments on novel view synthesis, depth estimation, and 3D geometry reconstruction demonstrate the effectiveness of WorldRoPE for multi-view Transformers.

</details>


### [173] [SyncReward: Human-Aligned Synchronization Reward for Audio-Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16338)
*Kaisi Guan,Ying Ba,Wenjing Wang,Xihua Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Ruihau Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SyncReward：基于语义-时序对比预训练与人工评分监督的音视同步奖励模型；并发布80K专业评分数据集与1K基准，显示现有指标与人类判断偏差大。SyncReward既是更优评价指标，又能作为奖励提升生成同步性（best-of-N与RL后训练）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成进步显著，但音频与视觉常出现时间错位；缺乏大规模人工标注、可靠基准与对同步敏感的评价模型，导致难以衡量/优化同步性。

Method: 1) 构建SyncReward-Data-80K：覆盖Speech/Onset/Instrument/Ambient四类事件，来自8个生成器的片段，由专业人员打分；2) 构建SyncReward-Bench：1K片段，多人独立评分作为保留基准；3) 系统评测现有专用同步指标与通用多模模型，量化其与人评不一致；4) 训练SyncReward：在真实片段上进行语义-时序对比预训练，在生成片段上使用人工评分进行监督学习；并作为评价指标与奖励信号用于best-of-N选择与RL后训练。

Result: 基于SyncReward-Bench，现有指标与人类判断显著不一致；SyncReward与人类评分一致性最高。将其作为奖励用于推理时best-of-N与RL后训练，可显著提升音视同步。

Conclusion: 通过数据、基准与奖励模型一体化方案，解决音视同步评测与优化缺口。SyncReward成为当前与人类一致性最强的同步度量，并可直接提升生成模型的同步质量；数据、基准与代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed rapid progress in joint audio--visual generation. However, audio--visual synchronization remains a challenge, as generated speech and sound often temporally misalign with visual events. A key bottleneck is the lack of human-annotated data, reliable evaluation benchmarks, and synchronization-aware evaluation models for generated audio--visual outputs. To address these limitations, we conduct a comprehensive study spanning data, benchmarking, and reward modeling. We first introduce SyncReward-Data-80K, a dataset containing 80K professional synchronization ratings for clips from eight audio--visual generators. A taxonomy-guided construction pipeline covers four sound-event families: Speech, Onset, Instrument, and Ambient. We further construct \textbf{SyncReward-Bench}, a held-out benchmark of 1K clips, each independently rated by multiple professionals. Using this benchmark, we systematically evaluate specialized synchronization metrics and omni-modal models, revealing their substantial misalignment with human judgments. Finally, we develop SyncReward, a reward model that combines semantic--temporal contrastive pretraining on real clips with human supervision on generated clips. As an evaluation metric, SyncReward achieves the strongest agreement with human ratings among the evaluated methods. As a reward, it improves synchronization through inference-time best-of-$N$ selection and reinforcement learning based post-training. We will release the data, benchmark, model, and code at https://github.com/WeChatCV/SyncReward.

</details>


### [174] [VideoSlate: Stateful Commit Control for Temporally Grounded Agentic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16357)
*Shuo Zheng,Yuanjie Dang,Peng Chen,Jianjun Xiang,Ronghua Liang,Mohammed Bennamoun,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoSlate提出一种有状态的“提交-控制”框架，通过共享证据黑板管理候选与当前视频片段的证据与验证，利用认证器决定是否修订时间片段，从而在视频定位与问答任务中通过选择性修订提升时序定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身/代理式视频推理系统在多阶段收集与评估证据时，容易在修订中引入两类问题：一是修正定位错误的同时可能丢弃已存在的有效证据；二是证据累积与状态更新耦合，导致难以在全局信息下理性决策。需要一种机制，将跨阶段的证据组织与时间状态控制相连接，使累积信息参与修订评估，同时用显式采纳决策来控制下游视觉证据。

Method: 提出VideoSlate：1) 以“类型化证据黑板”共享与记录候选片段、来源、构建过程、验证器观测等；2) 设计“现任相对（incumbent-relative）认证器”，同时比较候选、当前片段与相关提案，评估修订支持度；3) 控制器依据认证结果做出两类决定：授权修订或保留当前片段，将证据累积过程与状态更新解耦，并在黑板上记录每次决策与其支撑证据。

Result: 在视频定位与带定位的问答多个基准上，VideoSlate通过选择性修订提升了时序定位性能，相比基线取得更优结果（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 通过将共享证据黑板与可审计的提交-控制机制结合，VideoSlate实现了对修订的精细授权，兼顾证据累积与状态稳定性，进而提升视频时序定位与下游QA的表现。

Abstract: Agentic video reasoning systems gather and assess video evidence across stages, selecting relevant spans as visual evidence for answering questions. New candidates and observations prompt reassessment of the current span, but revisions can correct localization errors or discard useful evidence already present in the adopted span. Systems therefore need to connect evidence organization across stages with temporal state control, allowing accumulated information to jointly inform revision assessment while explicit adoption decisions govern how assessment outcomes affect downstream visual evidence. We introduce VideoSlate, a stateful commit-control framework built around shared evidence. A typed evidence blackboard links candidate spans, provenance, construction, and verifier observations, enabling an incumbent-relative certifier to assess revision support from the candidate, current span, and related proposals. Based on certification, the controller authorizes a revision or retains the current span, separating evidence accumulation from state updates and recording each decision and its evidence on the blackboard. Extensive evaluations across video grounding and grounded question-answering benchmarks show that VideoSlate can improve temporal localization through selective revision.

</details>


### [175] [From Sound to Sense: An Anatomy of Semantic Supervision for Speech-Driven Visual Grounding in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16364)
*Zhongyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究语音驱动的遥感视觉指代（RSVG），通过将冻结的Whisper语音路径接入共享解码器的文本-视觉地理定位模型，系统性评估“直连语音→框”与“ASR级联→文本→框”的优劣，并发布两个大规模合成语音数据集与零/少见组合拆分；结论：直连是否优于级联取决于识别器质量，强ASR几乎达到了文本上界并显著优于所有直连模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSVG多依赖文本输入，打字受限且丢失语调意图；直接语音到框的范式（如RSTalker）流行，但其学到的语言语义未被量化评估。需要一个统一架构来公平对比直连与级联系统，并剖析语义注入渠道对学习效果的影响。

Method: 将冻结的Whisper作为语音编码器接入一个VLTVG主干，使语音与文本共享同一解码器；利用由DIOR-RSVG文本合成的语音，使配对文本在训练期充当教师与上界。设计三类语义注入通道：特征模仿蒸馏、词级CTC监督、两阶段ASR预训练。用线性语义探针分析表征。发布Audio-DIOR-R与Audio-DIOR-Comp数据集与2x2声学-语义协议以评测泛化。

Result: 在弱域内ASR（CTC=0.53）级联下，直连模型在Acc@0.5上领先9.0点，错误主要集中在空间关系词；但在强域内ASR（CTC=0.029）下，级联系统达到文本上界（80.8 vs 81.0）并比最强直连模型高8.8点。

Conclusion: “反ASR”前提仅在弱识别器成立；当ASR足够强时，级联（ASR→文本→定位）与文本上界持平并显著优于端到端直连。共享解码器与三种语义注入渠道提供了公平评估框架与可诊断工具。

Abstract: Speech-driven visual grounding in remote sensing (RSVG) removes the typing bottleneck of text-based interaction and is intended to preserve prosodic intent, enabling hands-free target acquisition in time-critical missions. The direct-audio paradigm, mapping raw speech straight to a bounding box and bypassing automatic speech recognition (ASR), was recently adopted by RSTalker, but what such grounders actually learn about language remains unmeasured. SemEcho grafts a frozen Whisper speech pathway onto a VLTVG grounding host so that speech and text share one decoder, making the paired text, free by construction since speech corpora in this task are synthesized from DIOR-RSVG expressions, a training-time teacher and a fair, same-host text upper bound. On this instrument we dissect three semantic injection channels (feature-mimicry distillation, word-level CTC supervision, and two-stage ASR pretraining) with linear semantic probes, and release Audio-DIOR-R (80,973 training clips; 10 timbres x K=3; Qwen3-TTS synthesis with online sox noise lambda in [0, 0.4]) and Audio-DIOR-Comp, a re-split holding out 17 attribute-category combinations under a 2x2 acoustic-semantic protocol. Our anatomy yields four findings. (i) Whether direct alignment beats a domain-trained ASR cascade is decided by recognizer quality: against a weak in-domain cascade (CTC 0.53) direct wins by 9.0 Acc@0.5 points with transcription errors concentrated on spatial-relation words, but a strong in-domain cascade (CTC 0.029) reaches the text upper bound (80.8 vs. 81.0) and beats even the strongest direct model by 8.8 points; the anti-ASR premise holds only against weak recognizers.

</details>


### [176] [Throwing4: Phase-Aware Pose Modeling for Throw-Distance Prediction in Elite Athletics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16383)
*Bikash Kumar Badatya,Kartike Tiwari,Jyotirmoy Amin,Ravi Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出在转播环境下，用二维姿态序列预测精英田径投掷（标枪、铁饼、链球、铅球）成绩，并验证显式技术阶段（phase）对齐能降低预测误差。作者构建Throwing4数据集（500段视频，逐帧阶段标注与官方距离）。分阶段编码并融合比整段序列直接建模更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投掷项目动作具有清晰的生物力学阶段划分，且成绩为连续量（距离），适合可解释的性能建模。但转播视频噪声大、视角不稳，直接基于全序列姿态预测往往不稳健。作者想检验：显式的阶段对齐是否能作为诱导偏置，提升从转播姿态到成绩的回归性能。

Method: 1) 构建Throwing4数据集：涵盖四类投掷，提供2D姿态序列、逐帧阶段标注、官方距离。2) 将成绩预测表述为回归问题。3) 两种建模范式对比：a) 全序列统一建模；b) 阶段条件化：将每次尝试划分为四个技术阶段，分别用图结构姿态编码器学习阶段特征，再进行跨阶段融合。4) 在多种图卷积/图编码器上统一实验评估。

Result: 在不同图姿态编码器上，一致观察到：引入阶段结构后，距离预测误差显著降低，结果更稳定，优于将整段动作视为无结构序列。

Conclusion: 显式的生物力学阶段信息是有效的诱导偏置，可提升基于姿态的投掷成绩建模与鲁棒性。Throwing4数据与评测协议也为细粒度技术分析、可解释体育分析以及从转播视频的稳健建模提供了基础。

Abstract: Elite athletics throwing provides a structured biomechanical action sequence together with an objective continuous outcome, namely throw distance, making it a suitable setting for performance-oriented sports analytics. In this work, we investigate whether explicit phase alignment of pose dynamics improves throw-distance prediction under realistic broadcast conditions. To support this study, we curate Throwing4, a dataset of 500 elite throwing clips spanning javelin, discus, hammer, and shot put, with corresponding two-dimensional pose sequences, frame-level phase annotations, and official distance labels. We formulate throw-distance prediction from broadcast-derived pose as a regression problem and compare full-sequence modeling against a phase-conditioned approach that partitions each attempt into four technique stages and fuses phase-specific motion representations. Experiments across multiple graph-based pose encoders show a consistent reduction in prediction error when phase structure is incorporated, indicating that phase-aligned modeling provides a more stable representation than treating the entire motion as an unstructured sequence. These findings suggest that explicit biomechanical phase information is a useful inductive prior for pose-based performance modeling in elite athletics throwing. Beyond score prediction, the dataset and protocol provide a basis for future work on fine-grained technique analysis, interpretable sports analytics, and robust modeling from broadcast video.

</details>


### [177] [Recovering from Pose-Selection Failures in Repeated Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16409)
*Jaehyun Kim,Wonseok Kang,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种利用历史定位结果进行姿态重选的方法HPR，在重复结构导致误选时用过去最多5次的粗略位置预测来筛选当前候选姿态，几乎不增加计算并在多种配置下显著提升召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位在含有重复结构的场景中，错误姿态可能获得比真实姿态更多内点支持，导致误判；需要一种无需额外传感器、可快速纠正此类误选的机制。

Method: 从最近最多5次的定位结果生成粗位置预测（forecast），对当前查询的若干姿态候选进行“历史一致性”评估：选择与最佳预测最匹配的替代候选；只有当所有拟合良好的预测都更接近该替代候选而非原本本地化器给出的姿态时，才接受替代。方法不使用里程计，不优化姿态本身，仅用于在候选之间做选择。

Result: 在HLoc、基于MASt3R的姿态生成与基于JIST的检索等12种主配置中，使用单一开发集阈值即可在5m/10°标准下提升召回，失败回退率<0.4%。在NAVER Gangnam B1数据上，修正HLoc的97次失败，仅牺牲6次成功；在三套HLoc测试集上，即使在更严格的0.25m/2°标准下仍有收益。在候选集上的中位运行时<1ms（不含姿态生成）。在定位频率降低时恢复能力减弱。

Conclusion: HPR用历史输出引导当前候选重选，有效缓解重复结构导致的误定位，计算代价极低、无需额外传感器，并在多种系统与数据集上稳健提升召回；但其效果依赖足够频繁的定位历史。

Abstract: Repetitive structures can give an incorrect pose candidate more inlier support than an accurate one, causing visual localization to select it. History-Guided Pose Reselection (HPR) uses coarse position forecasts from up to five past localization outputs to distinguish well-separated current-query candidates. The best-fitting forecast proposes an alternative; HPR accepts it only if every well-fitting forecast is much closer to it than to the localizer's pose. HPR needs no odometry and returns the selected candidate unchanged, using prediction for selection rather than pose refinement. With one development-set threshold, HPR improves recall within 5 meters and 10 degrees in all 12 main configurations of Hierarchical Localization (HLoc), MASt3R-based pose generation, and JIST-based retrieval, losing successes on fewer than 0.4 percent of queries. On NAVER Gangnam B1, HPR corrects 97 HLoc failures and loses six successes; gains persist within 0.25 meters and 2 degrees on all three HLoc test sets. Median HPR runtime on HLoc candidates is below one millisecond, excluding pose generation. Recovery weakens under less frequent localization.

</details>


### [178] [Not All Challenges Are Equal: Evaluating Interactive Liveness Detection Against Real-Time Face Swaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16417)
*Anton Firc,David Drtil,Martin Perešíni,Milan Šalko,Vojtěch Staněk,Kamil Malinka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在远程身份验证中，通过随机动作挑战来揭露实时换脸（DeepFaceLive、FaceFusion）攻击；主动挑战显著优于静态检测，并且响应时延也是区分真伪的补充信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动深度伪造检测器对新生成器泛化差，交互式挑战常缺乏哪类动作最有效的实证依据；实时换脸可在视频到达验证方前替换人脸，威胁远程认证。

Method: 在108次真人会话中（6名受试者、24个被冒充身份、两种换脸工具，经虚拟摄像头注入），设置包含静态校准段与随机动作挑战（姿态、组合、表情、手遮挡等）的验证流程；使用冻结的UCF空间检测器评估静态段与各主动动作窗口AUC，并测量完成验证的端到端用时。

Result: 静态校准段以冻结UCF空间检测器达会话级AUC 0.878；主动动作窗口平均AUC 0.988，姿态与组合动作最高，表情无显著提升；对配置了遮挡掩码的FaceFusion，手遮挡得分低于静态基线；完成验证的端到端用时区分真伪的会话级AUC为0.732（包含合成与传输延迟）。

Conclusion: 交互式挑战需针对当前攻击配置进行实测挑选；姿态/组合类动作最有效，表情作用有限；在部署中可将响应时延作为附加信号，与视觉检测结合以提升对实时换脸攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Remote identity verification assumes that a camera stream shows a live person, yet public real-time face-swap tools can replace that person before the stream reaches the verifier. Passive deepfake detectors generalize poorly to new generators, while interactive challenges are often selected without evidence of which gestures expose current attacks. We evaluate randomized gesture challenges in 108 live sessions with six subjects, 24 impersonated identities, and two face-swap tools, DeepFaceLive and FaceFusion, injected through a virtual camera. The static calibration segment reaches a session-level AUC of 0.878 with a frozen UCF spatial detector, while the mean across the active-gesture windows reaches 0.988. Pose and combined gestures have the highest point estimates, whereas expressions show no clear improvement. Hand occlusion against FaceFusion, configured with an occlusion mask, scores below the static baseline. The end-to-end time required to satisfy the validator also distinguishes attacks from genuine sessions, achieving a session-level AUC of 0.732, although this signal includes synthesis and transport delays. These results show that challenge sets should be tested against current attack configurations and that response time can provide an additional, deployment-specific signal.

</details>


### [179] [VisExMEM: Visual Evidence-Based Explainable Multimodal Evaluation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16441)
*Subhadeep Roy,Christoph Leiter,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisExMEM是一种无参考的图像-文本评估器：把字幕分解为原子主张，定位图证，逐条核验，并将支持度汇总为全局分数，同时给出与证据一致的可解释说明；并发布AGITA-Bench数据集。9B模型在八个基准上对开源方法领先，接近GPT-5.6 Sol。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图文评估多给单一全局分数，难以知道图像支持哪些语义细节；即使有可解释指标，支撑说明的视觉证据仍难以验证。需要一个能分解主张、定位证据、逐条核验并提供一致解释的评估框架。

Method: 提出VisExMEM：将字幕分解成原子主张；在图像中定位相应视觉证据；对每条主张进行支持度判定；将主张级支持度聚合为全局分数；解释与证据和判定共享。基于Qwen3.5训练4B与9B模型，使用多任务SFT、反事实精炼与基于证据感知的GDPO。构建AGITA-Bench，含600对图文与8.7K原子主张，用于全局与主张级评估。

Result: VisExMEM-9B在八个基准上取得开源评估器最高的描述性综合指标，比VQAScore高11.7分，并与GPT-5.6 Sol具有竞争力。对未调参的Qwen3.5与GPT-5.6 Sol，分解式提示相比整体式提示带来3.6–5.1分提升。人评显示其解释优于既有可解释指标，接近GPT-5.6 Sol。

Conclusion: 通过原子主张分解与证据定位，VisExMEM在准确性与可解释性上同时提升，并提供新基准AGITA-Bench推动细粒度图文评估研究。

Abstract: Current image-text evaluation often relies on a global score, which gives limited insight into which semantic details are supported by the image. Explainable metrics provide rationales, but the visual evidence supporting those rationales can still be difficult to verify. We introduce VisExMEM, a reference-free evaluator that decomposes captions into atomic claims, localizes visual evidence, verifies each claim, and averages claim-level support into a global score. Explanations use the same evidence and decisions. We train Qwen3.5-based 4B and 9B models on 100K samples with multi-task SFT, counterfactual refinement, and evidence-aware GDPO. Across eight benchmarks, VisExMEM-9B achieves the highest descriptive composite among open-source evaluators in our comparison, exceeding VQAScore by 11.7 points while remaining competitive with GPT-5.6 Sol. We also introduce AGITA-Bench, containing 600 human-annotated image-text pairs and 8.7K atomic claims for global and claim-level evaluation. For untuned Qwen3.5 and GPT-5.6 Sol controls, decomposed prompting raises the same composite by 3.6-5.1 points relative to holistic prompting. Human evaluation shows that VisExMEM explanations outperform prior explainable metrics and approach GPT-5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [180] [Pixels or Code? Benchmarking Instruction-Guided Visual Artifact Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16444)
*Shun Guo,Shuizhou Shi,Boao Wang,Wei Xiong,Siyu Sun,Jiangtong Li,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VAO（视觉制品操作）能力与评测基准VAO-Bench，统一评估图表/图纸/网页等可执行视觉制品的直接编辑、依赖更新与内容保留；比较像素编辑、代码生成、多工具代理三范式，发现代码可执行系统在依赖更新更优、像素编辑更快且质量高；显式依赖声明可显著提升两者，隐式更新推断是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实工作流中广泛依赖可视化制品（图表、技术图、网页、版面）。实际编辑需同时满足：可直接修改、能进行依赖感知更新、保持无关内容不变，并遵循领域约束。现有基准分散、聚焦子问题（如级联图表更新），缺乏跨域、统一协议与可复现实验，难以全面衡量系统在复杂视觉编辑中的可靠性。

Method: 提出统一的“图像仅输入”评测协议：给定同一源图像和指令，对三类系统（像素空间编辑器、代码生成多模态模型、工具型代理）在九类视觉制品与三种难度下执行编辑。构建VAO-Bench：1,573个人工筛选任务，使用可执行制品与确定性渲染追踪参考变换，并标注三类要求（直接编辑、依赖更新、内容保留）。设计多维指标：直接编辑准确度、依赖更新能力、内容保留度、领域合法性、视觉质量。

Result: 实验表明：可执行代码范式更擅长处理依赖更新；像素编辑器执行更快、视觉质量强；工具代理与代码系统在明确给出依赖时性能显著提升。隐式依赖推断是主要性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: VAO-Bench为视觉制品编辑提供可复现的统一评测基准，能够系统比较不同生成范式的优劣，强调显式依赖表达的重要性，并指向提升隐式依赖推断作为未来关键方向。

Abstract: Visual artifacts such as charts, technical diagrams, webpages, and document pages underpin scientific, commercial, and engineering workflows. Editing these artifacts requires direct modification, dependency-aware updates, preservation of unaffected content, and compliance with domain constraints. We define this capability as Visual Artifact Operation (VAO). Prior benchmarks test parts of this problem, including cascading updates in charts. We study direct edits, dependent updates, and preservation across visual domains and pixel, code, and agent paradigms under one image-only protocol. We introduce Visual Artifact Operation Bench (VAO-Bench), comprising 1,573 manually screened tasks across nine artifact categories and three difficulty levels. VAO-Bench uses executable artifacts and deterministic rendering to trace reference transformations and annotate direct edits, dependent updates, and preservation constraints. All systems receive the same source image and instruction; we evaluate pixel-space editors, code-generating multimodal systems, and tool-using agents on direct-edit accuracy, dependency-aware update, content preservation, domain validity, and visual quality. Experiments show that executable-code systems better support dependent updates, while pixel-space editors offer faster execution and strong visual quality. Explicitly stating dependencies improves both paradigms, identifying implicit-update inference as a central bottleneck. VAO-Bench provides a reproducible foundation for dependable visual artifact editing across generation paradigms.

</details>


### [181] [Tikhonov-Stabilized Bezier Representation Forecasting for Training-free Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16448)
*Lei Zhu,Mujie Lin,Ruochong Zheng,Guangyi Wang,Hao Li,Peng Jin,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BeziCast：一种无需训练、基于低阶贝塞尔轨迹的扩散模型推理加速方法，通过稳定拟合与风险防护，显著减少去噪器调用并保持高生成质量，在图像与视频模型上实现约4–5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型（尤其是DiT）推理代价高，因需多次调用去噪器。现有特征缓存加速方法常用有限差分外推，长间隔不稳定且在模块级预测上误差累积，影响质量与稳定性。需要一种无需改动生成器/采样器、能稳定长间隔预测且减小误差累积的部署友好方法。

Method: 引入BeziCast：对靠近输出端的去噪表征进行低阶贝塞尔（Bézier）轨迹预测。以Tikhonov正则的拟合估计贝塞尔控制点，获得平滑、容量可控的时间参数化，避免高阶导数估计并将轨迹容量与缓存-查询密度解耦。推导等价外推权重，并加入“凸包式护栏”：检测高风险预测，将其柔性拉回到简单形投影的保守估计，从而缓解激进外推带来的不稳。全流程无训练、无需改动生成器或采样器，仅利用缓存特征进行拟合与预测。

Result: 在先进图像与视频扩散模型上验证有效，较基线保持更好生成质量并获得显著加速：对FLUX.1最高约4.79倍、对HunyuanVideo最高约4.11倍，加速同时优于现有缓存-外推基线的质量保持。

Conclusion: BeziCast通过低阶贝塞尔轨迹与正则化拟合、配合凸包护栏，实现稳定、训练无关的特征外推与去噪加速，减小误差累积与长间隔不稳定问题，在多模型、多任务上带来显著且高质量保持的推理提速。

Abstract: Diffusion models, particularly Diffusion Transformers, achieve strong image and video generation quality but remain expensive at inference time due to repeated denoiser evaluations. Feature caching offers a deployment-friendly acceleration strategy by reusing or predicting intermediate representations without retraining the generator or modifying the sampler. However, existing cache-then-forecast methods often rely on Taylor-style finite-difference extrapolation, which can become unstable over longer cache intervals, and typically predict local module outputs whose errors may accumulate through subsequent denoiser blocks. We propose \textbf{BeziCast}, a training-free diffusion acceleration framework that forecasts output-proximal denoising representations using low-order B'ezier trajectories. BeziCast estimates B'ezier control points via Tikhonov-stabilized fitting, providing a smooth, capacity-controlled temporal parameterization that avoids explicit high-order derivative estimation and decouples trajectory capacity from cache-query density. To moderate aggressive extrapolation, we further derive equivalent forecasting weights and introduce a convex-hull-inspired guardrail that detects high-risk predictions and softly pulls them toward a conservative simplex-projected estimate. Extensive evaluations across advanced image and video diffusion models demonstrate the effectiveness of BeziCast. In particular, BeziCast accelerates FLUX.1 by up to (4.79\times) and HunyuanVideo by up to (4.11\times), while preserving substantially better generation quality than competing acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [182] [Not Every Cue Is Evidence: Cue Supportability for Forensic Distillation in Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16452)
*Binjia Zhou,Dawei Luo,Shuai Chen,Feng Xu,Haoyuan Li,Jia wen Seow,Jiachi Wang,Jie Lei,Zunlei Feng,Yijun Bei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于可解释人脸伪造检测的“双重可支撑性审计”蒸馏框架：在将特权线索用于学生模型前，先筛除不可由图像与上下文支撑的线索，只保留既可由其余特权信息预测、又与图像中高归因区域因果关联的线索，以减少“答对但证据错”（CAWE）。在8个基准上，该方法在保持或提升检测精度的同时显著提高解释的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释伪造检测常从伪造生成管线提取训练线索（特权线索），但实际输入图像质量/分辨率有限，难以观测这些细粒度痕迹。若直接以此监督学生模型，会迫使其生成看不见的“证据”，导致模板化或过泛化的解释，即CAWE问题。需要一种机制，确保用于蒸馏的线索确实能从当前图像与可用上下文中被感知与支撑。

Method: 提出“双重可支撑性审计”（dual supportability audit）以筛选待蒸馏的特权线索：1) 可预测性测试：候选线索需能由剩余特权上下文被可靠预测；2) 因果可支撑性：线索需与当前图像中的高归因视觉区域存在因果联系（如通过归因/遮挡等干预评估）。仅满足两者者赋予高蒸馏权重，其他线索权重降低或过滤，避免不可感知细节泄漏到学生。

Result: 在8个数据基准上，方法维持或提升检出率/准确率，同时显著提升解释忠实度（faithfulness），优于现有蒸馏与特权监督范式。

Conclusion: 通过在蒸馏前对特权线索进行双重支撑性审计，可有效抑制CAWE，令学生模型的解释与可观测图像证据一致，在不牺牲甚至提升检测性能的前提下，提高可解释性与实用性。

Abstract: Explainable face forgery detection requires not only correct judgments but also forensic evidence supported by the input image. However, training cues are typically derived from the forgery generation pipeline. Since images available to the model are of limited quality and resolution, it is difficult to observe the fine-grained traces these cues refer to. Training methods that further supply such cues as privileged supervision exacerbate the problem, as the student is taught to articulate evidence it cannot perceive. The student consequently resorts to templated or over-generalized expressions, giving rise to the correct-answer, wrong-evidence (CAWE) failure. We propose a dual supportability audit that filters privileged cues before distillation. Each candidate cue must be predictable from the remaining privileged context and causally tied to high-attribution visual regions in the current image. Only cues satisfying both conditions receive substantial distillation weight, preventing unsupported details from leaking into the student. Experiments across eight benchmarks show that our method maintains or improves detection accuracy while significantly raising explanation faithfulness compared to existing paradigms.

</details>


### [183] [GHOST: Geometry-Constrained Hybrid Diffusion for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16455)
*Changqun Feng,Wangxiandi Yin,Xin Hu,Ruiting Dai,Feiyang Yang,Jielei Wang,Tao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHOST是一个面向开放词汇的3D场景图生成框架，通过几何约束的混合扩散过程，联合建模连续的节点几何与离散的关系谓词，实现几何一致的关系预测；在3DSSG上于封闭与开放设置均达SOTA，空间谓词较RelationField提升17+点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇3D场景图生成不仅需理解对象语义，还需准确把握度量级空间关系。现有方法依赖语言相似度，容易学到语义共现而非显式几何，导致空间关系（如上/下/相邻）预测薄弱。为此需要一种能把语言谓词与可微几何约束对齐、并在生成过程中保持几何一致性的框架。

Method: 提出GHOST：1) 几何→约束→谓词的混合扩散去噪流程，联合连续节点几何与离散边谓词；2) 语言引导的约束诱导，将谓词嵌入映射为可微的几何约束，用于布局细化与谓词重加权；3) 几何-拓扑协同去噪器，利用噪声自适应的交互与方向感知建模耦合节点与边推理。

Result: 在3DSSG数据集上取得SOTA：在封闭集与开放词汇设置均优于现有方法，尤其在空间谓词上相比RelationField提升超过17个点。

Conclusion: 把语言谓词对齐到可微几何约束，并在扩散过程中协同建模几何与拓扑，可显著提升开放词汇3D场景图中的空间关系预测与整体性能。

Abstract: 3D scene graph generation requires understanding both object semantics and metric spatial relations. However, existing open-vocabulary methods often struggle with spatial predicates because language similarity reflects semantic co-occurrence rather than explicit geometry. We propose GHOST, a Geometry-constrained Hybrid diffusion framework for Open-vocabulary 3D Scene graph generaTion. GHOST jointly models continuous node geometry and discrete edge predicates, following a geometry-to-constraint-to-predicate denoising process to ensure geometrically consistent relation prediction. It introduces language-grounded constraint induction, which maps predicate embeddings to differentiable geometric constraints that guide both layout refinement and predicate reweighting. A Geometry-Topology Collaborative Denoiser further couples node and edge reasoning through noise-adaptive interaction and orientation-aware modeling. Experiments on 3DSSG demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both closed-set and open-vocabulary settings, including over 17-point gains over RelationField on spatial predicates.

</details>


### [184] [Teaching Vision-Language Models to Decline: Support Completion Against Mirage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16465)
*Alfaj Uddin Ahmed,Md. Shaown Miah,Md Arid Hasan,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出TIDE训练协议，通过在训练中加入空白图、同域错误图、异域图并要求模型选择“拒答”，系统性减少视觉-语言模型在未见证据上作答的“海市蜃楼”（mirage）现象；理论上证明任何不看图的预测器在该目标下都次优，实验在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上显著降低无效证据上的错误作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以交叉熵为主的训练目标不足以区分“看图作答”和“忽略图作答”，因为优化仅由答案与图像的条件互信息拉开差距，且训练样本不奖励拒答，导致模型会对无效或缺失图像依然自信作答，带来审核与安全风险。

Method: 提出“support completion（支撑补全）”/TIDE：对每个训练问题提供四种证据条件——有效图像、空白图、同域错误图、域外图；为后三者标注“拒答”目标。证明在该目标下：1）任意图盲预测器相对最优解至少劣于1.04 nats/样本；2）最优解在反事实条件上恰当拒答，同时保持对有效图像的行为；3）有限模型在答或拒的错误下界由其表示对有效/无效证据的可分性决定。并与若干替代方法比较：无标注grounding、似然优势对比、先补全后grounding等。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、冻结参数、独立评审设置下，三条件（不含全部四类）前身方案将无效证据上的mirage率从0.759降至0.097，空白图率从0.700降至0.003，代价是假阳性拒答率0.126。无标注grounding反而将mirage升至0.859；仅基于似然优势对比几乎无效（降至0.718），因空图项无梯度；在支撑补全后再做grounding会再度打开mirage。四条件全方案与多基座模型、种子与部件消融仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 交叉熵训练会纵容图盲预测；TIDE通过加入反事实证据并显式奖励拒答，可在理论与实证上显著压制mirage，同时保持对有效图像的表现。答/拒性能最终受表示分离度限制，提示未来需在表示学习与拒答判别上协同优化。

Abstract: Vision-language models answer questions about images they were never shown, a failure called mirage. Such an answer can be correct without being justified by the evidence, and the difference matters wherever a model's output is checked by someone who did not see its input. We locate the failure in the training objective: cross-entropy separates a model that reads the image from one that ignores it by only the conditional mutual information between answer and image, and no training example rewards declining. We formalise support completion, TIDE, which also shows each training question with a blank image, a same-domain wrong image and an out-of-domain image, with targets that decline, and prove that under TIDE every image-blind predictor is suboptimal by at least 1.04 nats per example, that the optimum declines exactly on the counterfactual kinds while keeping its behaviour on valid images, and that for any finite model the error of its answer-or-decline decision, declining on valid input plus answering on invalid input, is bounded below by how well its representation separates valid from invalid evidence. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B under a frozen held-out protocol with two judges, the three-condition predecessor of TIDE reduced the invalid-evidence mirage rate from 0.759 to 0.097 and the blank-image rate from 0.700 to 0.003 at a false-abstention cost of 0.126; label-free grounding raised the rate to 0.859, a likelihood-advantage contrast moved it only to 0.718 because its empty-image term carries no gradient, and grounding applied after support completion re-opened the mirage. The four-condition protocol on three base models, with seeds and component ablations, is in progress and not reported here.

</details>


### [185] [Auditing Observation-Quality Shortcuts in the Evaluation of Person-Centered Motion for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16471)
*Chaehyun Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示：现有视频异常检测在以人为中心的紧急/暴力场景中大量依赖“观测质量”捷径（检测、姿态、跟踪的好坏），而非真正的异常运动模式。通过质量诊断、配平与正交化评估后，多线索运动特征才展现出超越质量特征的判别力，但质量因素仍有残余影响。结论：高性能并不等于学到了异常运动，需审计人观测管线的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAD常以运动异常作为信号，但实际高分常可能来自检测、姿态、跟踪等人观测管线的质量差异，未必对应安全关键事件。作者希望厘清：当前基于运动的异常判别，究竟在学“任务相关运动”还是在利用“观测质量”差异这一捷径。

Method: 提出“观测质量感知”的评估框架：1) 质量诊断：量化11项观测质量变量（检测、姿态、跟踪相关）；2) 质量配平：用基数匹配使各质量变量在正负样本间达到预设边际平衡阈值；3) 运动正交化：与质量特征解耦，比较“质量仅特征”和“多线索运动特征”；4) 鲁棒性分析：在Logistic回归、随机森林、RBF-SVM等多模型下对比，并评估AUC与稳定性。数据聚焦UCF-Crime中的以人为中心类别。

Result: 在原始样本分布中，仅用质量特征即可匹敌或超过多线索运动特征的判别，说明存在强“观测质量捷径”。在完成基数匹配、使11个质量变量均低于预设边际失衡阈值后，多线索运动在LR、RF、RBF-SVM上稳定优于仅质量特征，ROC-AUC提升约0.046–0.141。但非线性质量模型配平后仍高于随机水平，表明除已测量的边际质量外仍存在可被质量特征捕捉的判别信息。

Conclusion: VAD高分并不能直接证明模型掌握了异常运动模式。必须审计检测、姿态、跟踪等人观测管线并通过质量感知评估框架控制偏差；在显式控制质量后，运动特征仍提供额外判别力，但并非“质量无关”或“因果级”理解。框架为识别并消除观测质量捷径、验证运动判别是否经受质量控制提供了系统协议。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) identifies deviations from normal patterns, but such deviations do not necessarily correspond to safety-critical events. We focus on human-centered emergencies and violence and investigate whether motion-based anomaly discrimination reflects task-relevant motion information or observation-quality differences arising from person detection, pose estimation, and tracking. We introduce an observation-quality-aware evaluation framework that combines quality diagnostics, quality balancing, motion orthogonalization, and robustness analyses. On human-centered categories of UCF-Crime, quality-only features match or exceed the discrimination of multi-cue motion in the original cohort, revealing a strong observation-quality shortcut. After cardinality matching brings all 11 measured quality variables below a prespecified marginal balance threshold, multi-cue motion consistently outperforms quality-only features across Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and RBF-SVM, yielding ROC-AUC gains of 0.046-0.141. Because nonlinear quality-only prediction remains above chance after matching, we interpret this result as additional discrimination beyond measured marginal quality rather than as evidence of quality-free or causal motion understanding. These findings show that high VAD performance should not be interpreted as evidence of abnormal-motion modeling without auditing the reliability of the person-observation pipeline. Our study provides a systematic protocol for evaluating observation-quality shortcuts and for assessing whether motion-based discrimination survives explicit control of detection, pose-estimation, and tracking reliability.

</details>


### [186] [MiMaAlign: Hierarchical Micro-Macro Alignment for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16482)
*Yansong Liu,Litong Gong,Weijie Li,Tiezheng Ge,Ming Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiMaAlign 提出一种分层（微观-宏观）对齐框架，向现成T2V扩散模型注入物理可行性的先验：用Neural ODE对齐连续运动（微观），用指令化VLM对齐事件与交互常识（宏观），仅以训练期辅助损失对齐不同深度的生成器特征；在不损伤通用画质的前提下，显著提升VideoPhy/VideoPhy2物理常识指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V视频生成虽清晰，但常出现物理不连贯与不合常识的交互。已有“表示对齐”多以单层级的通用视频特征或短程运动为监督，无法系统注入物理可行性（连续动力学+事件常识）这一多层级结构的知识。

Method: 构建微—宏两类教师并分层对齐到扩散模型：1) 微观（连续动力学）教师：以Neural ODE学习连续时间运动表征，将扩散特征与该表征对齐，约束短时/连续轨迹；2) 宏观（事件常识）教师：以指令提示的VLM抽取物体交互与事件语义表示；3) 训练期仅增加辅助目标，将微观监督对齐到较浅层特征、宏观监督对齐到较深层特征，使生成器内化对应物理先验；4) 基于CogVideoX-2B实施，不改变推理流程。

Result: 在VideoPhy上PC分数相对提升最高达21.0%，在更难的VideoPhy2上也有显著增益；在VBench上保持总体视频质量，并得到盲测用户偏好支持。

Conclusion: 物理可行性需要分层表征对齐：以Neural ODE注入连续动力学、以VLM注入事件常识，并将二者匹配到扩散模型不同深度，可显著提升T2V物理一致性且不牺牲画质。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models have made remarkable progress in generating high-fidelity visuals, yet their outputs still often exhibit physically implausible phenomena, including discontinuous dynamics and implausible event interactions. Existing representation alignment methods mainly transfer generic video features or short-range motion cues, treating physical plausibility as a single-level representation problem. To address this issue, we propose MiMaAlign, a hierarchical micro-macro alignment framework that transfers complementary dynamics-oriented and event-oriented knowledge into different abstraction depths of a pretrained video diffusion model. Specifically, micro-level continuous dynamics alignment imposes constraints by aligning diffusion features with a continuous-time motion representation learned by a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE), while macro-level event-commonsense alignment provides representations of object interactions and event semantics through an instruction-prompted vision-language model (VLM). With training-only auxiliary objectives, we align the complementary supervision from the two teachers with shallower and deeper generator features, respectively, enabling the generator to internalize the corresponding physical priors. Empirical evaluations show that, on CogVideoX-2B, MiMaAlign achieves a relative improvement of up to 21.0% in the Physical Commonsense (PC) score on the VideoPhy benchmark over the baseline, and also delivers substantial gains on the highly challenging VideoPhy2 benchmark, while maintaining general video quality on VBench and being supported by blind user comparisons.

</details>


### [187] [Self-Verifier Capture in Unified Visual Generators: Diagnosing and Repairing Closed-Loop Self-Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16500)
*Binyan Chen,Tong Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究统一视觉生成模型在自评奖励闭环中的失效：自校验器被模型策略利用甚至被共同权重重写，导致“自奖励上升、外部质量下降”。作者提出冻结并加锁奖励读出、再用稀疏外部锚点与漂移控制器校准，从而在极低外评覆盖率下显著提升GenEval与DPG-Bench表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成常用图像空间奖励，需要解码和独立评审，昂贵且稀疏。统一视觉生成器可用自身理解通路产生“免解码”内部奖励并自我优化，但可能产生自我强化偏差。作者动机是诊断这种自奖励闭环的失败模式并提出高效、低外评成本的修复方案。

Method: 1) 定义并分析“自验证器劫持”(self-verifier capture)：共享/冻结单元在Scale-RAE上匹配语义与去噪，分离两条失效路径：策略利用冻结语义裁判的流形盲点；共享权重更新改写裁判本身。2) 观察冻结裁判会陈旧，反复自刷新只能部分恢复且越来越弱。3) 提出两步修复：a) 锁定已预热的奖励读出，阻止被改写；b) 引入稀疏、参数不共享的外部锚点，抵消剩余利用。4) 使用未锚定rollout在多噪声层级保持语义奖励，形成密集潜空间奖励；5) 设计漂移触发的控制器，仅将外部锚点分配给高漂移rollout，并在组内传播校准；6) 在同等预算下比较固定剂量锚定与控制器分配。

Result: - 在Scale-RAE与更强的Show-o2上，均复现未锚定失败与修复的可转移性。- 完整方法在GenEval与DPG-Bench上显著优于结构匹配的“每个rollout都用裁判”的基线，同时仅对1/16的rollout进行外部评分。- 在1/32覆盖率时，四项对比中得分更高且总体不劣。- 控制器在覆盖变紧时相较固定剂量锚定带来更大收益。

Conclusion: 统一视觉生成器的自奖励闭环容易出现自验证器劫持，导致奖励—质量背离。通过锁定奖励读出防改写、加上稀疏外部锚点与漂移感知的锚点分配与校准，可在极低外评覆盖率下提供密集潜空间奖励与稳定对齐，跨架构迁移有效并提升基准性能。

Abstract: Image-space reward requires decoding and a separate judge; unified visual generators can instead reuse their understanding pathway for decode-free, self-supplied reward. Optimizing against this verifier closes the self-reward loop; shared trainable weights let one update move generation and scoring even with the reward gradient stopped. We call the resulting failure self-verifier capture: self-reward rises while independently measured quality falls. Matched semantic/denoising by shared/frozen cells on Scale-RAE isolate two routes: the policy exploits on-manifold blind spots of a frozen semantic judge, while shared updates additionally rewrite the judge. A frozen judge also becomes stale, and repeated self-refresh yields progressively weaker recoveries. The diagnosis yields a two-part repair: locking the warmed reward read blocks rewriting, while sparse, parameter-disjoint external anchors counter residual exploitation. Unanchored rollouts retain semantic reward across multiple latent noise levels, providing dense latent reward that terminal image-space judging cannot supply, while a drift-triggered controller allocates anchors to high-drift rollouts and propagates their calibration within each group. At matched budgets, the controller improves on fixed-dose anchoring, increasingly so as coverage tightens. Both the unanchored failure and the repair transfer to the higher-performing, architecturally distinct Show-o2. Across both bases, the complete method significantly outperforms a construct-matched every-rollout judge on GenEval and DPG-Bench while externally scoring only 1/16 of rollouts. At 1/32 coverage, it scores higher in all four comparisons and remains jointly non-inferior.

</details>


### [188] [KARAMEL: Knowledge-Aware Ranking for Multimodal Entity Linking in the Arts via Heterogeneous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16502)
*Raffaele Scaringi,Alejandro Sierra-Múnera,Gennaro Vessio,Giovanna Castellano,Ralf Krestel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KARAMEL框架与WikiMuSA数据集，用知识感知的多模态实体链接在文化遗产场景显著优于强基线，尤其在隐含/抽象提及上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态与异质数据中的实体发现/消歧困难，现有视觉-语言方法多停留在表层对齐，缺乏利用社会历史与结构化上下文进行推理，尤其在文化遗产等隐含知识丰富的场景。

Method: 构建KARAMEL：将视觉-语言编码器与异质图神经网络融合，在知识库与上下文关系图上进行知识感知的表征学习与推理，实现图中视觉与文本提及到实体的链接与消歧。并发布WikiMuSA数据集，包含艺术品图像、文本描述与结构化上下文，链接到Wikidata实体。进行实验与消融。

Result: KARAMEL在总体与难例（隐含/抽象提及、证据模糊或不充分）上超越强基线；消融表明领域特定、结构化上下文对表达能力和性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 知识感知的多模态架构能在复杂关系与不完全证据下实现稳健实体消歧；方法与数据虽以文化遗产为例，但对更广泛的科学多模态知识发现场景通用，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Discovering and disambiguating entities from multimodal and heterogeneous data is a key challenge in discovery science, where relevant knowledge is often implicit and distributed across modalities. We present KARAMEL, a framework for multimodal entity linking (MEL) - the task of aligning visual and textual mentions to entities in a knowledge base - in the cultural heritage domain. Unlike prior vision-language models that primarily rely on surface-level multimodal alignment, our approach incorporates sociohistorical and contextual knowledge through a knowledge-aware architecture that fuses a vision-language encoder with a heterogeneous graph neural network. This design enables contextual reasoning over complex relational structures and supports robust entity disambiguation under ambiguous or underspecified evidence. To benchmark multimodal discovery in this setting, we introduce WikiMuSA (Wikidata-based Multimodal Semantic data for Art), a new dataset that links artworks to Wikidata entities via images, textual descriptions, and structured contextual knowledge. Experimental results show that KARAMEL surpasses strong baselines, particularly on implicit or abstract mentions, while ablation studies highlight the critical role of domain-specific, structured context in learning expressive representations. While instantiated in the cultural heritage domain, KARAMEL addresses a general knowledge discovery problem and applies to other scientific settings involving multimodal observations and heterogeneous background knowledge. Both the dataset and source code are made publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [Deep Learning-based Mechanical Characterization of Synthetically Generated Plant Fibers under Transverse Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16529)
*Nikolaos Kegkeroglou,Bilal Sabillah,Myrto Inglezou,Vincent Placet,Céndric Clevy,Antonios Porichis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用深度学习替代高耗时的有限元以快速预测植物纤维横向受压的应变-应力演化并估计横向杨氏模量，单纤推理<1秒且精度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物纤维作为可持续增强材料很有潜力，但纤维微结构差异大，导致横向力学性能离散，给复合材料设计带来困难。传统依赖有限元模拟逐纤分析，计算昂贵，无法高通量覆盖真实纤维群体。需要一个既快又准的方法表征横向力学行为。

Method: 构建端到端深度学习管线：两个耦合的递归U-Net逐步预测横向压缩下纤维的形变与内部应力场的时序演化；随后一个回归CNN从最终应力场估计横向杨氏模量。训练数据来自1000个由真实大麻纤维横截面（含/不含腔）生成的合成几何体，并以有限元结果作为真值监督。

Result: 模型对时序应力场重建的SSIM为0.9574±0.0074，RMSE为0.0345±0.0079；对横向杨氏模量的估计达到R^2=0.938±0.024，MAE=24.31±1.23 MPa，MAPE=5.25±0.91%。推理时间每根纤维<1秒，对比有限元约5分钟，极大降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 该框架能快速且可靠地表征合成植物纤维的横向力学响应，为天然纤维增强复合材料设计提供高通量工具；相较以往仅在固定或理想几何上预测应力场的方法，其可递归解析完整时序演化，具有更强实用性。

Abstract: Plant fibers are increasingly used as sustainable reinforcements in composites, but the intrinsic variability of their microstructure causes substantial scatter in transverse mechanical behavior, complicating composite design. Characterizing this behavior relies on finite element (FE) simulations requiring several minutes of high-performance computing per fiber, making high-throughput characterization across realistic fiber populations impractical. In this study, we propose a deep learning framework replacing this FE bottleneck with a fast pipeline. It includes two coupled recursive U-Nets predicting the step-by-step evolution of fiber deformation and internal stress fields under transverse compression, followed by a regression CNN estimating the transverse Young's modulus from the final stress field. Unlike prior approaches predicting stress fields for fixed configurations or idealized geometries, our framework recursively resolves the full temporal stress evolution for synthetic fibers derived from real hemp cross-sections, with or without a lumen. Trained on 1,000 synthetic fiber geometries with FE ground truth, it reproduces the stress field sequence with a Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) of $0.9574 \pm 0.0074$ and a Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) of $0.0345 \pm 0.0079$. It estimates transverse Young's modulus with an $R^2$ of $0.938 \pm 0.024$, a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of $24.31 \pm 1.23$ MPa, and a Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of $5.25 \pm 0.91\%$. Inference requires under one second per fiber versus five minutes per FE simulation, drastically reducing computational cost. This offers a fast, reliable tool for mechanical characterization of synthetic plant fibers, with promising applications in designing natural fiber-reinforced composites.

</details>


### [190] [Heuristics are Not Enough: Calibrated and Corrected Caching for Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16545)
*Jiyue Jiang,Zhiyu Huo,Yuhan Chen,Yanruisheng Shao,Gong Zhang,Lei Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignCache提出一种闭环、自适应的特征缓存框架，使视频扩散模型在不训练的前提下更快且质量更稳。通过分阶段预算路由、在线阈值校准与带动量的草稿合成三件套，直接以真实最终误差为反馈，获得更优速度-质量折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散的特征缓存方法多用启发式差异度做决策：指标与真实生成质量不对齐；用单一全局阈值忽略去噪不同阶段的异质性；预算与近似误差间缺乏校验，系统性偏差难以纠正。需要一个能把调度决策与“对最终视频质量的真实影响”直接对齐、并可自我纠错的机制。

Method: 提出AlignCache闭环框架，包含三部分：1) 阶段自适应预算路由：将去噪轨迹划分为结构性不同的阶段，各自使用独立阈值并加入“陈旧度”守卫；2) 闭环阈值校准：在CFG输出层面做反事实误差度量，用EMA反馈在线调整阈值边界；3) 动量增强的草稿合成：以方向-幅值门控的残差外推结合锚点引导的无条件校正，提高缓存复用的稳定性。

Result: 在多种最先进视频生成模型上，AlignCache较现有方法实现更佳的加速-质量折中，即在相同质量下更快，或在相同速度下更高保真和稳定性。

Conclusion: 将缓存调度从静态、风险累积的启发式转为反馈驱动的自校正控制器，可系统性提升训练免调速策略的有效性与鲁棒性；AlignCache证明了闭环误差对齐与阶段化策略对视频扩散加速的普适价值。

Abstract: Feature caching has become a mainstream training-free strategy for accelerating video diffusion models by reusing intermediate outputs across denoising steps. However, existing adaptive methods rely on heuristic difference measures misaligned with actual generation quality, employ a single global threshold that ignores heterogeneous phase dynamics, and never validate their budget estimates against true approximation error, allowing systematic bias to propagate uncorrected. We propose \textbf{AlignCache}, a closed-loop caching framework that directly aligns scheduling decisions with their true influence on the final generated video through three tightly coupled components: (1) phase-adaptive budget routing that partitions the denoising trajectory into structurally distinct phases, each governed by an independent threshold and a staleness-aware guard; (2) closed-loop threshold calibration that exploits counterfactual error measurement at the classifier-free guided output level to adjust decision boundaries online via an EMA feedback loop; and (3) momentum-augmented draft synthesis combining direction-magnitude gated residual extrapolation with anchor-guided unconditional correction. By transforming scheduling from a static risk accumulator into a feedback-driven, self-correcting controller, AlignCache consistently achieves superior acceleration-quality trade-offs across state-of-the-art video generation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/jiangjyjy/AlignCache.

</details>


### [191] [NoduleFlow: Automatic 3D Lung Nodule Detection and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16548)
*Giulia Romoli,Filippo Ruffini,Francesco Di Feola,Valerio Guarrasi,Matteo Tortora,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NoduleFlow：无需先验标注即可在多时相CT序列上自动完成肺结节检测、分类与分割，并通过跨随访扫描的配准传播提升定位鲁棒性；在四个独立数据集上验证其泛化和潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低剂量CT肺癌筛查中，手工标注结节耗时、主观差异大，且跨随访定量细微变化困难。多数深度学习方法仅粗定位、依赖人工/先验标注，或将检测与分割割裂处理，限制临床可用性与效率。

Method: 构建NoduleFlow框架，由四个互联模块组成，直接输入多时相CT体数据，在推理阶段全自动运行：无需先验病灶标注、手动初始化或ROI预选；并探索利用跨扫描配准场在随访间传播候选区域，与传统逐次独立分析对比，以增强定位和时相一致性。

Result: 在四个独立数据集上系统评估，显示该端到端自动流程在结节检测、分类与分割任务上取得良好性能与泛化能力；跨扫描候选传播对定位有益（相较独立逐次分析）。

Conclusion: 自动化、跨时相的一体化结节分析流程可在筛查与诊断场景中提升准确性与效率；利用配准传播信息有助于稳健定位，凸显无监督/少监督临床部署的潜力。

Abstract: Low dose Computed Tomography (CT) is the standard imaging modality for lung cancer screening. On CT scans, lung cancer typically manifests as pulmonary nodules, whose manual identification is time-consuming and prone to observer variability, image quality limitations, and suboptimal reading conditions. Moreover, subtle temporal changes are often difficult to quantify reliably in CT scans from the same patient across follow-up examinations. Deep learning-based approaches for the automatic detection and segmentation of lung nodules have demonstrated improved accuracy and efficiency. However, most existing approaches provide only coarse region-of-interest localisation, rely on prior lesion annotations or human supervision, or treat detection and segmentation as separate tasks. In this work, we present NoduleFlow, a framework for lung nodule detection, classification, and segmentation directly from series of CT volumes. Our proposal consists of four interconnected modules and operates in an automated manner at inference time, without requiring prior lesion annotations, manual initialisation, or region-of-interest pre-selection. We further investigate whether nodule localization benefits from the propagation of candidate regions across follow-up scans through inter-scan registration fields, as opposed to conventional independent per-scan analysis. The generalisability of the framework is systematically evaluated across four independent datasets. The results underscore the potential of automated pipelines for lung nodule analysis in screening and diagnostic settings.

</details>


### [192] [VLA-GAUGE: Systematically Evaluating Generalization and Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16567)
*Jun Xiao,Rui Ye,Dong Cao,Jinyu Gu,Ying Wang,Zhiliang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLA-GAUGE提出统一诊断评测框架，系统衡量VLA在零样本泛化与组合扰动鲁棒性上的真实能力，并给出可操作的改进路径。实证显示多款VLA在该基准下表现低迷，但基于诊断的定向微调能大幅提升零样本与鲁棒性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA在熟悉任务上高成功率可能掩盖了对新任务的泛化不足与对组合扰动的脆弱性。既有评测难以提供细粒度能力诊断、缺乏系统的组合扰动覆盖，也缺少把诊断结果转化为有针对性的模型改进方法。

Method: 提出VLA-GAUGE：以统一的可执行任务表示为核心，构建六大能力套件的成对ID/OOD任务；进行单因子敏感性分析与组合扰动筛查/评测；据诊断结果执行定向数据增强与模型微调。统一协议下评测12个不同规模与动作建模范式的VLA。

Result: 在VLA-GAUGE上，平均零样本得分仅22.3%；SimpleVLA-RL仅8.0%，排名倒数第二。在三种代表性组合扰动下，平均R-Score为9.3。基于诊断的定向微调，使π_{0.5}在选定扰动组合与留出场景上的零样本与鲁棒性分别提升72.4与31.8分，后者超过LIBERO-Plus单因子微调仅10.9分的增益，且所需成功轨迹更少。

Conclusion: 熟悉任务上的高成功率并不代表对未见任务的强零样本泛化或对组合扰动的鲁棒性。VLA-GAUGE提供的诊断信号能有效指导针对性数据与模型改进，从而显著提升VLA的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have achieved high success rates on familiar manipulation tasks, yet it remains unclear whether such scores reflect genuine zero-shot generalization and perturbation robustness. Existing evaluation frameworks provide limited capability-level diagnosis and combinatorial perturbation coverage, often without connecting diagnosis to targeted model refinement. We introduce VLA-GAUGE, a diagnostic framework that unifies both evaluations under a shared executable task representation. It combines (1) paired in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks across six capability suites, (2) single-factor sensitivity analysis with combinatorial perturbation screening and evaluation, and (3) diagnosis-driven refinement through targeted data augmentation and model fine-tuning. We evaluate 12 VLAs spanning different model sizes and action-modeling paradigms under a unified protocol. We find that (1) The mean Zero-Shot Score on VLA-GAUGE is only 22.3%, with SimpleVLA-RL scoring 8.0% and ranking second to last among the evaluated models. (2) Under three representative combinatorial perturbation configurations, the mean R-Score is 9.3. (3) Diagnosis-driven fine-tuning improves $π_{0.5}$'s zero-shot generalization and robustness under a selected perturbation combination on held-out scenarios by 72.4 and 31.8 scoring points, respectively; the latter exceeds the 10.9-point gain from LIBERO-Plus single-factor fine-tuning despite fewer successful training trajectories. These results show that high reported success rates on familiar manipulation tasks do not guarantee strong zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks or robustness to combinatorial perturbations, while diagnostic evaluation provides actionable signals for targeted model improvement.

</details>


### [193] [Dense Endoluminal Mapping and Localisation from Monocular Bronchoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16570)
*Salvatore Esposito,Matias Mattamala,Daniel Rebain,Liang Zhao,Kev Dhaliwal,Mohsen Khadem,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用单目支气管镜RGB视频即可定位镜头并重建气道表面的SLAM系统，以深度网络提供尺度、特征匹配与ICP约束共同优化，实现无外部传感器的可靠导航与重建，在合成与离体数据上优于多项单目基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支气管镜定位常依赖电磁跟踪和术前CT等外部设备，增加成本与复杂度且在临床环境中受限。亟需仅凭镜头视频实现鲁棒的位姿估计与气道重建，以便更广泛、便捷地开展导航与诊断。

Method: 构建单目SLAM框架：1) 使用经百万级拟真气道渲染数据微调的Depth Anything 3作为深度骨干，直接输出具度量尺度的逐像素深度（提供全局尺度）；2) 用DINOv3特征通过自定义交叉注意力匹配器获得在黏膜等弱纹理区域的亚像素对应；3) 将深度与匹配因子连同基于系统自有增量重建的投影ICP稠密约束，共同纳入因子图优化，以表面证据稳定视觉因子在弱可观测方向上的退化；4) 在线重建气道表面并估计镜头位姿。

Result: 在基于患者CT渲染的6段气道上：平均绝对轨迹误差26.1 mm，平均表面F-score 77.5%，表面精度优于5个近期单目SLAM与前馈基线；相比可自由缩放对齐真值的MASt3R-SLAM（22.0 mm ATE）轨迹误差略高；ORB-SLAM3仅完成2/6条气道，而该系统完成全部6条并在2个离体标本上成功重建。

Conclusion: 单目RGB即可在支气管镜场景中实现有尺度定位与高质量表面重建；结合度量深度、强健特征匹配与基于自重建的ICP稠密约束的因子图优化，有效克服弱纹理与尺度不确定性，临床应用前景广阔并优于多项基线，但与可调尺度方法相比轨迹精度仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Bronchoscopy inserts a camera through the airways to diagnose endoluminal disease. Locating the camera along the airway usually requires additional equipment, such as an electromagnetic tracker and a preoperative CT scan. In this paper, we investigate the use of the monocular camera stream to localise the bronchoscope without requiring additional sensing. We introduce a monocular SLAM system that localises the tip of a bronchoscope and reconstructs the airway surface around it from RGB video alone. A Depth Anything 3 backbone, fine-tuned on over a million photorealistic airway renderings, returns metric per-pixel depth and is the system's only source of scale. DINOv3 features routed through a custom cross-attention matcher supply sub-pixel correspondences on mucosa where classical descriptors fail. Both enter a factor graph alongside a dense constraint from projective iterative closest point (ICP) registration against the system's own running reconstruction, so surface evidence stabilises the directions the visual factors leave weakly observable. On six airways rendered from patient CT scans, the system reaches 26.1 mm mean absolute trajectory error and 77.5% mean surface F-score. Its mean surface accuracy exceeds that of five recent monocular SLAM and feed-forward baselines. MASt3R-SLAM, which fits a free scale to the ground truth, has a lower mean trajectory error (22.0 mm). ORB-SLAM3 completes only two of the six airways, whereas our system completes all six and also reconstructs two ex vivo specimens.

</details>


### [194] [CineRecon: Benchmarking Frontier Models for Agentic Cinematic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16577)
*Jialei Wang,Zehan Wang,XiaoYang Wu,Ziang Zhang,Siyu Chen,Jiaer Huang,Yujia Zhang,Tianyu Pang,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineRecon 提出一个用于“从参考视频重建可编辑动态3D场景”的基准，含50段参考视频与360条标注评测剪辑，并用多维一致性判定评测多种表示设置下的重建能力；对12个模型评测显示相机/静态/动态一致性均存在显著失败，尤其主体运动与交互最难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影制作等需要可控视频生成；将视频转为可编辑、可渲染的动态3D场景能显式控制布局、动作与相机。缺乏系统化评测来衡量智能体在不同表示复杂度下重建与重渲染视频事件的能力。

Method: 构建CineRecon基准：包含跨影视、实拍与游戏的50段参考视频与360条带时间标注的评测片段；设定三种重建表示层级（Proxy/简化/Simplified与精细/Detailed），几何与外观要求逐级提升而对空间关系、相机与主体动态的要求保持一致；为评测建立按实体与时间对齐的“检查清单”，并用视听-语言评审（VLM judge）在11个类别下对齐参考帧与渲染帧，分为相机、静态、动态三大一致性维度；用各模型的agent harness执行“构建-动画-渲染-迭代优化”的工具链流程并打分。

Result: 对12个模型的评测显示在三大维度普遍存在失败；即便在最低要求的Proxy设定下，主体的运动与交互仍难以忠实再现；随表示复杂化（Simplified到Detailed），在几何与外观上要求提高，性能变化揭示了模型在细节与结构保持上的局限。

Conclusion: CineRecon为视频到可编辑动态3D场景重建提供了标准化评测协议与数据；现有模型在相机一致性、场景静态结构与尤其主体动态/交互方面仍显不足，提示需要改进运动建模、时序对齐与多实体交互表示，以及能在不同表示层级稳定泛化的重建与渲染策略。

Abstract: Reconstructing reference videos as editable dynamic 3D scenes is an important path toward controllable video generation for filmmaking: these scenes provide modifiable, renderable visual proxies that enable explicit control over scene layout, subject actions, and camera behavior. To evaluate agents' ability to reconstruct editable dynamic 3D scenes from dynamic reference videos, we introduce CineRecon, a benchmark comprising 50 reference videos and 360 annotated evaluation clips. The reference videos span film, real-world footage, and games. Given a complete video, an agent uses tools to construct, animate, render, and refine its reconstruction. We evaluate this task under three representation settings: Proxy, Simplified, and Detailed. These settings increase geometric and appearance requirements while retaining the same requirements for spatial relations, camera behavior, and subject dynamics. This design tests whether agents can reproduce the events in a video when detailed appearance is unnecessary, and how performance changes as representation requirements increase. For evaluation, we construct checklists that associate observable properties and events with specific entities and times. A vision-language judge compares timestamp-aligned reference and rendered frames across 11 categories, grouped into camera, static, and dynamic consistency dimensions. We evaluate 12 models using their corresponding agent harnesses and find failures across all three dimensions. Subject motions and interactions remain difficult to reproduce even under the Proxy setting.

</details>


### [195] [Code-Interactive Visual Reasoning for Financial Candlestick Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16587)
*Chenxi Zhang,Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao,Ziliang Gan,Rongjunchen Zhang,Shanshan Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fin-Thinker：一个能“写代码-执行-取证据-再推理”的K线图理解与推理模型，并配套构建训练数据FinKLine-32K、评测集FinKLineBench，三阶段训练显著提升答案准确性与证据落地性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融K线图问答常仅看最终答案对错，难以确保预测真正基于图表证据；而K线理解需定位视觉元素、跨日期与面板对齐，并结合数值与金融逻辑推理，单纯端到端回答难以可靠与可解释。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-9B基础上构建“代码交互”代理：生成并执行代码以解析图表、提取证据与计算；利用返回观测迭代推理。数据侧构建FinKLine-32K，含经回放校验的代码-观测轨迹（含纠错）。训练采用三阶段：1) 视觉-代码基础微调；2) 金融过程监督（成功推理与纠错续写）；3) 基于执行反馈的强化学习。评测集FinKLineBench覆盖1,200题、138任务类型，分别评估答案正确率、代码执行与证据对齐。

Result: 在FinKLineBench上，Fin-Thinker相对最佳直接回答基线将严格准确率提升13.1个百分点，相对带沙盒访问的同基座模型提升33.2个百分点；分阶段消融显示金融过程监督与执行反馈RL带来连续增益，且在GPT-5.6判定的证据落地准确性上同步提升。

Conclusion: 代码交互式、过程可监督并结合执行反馈的训练范式，能显著提升K线图多步推理的正确性与证据可追溯性；Fin-Thinker与FinKLineBench为金融可视推理提供了强有力的模型与评测基准。

Abstract: Financial candlestick understanding requires locating visual elements, aligning them across trading dates and panels, and combining observations through numerical and financial reasoning. Answer accuracy alone does not reveal whether predictions are supported by chart evidence. We introduce Code-Interactive Financial Candlestick Reasoning and present Fin-Thinker, a model based on Qwen3.5-9B that generates and executes code to acquire evidence and perform computations. The model uses the returned observations to guide subsequent reasoning. To train these capabilities, we construct FinKLine-32K with 32,184 replay-verified code-observation trajectories collected through teacher-guided student exploration. These include successful solutions with and without error recovery. Our three-stage training framework combines foundational visual-code fine-tuning, financial process supervision of successful reasoning and corrective continuations, and execution-feedback reinforcement learning. For evaluation, we introduce FinKLineBench with 1,200 questions across 138 financial task types to assess answer correctness, code execution, and evidence grounding separately. On FinKLineBench, Fin-Thinker improves strict accuracy by 13.1 and 33.2 percentage points over the best-performing direct-answer baseline among those evaluated and its base model with sandbox access, respectively. Training-stage ablations further show that financial process supervision and execution-feedback reinforcement learning yield successive improvements in both answer accuracy and GPT-5.6-judged grounded accuracy.

</details>


### [196] [JtT: Rethinking Point Trajectory Diffusion for Diverse and Rigid Motion from an Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16591)
*Gabrijel Boduljak,Yushi Lan,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JtT：一种在坐标空间直接生成点轨迹的扩散Transformer，可从单张图像预测刚体运动的多样且物理合理的未来轨迹；通过速度扩散、合适时间步调度与查询点RGB输入，显著提升刚性与多样性权衡，并在严格的评测协议下以约5000万参数超越既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单图像预测未来点轨迹的方法（含视频与轨迹生成器）即便扩大模型并用真实数据训练，仍难以在简单合成场景中产生既刚性又多样的物理可行轨迹；评测上亦缺乏对“多样性-物理合理性”权衡的严格审视。

Method: 提出JtT：简洁的坐标空间扩散Transformer，直接解码稠密/任意查询点的轨迹；系统探索点轨迹扩散的参数化与设计，发现“逐帧速度扩散”+合适的时间步调度是唯一能刻画刚性的参数化；在查询点处加入全分辨率RGB以修复边界失败；并采用更严格的、检查点平均的评测协议以避免大模型记忆化导致的偏差。

Result: 在新的严格评测（考虑模型大小、指标与停止时间，并进行checkpoint-averaging）下，JtT在分布性与刚性指标上均优于以往方法，刚性接近真值，同时参数量仅约50M；揭示更大模型在训练中会记忆单一真实未来，刚性提升但多样性下降。

Conclusion: 合适的架构与参数化（速度扩散+时间步调度+查询点RGB）是从单图像预测刚体运动的关键；与严格评测结合，JtT以更小模型实现更佳的刚性-多样性平衡，推进了无需几何先验的未来轨迹生成。

Abstract: We study forecasting rigid-body motion from a single image: generating diverse and physically plausible future trajectories for arbitrary points in a scene, without building rigidity into the architecture or relying on geometric inputs such as depth. Like video generators, recent trajectory generators fail at this task, even in simple synthetic Kubric scenes, despite scaling up model size and training on real data.
  We show how to solve this with the right architecture and parameterization. Our model, JtT, is a simple diffusion transformer that operates directly in coordinate space and decodes arbitrary and dense query patterns, without the trajectory VAEs or fixed grids assumed in prior work. Through a systematic study of the design space of point trajectory diffusion, we find that diffusing per-frame velocities is the only parameterization that captures rigidity, that it must be paired with the right timestep schedule, and that adding full-resolution RGB at the query points resolves the boundary failures of prior methods.
  However, evaluating such 2D models rigorously is itself non-trivial. To our knowledge, we are the first to observe that physical plausibility and diversity do not improve together during training: larger models, as used in prior work, memorize the single ground-truth future per training image, becoming more rigid while covering fewer possible futures.
  Yet prior work has paid little attention to this, so we show how model rankings depend on model size, metric, and stopping time, and adopt a stricter, checkpoint-averaged evaluation protocol. Under this protocol, JtT improves over prior methods on both distributional and rigidity metrics and approaches ground-truth rigidity, with only about 50M parameters.

</details>


### [197] [Detecting Chain-of-Thought Hijacking in Backdoored Multimodal Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16598)
*Zhifang Zhang,Qi Wei,Weitong Chen,Olaf Maennel,Wei Emma Zhang,zuomin ren,Lei Feng,Miao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InertiaGuard：利用视觉信念惯性（VBI）在测试时检测多模态大模型的CoT劫持，训练无关、阈值由少量干净迹校准，跨攻击和架构表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
