Motivation: 现有多模态后门防御多针对直接答复型MLLM，对在推理链内部发生、形似自我纠错的CoT劫持难以发现；仅检查文本链条亦难以区分“诚实”纠错与劫持扭转。

Method: 观察到诚实推理在高熵“灵光乍现”处修正答案，而劫持常以低熵、近确定性地推翻先前结论。提出“信念惯性”（推理片段的累积下一词熵减去其引发的答案信念修正量），并将其限制在图像相关片段得到“视觉信念惯性（VBI）”。在测试时计算VBI，若低于以少量干净轨迹标定的阈值则报警，形成训练无关的检测器InertiaGuard。

Result: 在多种攻击与基准上，InertiaGuard检测优于现有基线，且可泛化到不同模型架构与触发器类型。

Conclusion: VBI能有效区分诚实推理与被劫持的低熵扭转；基于此的InertiaGuard在无需额外训练的情况下实现稳健、可泛化的CoT劫持检测。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) to solve complex visual problems, but also exposes them to CoT hijacking: an adversary implants a backdoor through poisoned fine-tuning so that a visual trigger induces a hijacking turn, redirecting an otherwise faithful reasoning chain to an attacker-chosen answer. However, since the hijack acts within the reasoning chain, existing multimodal backdoor defenses designed for MLLMs that answer directly struggle to detect it. Moreover, inspecting the chain itself does not reliably expose the hijack, since the hijacking turn can resemble honest self-correction in form. In this paper, we find that honest reasoning tends to revise its answer at high-entropy "aha moments", whereas a hijack can overturn a confident answer through a near-deterministic turn. To quantify this contrast, we formalize this phenomenon as belief inertia, the cumulative next-token entropy of a reasoning span minus the answer-belief revision it induces, with markedly lower values for hijacked spans than for honest reasoning spans. Since hijacking tokens are also image-dependent, we further improve detection by restricting the measure to image-dependent spans, yielding visual belief inertia (VBI). Building on this, we propose InertiaGuard, a training-free, test-time detector that flags a trace when its VBI falls below a threshold calibrated on a small set of clean traces. Across various attacks and benchmarks, InertiaGuard achieves higher detection performance than existing baselines, while generalizing to different model architectures and trigger types.

</details>


### [198] [Answer It or Not? Towards Prompt-Adapted Adversarial Images on Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16622)
*Shunyuan Bai,Yuchen Ren,Le Yang,Chenhao Lin,Chao Shen,Zhengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Prompt-Adapted (ProAda)对抗图像：在保护视觉隐私时，区分并拒绝有害提问，同时尽量允许合法提问；通过联合优化图像扰动与可迭代更新的文本提示，显著优于以往“提示无关”的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLM的对抗图像大多一刀切地拒绝所有问题，导致合法使用受阻；而VLM交互以文本提示为核心，忽视提示动态会降低隐私保护的有效性与可用性。需要一种既能拦截有害查询又能保留正当用途的细粒度防护。

Method: 提出ProAda：1) 将任务设为双目标优化，最大化对有害提示的拒答概率、最小化对合法提示的拒答；2) 在优化过程中同时更新图像扰动与文本提示方向，借助一个简单的二分类器动态判断提示是否仍具“有害性”，据此自适应调整优化步；3) 在多数据集与多模型上进行训练/评估，并分析跨模型迁移与抗对抗防御能力。

Result: 在多种视觉隐私数据集上，ProAda对有害提示的拒绝率>90%，对合法提示的通过率>90%，整体优于现有提示无关的VLM对抗方法；同时展现出有竞争力的跨模型迁移性，并在面对反制/缓解策略时更稳健。

Conclusion: 将提示动态纳入优化可在隐私保护与可用性间取得更佳平衡。ProAda为VLM场景提供了更实用的对抗图像防护方案，并具备良好的迁移性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: With the advancement in language modeling, research on adversarial images has recently shifted from image classifiers to vision-language models (VLMs). Despite the new, text-prompt-driven nature of VLMs, we find that existing VLM adversaries have largely overlooked the dynamics of text prompts. In a typical use case, adversarial images can protect visual privacy by rejecting VLMs from answering questions about the image. However, existing protections are prompt-agnostic, i.e., indiscriminately rejecting any question, including those for potential legitimate use of the image. To address this limitation, we propose Prompt-Adapted (ProAda) adversarial images, which are optimized to balance the rejection of harmful prompts and the correct response of legitimate prompts. In particular, to achieve a more accurate adversarial direction, the text prompt is also iteratively updated with a simple classifier judging whether the harmfulness of the prompt remains. Extensive experiments on visual-privacy-related datasets validate that ProAda can reject over 90% of harmful prompts while allowing over 90% of legitimate ones, demonstrating superior overall performance compared to existing prompt-agnostic VLM adversaries. In addition, our ProAda achieves competitive overall cross-model transferability and improved robustness against countermeasures.

</details>


### [199] [Adaptive Interaction Network for Human Motion Prediction During Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16624)
*Mengyuan Liu,Yangting Lin,Qiongjie Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向协作场景的“机器人感知”人体运动预测：双分支网络联合建模人-机轨迹，通过局部-全局交互与自适应融合，并采用粗到细预测，在三数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往人体运动预测多仅考虑人/物体，忽略协作机器人对人的影响；人机异质性与复杂空间交互难以建模，需要能显式刻画机器人对人运动的影响、提高安全与效率。

Method: 提出AINet：主分支预测未来人体运动，辅分支估计机器人轨迹；两者经LGSI模块联结，捕捉细粒度与全局人机依赖；引入AWA模块按上下文自适应加权融合运动特征；采用粗到细策略，先生成粗伪轨迹，再基于其细化人体姿态。端到端联合优化。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的数据集上实现SOTA或显著提升（摘要未给出具体数值），验证了模型在人人机协作场景中的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模人机交互、结合局部-全局依赖与自适应特征融合、并采用粗到细预测，可显著提升机器人感知的人体运动预测性能；方法稳健、适用于多场景。

Abstract: Human motion prediction during human-robot collaboration is critical for achieving safe and efficient interactions in shared environments. Unlike previous studies that have primarily focused on humans and objects, we focus on the robot-aware human motion prediction task, which explicitly models the influence of robots on human motion. This task presents new challenges, such as capturing human-robot heterogeneity and modeling complex spatial interactions. To address these issues, we develop an adaptive interaction network (AINet) model that consists of two branches: a primary branch that predicts future human motion and an auxiliary branch that estimates robot trajectories. The two branches are jointly optimized and coupled via a Local-Global Spatial Interaction (LGSI) Module, which effectively captures fine-grained and global contextual dependencies between human and robot motion sequences. Then, we introduce an Adaptive Weighted Aggregation (AWA) Module to dynamically fuse motion features using context-dependent weights, thereby increasing adaptability across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we adopt a coarse-to-fine prediction strategy, in which a coarse pseudo-trajectory is first predicted, followed by the refinement of detailed human poses based on that trajectory. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets reveal that our proposed method achieves superior performance, validating its effectiveness in human-robot collaboration scenarios. Our code is available at: https://github.com/LyTingHub/AINet.

</details>


### [200] [REEL: Learning to Retrieve, Evaluate, and Evolve Visual Memory for Any Long-Horizon Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16632)
*Hang Xu,Guohui Zhang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Hu Yu,Jie Huang,Jiayin Xiao,Lin Song,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REEL：一个通用视觉记忆规划器，通过统一工具接口在多镜头长视频生成中检索/合成证据并更新持久记忆，用适配器对接不同黑盒视频生成器；三阶段训练（SFT→单镜头GRPO→多镜头GRPO）并在多个基准上显著提升跨镜头一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多镜头长视频生成将记忆与特定生成器耦合或依赖固定代理流程，缺乏可独立优化、能跨镜头目标的一般记忆策略，导致身份保持与状态变更控制不足。

Method: 将系统分为规划器（REEL）、适配器与生成器。REEL通过统一工具接口执行三类动作：检索参考、补全缺失视觉证据、更新持久记忆；适配器把证据计划映射为各后端支持的条件输入。训练数据集：REEL-SFT-4k与REEL-RL-1k。训练流程三阶段：1) 监督微调；2) 单镜头GRPO；3) 多镜头GRPO（在连续镜头间传递策略生成的记忆，以后验结果反向影响早期检索与记忆更新）。评测基准：REEL-Bench（含文本请求与定时上传参考图），以及ST-Bench与Core-100。

Result: 在JoyAI-Echo 1.5与MiniMax-H3后端，REEL相较无记忆基线将ST-Bench跨镜头一致性分别提升15.1%与20.7%；在REEL-Bench的Core-100上分别提升2.41与8.32分。

Conclusion: 一个与生成后端解耦、可泛化的视觉记忆规划器能显著提升长视频多镜头的一致性与受控性；多镜头GRPO训练与统一工具接口使同一策略跨黑盒生成器有效工作。

Abstract: Memory-based long-video generation aims to combine user control with long-range visual consistency. We organize this process into three functional components: a planner, an adapter, and a generator. Representative multi-shot systems couple memory to generator-specific interfaces or use predefined agent workflows, rather than independently training a memory policy for cross-shot objectives. We introduce REEL, a general-purpose visual-memory planner. Through a unified tool interface, REEL learns to retrieve references, create missing visual evidence, and update persistent memory. Backend-specific adapters translate its evidence plans into supported conditioning inputs, allowing the same policy to guide different black-box video generators. To train REEL, we construct two high-quality datasets, REEL-SFT-4k and REEL-RL-1k, covering diverse interaction patterns and prompt types. Training proceeds in three stages: supervised fine-tuning, single-shot Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and multi-shot GRPO. Multi-shot GRPO carries policy-generated memory across consecutive shots, so later outcomes guide earlier reference selection and memory updates. REEL-Bench tests identity preservation and intended state changes under text-only requests and timed reference-image uploads. On JoyAI-Echo~1.5 and MiniMax-H3, REEL improves cross-shot consistency on ST-Bench by 15.1\% and 20.7\%, and Core-100 on our proposed REEL-Bench by 2.41 and 8.32 points, respectively, over the no-memory baseline.

</details>


### [201] [Revisiting Natural Language Prompts from Large Language Models for Open-Vocabulary Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16666)
*Haichen He,Weibin Liu,Weiwei Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P2CLIP，通过类别、视频与帧三级提示设计与对齐增强，实现开放词汇视频识别的零/小样本性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在视频数据上微调CLIP因文本信号稀缺易过拟合；现有用LLM生成提示但缺乏对高质量提示如何帮助未见类、以及如何基于提示进一步增强视文对齐的系统探索。

Method: 1) 类别层：设计“通用-判别”提示（Generic-Discriminative Prompt），用LLM在保持通用语义的同时加入区分性要素，得到更判别的类别嵌入。2) 帧/视频层：用VLM与LLM生成帧与视频描述，丰富文本语义用于对齐。3) 提出知识引导的时序聚合（Knowledge-Guided Temporal Aggregation），以LLM抽取的专家知识指导视频的时序建模，优化联合视-文特征空间。

Result: 在多项基准上的零样本与小样本设置均显著优于对比方法，验证P2CLIP的有效性。

Conclusion: 高质量、层级化的提示设计与LLM知识引导的时序聚合可显著提升开放词汇视频识别的泛化与对齐能力；P2CLIP在多基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) like CLIP have shown great success in image tasks, however, applying CLIP to open-vocabulary video recognition poses challenges. Due to the limited text information, directly fine-tuning CLIP on video recognition datasets may lead to overfitting and hinder its generalizability. A prevalent method is to enrich text information by generating prompts using Large Language Models (LLMs). However, limited efforts have been made to explore how to generate higher-quality prompts that can contribute to classify unseen categories, and how to further enhance vision-text alignment based on these prompts. In this paper, we propose P2CLIP (Prompt-guided CLIP), which aims to bridge this gap by exploring the impact of prompts across three aspects: category, video, and frame. At the category level, we argue that LLM-generated prompts under simple instructions may suffer from semantic overgeneralization, which can blur the category boundaries and hinder the performance of the model. To address this issue, we design Generic-Discriminative Prompt using LLMs to obtain discriminative category embeddings with rich semantic information. At the video and frame levels, we first use VLM and LLM to generate frame and video descriptions for enhancing vision-text alignment. Then we propose a Knowledge-Guided Temporal Aggregation mechanism, which leverages the expert knowledge derived from LLMs to guide the temporal modeling of videos and encourage a better construction of the joint visual-textual feature space. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, including zero-shot and few-shot settings, demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [202] [EvoSpatial: Experience-Driven Skill and Tool Evolution for Spatial Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16679)
*Bin Yu,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Xiaopeng Lin,Yao Zhang,Haishan Liu,Haotian Li,Yuliang Wei,Kai Hu,Yichao Du,Zhirui Zhang,Bailing Wang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoSpatial是一个以经验驱动的视觉-语言空间推理框架，通过演化式地积累与链接技能与工具，既学会如何计算也学会何时信任结果，从而在20个基准上取得平均最优表现，超过SpatialClaw 4个百分点，并展现跨基准迁移与技能引导蒸馏带来的推理提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强的视觉-语言代理能感知与测量空间关系，但缺乏对几何假设的持续跟踪与中间证据校验；更难的是将这种过程性知识与其所支配的计算持久化并在新任务中复用。作者动机是建立一种机制，能把成功与失败的推理轨迹转化为可复用的技能与计算函数，支持经验累积与迁移。

Method: 提出EvoSpatial框架：1）在推理时检索已有技能、复用工具，并按需合成可复用的计算函数（generated helpers）；2）在提交答案后利用有监督的结果反馈对技能进行细化、对工具进行整合，形成“演化”的技能-工具库；3）在过程中显式记录并检查几何假设与中间证据，以决定何时信任计算结果；4）支持跨任务检索与复用，实现经验在推理时与训练时（基于轨迹）双重利用。

Result: 在20个空间推理基准上获得最高平均分，较SpatialClaw提升4.0个百分点；额外实验显示可进行跨基准迁移，并通过无需外部技能的技能引导蒸馏进一步提升推理质量。

Conclusion: 将空间推理中的过程性知识与计算结果共同累积、结构化并复用，可显著提升视觉-语言空间代理的性能与泛化；成功与失败轨迹皆可用于进化技能与工具，实现推理时复用与基于轨迹的训练双提升。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language agents compose perception and measurement to reason about spatial relationships, but effective execution also requires tracking geometric assumptions and checking intermediate evidence. Preserving this experience across tasks requires retaining procedural knowledge together with the computations it governs. We introduce EvoSpatial, an experience-driven framework that evolves a library of linked skills and tools. Our key insight is that successful and failed spatial trajectories reveal both how to compute and when to trust the result. EvoSpatial retrieves skills, reuses tools, and synthesizes reusable computational functions (generated helpers) during reasoning. After answer submission, supervised outcome feedback guides skill refinement and tool consolidation for subsequent tasks. Across 20 benchmarks, EvoSpatial attains the highest average score among the compared spatial agents, exceeding SpatialClaw by 4.0 percentage points. Further experiments show cross-benchmark transfer and improved reasoning through skill-guided distillation without external skills at evaluation. These results support accumulating spatial experience for both inference-time reuse and trajectory-based training.

</details>


### [203] [3D-VITAL: Visibility-aware Identity Training from Artificial Liftings of 3D Reconstruction model for Cross-View Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16692)
*William Grolleau,Astrid Sabourin,Guillaume Lapouge,Catherine Achard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D‑VITAL，用单视角重建成纹理网格并多视角渲染以提升单视角航拍‑地面跨视角ReID，通过可见性一致性过滤和可见性引导注意监督，过滤坏重建并聚焦可靠区域，刷新三大基准SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角（尤其航拍与地面）外观差异巨大，单视角训练到另一视角推理泛化差；用生成新视角可缓解，但单视角3D重建与渲染会带来几何失败与不可见区域纹理幻觉，需判别并利用可靠信息。

Method: 先将源图像单视角重建为纹理网格并渲染多新视角；利用渲染可得的逐像素可见性图。两阶段：1）Cross-View Consistency Filtering（CVCF）：在源/新视角的可见补丁上做跨视一致性检验，剔除几何错误网格；2）Visibility-Aware Attention Supervision（VAAS）：用可见性图在训练中引导注意力聚焦可靠（可见）区域，降低对幻觉纹理的依赖。整体框架称为3D‑VITAL。

Result: 在三个单视角航拍‑地面ReID基准上取得新的SOTA表现（具体指标未给出），表明利用可见性感知的过滤与监督有效提升跨视泛化。

Conclusion: 可见性信息是连接单视角3D重建与跨视ReID的关键。通过过滤错误几何并在训练中强调可见区域，3D‑VITAL稳健地利用合成视角信号，显著提升SV AG‑ReID性能；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: We address the Cross-View Object Re-Identification (ReID) task, aiming to match identities across drastically different viewpoints. We focus on Single-View Aerial-Ground ReID (SV AG-ReID), where models train on one viewpoint and must generalize to another at inference. To bridge this viewpoint gap, related work employs generative models to synthesize novel views from source images as additional training data. We leverage recent advances in large-scale single-view 3D mesh reconstruction to lift source images into textured meshes and to render them from new viewpoints. However, this pipeline produces unreliable data in two distinct ways. First, the reconstruction may fail and produce geometrically incorrect meshes, resulting in severe corruption in rendered novel views. Second, even when the geometry is correct, the rendered novel views contain hallucinated textures in regions not visible in the source image. The reconstructed mesh carries a per-pixel partition between regions visible from the source view and regions that the reconstruction model had to hallucinate, available as a deterministic byproduct of rendering. We introduce this per-pixel visibility map and exploit it in a two-stage design. Cross-View Consistency Filtering (CVCF) discards geometrically incorrect meshes by ensuring multi-view consistency between source and novel views over visible patches. Visibility-Aware Attention Supervision (VAAS) leverages the visibility map to guide the model's attention toward reliable regions of the rendered view during training. Combined, these two components enable our method, 3D-VITAL, to better leverage the synthetic signal from single-view 3D mesh reconstruction. 3D-VITAL sets a new state-of-the-art on three SV AG-ReID benchmarks. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/TurtleSmoke/3D-VITAL.

</details>


### [204] [PathPilot: An Adaptive Question-Conditioned Multi-Magnification Navigation Agent for Whole-Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16734)
*Zhengyang Xu,Han Li,Jingsong Liu,Ayako Ura,Shihui Zu,Shaohua Kevin Zhou,Nassir Navab,Peter Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PathPilot：一个多倍率WSI导航智能体，显式建模跨倍率定位与循证自适应探索，按病理医师工作流跨区域与倍率顺序整合证据，在五个队列的QA任务中优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI导航多在单一或预设倍率下运行，难以模拟病理医师在不同放大倍数间动态切换并顺序整合全局-细胞级证据的过程，导致跨倍率交互与自适应选择缺失。

Method: 设计PathPilot包含两大组件：1) CrossMagLoc：基于病理医生视野行为学习各倍率的诊断性定位，并融合相邻倍率信息，在当前ROI内选出最具诊断相关性的区域，作为后续问句条件探索的起点；2) EviRegionSearch：从该起点出发进行问句条件的循证搜索，逐步累积相关证据，并依据剩余证据需求自适应决定下一步导航动作。两者迭代交互，跨ROI与倍率顺序探索，直至证据充足。

Result: 在五个内部/外部队列的问答评测中均显著优于各类SOTA基线，展现了稳健的跨区域、跨倍率问句条件证据获取能力。

Conclusion: PathPilot以临床一致的多倍率导航策略实现跨倍率交互与自适应探索，能够像病理医师般顺序、知识驱动地整合证据，显著提升WSI诊断型QA性能。

Abstract: The diagnostic capability of WSI agents relies on effective slide navigation to identify diagnostically relevant regions and efficiently integrate evidence across the slide. While in clinical practice, pathologists dynamically examine slides across multiple scales, integrating global and cellular evidence in a sequential manner, existing AI navigation approaches mostly operate at a single fixed magnification or rely on predefined magnification traversal. This mismatch prevents existing methods from modeling cross-magnification interactions and adaptive magnification selection inherent to real diagnostic workflows. We propose PathPilot, a clinically consistent multi-magnification WSI navigation agent that explicitly models cross-magnification localization and evidence-adaptive exploration. Specifically, we introduce a CrossMagLoc that learns magnification-specific diagnostic localization from pathologist viewing behavior and integrates information from adjacent magnifications to identify the diagnostically most relevant region within the current ROI, thereby providing a diagnostically informed starting point for subsequent question-conditioned exploration. We further introduce an EviRegionSearch that starts question-conditioned exploration from this region, progressively accumulating question-relevant evidence and adaptively determining the next navigation action according to the remaining evidence requirement. Their iterative interaction enables PathPilot to sequentially explore across ROIs and magnifications until sufficient evidence is acquired. Extensive QA evaluations across five cohorts demonstrate consistent superiority over competing SOTA methods on inter/external cohorts, respectively, validating question-conditioned evidence acquisition across regions and magnifications that mirrors the sequential, knowledge-driven navigation of pathologists.

</details>


### [205] [VAttnPO: Visual Attention-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16735)
*Lihao Zheng,Zhenwei Shao,Zhou Yu,Yan Yang,Haoyi Sun,Qian Wang,Ning Mao,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VAttnPO，用答案条件的视觉注意引导，将序列级优势分解为位置感知的token级校正，显著提升多模态RL训练中对视觉证据利用的可验证性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证奖励的多模态RL多将同一序列优势分配给整段回复，无法显式刻画各token在使用视觉证据上的差异，造成信用分配粗糙、训练信号与模型实际视觉对齐不足。

Method: 在保持验证器给出的轨迹均值优势（GRPO样式）的同时，引入答案条件的视觉注意对齐信号：用冻结旧策略在“已知正确答案”下teacher forcing获得每个位置的视觉注意分布，和在线rollout在该位置的视觉注意分布计算Jensen–Shannon散度，配合轨迹居中、相对位置加权与信用平衡，将其转化为零均值、位置感知的token级优势修正，不依赖教师的token分布或显式注意匹配损失。并通过token干预、视觉索引置换、轨迹分析等诊断验证信号与行为、视觉对应及生成位置的关联。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，6任务图像AVE由70.97（GRPO）提升至73.50，且在结构化、视频与MME各指标均超越GRPO；在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct与InternVL3-8B-Instruct上同样获得一致增益。

Conclusion: VAttnPO以视觉注意一致性为导向细化token级信用分配，既保留可验证奖励的全局优势，又改进视觉证据利用与行为对齐，跨多模型与基准稳定提升性能。

Abstract: Multimodal reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards commonly assigns one sequence advantage to an entire response. This outcome-level signal distinguishes overall response quality but leaves changes in visual-evidence use across generated tokens implicit. We introduce VAttnPO (Visual Attention-Guided Policy Optimization), which uses answer-conditioned visual attention to refine token-level credit while preserving the verifier-derived trajectory-mean advantage. A frozen old policy constructs a visual reference by teacher forcing the known correct answer and produces a visual distribution for each rollout token. VAttnPO measures their agreement in visual-attention space using Jensen--Shannon divergence, rather than reading guidance from teacher output-token distributions or introducing an attention-matching objective. Trajectory centering, relative-position weighting, and credit balancing turn this agreement into a zero-mean, position-aware correction to the GRPO sequence advantage. Token intervention, visual-index permutation, and trajectory analysis provide diagnostic evidence linking this signal to model behavior, visual correspondence, and generation position. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, VAttnPO raises six-task image AVE from 70.97 with GRPO to 73.50 and scores higher on every reported structured, video, and MME metric. Gains over GRPO also hold on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and InternVL3-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [206] [FoveaTree: Information-Density Trees for Query-Guided Visual Token Pruning in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16744)
*Zihan Meng,Weidong Chen,Dexiang Hong,Xinyan Liu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoveaTree 是一个无需训练的两阶段视频视觉token裁剪框架，通过区域压缩可行性评估与层内注意力重整，在仅保留10%–50%视觉token时，仍在多项视频问答与视频字幕基准上取得SOTA的准确-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频LLM对每帧编码数百个视觉token，推理代价高。现有相似度合并或注意力选择方法要么不考虑空间层级结构、易丢关键信息（层级无感剪枝），要么受位置与注意力陷阱影响导致跨模态注意偏移（注意力漂移）。需要一种既能区域感知地压缩，又能稳住文本到视觉的相关注意的方案。

Method: 提出FoveaTree，两阶段、免训练：1）Merge-Gain密度估计：评估区域合并引起的特征失真，建立由四叉树派生的密度图，指导token的丢弃、合并与保留，体现“哪里可压缩、哪里必须保留”。2）Attention Tree Pruning：利用存活单元及继承的树拓扑，在LLM中间层重整文本-视觉注意，保留与文本相关的区域证据，进一步去除低相关内容。

Result: 在InternVL3.5-8B与Qwen3-VL-8B上，于五个视频问答与一个字幕基准，在50%、30%、10%视觉token保留率下均实现一致的SOTA整体表现，展现优异的准确-效率折中。

Conclusion: FoveaTree通过区域可压缩性建模与层内注意重整，缓解层级无感剪枝与注意力漂移难题，在大幅降低视觉token的同时保持甚至提升下游精度，适合作为通用、免训练的高效视频LLM前端。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video LLMs) encode each frame into hundreds of visual tokens, making video inference expensive. Existing methods reduce this cost through similarity-based merging or attention-based selection but face two challenges: Hierarchy-blind Pruning, which overlooks regional compressibility and can discard critical evidence, and Attention Drift, where positional effects and attention sinks shift text-to-vision attention away from task relevance. We propose FoveaTree, a two-stage, training-free visual-token pruning framework. First, Merge-Gain Density Estimation measures the feature distortion caused by regional merging and constructs a quadtree-derived density map to guide token dropping, merging, and retention. Second, Attention Tree Pruning uses the surviving units and inherited tree topology to refine text-to-vision attention in intermediate LLM layers, preserving relevant regional evidence while removing low-relevance content. We evaluate FoveaTree on InternVL3.5-8B and Qwen3-VL-8B across five video question-answering benchmarks and one captioning benchmark at 50%, 30%, and 10% visual-token retention. FoveaTree consistently achieves overall state-of-the-art performance across the evaluated settings, offering a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [207] [The Midas Touch for Metric Depth: Interpretable Metricization for Generalizable Depth Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16749)
*Mengtan Zhang,Yu Ma,Shiqi Jiang,Yi Feng,Wei Ye,Xieyuanli Chen,Hanli Wang,Qijun Chen,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MTDv2提出一种混合框架，将显式的相对深度度量化与数据驱动的细化结合，以从单目相对深度与稀疏度量观测中稳健恢复全局一致的度量深度，提升在多数据源与下游3D任务中的泛化与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目相对深度模型虽具强泛化，但存在尺度歧义与局部度量不一致，导致难以直接用于需要精确度量深度的感知任务。现有方法要么用全局仿射拟合（难以处理空间变尺度），要么用深度补全网络（对稀疏观测分布敏感，且未充分利用可泛化的相对深度先验）。

Method: 提出MTDv2：1）显式度量化阶段：进行分割级（segment-wise）仿射变换以恢复空间变化的度量尺度、保持相对深度几何；再做像素级细化，将度量观测沿累计几何变化最小的路径传播。2）数据驱动细化阶段：将单路径传播拓展为可学习的多路径传播，在保持可解释性的同时提升鲁棒性与精度。

Result: 在16个公共数据集上进行大量实验，MTDv2在不同稀疏观测源与多种深度估计范式下，均表现出稳健、可泛化的尺度恢复能力，并在多种下游3D感知任务中展现广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 结合显式度量化与可学习多路径细化可有效解决相对到度量深度的尺度与一致性问题，实现跨数据与任务的稳健度量深度恢复；方法具可解释性与强泛化，代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent foundation models have drastically improved the generalization of monocular relative depth estimation, yet recovering accurate metric depth from their estimates for depth perception tasks remains challenging due to scale ambiguity and local metric-scale inconsistency. Existing approaches recover metric depth from sparse observations using either explicit global affine transformation algorithms or implicit data-driven depth completion networks. The former cannot accommodate spatially varying relative-to-metric relationships, while the latter are sensitive to the distribution of sparse observations and may not fully exploit generalizable relative depth priors. To simultaneously address these issues, this study extends our previous work MTD and proposes MTDv2, a hybrid metric depth perception framework that combines explicit depth metricization with data-driven metric depth refinement. The explicit depth metricization process performs segment-wise affine transformations to recover spatially varying metric scales while preserving the geometric structure of relative depth, followed by pixel-wise depth refinement that propagates metric observations along paths with minimal accumulated geometric variation. The data-driven depth refinement process subsequently extends the explicit single-path depth propagation to a learnable multi-path formulation while preserving its interpretability. Extensive experiments conducted on 16 public datasets demonstrate the robust and generalizable depth scale recovery capability of MTDv2 across diverse sources of sparse observations and different depth estimation paradigms, as well as its broad applicability to downstream 3D perception tasks. Code will be publicly available at https://mias.group/MTD upon publication.

</details>


### [208] [Image Tokenizers Should Be Trained Between Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16759)
*Ole Beißwenger,Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Hendrik Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterpTok 通过在代码空间对训练样本进行“混合插值”监督，强化图像解码器对生成代码分布的适配，从而显著改善插值质量与扩散生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型（如潜空间扩散）输出的是图像分词器的代码，但生成器产生的代码往往偏离训练分布，解码器对这类“分布外”代码不稳，产生瑕疵。现有做法以加噪增强鲁棒，但生成代码更像训练码的加权混合而非“码+噪声”。因此需要直接监督“训练样本之间”的代码空间。

Method: 提出 InterpTok：在 MAETok 基础上微调解码器，以两张图的代码线性混合为输入；用对抗判别器评估解码结果是否与两源图一样自然；引入“混合比损失”约束输出保留输入代码的混合比例，避免坍塌并得到清晰、自然的双源组合。

Result: 在保持 MAETok 重建质量的同时，插值质量提升超三倍，优于近期基准中的所有分词器；匹配的扩散 Transformer 的无引导 FID 从 11.61 提升至 8.48；在采样步数减至 1/8 时仍达 8.11，而基线退化；且在训练步骤上达到与 MAETok 相同最终效果所需步骤减半。

Conclusion: 图像代码之间的空间值得被显式监督。通过对“代码插值”进行对抗+混合比约束训练，可显著增强解码器对生成代码的适配性，提升插值与生成质量与效率。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models generate images as codes of an image tokenizer, whose decoder turns them into the pixel image. Recent tokenizers improve how well these codes reconstruct an image and how easily a generator learns them. Yet a generator does not reproduce the codes of training images, and when a generated code lands outside what the decoder expects, the decoded image contains imperfections. Current methods make the decoder more robust to generated codes by adding noise during training. However, generated codes lie between the training codes and resemble their mixture more than one of them plus noise. To improve generated images, decoders should therefore be trained on codes in the space between training samples. We propose InterpTok, which fine-tunes MAETok to decode mixtures of image codes into natural images. Since no target image exists between two training samples, a discriminator judges whether each decoded mixture looks as natural as its two sources. A mixing ratio loss requires the decoded mixture to keep the mixing ratio of the two codes. Together, they prevent collapse to a trivial solution and yield a sharp and natural composition of both images. InterpTok keeps MAETok's reconstruction quality and improves interpolation quality more than threefold, the best of all tokenizers in a recent benchmark. Beyond interpolation, InterpTok improves the generation FID of a matched diffusion transformer from 11.61 to 8.48 without guidance, and even reaches 8.11 at an eighth of the sampling steps, where the generator on MAETok's codes worsens. The generator trained on our tokenizer also reaches the final result of the one trained on MAETok at half the training steps. Our findings show that the space between image codes can and should be explicitly supervised rather than ignored.

</details>


### [209] [Towards Complementary Keypoint Detection via Mixture of Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16762)
*Xiaoyong Lu,Guobao Xiao,Dong Liang,Songlin Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoD，通过蒸馏多种关键点检测器并以轻量路由器按patch自适应选择检测器，在单次前向中生成多样检测图并以匹配质量作为奖励训练路由器，跨预算一致优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一关键点检测器在不同场景结构与关键点预算下表现互补，单一选择导致关键点分布次优，影响稠密匹配与几何估计性能。

Method: 1) 异构检测器蒸馏：将多种现有检测器的检测图统一蒸馏到一个共享模型，单次前向即可并行预测多种“检测头”输出；2) 路由选择：将图像划分为patch，给定预算，使用轻量级router为每个局部区域选择最合适的检测器头；3) 训练信号：不依赖启发式监督，使用下游匹配质量（兼顾关键点数量与精度）的任务驱动奖励来训练路由器；4) 端到端集成于稠密匹配管线并以多数据集进行训练/验证。

Result: 在MegaDepth、ScanNet、HPatches上，MoD在广泛的关键点预算范围内稳定优于强基线，带来更优的关键点空间分布与更好的下游几何性能。

Conclusion: 通过将多检测器能力统一并用任务驱动的patch级路由自适应选择，MoD能在不同预算与场景下提供更匹配友好的关键点，提升整体几何视觉管线表现。

Abstract: Keypoint detection plays a central role in geometric vision pipelines. Existing detectors exhibit complementary strengths across different scene structures and keypoint budgets, and reliance on a single detector often results in suboptimal keypoint distributions. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Mixture of Detectors (MoD)} for dense matching pipelines, a unified framework for complementary keypoint detection through the adaptive composition of heterogeneous detectors. MoD first distills heterogeneous detectors into a unified model, allowing diverse detection maps to be predicted efficiently within a single forward pass. Detector selection is subsequently formulated as a patch-wise, budget-conditioned decision process, in which a lightweight router assigns the most suitable detector to each local region. To avoid heuristic supervision, the router is trained with task-driven rewards derived from downstream matching quality, jointly capturing both keypoint quantity and precision. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, ScanNet, and HPatches demonstrate that MoD consistently outperforms strong baselines across a wide range of keypoint budgets, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving keypoint distribution and downstream geometric performance.

</details>


### [210] [InfoVis: Unified Slide Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16784)
*Jiahao Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Chenyuan Wu,Haoqiang Zhang,Xin Luo,Yueze Wang,Defu Lian,Bo Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并评测一个统一的9B参数幻灯片生成与编辑模型InfoVis，基于大规模幻灯片原生数据与新基准VisSlideBench，在所有11项指标上领先开源模型，接近最强闭源表现，并公开模型、代码、数据与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态/文生图/编辑模型难以在同一画布上同时做到准确文本排版、遵循指令、视觉对齐、美学设计与可控编辑，且缺乏大规模“幻灯片原生”监督和系统化诊断评测。

Method: 1) 定义四个以幻灯片为中心的任务；2) 构建可扩展数据引擎：约1300万连续预训练记录+190万监督微调记录；3) 提出VisSlideBench：1527条经人工筛选、覆盖四任务、多维度、带自动评价且与盲评高度一致；4) 以FLUX.2-klein-base-9B为底座进行幻灯片特化后训练，得到统一的9B模型InfoVis；5) 评测并与外部SlidesGen-Bench对接工作流。

Result: 在VisSlideBench上，InfoVis在全部11项指标上领先所有开源模型，综合分7.94，接近闭源Nano-Banana-2的7.99；针对底座模型的幻灯片特化训练带来+1.6综合分，生成任务在“内容忠实度”提升最大；与闭源模型差距最小的是内容忠实度、最大的是美学质量；基于InfoVis的文档转PPT流程在SlidesGen-Bench上获得第二高美学分。

Conclusion: 面向幻灯片的统一生成与编辑通过大规模“幻灯片原生”数据与专用基准显著提升能力，InfoVis在开源阵营中全面领先并逼近闭源水平，剩余提升空间主要在美学质量；作者承诺开放模型、代码、数据与基准，促进该方向标准化与可复现研究。

Abstract: Generating and editing presentation slides requires models to combine accurate text rendering, instruction following, visual grounding, aesthetic design, and precise editing within one canvas. Existing work covers only subsets of these capabilities and lacks large-scale slide-native supervision and diagnostic evaluation. We formulate four slide-centric tasks and build a scalable data engine with over 13M continual-pretraining records and 1.9M supervised fine-tuning records. We also introduce VisSlideBench, a benchmark of 1,527 manually screened instances that covers all four tasks with task-specific, multidimensional evaluation. Its automatic rankings agree with blinded, dimension-wise human rankings. Using these resources, we train InfoVis, a unified 9B slide generation and editing model. On VisSlideBench, InfoVis leads every evaluated open-source model on all eleven metrics and scores 7.94 overall against 7.99 for Nano-Banana-2. Starting from FLUX.2-klein-base-9B, slide-specific post-training adds 1.6 points overall, with the largest generation gains in content faithfulness; the remaining gap to proprietary models is smallest in content faithfulness and largest in aesthetic quality. Built on InfoVis, our document-to-slide workflow achieves the second-highest Aesthetics score on the external SlidesGen-Bench. We will release the model, code, data, and benchmark.

</details>


### [211] [ViewStruct: View-Grounded Articulation and Connectivity Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16793)
*Wenjing Bian,Ranran Huang,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViewStruct提出一个从单/多视RGB+深度与分割中联合预测物体连接与关节的框架，使用视图对齐表示与重力对齐坐标，适配预训练视觉-语言模型，无需观察到运动或逐实例优化，在合成数据训练下对真实和未见类别泛化，并可用于机器人操作轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与陌生物体交互需要理解零部件如何组合与运动。现有方法常依赖类别先验、标准化朝向、观察到的关节运动，或昂贵的逐实例优化，泛化与效率受限。作者希望建立一个能从静态视图中、以与视图对齐的方式统一推断部件刚体分组、连接与关节参数，并能快速泛化到新类别与真实场景。

Method: 提出ViewStruct：输入为RGB图像+对齐的部件分割与深度（可选多视图/视频）。输出包括刚体分组、部件附件关系、关节类型与参数。核心是视图落地的结构表示：将预测显式绑定到观测区域，并在重力对齐、视图相对坐标中表示关节几何，避免依赖类别特定的规范姿态。通过适配预训练视觉-语言模型以数秒内输出结构，无需观测到的关节运动或每实例优化。

Result: 在基准上较现有方法取得更好的结构预测；在未见类别、不同资源库资产与真实人机交互视频上保持迁移性，尽管只用合成数据进行任务特定训练；仿真操作实验显示预测的关节足以支持机器人运动轨迹生成。

Conclusion: ViewStruct能从静态或有限视角输入中高效、泛化地联合推断物体结构与关节，减少对类别先验与动态观测的依赖，并在机器人操作中展现实用性。

Abstract: Interacting with unfamiliar objects requires understanding how their parts connect and move. We introduce ViewStruct, a framework for jointly predicting connectivity and articulation in objects and scenes. Given an RGB image with aligned part segmentation and depth, optionally supplemented by images or video, ViewStruct predicts rigid-body groupings, attachments, and joint parameters. Its view-grounded representation links predictions to observed regions and expresses joint geometry in a gravity-aligned, view-relative frame, without requiring category-specific canonical orientations. A pretrained vision--language model is adapted to predict structure in seconds, without requiring observed articulation motion or per-instance optimisation. Experiments demonstrate improved structural prediction over evaluated baselines and transfer to unseen categories, asset sources, and real-world human-interaction videos, despite task-specific training exclusively on synthetic data. Simulated manipulation experiments further demonstrate the utility of predicted joints for generating robot motion trajectories.

</details>


### [212] [X-Ragent: Tool-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Chest X-Ray Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16811)
*Yi Wang,Zhenlong Yuan,Yuchan Guo,Maolei Huang,Zijian Song,Dapeng Zhang,Rongbin Tan,Jiyuan Wang,Zhiyuan Wang,Fangfang Lin,Shuhan Song,Huawei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出X-Ragent：一种针对胸片推理的工具增强强化学习后训练框架，通过选择性寻证与有效性门控的量化测量获取，提升证据扎根的诊断表现，在三项基准上最优，ChestAgentBench上提升8.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸片解读常需将视觉征象与任务相关的外部证据结合，但工具增强的智能体缺乏两点：何时需要辅助证据的学习机制，以及如何阻止不适用的量化证据干扰诊断推理。

Method: 提出X-Ragent框架，解耦两类能力：1) 学会“何时寻求辅助证据”；2) 通过“有效性门控”来获取量化测量。构建Rad-CoT（7.7K样本），包含因果排序的工具轨迹与报告式推理，用于对Qwen3-VL-8B进行代理式SFT初始化；随后用GRPO进行策略优化，设计基于准确性门控的奖励：仅对给出正确诊断的轨迹增加过程级奖励（测量一致性与工具使用成本），避免用看似合理的过程掩盖错误诊断。

Result: 在三项胸片基准上取得最强结果；在完全隔离的ChestAgentBench上提高8.6分。模型能更有选择地寻证并仅接纳有效测量，提升推理的忠实性与可证据追溯性。

Conclusion: 通过将寻证策略学习与测量有效性门控相结合，并采用准确性门控的过程奖励，X-Ragent实现更可靠、成本敏感、证据扎根的CXR诊断推理，并在多基准上达到SOTA。

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation often requires integrating visual findings with task-specific evidence rather than relying on direct visual recognition alone. While tool-augmented medical agents can supply such evidence via external modules, they often lack explicit mechanisms for learning when auxiliary evidence is needed and for preventing inapplicable quantitative evidence from entering diagnostic reasoning. To address this, we propose X-Ragent, a post-training framework for tool-augmented reinforcement learning that decouples learned auxiliary-evidence seeking from validity-gated quantitative measurement acquisition for evidence-grounded CXR reasoning. We construct Rad-CoT, a 7.7K-sample corpus combining causally ordered tool-augmented trajectories with report-style diagnostic reasoning, and use it to initialize Qwen3-VL-8B via agentic supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We then optimize the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using an accuracy-gated reward that applies process-level terms for measurement consistency and auxiliary-tool cost only to correct trajectories, preventing plausible process behavior from compensating for an incorrect diagnosis. Across three CXR benchmarks, X-Ragent achieves the strongest results, including an 8.6-point improvement on the fully held-out ChestAgentBench. By seeking evidence selectively and admitting only valid measurements, X-Ragent supports more faithful and evidence-grounded CXR reasoning.

</details>


### [213] [Carve VFM: Carving Specialists out of Generalists, Automatic Task-Specific Compression of Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16813)
*Lotfi Abdelkrim Mecharbat,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Ibrahim Almakky,Martin Takáč,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Carve VFM提出一种无需重新训练主干、在几分钟内通过结构化剪枝从VFM中“雕刻”出致密小模型的方法，利用跨层容量分配搜索、恢复感知的适应性评分，以及迭代闭式低秩校正来弥补被移除部分，实现高精度与高吞吐的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调不减小模型、蒸馏成本高、传统结构化剪枝在高压缩下失败，因其将“子网络选择”和“能力恢复”割裂处理，无法兼顾跨层任务敏感度差异与层内可恢复性。需要一种联合决定选择与恢复、并能快速得到针对任务的紧凑模型的方法。

Method: 给定VFM、校准集和参数预算：(i) 搜索跨层的任务特定容量分配；(ii) 以自适应、考虑可恢复性的适配度准则引导搜索；(iii) 对被移除行为进行迭代的闭式低秩校正，并将校正并入存活权重，从而形成致密编码器。全过程在单GPU上数分钟完成，无需回骨干训练。

Result: 在12个基准与DINOv3、MetaCLIP-2的4个模型上，Carve VFM在8种主干-预算配置中的7种实现了被评估的致密结构化剪枝方法中的最高平均准确率；进一步微调后超越最强边缘模型（88.1%对85.8%）；同时减少计算与内存，并较未压缩模型获得1.3-2.0倍GPU吞吐。

Conclusion: VFM内部蕴含可直接提取的紧凑子网络；通过联合进行跨层容量分配与层内可恢复性驱动的选择与校正，可在极短时间内雕刻出兼具精度与效率的小模型，适于资源受限部署。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) provide transferable visual representations, but their inference cost limits deployment in many settings. Fine-tuning specializes a VFM without shrinking it; distillation shrinks it, but through expensive teacher-guided training. Structured pruning offers a more direct route by extracting a compact subnetwork from the VFM. However, as compression gets aggressive, existing methods fail because they treat subnetwork selection and recovery as separate axes. Across layers, capacity must follow task sensitivity rather than spread uniformly. Within a layer, a removal cannot be judged by importance alone; its impact also depends on how well the lost contribution can be recovered from what remains. Selection and recovery must therefore be decided jointly. We introduce Carve VFM, which resolves this interdependence. Given a VFM, a calibration set, and a parameter budget, it (i) searches for task-specific capacity allocations across layers; (ii) guides the search via an adaptive recovery-aware fitness criterion; and (iii) reconstructs removed behavior through iterative closed-form low-rank corrections merged into the surviving weights. The result is a dense encoder produced in minutes on a single GPU without backbone retraining. Across twelve benchmarks and four models from DINOv3 and MetaCLIP-2, Carve VFM achieves the highest reported mean accuracy in seven of eight backbone-budget configurations among the evaluated dense structured-pruning methods. Further fine-tuning surpasses the strongest evaluated edge model (88.1% vs. 85.8%), and carved models cut compute and memory while delivering 1.3-2.0x higher GPU throughput than the uncompressed one. These results show that the compact model you need is inside the VFM; it only has to be carved out.

</details>


### [214] [Growing to See: Efficient Pretraining of Self-Supervised Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16817)
*Amir Mohammad Karimi Mamaghan,Ferdinand Kapl,Andrea Dittadi,Karl Henrik Johansson,Kaitlin Maile,Johannes von Oswald,Stefan Bauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究在自监督视觉Transformer中采用“逐步加深（progressive depth growth）”训练策略：从浅层模型开始，训练过程中逐步插入新层。结果表明，在MAE与DINOv3两大框架中，该策略在保持或降低训练FLOPs（降13.9%–24.8%）的同时，提升下游任务表现（ImageNet线性探针最高+3.36个百分点），并重塑注意力的计算模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型领域的逐步加深训练近期被证明可提升训练效率与推理质量，并改变内部计算模式。然而其在自监督视觉Transformer中的表征质量与计算模式影响尚不清楚，特别是对主流方法（MAE、DINOv3）的系统性理解与量化证据缺失。

Method: 在MAE与DINOv3预训练中采用“从浅到深”的逐步加深方案：训练开始用浅层网络，随后在训练过程中插入新层；系统评估对下游“全局”（如分类）与“稠密”（如分割/检测）任务的影响，分析中间层表征质量与注意力模式变化（全局vs局部、层间演化）。

Result: 相较固定深度训练，逐步加深在相同或更少训练计算量下带来一致收益：训练FLOPs降低13.9–24.8%；ImageNet线性探针准确率最高提升3.36个百分点；中间层的下游表现同步提升；注意力计算模式被重塑——在MAE中呈现全局与局部注意力的周期性交替，在DINOv3中更早出现从局部到全局的进程。

Conclusion: 逐步加深是高效且有效的自监督预训练策略：以更少计算获得更强下游性能，并加深了对视觉Transformer内部计算如何随网络生长而变化的理解。

Abstract: Progressive depth growth builds large networks by starting with shallow models and inserting layers during training. Recent advances in large language models have renewed interest in this longstanding approach, demonstrating benefits for training efficiency and reasoning alongside changes in internal computations. However, how growing affects representation quality and computational patterns in self-supervised vision transformers remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate progressive depth growth in two popular self-supervised families, namely masked autoencoders (MAE) and DINOv3, and analyze how growing affects downstream performance, intermediate representations, and internal computations. Across global and dense tasks, we show that growing improves downstream performance while reducing training FLOPs by 13.9--24.8%, with ImageNet linear-probe accuracy gains of up to 3.36 percentage points. Growing also improves downstream performance at intermediate layers and reshapes internal computational patterns, introducing recurring alternations between global and localized attention in MAE and earlier local-to-global attention progression in self-distilled DINOv3. Together, these findings establish progressive depth growth as an effective and efficient pretraining strategy for improving downstream performance across global and dense visual tasks with less computation, and advance our understanding of how growing shapes visual computation.

</details>


### [215] [A Multi-Task Deep Learning Framework for Pose-Invariant Face Recognition and Facial Landmark Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16849)
*Ali Ashkeyev,Zukhra Abdiakhmetova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个多任务深度学习框架，在统一架构下同时进行跨姿态人脸识别与五点人脸关键点定位；在改造的CPLFW多任务数据集上验证，识别准确率81.58%，关键点定位MSE为96.38。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大姿态变化会导致人脸外观与可见判别特征变化，单一识别模型易退化；希望通过联合学习身份与结构信息，获得对姿态鲁棒的特征表示。

Method: 将CPLFW扩展为带身份标签与五点关键点标注的多任务数据集；搭建共享输入、双分支网络：ResNet50负责身份识别，EfficientNetV2B0+空间注意力负责关键点定位；采用加权多任务损失联合优化，促进语义与空间表征的互补学习。

Result: 在构建的数据集上验证：身份识别验证集准确率81.58%；关键点回归MSE为96.38，表明联合优化有助于跨姿态鲁棒表征。

Conclusion: 多任务学习与注意力机制可在非受限场景下有效提升跨姿态人脸分析的鲁棒性；联合身份分类与关键点回归是一条可行的统一优化途径。

Abstract: Face recognition systems often experience performance degradation under large pose variations due to changes in facial appearance and visibility of discriminative features. This study presents a multi-task deep learning framework that jointly addresses pose-invariant face recognition and facial landmark localization within a unified architecture.
  To enable simultaneous optimization of identity recognition and facial structure learning, the original Cross-Pose Labeled Faces in the Wild (CPLFW) dataset was adapted into a multi-task learning dataset by associating facial images with identity labels and five-point landmark annotations. The proposed framework consists of two task-specific branches operating on a common facial input: a ResNet50-based branch for identity recognition and an EfficientNetV2B0-based branch with spatial attention for facial landmark localization. The two objectives are jointly optimized using a weighted multi-task loss, allowing the model to learn complementary semantic and spatial facial representations.
  The proposed framework was evaluated on the prepared CPLFW-based multi-task dataset. Unlike the original CPLFW benchmark, which focuses primarily on identity recognition, the proposed dataset adaptation incorporates five-point facial landmark annotations to enable joint recognition and localization learning. Experimental results demonstrate that the model achieves a validation recognition accuracy of 81.58% and a facial landmark localization mean squared error of 96.38. The results indicate that combining identity classification and landmark regression within a single optimization framework provides an effective approach for learning robust facial representations under cross-pose conditions
  The proposed method demonstrates the potential of multi-task learning and attention-based feature refinement for unconstrained facial analysis.

</details>


### [216] [Towards Cost-Efficient Video Dataset Construction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16857)
*Mohammed Shabot,Jonathan Adams,Jan Van den Stock,Joost Vennekens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出使用大型语言模型基于视频元数据进行预筛选，减少视频下载与标注成本，并与关键词启发式和人类标注进行系统比较与成本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频分类模型兴起导致对大规模训练数据集需求剧增，但现有数据集构建需先下载候选视频再判断相关性，带来巨大的存储、时间和标注开销，亟需一种在下载前进行可靠筛选的方法。

Method: 利用LLM读取视频元数据（标题、描述、标签等）进行相关性预判，作为下载前过滤步骤；以四名独立人工标注的多数投票为黄金标准，并用Gwet’s AC1评估标注一致性；将LLM与两种无LLM关键词启发式基线比较；通过留一评审者法(LOO)与加入一评审者法(AORI)评估LLM作为“虚拟标注者”的表现；并进行成本对比；开源实现以确保可复现性。

Result: LLM基于元数据的预筛选在与严格的人类多数票基准对比下优于或至少可比于关键词启发式，并在LOO/AORI框架下展示出作为虚拟标注者的竞争力；显著降低了下载与人工标注相关的存储、时间与经济成本。

Conclusion: 在数据集构建流程中引入LLM元数据预筛选可有效减少资源消耗，同时保持或提升筛选质量；该方案具有可复现性并优于简单关键词基线，值得在大规模视频数据收集管线中采用。

Abstract: Research on video classification models has witnessed significant growth recently, accompanied by a significant increase in the demand for datasets to train such models. Existing video dataset curation approaches share a common bottleneck: candidate videos must be downloaded before any relevance judgment is made, which results in storage, time and annotation costs. In this work, we present a filtering step, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to pre-screen videos based on their metadata, before actually downloading these videos, thereby saving on these costs. We evaluate the LLMs against a strict human majority vote derived from four independent annotators and validate the reliability of the annotations through inter-annotator agreement analysis (Gwet's AC1). We compare the LLM pre-filter against two no-LLM baselines, namely two keyword heuristics. Via a Leave-One-Rater-Out (LOO) mechanism and an Add-One-Rater-In (AORI), we evaluate the performance of the LLMs as virtual annotators. Finally, we report cost comparisons relative to existing approaches. To ensure reproducability, we made our implementation available on github.

</details>


### [217] [Vision Models Capture Complementary Representations in Children That Converge in Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16860)
*Domenic Bersch,Timothy Schaumlöffel,Michela Proietti,Antonia Franaszek-Traczewska,Hannah Elisabeth Zwad,Siying Xie,Marlena Baldauf,Teresa Sylvester,Christina Maria Schätz,Bert Turtleton,Moritz Köster,Stefanie Höhl,Radoslaw Cichy,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究使用时间分辨表征相似性分析（RSA）对8岁、12岁与成人EEG进行比较，发现CLIP这类图文对齐模型在儿童期已与大脑表征显著对齐，而纯视觉模型的对齐度在更晚发展阶段更强；两类模型捕获互补结构，且CLIP在同架构的纯视觉对照之上仍保留额外对齐；对齐主要由语义（类别/有生性）驱动，低层视觉特征对齐在各年龄段都较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不清楚图文对齐模型（如CLIP）与纯视觉模型（如仅图像训练的CNN/ViT）在人类发展不同阶段与大脑表征的关系如何变化；理解这一点有助于把AI模型作为“可比较的大脑模型”，以及为可随时间学习和适应的AI提供发展性约束。

Method: 对8岁、12岁与成人参与者采集EEG，采用时间分辨RSA，将EEG随时间的表征与两类模型（图文对齐与纯视觉）的表征距离矩阵进行对齐评估；进行残差分析以分离重叠与互补成分；分解对齐来源，比较语义（类别、有生性）与低层图像特征的贡献。

Result: CLIP在儿童期已与大脑表征显著对齐；纯视觉模型的对齐在更晚发展阶段增强；残差分析显示两类模型具有互补结构，且从8岁起CLIP相对于同架构的纯视觉模型仍保持额外对齐；总体对齐主要由语义结构驱动（类别/有生性表征随年龄增强），而对低层图像属性的对齐在各年龄段均较弱。

Conclusion: 图文对齐与纯视觉模型分别捕获发育中视觉表征的互补方面；CLIP型模型在早期就与大脑语义结构对齐，纯视觉模型的优势随发展增加。两类模型可为面向终身学习与适应的AI系统提供发展性约束与启示。

Abstract: How the relationship between image-text aligned and vision-only models changes across development remains unknown. We applied time-resolved representational similarity analysis to EEG data from 8-year-olds, 12-year-olds, and adults. CLIP showed significant model-brain alignment already in children, whereas vision-only models aligned more strongly later in development. Residual analyses revealed complementary structure, with CLIP retaining alignment beyond an architecture-matched vision-only model from age 8. Much of the model-brain alignment reflected semantic structure, with EEG representations of category and animacy strengthening toward adulthood, while alignment with low-level image properties remained weak across age. Together, these findings suggest that image-text aligned and vision-only models capture complementary aspects of developing visual representations and may provide useful constraints for AI systems that aim to learn and adapt over time.

</details>


### [218] [From Volpiano to Pixels: 30,000 Lines of OMR Ground Truth for Medieval Chant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16862)
*Robert Klugseder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建并公开了基于Cantus Database的中世纪圣咏OMR大规模配对数据集与生成管线，并验证其显著提升跨谱式识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪圣咏的OMR长期缺少将手稿图像与音高/符号对应起来的跨谱式训练数据，导致模型泛化差、不同记谱家族之间适配困难。作者希望利用现有的Volpiano转写资源，把文本音高信息与图像自动对齐，解决数据匮乏与标注成本高的问题。

Method: - 数据构建：基于Cantus Database的Volpiano转写，开发自动化管线生成30,567条图像-目标序列对（含339段经文碎片），总计1,952,143个token，来自10个来源、11种导出。
- 版面与记谱解析：定位五线谱/谱系、推断谱号并将已知音高映射到谱位；针对不同版式与记谱法设计6类检测器。
- 学习型补强：在启发式失败处，训练两个小型U-Net从人工标注学习谱线与谱号字母。
- 质量评估：抽样审查导出行，记录每行0–4个音高或谱号错误（定义不统一，非严格审计）。
- 识别模型：从零训练CRNN；对比此前主要基于15–16世纪奥地利来源训练的模型。进行留出手稿实验（排除单一手稿训练后在其上评测）。并使用模型辅助对齐再回收2,686行。

Result: - 数据集：30,567行、约195万token，覆盖多种来源与记谱。
- 识别性能：新CRNN在3,086条测试行上符号错误率9.6%，显著优于旧模型的45.3%。
- 泛化：三次留出手稿实验中，被排除手稿的错误率为21.5–31.0%，显示跨手稿仍具挑战但较以往显著改善。
- 额外回收：模型辅助对齐新增2,686行。

Conclusion: 基于Volpiano的自动配对与多检测器/小型U-Net相结合的管线，成功构建大规模跨谱式OMR训练资源，并显著降低中世纪圣咏识别错误率。尽管抽样评审非统一、跨手稿泛化仍待提升，但该资源与方法为后续更全面的OMR研究与基准评测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Optical music recognition (OMR) for medieval chant lacks training data linking manuscript images to musical symbols across notational families. Using existing Volpiano transcriptions from the Cantus Database, we created 30,567 image-target pairs from ten sources in eleven exports, including 339 verse fragments. Their target sequences contain 1,952,143 tokens. The pipeline locates staff systems, infers clefs and maps known pitches to staff positions. Six detector families handle the different layouts and notations. Where heuristics failed, two small U-Nets learned staff lines and clef letters from manual annotations. Reviews of sampled export lines found zero to four pitch or clef errors, but used different error definitions and do not provide a uniform audit of the corpus. A CRNN trained from scratch achieved a symbol error rate of 9.6 % on 3,086 test lines. The earlier model, trained mainly on Austrian sources of the 15th and 16th centuries, scored 45.3 %. In three separate training runs, each excluding one manuscript, error rates on the excluded manuscript were 21.5-31.0 %. Model-assisted alignment recovered a further 2,686 lines.

</details>


### [219] [EviStress: Evidence Stress Learning for Generalizable Face Forgery Detection with Textual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16879)
*Jiuyao Jing,Xuanyu Yi,Yu Zheng,Chunlei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviStress：在CLIP+LoRA基础上，通过“证据加压训练+多模态指令微调”实现更强泛化的人脸伪造检测与可解释输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web上的人脸伪造来源多样、管线不断演进，传播中还会经历各种压缩/裁剪/噪声等处理；现有检测器在未见操控与视觉扰动下易失效。作者观察到LoRA适配的CLIP检测器在决策上过度依赖少数视觉patch，导致对这些证据的变化非常敏感，需提升对证据变化的鲁棒与泛化。

Method: 两阶段框架EviStress：1) 训练期证据加压——Evidence-Targeted Patch Perturbation (ETPP) 估计各patch的证据依赖度（融合全局、关系与语义线索），按依赖度分配扰动概率并进行正相关扰动，显式“压力测试”高度依赖的视觉证据；并用Discriminative Variation Preservation (DVP) 约束干净与扰动表征的残差，保持真实性判别信息不丢失。2) 推理期可解释多模态——Intrinsic Evidence Instruction Tuning (IEIT)：将已适配的CLIP编码器作为LLaVA的视觉塔，进行参数高效的多模态指令微调，使模型可直接基于伪造敏感表征输出真伪预测与文本解释。

Result: 在跨数据集、跨伪造方法与常见腐蚀（压缩、噪声等）条件下均取得更好的泛化与鲁棒性；多模态模型可同时给出真实性判断与人类可读解释。

Conclusion: 通过针对“高度依赖证据”的有目标扰动训练与判别信息保持约束，配合多模态指令微调，EviStress显著提升人脸伪造检测的跨域泛化和可解释性，并能在真实Web分布转移与退化下保持稳健表现。

Abstract: The rapid spread of face forgeries on the Web challenges the credibility of visual content. In practical Web environments, manipulated media originate from evolving generation pipelines and undergo diverse processing during dissemination, making reliable authenticity assessment under unseen manipulations and visual changes a critical challenge. We observe that a standard LoRA-adapted CLIP detector exhibits uneven patch-level decision dependence, concen trating a disproportionate share of class-specific relevance on a small fraction of visual patches. To improve robustness to variations in such highly dependent evidence, we propose EviStress, a two-stage framework for generalizable face forgery detection with textual explanations. In the first stage, Evidence-Targeted Patch Perturbation (ETPP) estimates patch dependence from global, relational, and semantic cues and assigns positively correlated perturbation probabilities, explicitly stress-testing highly dependent visual evidence during training. We further introduce Discriminative Variation Preservation (DVP), which constrains the residual between clean and perturbed representations to retain authenticity discriminative information. In the second stage, Intrinsic Evidence Instruction Tuning (IEIT) transfers the adapted CLIP encoder into LLaVA as its vision tower and performs parameter-efficient multimodal instruction tuning, enabling authenticity prediction and textual explanation directly from the learned forgery-sensitive representation without an external detector. Experiments demonstrate improved cross-dataset and cross-method generalization and corruption robustness, with the multimodal model providing authenticity predictions and human-readable explanations.

</details>


### [220] [Barycentric Guidance: Turning Foundation Image Editors into Continuous Affective Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16892)
*Harvey Mannering,Zhiwu Huang,Adam Prügel-Bennett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“重心引导”（Barycentric Guidance），把现成的大型图像编辑器转化为在价—唤醒（VA）空间上可连续、准确可控的情感编辑器，无需训练或微调，显著提升VA命中率、覆盖度与多样性，并能泛化到人脸以外的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VA的图像情感编辑多依赖任务/域特定训练，只能在见过的VA区域内工作，跨数据集脆弱；而通用的基础图像编辑器虽能高质编辑，却因文本编码器的非线性情感表示导致提示词插值不稳定，难以实现连续、精确的VA控制。需要一种既训练自由、又能在VA平面上连续可靠控制情感的通用方法。

Method: 提出训练自由的“重心引导”框架：将目标VA点映射为单纯形上的加权系数（barycentric weights），用作对预训练基础编辑器的引导信号，从而在不改动模型、无额外数据/微调的前提下实现VA控制。该映射使编辑在三方面可控：精确命中目标VA、覆盖更广VA区域、并保持连续可调。

Result: 在三个有人脸数据集上，相比最强基线：VA目标准确度提升25–29%；VA平面覆盖提升28–50%；表情多样性提升29–49%。方法还能泛化至动物、艺术作品与复杂场景等非人脸图像。

Conclusion: 重心引导把通用基础编辑器变为连续的情感控制器，实现训练自由、跨域泛化与高质量编辑，显著提升VA命中、覆盖与多样性，统一解决了精确、广覆盖与连续控制三大难题。

Abstract: Emotions are expressed on a continuum and are naturally described in the valence-arousal (VA) space. Existing VA-based image editors are typically training-based and domain-specific, limiting them to VA regions seen during training and making them brittle across datasets and domains. Meanwhile, despite producing high-quality edits, state-of-the-art foundation image editors are not continuous affective controllers: non-linear emotion representations in text encoders makes prompt interpolation poorly calibrated, so accurate and continuous VA control remains unreliable. To fill this gap, we introduce Barycentric Guidance, a training-free framework that turns pretrained foundation image editors into continuous affective controllers over VA, requiring no finetuning, extra data, or model changes. Our method maps any target VA point to simplex-based guidance weights, enabling VA controllability in three aspects: (i) accurate targeting of VA states, (ii) broad coverage across the VA plane, and (iii) continuous control. Across three face datasets, we improve VA target accuracy by 25-29%, expand VA-plane coverage by 28-50%, and increase expression diversity by 29-49%, over the strongest baseline. Unlike prior domain-specific methods, our training-free approach exploits the foundation model's inherent versatility, enabling generalization beyond human faces to animals, artworks, and complex scenes within a single framework. Code is available at https://github.com/harveymannering/BarycentricGuidance.

</details>


### [221] [Mining Cross-Modal Perception Gaps in Autonomous Driving: When Camera and LiDAR Disagree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16906)
*Hongsen Cao,Mona Jaber,Shanxin Yuan,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过大规模统计与因果式归因分析，揭示多模态3D检测中“相机与激光雷达互补且按距离分工”的常见假设并不成立，并将挖掘到的空间依赖结构编码为可审计的“模态可靠性图”，以轻量注意力注入现有融合检测器，带来可观增益与可解释性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态3D检测依赖端到端学习出的融合权重，缺乏可解释与可审计性；社区默认的距离分工与加性失效假设可能并不真实，影响安全部署。需要一种能在空间上分层刻画各模态可靠性的分析与利用方法。

Method: 1) 构建跨 nuScenes、KITTI、Waymo 的约40万目标级样本库，并按“由哪些模态检测到”进行标注；2) 提出空间分层特征归因（SSFA），以距离带为条件对Shapley归因，避免全局平均掩盖结构；3) 挖掘模态优势与失败驱动的空间结构（类别、距离、方向、点云密度等交互）；4) 将统计得到的模态可靠性编成“模态可靠性图”（MRM），通过轻量注意力层注入四个预训练融合检测器，少量参数微调。

Result: 发现三条与主流假设相悖的结构性规律：a) 模态偏好边界随目标类别走，交通锥被相机单独看到的概率（16.8%）近为卡车（2.9%）6倍，且与距离无关；b) 相机-only 检测在10–30 m 达峰，超出传统认为的LiDAR主导带；c) 失效驱动非加性，距离×点密度交互的影响超过视角主效应；并且模态主导方向上非平稳，车后更偏LiDAR。将MRM注入四个检测器后，mAP提升最高达+1.3，微调参数<4%，2–3 GPU小时完成。

Conclusion: 多模态融合的可靠性应从黑盒权重中解放为可审计的显式量。通过SSFA揭示的空间与类别依赖结构，并以MRM指导融合，可在低开销下提升性能与安全可解释性，为实际部署提供审计依据。

Abstract: Multi-modal fusion for 3D object detection assumes that cameras and LiDAR are broadly complementary. In practice, how much each modality is trusted at a given location is decided by learned weights that are neither characterized nor auditable. We label every ground-truth object by which modalities detected it, then mine the resulting 0.4M-object database across nuScenes, KITTI, and Waymo. Because the patterns shift with distance from the ego vehicle, we read the mined model through Spatially Stratified Feature Attribution (SSFA), which conditions Shapley attribution on distance zones and exposes structure that global attribution averages away. Three assumptions built into the current fusion design do not hold. The modality-preference boundary tracks the object itself: a traffic cone is nearly six times as likely as a truck to be seen by the camera alone (16.8% vs. 2.9%), regardless of range. Camera-only detections peak at 10-30 m, inside the band usually ceded to LiDAR, and fall away on either side. The drivers of failure are not additive either: the distance - density interaction alone outweighs the entire main effect of viewing direction. We further find that modality dominance is directionally non-stationary, with objects behind the vehicle being markedly more LiDAR-dominant than those in front. We compile the mined structure into a Modality Reliability Map and inject it into four pretrained fusion detectors through a lightweight attention layer. This improves performance by up to 1.3 mean Average Precision (mAP) while fine-tuning under 4% of their parameters in 2-3 GPU-hours. Our work lifts reliability out of the weights and treats it as a quantity that can be examined before deployment. That is what a safety measure for multi-modal perception must rest on.

</details>


### [222] [ConDenseDrive: Knowledge-Dense, Token-Sparse Visual Encoder for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16908)
*Shengzhen Li,Yongkang Li,Hao Gao,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConDenseDrive提出一种面向端到端自动驾驶的“知识密集、token稀疏”的视觉编码器，通过多源知识蒸馏与时空上下文集成，再以寄存器引导的token压缩，实现更快、更准的规划；在NAVSIM与HUGSIM上达SOTA并有2×推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶视觉编码器多从通用图像预训练初始化，且对多相机多帧的所有patch密集计算，导致表示能力不足与推理效率低，难以在严格时延下兼顾感知与规划质量。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 知识密集化：从三类视觉基础模型蒸馏互补的视觉、语义、几何知识到编码器；2) 适配多帧端到端驾驶：将时空上下文注入到“寄存器tokens”，使其服务于下游规划；3) token稀疏化：利用寄存器引导的token压缩，筛除冗余patch，减少计算。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1获得94.5 PDMS，v2获得90.8 EPDMS；在HUGSIM零样本评测达37.4 HD-Score；在保持或提升规划性能的同时，实现约2.0×速度提升。

Conclusion: 优化视觉编码器（通过多源知识蒸馏与token稀疏化）是实现可靠且高效端到端驾驶的有效途径；所提ConDenseDrive在精度与延迟间取得更优权衡，并具备较强泛化能力。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires rich spatiotemporal representations to support scene understanding and trajectory planning under strict latency constraints. However, most systems initialize their visual encoders from generic image pretraining and densely process all patch tokens for each camera view and input frame, limiting both representation quality and inference efficiency. To address these challenges, we present ConDenseDrive, a knowledge-dense and token-sparse visual encoder for end-to-end autonomous driving. Our three-stage framework first makes the encoder knowledge-dense by distilling complementary visual, semantic, and geometric knowledge from three vision foundation models. It then adapts the distilled encoder to multi-frame end-to-end driving and integrates spatiotemporal context into register tokens for planning. Finally, it makes the encoder token-sparse by applying register-guided token compression, reducing redundant patch computation and yielding a 2.0$\times$ speedup while improving planning performance. ConDenseDrive achieves 94.5 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.8 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, and reaches 37.4 HD-Score in zero-shot evaluation on HUGSIM. Extensive experiments and qualitative analyses demonstrate that visual encoder design provides an effective route to reliable and efficient end-to-end driving.

</details>


### [223] [FOCUS: A Subspecialty Foundation Model for Intraoperative Central Nervous System Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16914)
*Xing Liu,Yifan Guo,Hongtai Lv,Lei Lou,Yuyi Tan,Jiang Du,Xijia Zhang,Ming Zhang,Chao Pang,Minglei Li,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Zhu,Song Zhang,Rong Yu,Hanlin Shu,Wenzhuo Xu,Tao Jiang,Cheng Lu,Jie Tian,Qing Chang,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOCUS 是一个针对中枢神经系统（CNS）术中冰冻切片的专科级基础模型，预训练于27,930张富含CNS的冰冻切片，在多项内部诊断与生物标志物预测任务上取得领先（平均AUC分别为0.969与0.877），在时间外与外部队列中仍保持优势，并显著提升初级神经病理医师在分层诊断与淋巴瘤检出中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中冰冻切片对CNS病变的手术决策至关重要，但受制于冰冻伪影、诊断复杂度与时间压力。通用病理基础模型对CNS冰冻样本见得少，难以在这一关键场景提供稳定、可操作的支持，亟需一个面向CNS冰冻切片的专科大模型以提升诊断准确性、粒度与时效性。

Method: 构建并预训练FOCUS模型，训练集包含27,930张富含CNS组织的冰冻切片；在11个诊断任务与12个生物标志物预测任务上评估；将10个诊断任务组织为与WHO一致的分层诊断体系；单独设置淋巴瘤检出任务以匹配术中关键决策；进行时间外与外部队列验证；开展6位神经病理科医师的读片者研究，评估模型辅助对不同资历读者的影响。

Result: FOCUS在内部11项诊断任务与12项生物标志物预测任务中取得领先，平均AUC分别为0.969与0.877；在时间外与外部队列中保持领先。分层诊断评估中，模型辅助使初级读者达到或超过未辅助的高级读者水平；在淋巴瘤检出中，初级读者敏感度由81.6%提升至94.7%。

Conclusion: 专科化、以CNS冰冻切片为核心的FOCUS能提升术中病理的准确性与诊断粒度，提供与WHO一致的分层诊断支持，并为淋巴瘤相关的手术切除决策与分子层面信息提供即时、可操作的参考，展现出在术中CNS病理中的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Intraoperative frozen-section diagnosis of central nervous system (CNS) lesions guides surgical decisions but is challenged by artefacts, diagnostic complexity and time constraints. Most pathology foundation models have limited exposure to CNS frozen tissue. Here we present FOCUS, a subspecialty foundation model pretrained on 27,930 frozen-sections enriched for CNS tissue. FOCUS led 11 internal diagnostic and 12 biomarker-prediction tasks, with mean AUCs of 0.969 and 0.877, respectively, retaining leading performance in temporal and external cohorts. To provide more comprehensive diagnostic information, we further organized ten diagnostic tasks into a WHO-aligned diagnostic hierarchy and separately evaluated FOCUS for lymphoma detection, a critical task for intraoperative surgical decision-making. In reader assessments by six neuropathologists, FOCUS assistance enabled junior readers to approach or exceed unassisted senior readers in hierarchical diagnosis and raised junior readers' lymphoma sensitivity from 81.6% to 94.7%. FOCUS could improve diagnostic accuracy and granularity, inform lymphoma-related resection decisions and provide provisional molecular information. Such capabilities highlight the potential of FOCUS to provide robust, clinically actionable support for intraoperative CNS pathology.

</details>


### [224] [UniMetric: Universal Metric Scale Recovery for Visual Geometry Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16945)
*Lei Yang,Yuner Zhang,Ziyang Liu,Ziying Song,Kai WU,Yiyang Gao,Jiangtao Su,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniMetric：一种无需训练的通用度量尺度恢复框架，通过从VLM获取对象尺寸先验并与几何重建对齐，利用凸优化在序列级统一确定尺度；在新基准MetricBench上显著降低多种GFM的深度、重建与轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型推动具身推理与规划，但现有视觉几何基础模型缺乏可靠的度量尺度，导致用网络视频进行具身预训练困难；传统重建常仅得相对尺度而非公制尺度。作者希望在无额外传感器和任务特定监督下，为任意GFM自动恢复一致的公制尺度。

Method: 1) 度量先验获取：用视觉-语言模型提取“熟悉物体”的高度区间先验；2) 语义-几何对齐：在每帧沿支撑面法向测量重建物体的相对高度，将先验转化为尺度区间；3) 全局尺度恢复：通过凸优化综合多帧/多物体的尺度区间，若区间重叠取可行解，否则给出唯一折中；将得到的序列级尺度应用到点云与相机平移。

Result: 构造了MetricBench基准（12个具身、驾驶、路侧子集，具备传感器真值）；在9个几何基础模型上，UniMetric平均将深度Abs Rel减少54.2%、重建Chamfer距离减少83.8%、轨迹误差减少65.6%，三项任务在套件层面全部模型均获提升。

Conclusion: 利用熟悉物体的不确定尺寸先验即可为GFM提供稳健的序列级公制尺度，且无需训练或额外传感器；多观测融合提升尺度准确度。UniMetric与MetricBench为具身学习与视频重建的度量评估和实际部署提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models, exemplified by GPT-6 Astra, raise the prospect of general-purpose embodied reasoning and planning. Using Internet videos for embodied pretraining calls for reliable physical-scale annotations. Yet visual geometry foundation models often recover scene structure and camera motion without reliable metric scale. We show that uncertain size priors of familiar objects can jointly constrain a reconstruction's shared scale. Building on this insight, we present UniMetric, a universal and training-free metric scale recovery framework for geometry foundation models, without additional sensors or task-specific metric supervision. Specifically, Metric Prior Acquisition elicits object height intervals from a vision-language model. Semantic-Geometric Alignment measures reconstructed object heights along the estimated support-surface normal in each frame and converts the priors into scale intervals. Global Scale Recovery reconciles these intervals through a convex objective, yielding a jointly feasible scale when they overlap and a unique compromise otherwise. The estimated sequence-level scale is applied uniformly to reconstructed points and camera translations. Controlled experiments with real VLM priors show that combining observations improves scale accuracy with exact relative object heights. To evaluate metric accuracy on GFM reconstructions without ground-truth scale alignment, we introduce MetricBench, covering twelve embodied, driving, and roadside subsets with sensor-derived ground truth. Across nine geometry foundation models, UniMetric reduces mean depth Abs Rel, reconstruction Chamfer distance, and trajectory error by 54.2%, 83.8%, and 65.6%, respectively, improving every model on all three tasks at the suite level. We release the UniMetric code and MetricBench data.

</details>


### [225] [DDVR-Net: Degradation-Aware Decoupling and Visibility-Guided Restoration for Historical Document Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16956)
*Jiu Sha,Yu Weng,Mengxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDVR-Net，用可见性引导的上下文解码与退化解耦，在藏/蒙/维多语低资源历史文档中稳健识别并显著超越现有方法；并发布含18.7万对样本的基准与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文献常因墨蚀、裂损等严重退化，字符可见性混杂；传统OCR在证据稀缺时失效，而语言模型易过度校正清晰字符。需要一种既能利用上下文修复退化、又能避免对清晰区域过拟合的识别框架，并为低资源文字（藏、蒙、维）构建真实可用的评测基准。

Method: 提出DDVR-Net：在特征层面将字形固有结构与物理退化解耦；预测像素/局部可见性以评估字符可靠性；通过可见性门控选择性激活上下文解码器，仅对低可见区域进行上下文恢复，保持高可见区域的视觉识别结果。训练上结合大规模合成数据与真实标注的渐进式训练，构建包含藏、蒙、维多读序的18.7万对文档的低资源多语基准。

Result: 在三种文字及多种阅读顺序的场景上，DDVR-Net较现有方法取得一致且显著提升（具体数值未给出），对退化场景更稳健，降低合成到真实的域差。

Conclusion: 可见性引导的退化感知解耦与选择性上下文恢复，能在低资源历史文档识别中兼顾修复与保真；所发布的代码与大规模基准为后续研究提供了有效框架与数据基础。

Abstract: Historical document analysis is crucial for cultural heritage preservation and low-resource language digitization. However, real historical documents often suffer from severe degradation, such as ink erosion and structural damage, causing mixed-visibility character sequences. Conventional OCR systems struggle in such evidence-limited scenarios, while contextual language models may over-correct visually clear characters. To address these challenges, we propose DDVR-Net, a degradation-aware decoupling and visibility-guided restoration framework for historical document recognition. DDVR-Net decouples intrinsic glyph structures from physical corruption at the feature level and predicts local visibility to estimate character reliability. A contextual decoder is selectively activated through a visibility-guided gate, restoring degraded characters while preserving clear visual recognition. Furthermore, we construct a low-resource multilingual benchmark covering Tibetan, Mongolian, and Uyghur scripts with 187000 document pairs, combining large-scale synthetic data and progressive training with real-world annotations to reduce the synthetic-to-real gap. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DDVR-Net consistently outperforms existing methods across three languages and diverse reading orders, providing an effective framework and benchmark for low-resource historical document recognition. Code and Benchmark: https://github.com/Shajiu/DDVR-Net

</details>


### [226] [Proactive Physical AI through Continuous Observation and Timely Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16967)
*Zhenhua Yang,Jiafeng Liang,Xingyu Zhou,Yi Jiang,Xiayu Cao,Zui Chen,Luqi Wang,Haiyang Sun,Yufei Xiang,Chang Li,Rongchuan Mu,Bowen Chi,Zihe Tong,Mingyu Li,Huijie Ma,Yiqiao Yang,Baoqi Ren,Zhiming Ma,Zhihao Zhu,Jiahao Ji,Zhuoyao Wang,Qianyu Wang,Xiangyu Liu,Wang Zixiang,Shunliang Zhang,Ming Liu,Xiaocheng Feng,Duyu Tang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“前瞻式物理智能（Proactive Physical AI）”，让系统在无人指令时基于持续感知主动介入并执行动作；实现为ProAgent，含流式Jev时机模型（StreamingJev）与响应模型；通过视频自动标注管线进行规模化训练；在基准与真实机器人上显示更准的介入时机、更少误触发，并能将新出现需求转化为物理行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界任务常在环境状态变化中“涌现”，人在明确提出指令前，最佳干预窗口可能已错过。现有物理AI多被动执行显式目标，缺乏在对的时机主动识别与介入能力，因而需要一种能够持续感知、判断何时与是否介入，并将判断转化为动作的框架。

Method: 构建ProAgent，包括：1) StreamingJev 前瞻时机模型，持续从视频流判断是否/何时需要干预（Jev风格的“触发事件”建模）；2) 响应模型，根据判断结果生成对人的反馈或调用设备能力执行物理动作；3) 自动化数据管线，将原始视频转为“前瞻时机”监督信号以支撑大规模训练；并在含显式目标与“常驻角色”两种设置下进行评测。

Result: 相比源骨干基线，ProAgent在干预时机更准确、误触发更少；在有显式目标与常驻角色场景均表现出色。真实机器人实验证明能识别新出现的需求，并转化为恰当的物理执行。

Conclusion: 主动时机判断与响应联动可实现前瞻式物理智能；通过视频弱监督的规模化训练可提升时机判断；在仿真与实机中均证实更优触发质量与实际可执行性，表明该范式适用于动态、开放环境中的长期服务与协作。

Abstract: Physical AI is commonly formulated around an explicitly specified task: given a goal or instruction, a system perceives, reasons, and acts. In many real-world settings, however, important tasks emerge from continuously changing world states before a person explicitly formulates them. We study this complementary problem as Proactive Physical AI, where an AI system continuously observes the physical world, identifies situations that warrant intervention, and initiates appropriate actions at the right time. We develop ProAgent, an end-to-end system built around two components: a Jev-style proactive timing model (dubbed StreamingJev) that determines whether and when intervention is needed, and a Response Model that generates responses for people or invokes available device capabilities for physical execution. To support learning at scale, we develop an automated data pipeline that converts videos into supervision for proactive timing. Experiments demonstrate strong proactive performance under both explicit objectives and standing roles, combining more accurate intervention timing with fewer false triggers than the source-backbone baseline. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate ProAgent's ability to identify emerging needs and translate them into appropriate physical actions.

</details>


### [227] [The Value of Being Wrong: Self-Mined Visual In-Context Learning from Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16977)
*Shu Zhao,Tan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SMILE方法：用模型自身历史错误作为“反事实”示例，作为视觉ICL的演示池；测试时用当前预测引导在语义空间检索最可能重犯的错误示例，从而提升各任务表现并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉ICL效果高度依赖示例选择；基于反事实的选择虽有效，但需要多阶段流水线（属性提取、描述工程、图像组合匹配），复杂且耗时。作者观察到：模型的预测错误天然体现了其失败模式，可直接充当反事实示例。

Method: 离线阶段：对目标VLM收集并记录其在训练/开发或历史交互中的错误样例，构建模型特定的“困难负例”池。在线阶段：对新查询先做预测，用该预测作为方向信号，在语义嵌入空间中检索与当前错误倾向最相似的历史错误；将这些检索到的错误样本作为ICL演示喂给模型，从而在闭集分类与开放式VQA中统一适用，无需任务特定设计。

Result: 在四个基准与四个VLM上，相比SOTA基线平均提升+3.09分，同时推理延迟降低约5倍。

Conclusion: 用自挖掘的错误作为反事实演示，能以更简单、统一、低延迟的流程显著提高视觉ICL效果，覆盖不同任务与模型；表明“预测驱动的错误检索”是有效且通用的示例选择策略。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning (ICL) enables vision-language models (VLMs) to adapt at test time via demonstration examples, but its effectiveness depends critically on the examples selected. Recent counterfactual selection methods improve ICL by constructing demonstrations that expose how individual attribute changes affect the answer. However, they require multi-stage pipelines involving attribute extraction, caption engineering, and composed image matching. We propose a simpler alternative grounded in a key observation: a model's own prediction errors are natural counterfactual demonstrations that directly encode its failure modes. Our method, SMILE (Self-Mined Visual In-Context Learning from Errors), builds a model-specific pool of hard negatives offline by recording the target VLM's own mistakes. At test time, the prediction serves as a direction signal that surfaces, in the semantic embedding space, the past mistakes most likely to recur for the current query. This prediction-conditioned identification provides a unified mechanism for both closed-set classification and open-ended visual question answering without task-specific design. Across four benchmarks and four VLMs, SMILE delivers an average task-score gain of +3.09 points over the SOTA baseline at 5x lower latency. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/SMILE/

</details>


### [228] [AUGUstus: Autonomous Driving VLA Model with Unified Generation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16985)
*Yuqi Ye,Caigao Jiang,Zhuonan Wang,Congyuan Pei,Chao Qu,Changhao Peng,Junhong Lin,Qize Yu,Zetian Song,Guangzhao Fu,Chongruo Wu,Shiyu Huang,Shuchang Zhou,Honggou Yang,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AUGUstus 将扩散式动作生成与自回归式CoT理解统一到单模型中：训练时先生成动作、再生成CoT以提供监督；推理时仅输出动作，实现低时延且保留VLM理解带来的安全与可解释性。联合CoT监督与统一GRPO优化带来SOTA规划表现与实车12Hz运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA要么直接回归动作、忽视语言理解导致不安全与不可解释；要么自回归顺序生成CoT与动作，虽充分利用理解但时延高、难部署。需要一种既能利用CoT提升安全性与准确性、又能低时延部署的方法。

Method: - 统一架构：单模型内“扩散式动作生成+自回归CoT理解”。
- 生成顺序：训练时先动作后CoT，使CoT仅作为监督信号，保持共享表征中的空间理解；推理仅输出动作以降时延。
- 联合监督：Joint CoT supervision 保留VLM空间理解，提升轨迹安全准确。
- 优化：AUGUstus-GRPO（统一GRPO）联合优化动作与CoT。
- 训练/部署：大规模GPU训练（512×H800，约2天），在仿真与真实车辆上验证。

Result: - NAVSIMv1：PDMS 94.6；NAVSIMv2：EPDMS 90.7（均为SOTA且无需scorer）。
- nuScenes：平均L2误差0.27 m、碰撞率0.05%，优于对比方法。
- 实车：内部1000 km评估，原型车以12 Hz运行。

Conclusion: 通过“理解服务于生成”的统一范式，AUGUstus在保证低时延的同时利用CoT提升轨迹质量与安全，取得仿真、真实数据与实车的SOTA表现，显示出端到端自动驾驶落地潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong potential for end-to-end autonomous driving by leveraging world knowledge and understanding to excel in long-tail scenarios. However, current VLAs have two limitations: 1) Degeneration into vision-action (VA) models. For low latency, regression/flow-based methods directly generate actions but underuse VLM understanding, causing unsafe, unexplainable actions. 2) Autoregressive high latency. Autoregressive methods sequentially generate chain-of-thought (CoT) and action tokens, fully leveraging VLM understanding, but high latency hinders deployment. To enable low-latency deployment while using CoT to assist action generation, we propose AUGUstus, an Autonomous-driving VLA model with Unified Generation and Understanding. We unify diffusion-based action generation and autoregressive CoT understanding in one model, where understanding only serves generation. Joint CoT supervision preserves VLM spatial understanding in shared representation for safer, more accurate trajectories. AUGUstus-mode orders action generation before CoT, so CoT supervises reliable actions during training while inference needs only action output, enabling low latency. AUGUstus-GRPO, a unified GRPO algorithm, jointly optimizes action and CoT, improving action performance. Experiments show AUGUstus achieves 94.6 PDMS on NAVSIMv1 and 90.7 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2, SOTA planning without scorer module. On nuScenes, it achieves average L2 error 0.27 m and average collision rate 0.05%, best among compared methods. To validate AUGUstus practicality, we train on in-house data with 512 NVIDIA H800 GPUs for ~2 days, evaluate on an internal 1,000 km dataset, and preliminarily deploy on a real vehicle, achieving 12 Hz operation. Project page: https://augustus-ad.github.io

</details>


### [229] [Self-Supervised Learning of Semantically Consistent Visual Features via a Simple 3D Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16988)
*Sebastian Day,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督方法，在数据集层面用对比学习与分布匹配学习类别的粗形状与外观，并配合几何损失，改进DINOv2/Stable Diffusion等视觉特征在对称/重复部件下的对应歧义；跨多个复杂类别取得较好定量效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉特征虽能提供高质量语义对应，但在左右歧义、对称性与重复部件场景下易出错。以往改进多依赖监督（关键点/位姿）或强拓扑假设（如球面），限制了可扩展性与通用性，亟需无需强监督且能适用于拓扑复杂类别的方法。

Method: 在无需实例级分析合成的前提下，提出自监督框架：1) 数据集层面的对比学习，捕获类别内判别性；2) 分布匹配，使合成特征的全局统计与真实图像特征一致，从而学习类别的粗形状与外观；3) 额外几何损失，约束局部几何一致性，提升局部对应质量；整体目标使生成/学习到的特征在全局与局部统计上逼近真实特征。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的、拓扑复杂的物体类别上进行定量评测，相比基线改善了语义对应精度，尤其在对称与重复结构导致的左右/部件歧义方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 无需监督标注与强拓扑先验即可学习到更具判别性的特征，缓解对称/重复导致的对应歧义，方法通用且可扩展，在多类别上验证有效。

Abstract: Visual features extracted from vision models like DINOv2 or Stable Diffusion enable high quality semantic correspondence between images. However, such features exhibit documented limitations that hinder accurate correspondence estimation such as the inability to disambiguate the left from right for categories exhibiting symmetries or repeated parts. Previous attempts to improve these features rely on using supervised data such as annotated keypoint pairs or camera pose information, or make restrictive topological assumptions, such as modelling the object of interest as a 3D sphere. We introduce a new self-supervised approach that allows us to learn features for topologically complex object categories using a simple prior. Instead of relying on an expensive instance-level analysis by synthesis pipeline, our approach makes use of contrastive learning and distribution matching at the global dataset-level to learn the coarse shape and appearance of a category. Combined with additional geometric losses, this enables us to replicate the global and local statistics of visual features which match those that are extracted from real images. Quantitative results are presented across several challenging object categories, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [230] [MaskSense: Confronting the Visual Exploration Trap in Masked Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17009)
*Yawen Shao,Jie Xiao,Kai Zhu,Yu Liu,Hongchen Luo,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Wei Zhai,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaskSense，用于将强化学习有效应用于掩码生成模型（MGMs），通过新的采样与优化机制避免“视觉探索陷阱”，在多项文生图基准上显著提升性能（GenEval 54%→86%，HPS 28.89→36.39）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
