Motivation: 现行实例检索评测常通过增加海量干扰图提升区分度（如ILIAS的1亿干扰图），但需要为每个模型在全部图片上提特征，耗费数百GPU小时与数百GB存储，严重制约研究与消融效率。

Method: 从理论上刻画“随图库规模增长，正样本被干扰样本超越的数量”服从二项分布，提出每个查询的“检索难度”（gallery规模无关），可由小样本随机子集估计；采用抽样秩/极值理论（EVT）等尾部分布建模，将小样本估计外推到任意图库规模，并输出目标尺度下的检索指标（如mAP@K）。

Result: 在ILIAS与INSTRE上真实100M规模、以及GLDv2的759k干扰池上验证：仅用5%随机样本（索引量减少20倍），各骨干网络基于尾部分布(EVT)的估计即可与大规模mAP收敛，平均绝对误差约0.005、总体偏差约±0.01；推荐系统的计数基线普遍高估（+0.037到+0.068）；直接在同一子集上“天真”报告指标误差更大（+0.08到+0.13）。

Conclusion: 大规模网页级检索评测存在可预测的缩放规律；通过每查询可估的难度与EVT外推，可在小样本上准确复现超大规模评测，显著降低计算、存储和碳足迹；作者开源代码和每查询难度以促进更快、更廉价、更绿色的评测。

Abstract: A common practice in instance retrieval is to grow the gallery size to improve a benchmark's discrimination capability. One prominent example is ILIAS, which adds up to 100 million distractor images. The prevailing assumption is that faithful evaluation demands web scale, and that indexing all 100M images through the backbone is the unavoidable price of a comparable number. That price is steep: hundreds of GPU-hours and ~200 GB of descriptors per model, paid again for every method or ablation. We show the web-scale indexing price is largely avoidable: evaluation itself obeys a scaling law. The number of distractors that outrank a positive is Binomial in the gallery size. We define the per-query retrieval hardness, a gallery-scale agnostic quantity that yields a retrieval metric at any scale, and demonstrate it can be derived from a small random sample. The underlying sampled-rank machinery is long established in recommender-system and biometric evaluation, but to our knowledge has never been adapted to visual retrieval. We make the law per-query and estimable, and validate it at true 100M scale on ILIAS and INSTRE, and on the full 759k-distractor Google Landmarks v2 pool. Concretely, within a 5% sample (a flat 20x less indexing), every backbone's tail-aware (EVT) estimate has converged onto the large-scale mAP, to within ~0.01 (mean absolute error 0.005), across five backbones, three benchmarks, and two distractor pools (mAP@1000 at 100M on ILIAS/INSTRE; the official mAP@100 at the full 759k pool on GLDv2); the recommender-systems count baseline over-predicts everywhere (+0.037 to +0.068). This is an order of magnitude better than naively reporting the metric on the same subset (+0.08 to +0.13). We release code and the per-query hardness to facilitate faster, cheaper, and greener retrieval evaluation.

</details>


### [297] [PolUR: Polarization Aware Multimodal Degradation Perception for Underwater Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17991)
*Wenshuo Zhang,Xiangyue Zhang,Junlin Li,Wenjie Tian,Yubo Zheng,Hongli Xu,Jingyu Ru,Xinde Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PolUR：一个联合利用偏振与RGB的多模态、语言驱动水下图像恢复框架，并构建MixPolar基准；在退化感知与恢复上取得有竞争力表现且具泛化与下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下复原多依赖RGB或单一物理建模，难以在混合退化（偏色、雾化、弱光）下稳健恢复；虽然偏振观测提供物理线索，但未与原始强度信息及语义先验联合建模。

Method: 提出PolUR：以结构化语义指令驱动的统一多模态框架，联合使用偏振与RGB，显式识别退化类型与严重程度，并以“退化感知语义特征”指导联合恢复表征；同时构建含真实退化与文本描述的MixPolar数据集，用于系统评测退化感知、恢复与下游任务。

Result: 大量实验表明PolUR能有效融合偏振与RGB信息，在退化感知与图像恢复上取得有竞争力的指标，并在跨场景与下游任务中展现良好泛化与实用性。

Conclusion: 语言驱动的多模态（偏振+RGB）联合建模可更稳健地应对水下混合退化；新基准MixPolar促进系统化评测与方法发展。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality images from degraded underwater observations is crucial for underwater exploration. Polarization observations show promise as an effective source of complementary physical cues for recovering target radiance under clear-water conditions. However, existing methods have yet to jointly model raw intensity information and polarization cues to enable image restoration driven by an understanding of mixed degradations (color casts, haze, and low light). To overcome these limitations, we propose PolUR, a unified multimodal language-driven framework that jointly uses polarization and RGB information to identify degradation types and severity levels through structured semantic instructions and enhances joint restoration representations under the guidance of degradation-aware semantic features to improve restoration robustness under mixed degradations. We further construct a new benchmark named MixPolar, which contains underwater images exhibiting real-world degradations and their corresponding degradation descriptions. MixPolar supports systematic evaluation of degradation perception, image restoration, and performance on downstream tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PolUR effectively integrates polarization and RGB information, achieving competitive performance in degradation perception and image restoration, while also demonstrating generalization capability and utility for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [298] [Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Supervised Framework with Supervised Contrastive Learning for Sugarcane Leaf Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17996)
*Shalini Dangi,Divya Teja,Omkar Kabde,Shahid Shafi Dar,Nagendra Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出U-SSDNet：一种不确定性感知的Mean-Teacher半监督病害检测网络，用更少标注数据在甘蔗叶病任务上接近全监督表现并优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物病害检测标注昂贵且难以获取，同时存在类内差异大、类间细微相似、噪声标签风险等问题，需要一种能利用未标注数据、降低确认偏置并提升特征可分性的鲁棒方法。

Method: 1) 预处理：离散小波变换增强病斑可见性、降低类内差异；2) 半监督框架：基于Mean-Teacher，引入不确定性感知；3) 伪标签优化：用KL散度约束/筛选以减轻确认偏置；4) 表征学习：采用监督式对比学习拉近同类、拉远异类，提升类间分离度。

Result: 在甘蔗叶病数据集上，用显著更少的标注样本即可达到接近全监督的性能，并在多项指标上稳定优于最先进的监督与半监督基线。

Conclusion: U-SSDNet有效解决标注稀缺与噪声问题，增强特征判别性并利用未标注数据，实现高效且鲁棒的甘蔗叶病检测，可推广至类似作物病害场景。

Abstract: Sugarcane leaf diseases severely impact crop yield and quality, making accurate and timely detection critical for sustainable agriculture. Conventional deep learning models for plant disease detection depend on large annotated datasets, which are expensive to obtain and challenging to prepare due to challenges commonly observed across crop disease detection tasks, including (i) high intra-class variation caused by diverse symptom expressions across growth stages, (ii) subtle inter-class similarities between diseased and healthy tissues, and (iii) the risk of noisy labels degrading model reliability. To handle these challenges, we presented an Uncertainty-aware Mean-Teacher based Semi-Supervised Learning Disease Detection Network (U-SSDNet) that leverages unlabeled data while maintaining robustness. First, discrete wavelet transform preprocessing is applied to enhance lesion visibility and reduce intra- class variation. Subsequently, the KL divergence is employed to refine the pseudo-labels and reduce the impact of confirmation bias. At the same time, supervised contrastive learning is applied to make the features more distin- guishable and improve the separation among different classes. Experimental evaluations on sugarcane leaf disease datasets, considered a representative case study, demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves performance close to that of fully supervised models while requiring far fewer annotated samples and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and semi-supervised approaches.

</details>


### [299] [Surface Reconstruction from Range Scans Under Visibility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18005)
*Gabriel Lenain,Edmond Boyer,Jean Ponce*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种利用传感器光线可见性的信息，将表面重建转化为沿采集射线的“首个表面交点”稳健回归的问题；以1-Lipschitz MLP表示隐式曲面，使用球面追踪与Huber损失，在含噪与离群情况下显著更稳健，同时在干净数据上具竞争力，并揭示模型容量与鲁棒性/保真度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经隐式重建方法多忽略传感器位置与可见性，转而依赖几何先验与正则，导致在噪声与离群值存在时要么过拟合要么过度平滑。需要一种直接利用采集射线深度观测并在噪声下稳健拟合的方法。

Method: - 将重建表述为：对每条采集射线，预测其“首个表面交点”与量测深度进行稳健回归；若预测交点落在传感器与观测点之间的线段内则予以惩罚。
- 用满足1-Lipschitz约束的MLP作为SDF零水平集表示，使得首交点可通过线性收敛的sphere tracing高效求解。
- 使用Huber损失增强对离群噪声的鲁棒性。
- 训练不需要定向法向、离面采样点或Eikonal正则。

Result: 在含不同水平噪声与离群的合成与真实数据上，与现有方法相比：
- 干净数据：精度具竞争力；
- 中等乃至较强腐败：显著提升保真度与鲁棒性（更少伪面、形状细节保持更好）。

Conclusion: 显式利用传感器可见性并进行首交点稳健回归，可在无需常见正则与额外监督的情况下实现高质量、鲁棒的表面重建。分析显示容量-鲁棒性权衡：更大容量在干净数据上拟合更好，但对测量误差更敏感；通常在中等容量处达到最佳折中。

Abstract: Surface reconstruction from range scans is challenging in the presence of noise and outliers, as fidelity to observations must be balanced against sensitivity to measurement errors. Yet, most neural approaches discard the sensor positions and corresponding visibility information, compensating for this loss with geometric priors or regularization terms. We instead formulate surface reconstruction as robust regression of the predicted first surface intersection along each acquisition ray against its measured depth, which penalizes predicted surface intersections within the segment between the sensor and the observed point. We represent the surface as the zero set of a 1-Lipschitz multi-layer perceptron, allowing the use of linearly convergent sphere tracing to compute the first hit, and use a robust Huber loss for regression. Our formulation requires neither oriented normals, sampled off-surface points, nor an eikonal loss. Comparisons with the state of the art on synthetic and real data with varying levels of noise and outlier contamination demonstrate competitive performance on clean data and much improved fidelity and robustness under even moderate corruption. Our analysis further confirms a trade-off between fidelity and robustness: greater fitting capacity improves data fidelity in the clean case but increases sensitivity to the measurement errors inherent in real acquisitions, with a sweet spot typically emerging at intermediate capacity.

</details>


### [300] [Dynamic Interpolation for Training-Time Prediction Reshaping in Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18018)
*Peirui Zhu,Shijie Hao,Jingxi Liao,Richang Hong,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个仅训练期的动态插值模块（DITM），通过亮度感知插值抑制全局亮度误差主导，放大局部残差的梯度权重，显著提升低照度增强模型后期训练的去噪与细节恢复效果；推理时完全移除，无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低照度增强中亮度不足、噪声放大、结构退化强耦合；现有方法要么用复杂架构增加部署成本，要么依赖经验权重的多损失函数不稳健。作者从训练动态出发，发现亮度相关误差先快收敛，而噪声/结构伪影后期优化不足，需一种不增参且可在训练后期重塑梯度关注的机制。

Method: 在监督式LLIE训练中插入DITM：对当前模型预测I_pred与其“均值亮度对齐”版本I_align（将I_pred按GT的均值亮度缩放得到）进行亮度感知插值，得到I_mix作为前向输出用于计算损失；随着训练进程动态调节插值，使全局亮度差的相对贡献下降，让局部误差在反传中更占权，促进去伪影与细节恢复。DITM不引入可学习参数，仅训练期启用，推理时移除。

Result: 在多基准数据集与多种骨干（CNN/Transformer/Mamba）上，一致提升保真度与感知质量；在不改变推理架构与计算量前提下获得增益。

Conclusion: 通过训练期的亮度感知动态插值，重塑误差分布与梯度关注，可在不增参、零推理开销的条件下稳健提升LLIE的噪声抑制与细节重建效果，适配多种架构与数据集。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) is an important image/video processing problem for reliable visual systems under poor illumination, yet it remains challenging due to the strong coupling among insufficient illumination, noise amplification, and structural degradation. Existing methods typically rely on sophisticated network architectures or engineered objective functions. While effective, the former increases model complexity and deployment burden, whereas the latter relies on empirically tuned loss weights. We revisit supervised LLIE from a training-dynamics perspective and observe that brightness-related errors tend to converge early, while residual noise and structural artifacts often remain insufficiently optimized in later stages. To address this issue, we propose a Dynamic Interpolation Training-only Module (DITM), which introduces no additional learnable parameters and adaptively reshapes the model prediction for supervised feed-forward LLIE training. Specifically, DITM employs brightness-aware interpolation, in which the model prediction is combined with a mean brightness-aligned image constructed by rescaling the model prediction according to the mean brightness of the ground-truth image. By reducing the relative dominance of global brightness discrepancy, DITM makes residual local errors more influential during backpropagation after global luminance has largely stabilized, thereby facilitating artifact suppression and fine-detail restoration in later training stages. DITM is used only during training and is fully removed at inference, introducing no additional inference cost. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets and CNN-, Transformer-, and Mamba-based LLIE backbones show that DITM consistently improves fidelity and perceptual quality while preserving the inference-time architecture and introducing no additional inference-time computation.

</details>


### [301] [FlowSim: Rendering-Free Closed-Loop Simulation for Evaluating End-to-End Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18030)
*Hanfeng Wu,Alvaro Marcos-Ramiro,Michael Schmidt,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSim 提出一种无需图像渲染的闭环仿真：直接在潜表示空间中进行状态过渡预测，并用原有冻结的下游模块评估E2E驾驶行为，在精度相当或更优的同时显著加速（每步0.04–0.06s），降低sim2real差距与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有E2E驾驶闭环评估依赖图像级仿真与重新编码感知，计算昂贵且存在仿真到现实的域差。作者质疑：是否必须在图像空间做仿真？希望通过跳过渲染与重编码，直接在潜空间评估，从而提升效率与稳定性。

Method: 提出FlowSim：1) 以“上一帧潜向量+目标帧的符号级场景条件”作为输入，直接预测下一帧潜表示；2) 将预测潜表示送入原模型的冻结下游模块（规划、控制等），避免图像渲染与感知重编码；3) 显式跨帧体素/智能体关联，提升潜状态转移与滚动稳定性；4) 可变时域仿真，从共同的真实锚点出发进行长时域预测，减少中间误差累积；5) 在稠密与稀疏潜表示两种实例化上验证。

Result: 在nuScenes与NavSim上，FlowSim在不同仿真时域与潜表示形态下，对下游行为的一致性（与参考源的吻合度）可与图像级仿真持平或更优；单步仿真耗时0.04–0.06s，显著快于对比的图像空间方法。

Conclusion: 直接预测潜表示可实现高效、稳定的闭环评估，减少sim2real差距与计算开销；FlowSim是图像空间仿真的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of end-to-end (E2E) driving systems typically relies on generating sensor observations and re-processing them through the perception encoder, introducing substantial computational cost and a sim2real gap between simulated and real sensor observations. In this paper, we ask a more fundamental question: is image-space simulation necessary for closed-loop evaluation of E2E driving systems? We propose FlowSim, a rendering-free simulation paradigm that predicts the internal latent representation directly from a previous latent and target-frame symbolic scene conditions. The predicted latent is then processed by the original frozen downstream modules, eliminating image rendering and perception re-encoding. Explicit cross-frame agent association improves latent transition prediction and rollout stability, while variable-horizon simulation enables long-horizon prediction from a common real anchor without accumulating intermediate rollout errors. Experiments on nuScenes and NavSim demonstrate that FlowSim preserves downstream behavior competitively with, and often exceeds image-space simulation in source-to-reference agreement across variable simulation horizons and both dense and sparse latent representations. By predicting the latent directly, both instantiations of FlowSim simulate each step in 0.04--0.06s, substantially faster than the image-space simulation methods they are compared against. These results establish FlowSim as an efficient and practical alternative to conventional image-space simulation methods.

</details>


### [302] [COMPASS: Contextual Moment Prior Alignment for Generalizable AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18034)
*Zhan Yang,Tiancheng Shen,Weijie Lyu,Shuo Chen,Bernard Ghanem,Yibo Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COMPASS：利用小规模无标注语义代理集估计目标域激活统计，将协方差纳入权重分解，冻结与目标相关的稳定子空间，仅在残差子空间适配，从而提升AIGI与deepfake跨域泛化并达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VFM在有限训练分布上微调会损害可迁移性（不对称现象）。基于主成分幅值的子空间适配虽可缓解，但与数据无关，未显式体现目标域语义，易导致语义错配，需一种数据感知的子空间适配方法。

Method: 引入COMPASS框架：1) 准备小型无标注语义代理集（与目标域相关）。2) 用其计算目标域激活的一阶/二阶矩，特别是协方差。3) 将这些统计融入对预训练权重的分解，得到“目标相关稳定子空间”和“可适配残差子空间”。4) 冻结前者作为语义参考，仅在残差子空间中学习与伪造相关的变化；代理校准为无梯度过程。

Result: 在跨数据集、跨方法、联合跨数据集-方法、以及开放世界跨生成器评测中表现强泛化，且在Deepfake与AIGI检测基准上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 数据感知的子空间分解与无梯度代理校准能保留目标相关语义并在残差中适配伪造模式，有效缓解不对称现象，显著提升AIGI与deepfake检测的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Generalizable AI-generated image (AIGI) and deepfake detection remain challenging due to the asymmetry phenomenon, where adaptation to limited training distributions can degrade the transferable representations of Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Recent subspace adaptation methods mitigate this issue by freezing high-magnitude principal components while adapting the residual subspace. However, such magnitude-based decomposition is data-agnostic and does not explicitly account for target-relevant semantics, potentially leading to semantic misalignment. We propose COMPASS (COntextual Moment Prior Alignment for Subspace Separation), a data-aware subspace adaptation framework that uses a small unlabeled Semantic Proxy Set to estimate target activation statistics. COMPASS incorporates activation covariance into the decomposition of pre-trained weights, preserving target-relevant representations as a stable semantic reference while the residual adapter learns forgery-related variations. The proxy calibration requires no gradient-based optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong generalization across cross-dataset, cross-method, joint cross-dataset-method, and open-world cross-generator evaluations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on both Deepfake and AIGI detection benchmarks.

</details>


### [303] [Scaling Vision Transformers Without Training: Prune-and-Merge Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18039)
*Vatsal,Soumil Malhotra,Priyanshu Chahal,Sanjeev Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需再训练的ViT令牌压缩框架Prune-and-Merge：先基于类注意力剪枝，再按相似度合并，显著减算力同时在鲁棒性与精度上优于现有方法，并通过RAD与PFR两指标验证预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT因token两两交互带来二次复杂度，推理成本高；训练-free的token缩减虽能提效，但其在各种图像变换下的鲁棒性与预测一致性尚缺系统评估。

Method: 在现成预训练ViT/DeiT上，先用类注意力得分剪除不重要token，再对剩余相似token进行聚合/合并以保留语义；并提出两个鲁棒性指标：RAD（相对精度下降）与PFR（预测翻转率）来量化在常见图像变换下的稳健性。

Result: 在ViT与DeiT上，无需再训练即可在保持或提升精度的同时减少计算量，优于现有训练-free token缩减方法；在多种图像变换下RAD较低、PFR较小，显示预测更稳定。

Conclusion: 混合式“剪枝+合并”的token缩减在不引入幻觉式错误的前提下降低计算并保持鲁棒性与准确性，适合作为现成ViT的高效部署方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers achieve strong performance but incur high computational costs from quadratic token interactions. Training-free token reduction methods improve efficiency, but their robustness remains underexplored. We propose Prune-and Merge ViT, a training-free framework for off-the-shelf models. It combines class attention-based pruning with similarity-based merging to reduce redundancy while preserving semantic information. To evaluate robustness we introduce two metrics. Relative Accuracy Drop (RAD) measures relative accuracy degradation under transformations, and Prediction Flip Rate (PFR) quantifies prediction consistency. On pretrained ViT and DeiT, Prune-and-Merge reduces computation competitively while maintaining robustness and better accuracy than existing token reduction methods. Our analysis suggests that hybrid token reduction does not introduce hallucination-like behavior and preserves prediction stability for common image transformations.

</details>


### [304] [LensErase: A Foundation Model for Erase Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18072)
*Yunuo Chen,Xiuyu Wu,Qinhong Yang,Jinjing Zhao,Yanchen Dong,Sirui Zhang,Dong Chen,Fangyun Wei,Lei Shi,Ji Li,Dongdong Chen,Guo Lu,Yan Lu,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LensErase 是一个通用的、基于掩膜的“擦除任意内容”基础模型，依托1.01亿配对数据、从文本生成模型迁移并引入Mask-Aware RoPE与多阶段优化，在多基准上显著提升擦除质量与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像内容擦除方法在对象类型多样性、尺度/分辨率适配、文本与水印等复杂场景、以及编辑区域外内容保真方面存在不足，且缺乏统一的大规模数据与通用基础模型支撑。作者希望构建一个覆盖广谱真实擦除需求的基础模型，作为擦除相关应用与系统的通用底座。

Method: 1) 数据：构建LensErase-100M，含1.0107亿组源图-掩膜-目标图配对，覆盖多对象、文本/水印、不同尺度与纵横比。2) 预训练：从文本到图像模型Lens出发迁移为掩膜擦除任务，利用其强生成先验。3) 表征：提出Mask-Aware RoPE（三维旋转位置编码），将掩膜信息直接注入源图token而非单独引入掩膜序列。4) 训练：监督微调+基于契约引导、区域感知的强化学习奖励，优化擦除质量与编辑一致性。5) 推理：采用TraceFusion，在有效编辑区外保持高保真内容。

Result: 在六个公开基准与自建LensErase-Bench上，相较现有方法在参考图像客观指标与无参考的多模态大模型（VLM）评价上均取得大幅提升，显示更强的通用性与稳健性。

Conclusion: LensErase凭借大规模数据、从生成模型迁移、Mask-Aware RoPE、SFT+RL训练与TraceFusion推理流程，形成可覆盖多类真实擦除场景的基础模型，为后续擦除类应用与系统提供通用能力与性能上限。

Abstract: We introduce LensErase, a general-purpose mask-based Erase Anything foundation model designed to cover a broad spectrum of real-world erase scenarios. LensErase supports diverse objects, visual text, watermarks, associated visual effects, multiple-object removal, and a wide range of object scales, aspect ratios, and image resolutions. First, to enable such broad coverage, we construct LensErase-100M, a large-scale dataset containing 101.07 million paired source-mask-target examples spanning diverse erase scenarios. Second, we pretrain LensErase from the text-to-image model Lens, adapting its strong generative prior to mask-based erasing. To efficiently incorporate the source image and erase mask, we propose Mask-Aware RoPE, a 3D rotary positional encoding that directly injects mask information into the source-image tokens without introducing a separate mask token sequence. Finally, we refine the model through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with contract-guided, region-aware rewards, and apply TraceFusion at inference to preserve high-fidelity content outside the effective edit region. Across six public benchmarks and the proposed LensErase-Bench, LensErase achieves substantial improvements under both reference-based objective metrics and reference-free VLM evaluation. We hope LensErase can serve as a foundational model for erase-related applications and systems.

</details>


### [305] [APT: Feedback-Driven Agentic Post-Training for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18097)
*Sirui Zhang,Fangyun Wei,Dong Chen,Jinjing Zhao,Yitong Wang,Yunuo Chen,Baining Guo,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APT是一种全自动、可自适应的文本到图像模型后训练框架，通过评估-诊断-数据生成的闭环，让模型当前能力分布驱动下一轮训练，重点强化薄弱能力；在多模型、多基准、多架构上验证为模型无关、基准无关、架构无关且全自动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I后训练依赖人工数据与固定分布，难以随着模型能力变化而自适应优化，且缺乏对不同基线/架构的通用自动化流程。需要一种能根据模型实时能力画像动态构造训练目标与数据的系统。

Method: 提出APT框架，编排三个智能体：1) 从用户需求（或通用场景）自动生成评测集；2) 诊断模型长处与失败模式，形成能力画像；3) 按诊断结果自适应生成RL训练数据，调节提示词分布以加权薄弱能力。循环执行评估-诊断-更新，形成递归自改进闭环；并提供通用评测工具以覆盖广泛多样的评测场景。

Result: 在多种基础模型、公开基准与不同生成架构上实验，APT均带来性能提升，表现出模型无关、基准无关与架构无关的特性，并实现全自动流程。

Conclusion: APT以自动化、适应性和通用性改进T2I后训练，通过闭环自适应数据与任务构造优先强化弱项，减少人工干预，适用于广泛模型与场景。

Abstract: Post-training is crucial for improving text-to-image models, yet existing pipelines typically rely on manually curated training data and fixed training distributions. We introduce APT (Agentic Post-Training), a fully automated framework that adaptively post-trains a base model according to its evolving capability profile. APT coordinates three agents to construct evaluation sets from user requirements, diagnose model-specific strengths and failure modes, and generate reinforcement learning data with an adaptive prompt distribution that emphasizes underperforming capabilities. By repeatedly evaluating, diagnosing, and updating the generator, APT lets the current model's behavior shape its next training tasks, forming a feedback loop inspired by recursive self-improvement. For general-purpose settings without user-specified evaluation scenarios, we further develop an evaluation harness that enables broad and diverse evaluation set generation. Experiments across multiple base models, benchmarks, and generation architectures demonstrate that APT is model-agnostic, benchmark-agnostic, architecture-agnostic, and fully automated.

</details>


### [306] [H-DDCM: Hadamard Codebooks for Fast Generative Image and Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18125)
*Or Ronai,Vladimir Kulikov,Tomer Michaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出H-DDCM：用隐式随机Hadamard字典和表无关的词典编码，替代高开销的高斯码本，使训练免调的扩散/流模型压缩在高维下更高效，并扩展到自回归视频流与流式压缩，实现单GPU近实时、长时长视频压缩，兼顾率-失真-感知性能并显著降内存与算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免调生成式压缩（如基于扩散/流）虽在极低码率具备高感知质量，但高斯密集码本在高维潜空间内记忆与计算成本巨大，组合式比特流编码也变得难以承受，限制了可扩展性与实时应用。

Method: 1) 将高斯码本替换为隐式随机Hadamard字典；利用快速Walsh–Hadamard变换以O(d log d)投影/重建与O(d)内存隐式合成全维词典。2) 设计表无关的字典序编码，使组合式编码在大维度可行。3) 将框架适配到自回归视频流模型，并提出流式压缩：周期性将最近重建帧再编码进新的KV-cache前缀，在不增加传输比特的情况下保留跨片段的时序上下文，实现长时长、近实时视频压缩。

Result: 在图像与视频基准上，H-DDCM在率-失真-感知权衡上与最先进方法竞争，同时显著降低内存与计算开销；支持单GPU近实时长视频生成式压缩。

Conclusion: 隐式Hadamard字典与高效组合编码消除了密集码本的扩展瓶颈，使训练免调的生成式压缩在高维图像/视频场景下变得可扩展、实用，并保持优良的感知质量与压缩效率。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion-based compression methods repurpose the generative priors of a diffusion or a flow model to compress data without retraining them as task-specific codecs. Recent codebook-based approaches such as Turbo-DDCM achieve strong perceptual quality at ultra-low bitrates, but their reliance on dense Gaussian codebooks remains costly in both memory and computation, and scales poorly with the large latent dimensions of modern image and video generators. Their combinatorial bitstream encoding further becomes computationally heavy at this scale. We introduce H-DDCM, a scalable generative compression framework that replaces Gaussian codebooks with implicit randomized Hadamard dictionaries. The fast Walsh-Hadamard transform allows for implicitly synthesizing full-dimensional dictionaries with $O(d \log d)$ compute for projection and reconstruction and $O(d)$ memory, without materializing the full-dimensional dictionary. We further introduce an efficient table-free lexicographic coding scheme that makes combinatorial encoding practical at larger dimensions. Beyond images, we adapt the framework to an autoregressive video flow model and develop a streaming compression scheme that periodically re-encodes recently reconstructed frames into a fresh KV-cache prefix, preserving temporal context across segments at no additional transmitted bits. This enables long-form, close to real-time generative video compression on a single GPU. Across image and video benchmarks, H-DDCM achieves competitive rate-distortion-perception performance while substantially reducing the memory and computational overhead of codebook-based training-free generative compression.

</details>


### [307] [Human-Inspired Asymmetric Collaboration for Dual-Student Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18133)
*Huiyi Ren,Ziming Fang,Jiaming Cai,Rui Xu,Haojie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出人类启发的非对称协作(HAC)用于SSMIS双学生学习，通过自适应强增强难度控制与几何保持提升分割；以强化学习动态调参强增强（RDC），并以Gram矩几何保持约束（G2PC）维持三分支早期几何，避免学生同质化；在LA、Pancreas、BraTS-2019上验证有效与“因材施教”的难度校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双学生半/弱监督医学图像分割常用弱-强视图以提升预测多样性，但强增强过弱会同质化，过强会破坏解剖证据并导致协作不稳。类似人类教学中根据学习者反馈动态调节任务难度，作者希望让强增强难度“因人制宜”，并在共享监督下保持两学生的结构差异与几何一致性。

Method: 提出HAC闭环双学生框架：1) RDC（强化学习引导的难度校准）把强增强控制建模为窗口级RL过程，观测包含历史增强、已施加扰动与训练反馈，动作为调整增强幅度、操作数量与幅度范围；2) G2PC（Gramian几何保持约束）在训练早期以全局体积与成对面积约束维持三分支（弱、强、教师/共享）表征的Gram几何，防止共享监督使两个学生同质化；整体维持弱-强学习的非对称性与稳定协作。

Result: 在LA、Pancreas、BraTS-2019三个数据集上做了全面实验，显示HAC在分割精度上优于或不劣于对比方法，并呈现出与学生能力相匹配的自适应难度曲线与参数调整行为，证明了RDC的能力感知与G2PC的稳定化作用。

Conclusion: 通过人类教学启发的闭环调控，HAC在不破坏解剖证据的前提下维持预测多样性与协作稳定性；RDC实现强增强的自适应难度分配，G2PC抑制同质化，整体提升SSMIS分割性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dual-student learning enriches predictive diversity in SSMIS by introducing a strongly augmented view alongside a weakly augmented view. Insufficient strong augmentation may homogenize the students, whereas excessive augmentation can corrupt anatomical evidence and destabilize collaboration. This challenge echoes feedback-regulated human instruction, where learners receive differentiated tasks and the task difficulty is continually adjusted according to their evolving responses. Inspired by these, we propose Human-Inspired Asymmetric Collaboration (HAC), a closed-loop dual-student framework that maintains weak--strong learning through adaptive difficulty calibration and geometry preservation. Specifically, Reinforcement-guided Difficulty Calibration (RDC) formulates strong-augmentation control as a window-level reinforcement learning process, using augmentation history, realized perturbations, and training responses to dynamically adjust the augmentation magnitude, operation number, and magnitude range. Meanwhile, the Gramian Geometry Preservation Constraint (G2PC) preserves the early-stage three-branch geometry through global volume and pairwise area constraints, preventing shared supervision from homogenizing the two students. Comprehensive evaluations across LA, Pancreas, and BraTS-2019 validate both the segmentation effectiveness and capability-aware difficulty calibration of HAC.

</details>


### [308] [FlowCred: Hierarchical Credit Assignment and Functional Coordination for Joint Text-to-Video Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18142)
*Mingxuan Zhu,Yanwei Huang,Shiyuan Liu,Xinzhi Zhang,Shujie Li,Yuanxing Zhang,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowCred提出一个用于文本生成视频（T2V）联合后训练的双层信用分配与协调框架，通过共享随机性下的分层信用分配和功能性协调，分别稳健地区分提示贡献与生成器残差贡献，并避免两者重复优化，从而在不增加解码或评估预算的前提下显著提升多项基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V系统常同时优化提示策略与视频生成器，但二者共享同一终端奖励会导致两类歧义：1）提示质量与生成器的随机性相互混淆；2）提示与生成器可能各自实现重叠的局部改进，造成低效或相互抵消。需要一种方法将信用清晰地分摊给提示与生成器，并在联合训练中使两者协同而非冲突。

Method: 提出FlowCred，包括两大核心：1）分层信用分配（hierarchical credit assignment）：在匹配的提示-随机种子（prompt-seed）网格上开展回合展开，用共享随机性分解提示层面的贡献与在给定提示条件下生成器的残差贡献；2）功能性协调（functional coordination）：在真实生成轨迹的状态处探测当前策略的备选提示，为提示优化提供“感知生成器”的证据，同时对那些已能通过条件改变（提示）获得支持的生成器更新进行选择性衰减，减少重复优化。

Result: 在多项T2V基准上均优于匹配的联合训练基线。在LTX-2.5上，将T2V-CompBench分数从61.47提升到64.46、将StoryEval从55.58提升到58.08；无需增加视频解码或奖励模型调用预算。消融与因子化分析显示提示塑形与生成器路由各具补益；人工评测与在不同主干与更新器家族上的迁移结果进一步验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在共享随机性下进行分层信用分配，并以功能性协调抑制提示与生成器的重复更新，FlowCred实现了对提示策略与生成器的有效协同优化，在保持预算不变的情况下，稳定提升T2V系统的生成质量与一致性，且具有可迁移与人评背书。

Abstract: Prompt optimization and generator post-training are two complementary ways to improve text-to-video (T2V) generation, and recent systems increasingly train both modules jointly. However, optimizing a prompt policy and a video generator from the same terminal reward creates two ambiguities: prompt quality is confounded with stochastic generation, and the two modules may realize overlapping local improvements. We introduce FlowCred, a two-level framework for joint T2V post-training. First, hierarchical credit assignment uses matched prompt-seed rollout grids to separate prompt-level credit from prompt-conditioned generator residuals under shared randomness. Second, functional coordination probes alternative current-policy prompts at states from actual generation trajectories, providing generator-aware evidence for prompt optimization while selectively attenuating generator updates already supported by conditioning changes. Across multiple T2V benchmarks, FlowCred consistently improves matched joint-training baselines. On LTX-2.5, FlowCred improves a strictly matched joint-training control from 61.47 to 64.46 on T2V-CompBench and from 55.58 to 58.08 on StoryEval, without increasing decoded-video or reward-model-call budgets. Factorial and realized-update analyses show complementary contributions from prompt shaping and generator routing, while human evaluation and transfer across backbones and updater families provide further supporting evidence.

</details>


### [309] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Zero-Shot Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18161)
*Oriol Rabasseda,Zenjie Li,Kamal Nasrollahi,Sergio Escalera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）用于零样本HOI识别/检测的系统性研究，通过外部目标检测+MLLM推理的管线，在HICO/HICO-DET/V-COCO上达到零样本SOTA（HICO 53.50 mAP，HICO-DET 23.69 mAP），并提供统一提示策略与将logit转为交互分数的评测框架及新QA标注与视觉提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HOI依赖大量标注和固定谓词集，难以适应开放世界与动态场景；MLLM具备跨模态、零样本推理潜力，但在HOI领域尚未被系统评估与利用，缺乏统一基准与有效提示方法。

Method: 构建两阶段管线：外部检测器定位人和物体；MLLM进行零样本交互推理。提出：1）统一的零样本提示策略分类与基准；2）将token级logit转为交互分数以与专用HOI模型公平比较；3）为标准HOI数据集构造QA式标注；4）两种视觉提示Objects as Context与Black Other Objects，引导关注目标同时保留场景。

Result: 在HICO、HICO-DET、V-COCO上进行零样本评测，取得SOTA：HICO 53.50 mAP、HICO-DET 23.69 mAP，并在统一基准下优于现有专用HOI零样本方法。

Conclusion: MLLM在HOI零样本识别/检测上具备强大潜力；所提基准、评分方法与视觉提示为后续模型与数据集提供了可复用框架，推动开放世界的人物-物体交互理解。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong zero-shot reasoning across diverse domains, but their potential for Human-Object Interaction (HOI) remains unexplored. Unlike traditional HOI approaches that rely on extensive labeled datasets and fixed interaction taxonomies, MLLMs offer flexible reasoning, making them well-suited for dynamic, real-world scenarios. In this work, we present the first systematic study of MLLMs for HOI recognition and detection within a pipeline that combines an external object detector for entity localization with zero-shot multimodal reasoning for interaction understanding. We introduce a benchmark framework for principled zero-shot evaluation, consisting of (i) a unified taxonomy of zero-shot prompting strategies, and (ii) a methodology that transforms token-level logits into interaction scores, enabling fair comparison with specialized HOI models under standard metrics. To support prompt-based evaluation, we additionally construct new question-answer annotations for standard HOI datasets, transforming them into a format suitable for MLLMs. Building on this, we propose two visual prompting strategies, namely Objects as Context and Black Other Objects, that guide MLLMs to target objects while preserving scene context. Experiments on HICO, HICO-DET, and V-COCO demonstrate state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, achieving 53.50 mAP on HICO and 23.69 mAP on HICO-DET. Overall, our work provides a principled benchmarking framework for MLLMs in HOI, opening the door to new models and datasets and enabling flexible open-world interaction understanding.

</details>


### [310] [VARI: Robust Text-to-Video Generation via Group-Structured Intent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18189)
*Mingxuan Zhu,Hohei Chan,Shiyuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VARI框架，通过将同一视觉意图的多种经自动验证的提示表述视为“群组监督单元”，提升T2V模型在等价提示表述间的一致性与鲁棒性，并在多项基准上取得SOTA且无需推理时额外模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V模型对提示措辞高度敏感，意义等价的表述会导致语义实现与质量显著波动。现有方法或只在推理时改写提示，或在训练时仅用单一表述监督，无法直接优化跨等价表述的可靠性与一致性。

Method: 提出VARI（Verified Alignment over Representations of Intent）：1) 构建“表述-种子”匹配的生成池；2) 跨表述对每个视频进行打分，执行与措辞无关的结构化意图审计，标记有效实现与语义失败；3) 以群组为单位训练：a) 群组一致蒸馏（GCD）将每个已验证表述对齐到可互换的有效实现；b) 表述对比的Flow-DPO（FC-DPO）基于审计得到的正负样本学习偏好，重点挖掘同种子但不同表述的对比信号。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo-1.5上，VARI在T2V-CompBench、VBench、StoryEval均达最高；在扩展到多表述版本的T2V-CompBench与VBench上，相比最强提示级基线，提升最弱表述对齐度分别为+2.45与+3.55分，跨表述性能下降分别降低30.5%与32.2%，且保持画质。

Conclusion: 将等价提示表述作为共享监督单元并进行群组级对齐与偏好学习，可显著增强T2V模型的跨表述鲁棒性与一致性；效果经人评、自然字幕对照、提示增强、多样性分析与跨模型迁移（至LTX-2.3）验证，且无需额外推理时开销。

Abstract: Modern text-to-video (T2V) models generate visually compelling videos, yet meaning-preserving changes in prompt wording can substantially alter semantic realization and overall quality. Existing prompt rewriting modifies the inference input, whereas conventional post-training constructs supervision from one prompt formulation at a time; neither explicitly optimizes reliability across equivalent forms. We introduce VARI (Verified Alignment over Representations of Intent), a group-structured framework that treats automatically verified formulations of the same visual intent as a shared supervision unit. VARI builds matched form-seed rollout pools, cross-scores each video against all forms, and applies a wording-independent structured intent audit to identify valid realizations and hard semantic failures. Group-Consistent Distillation (GCD) aligns every verified form with interchangeable valid realizations, while Form-Contrastive Flow-DPO (FC-DPO) learns group-level preferences from audited positive-negative pairs, prioritizing same-seed, different-form contrasts. On HunyuanVideo-1.5, VARI attains the highest T2V-CompBench, VBench, and StoryEval scores among the evaluated methods. On prompt-form extensions of T2V-CompBench and VBench, it improves weakest-form alignment over the strongest prompt-level baselines by 2.45 and 3.55 points and reduces cross-form drop by 30.5% and 32.2%, respectively, while preserving visual quality. Human evaluation, natural-caption controls, prompt-enhancement experiments, diversity analyses, and transfer to LTX-2.3 further support the robustness and transferability of these gains, while VARI requires no additional inference-time module.

</details>


### [311] [Closing the Estimation Gap: Training-Free Transductive Inference for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18209)
*Changyi Li,Zeyu Yang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE：一种无需训练的跨域小样本动作识别测试时推断框架，利用未标注查询样本进行两阶段转导式原型与分类器精炼，显著缩小因支持样本稀缺带来的“估计差距”，在五个数据集上1/5-shot均有稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CD-FSAR推理依赖由极少支持样本估计的原型与决策边界，尽管表征学习已很强，但作者的“oracle”分析显示：若用查询真值构造原型/边界，性能可提升7–20个百分点，说明主要瓶颈在测试时估计不准而非表征不足。

Method: TRACE为训练外、骨干冻结的两阶段转导推断：1) 置信阈值转导原型精炼（CTPR）：用高置信预测的查询样本迭代更新支持原型；2) 转导分类器精炼（TCR）：以精炼原型初始化一个轻量逐episode分类器，并通过鼓励对全部查询样本的高置信、类均衡预测进行自适应优化，从而同时改进原型与决边。

Result: 在HMDB51、UCF101、SSv2、Diving48、RareAct五个目标数据集、1-shot与5-shot设置下，相比强基线均有稳定增益，其中HMDB51的1-shot提升超过6个百分点，并能收复oracle估计所揭示差距的很大一部分。

Conclusion: 测试时的估计改进（利用未标注查询进行转导精炼）是应对极端支持稀缺的关键补充，能在不改动骨干表征的前提下广泛提升CD-FSAR性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot action recognition (CD-FSAR) aims to recognise unseen action classes in a target domain from a handful of labelled videos. Despite advances in representation learning, CD-FSAR inference still relies on prototypes estimated from extremely limited support examples. To assess this bottleneck, we perform an oracle analysis with the representation fixed. Constructing oracle prototypes and decision boundaries from ground-truth query labels improves 5-way 1-shot accuracy by 7--20 percentage points across five target datasets. This reveals a substantial estimation gap: the representation contains useful discriminative information, but prototypes and boundaries estimated from scarce supports fail to capture each episode's true class structure. Motivated by this, we propose TRACE, a training-free transductive inference framework that improves episode-level estimation without modifying the backbone. TRACE comprises two complementary stages. Confidence-Thresholded Transductive Prototype Refinement (CTPR) refines support-based prototypes using confidently predicted queries. The refined prototypes initialise a lightweight per-episode classifier, which Transductive Classifier Refinement (TCR) further adapts by encouraging confident, class-balanced predictions over all queries. Together, the two stages improve prototype and boundary estimation, enabling better use of information in the unlabelled queries. Across five target datasets (HMDB51, UCF101, SSv2, Diving48, and RareAct) in both 1-shot and 5-shot settings, TRACE consistently improves a strong CD-FSAR baseline, including gains of more than six percentage points on HMDB51 1-shot, while recovering much of the oracle-estimated gap. These results suggest that improving test-time estimation is a powerful, broadly applicable complement to representation learning for CD-FSAR under extreme support scarcity.

</details>


### [312] [Heading Hierarchy Reconstruction for Complex and Long Documents with Level-Wise Multimodal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18238)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种带有分级多模态记忆的VLM框架，逐页增量重建文档标题层级，并发布大型长复杂文档基准CLDoc-HHRBench，实验在多个基准上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型做OCR/版面要素抽取虽准，但页级预测缺乏跨页一致的文档结构；同时受分辨率与上下文长度限制，无法一次处理几十到上百页，导致标题层级重建不稳定，影响RAG等应用。

Method: 设计“按层级的多模态记忆”机制：处理新页时，从历史页中为每个层级保留一个近期锚点（anchor），让VLM同时看到各层级标题的视觉、文本与版面模式；通过锚点选择在紧凑记忆与跨页一致性间平衡，并逐页增量预测标题等级（用#数量表示）。

Result: 在新提出的CLDoc-HHRBench（250篇、11,212页、专家标注）上，以及三个现有基准上进行评测，所提方法优于对比方法，展现更高的一致性与准确性。

Conclusion: 分级多模态记忆能在不需一次性处理整本文档的条件下，显著提升长复杂文档的标题层级重建质量；基准集推动后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have been widely adopted for document parsing and optical character recognition (OCR), as they can accurately extract page-level elements. However, downstream applications such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) also require coherent document-level structure. Although these models indicate heading levels using different numbers of preceding ``\#'' symbols, page-by-page prediction often lacks document-level consistency. In practice, input-resolution and context-length constraints make it infeasible for a VLM to jointly process all page images in documents containing tens or hundreds of pages. To address this limitation, we propose a VLM-based framework that incrementally reconstructs heading hierarchies with a \emph{level-wise multimodal memory}. The memory retains a compact subset of all previously processed pages, selected by anchors. Our framework supports flexible anchor selection, and in this work we select the most recent node at each level, allowing the VLM to observe the visual, textual, and layout patterns of headings at every level. For comprehensive evaluation, we also introduce the Complex and Long Document Heading Hierarchy Reconstruction Benchmark (CLDoc-HHRBench), comprising 250 documents and 11,212 pages with expert-verified annotations. Experiments on CLDoc-HHRBench and three existing benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [313] [LiFUSE: Frequency-Spatial Unified Self-Supervised Pretraining for Liver Disease Analysis on Non-contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18243)
*Hao Zhang,Yi Huang,Wei Liu,Yuan Gao,Zeju Li,Xu Han,Yu Shi,Ling Zhang,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiFUSE：在NCCT上联合频域与空域的自监督预训练，提升肝脏结构与病灶表征，三类下游任务与跨域迁移均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCCT因低成本与安全性用于机会性肝病筛查，但低对比分辨率、无动态强化、解剖复杂度高，导致微小、异质、多灶肝病灶难以检测；传统体素级重建的自监督学习主要依赖低层强度模式，难以学到与病灶相关的判别特征，需要更好地挖掘NCCT的互补信息。

Method: 提出LiFUSE双分支自监督框架：1) 频域分支对体数据做随机带阻遮蔽并进行频谱重建，促使模型关注病灶与周围组织在频率成分上的细微差异；2) 空域分支进行混合粒度的掩膜体积重建，覆盖不同尺度与形态的病灶；3) 跨域一致性约束耦合两分支，使频域与空域表征对齐，获得更具判别性的肝脏与病灶表示。

Result: 在内部分布的三项临床相关任务及一个公共数据的目标域迁移设置中，LiFUSE稳定且显著优于多种强基线，显示更强的泛化与判别能力。

Conclusion: 频域-空域统一自监督学习能在NCCT上显著提升肝脏疾病分析与筛查潜力，适合用于大规模机会性筛查场景。

Abstract: Given the growing global burden of liver tumors and diffuse liver diseases, non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) is increasingly used for opportunistic screening because of its low cost and favorable safety profile. However, downstream tasks such as tumor detection and fine-grained disease diagnosis remain challenging due to NCCT's inherently low tissue contrast, lack of hemodynamic enhancement, and complex anatomical background. These limitations make subtle, heterogeneous, and multifocal hepatic lesions difficult to identify. Although large-scale self-supervised pretraining can learn robust representations from unlabeled NCCT scans, conventional voxel-wise reconstruction does not fully exploit the complementary information available in NCCT. Under weak NCCT contrast, low-level intensity patterns become less informative for pathology recognition, limiting the capture of lesion-specific discriminative features. In this paper, we propose the Liver Frequency-Spatial Unified Self-Supervised Pretraining (LiFUSE). The frequency branch applies random band-stop masking and spectrum reconstruction to encourage the model to capture subtle differences between lesions and surrounding tissues, while the spatial branch performs hybrid-granularity masked volumetric reconstruction to accommodate lesions of diverse sizes and morphologies. Finally, a cross-domain consistency scheme couples the spatial and frequency branches, yielding more discriminative representations for hepatic structures and lesions. Extensive experiments on three clinically relevant tasks across internal cohorts and a public target-domain transfer setting show that LiFUSE consistently outperforms strong baselines. These results suggest that unified frequency-spatial learning can improve liver disease analysis on NCCT, highlighting its potential for large-scale screening.

</details>


### [314] [FALLNEST: Dual-Domain Dataset Condensation of Imbalanced IMU Data for Wearable Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18260)
*Rashi Niyas P,Ritika Jain,Hitika Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FALLNEST：将大型失衡IMU数据集“蒸馏”成极小且类均衡的合成训练集，使小模型在极少样本上也能高效、稳健地检测跌倒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴跌倒检测面临两大瓶颈：真实跌倒极少导致类别严重失衡、标注IMU数据稀缺；端到端深度模型需要在大规模失衡数据上训练，代价高且常需重采样，既耗算力也可能损失信息。

Method: 在双域时间序列凝缩(CondTSC)基础上，提出FALLNEST：1) 7通道输入，含加速度矢量模以强化跌倒动力学；2) 使用类别均衡的K-Means对合成样本初始化；3) 归一化梯度匹配并配合梯度裁剪，稳定蒸馏优化；4) 基于MMD的嵌入对齐，确保合成数据在特征空间贴近真实分布；5) 多视角频域(Fourier)增强；由此直接在凝缩集合内实现类均衡，无需外部重采样。

Result: 在SisFall、KFall、MobiFall上采用严格被试划分与自然失衡测试集，5个随机种子下评估Accuracy、Macro-F1、PR-AUC。仅用32–146个合成窗(≈1%训练窗)训练102K参数的TinyCNN，即可达93.5–95.8%平均准确率、0.89–0.98 PR-AUC，准确率标准差1.0–1.7%。

Conclusion: 数据中心的凝缩与类均衡策略显著降低训练成本并缓解类别失衡，小模型在极少合成样本上即获得高精度、稳健的跌倒检测表现，表明FALLNEST对可穿戴跌倒检测具有实用价值。

Abstract: Wearable fall detection is hampered by rare fall events, scarce labeled IMU data, and the cost of training deep models on large, imbalanced corpora. Existing methods either rely on hand-crafted features with shallow classifiers, which limits learning from raw signals, or train deep networks on full datasets that require re-sampling to counter class skew and demand substantial data and compute. To address this, we propose a novel Fall detection framework via Normalized-gradient and Embedding matched Synthetic Time-frequency condensation, dubbed FALLNEST, a data-centric approach that distills large, imbalanced IMU datasets into tiny, class-balanced synthetic training sets. Building on dual-domain time-series condensation (CondTSC), FALLNEST introduces a fall-specific 7-channel input with accelerometer vector magnitude, class-balanced K-Means initialization, and normalized gradient matching with gradient clipping, combined with MMD-based embedding alignment and multi-view Fourier-domain augmentation, so that class balance arises from the condensed set itself rather than from re-sampling. We evaluate on SisFall, KFall, and MobiFall using strict subject-wise splits and naturally imbalanced test sets, reporting accuracy, Macro-F1, and PR-AUC over five seeds. A 102K-parameter TinyCNN trained on only 32 to 146 synthetic windows (about only 1% of the training windows) reaches 93.5-95.8% mean accuracy and 0.89-0.98 PR-AUC, with standard deviations of 1.0-1.7% in accuracy, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [315] [Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18276)
*Thierry Bouwmans,Anastasia Zakharova,Belmar Garcia,Marwa Chendeb El Rai,Meghna Kapoor,Badri Narayan Subudhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于红外小目标检测的全面综述，系统梳理传统模型、低秩稀疏分解方法与深度学习方法，汇总资源与数据集，并讨论挑战与未来方向；论文分为两部分：Part 1（模型驱动）与 Part 2（数据驱动）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽多且近二十年发展迅速，但缺乏覆盖传统方法、低秩稀疏分解与深度学习在内的“端到端”整体回顾；同时需要按应用难场景（杂波、噪声、弱小目标、运动、云海/海天线等）对方法进行对比，并汇总社区可用资源。

Method: 对文献按建模范式分类：数学模型（如背景抑制、目标显著性）、机器学习模型、信号处理模型；重点梳理模型驱动（传统与低秩-稀疏分解）与数据驱动（深度学习）两大阵营，分析各自能处理的关键情形；同时汇总公开数据集与代码库，并给出研究路线图。

Result: 形成系统分类框架与对比分析，明确不同方法在复杂场景下的适用性与局限，整理可用数据集/库，为研究复现与基准评测提供参考；给出研究空白与趋势。

Conclusion: 综述提供了传统与最新方法的全景视图：模型驱动方法在可解释性与小样本下仍具优势，深度学习在复杂背景与鲁棒性上表现突出但依赖数据与泛化；建议在多模态融合、弱/无监督学习、合成数据与真实域自适应、实时与轻量化、可解释与可认证评测等方向持续推进。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is often used in different civilian and military applications to detect static or moving targets in scenes like in ground, air and sea surveillance. First valuable research has emerged since 30 years ago and the two last decades witnessed very significant publications in this field following the rise of low-rank and sparse decomposition (model-based methods) and deep learning models (data-based methods). Furthermore, several surveys can be found in the literature but none of them addresses an overall review in this field. In this context, the aim of this paper is to provide a complete survey of the traditional and recent approaches in infrared small target detection. First, we categorize the different approaches found in the literature in terms of the modeling models, i.e mathematical models, machine learning models and signal processing models. Then, we have discussed them in terms of the critical situations that they claim to handle. Furthermore, we present the available resources, datasets and libraries. Then, we conclude with several promising directions for future research. For the sake of reading, this survey is divided into two parts: 1) "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1)" about conventional and low-rank and sparse decomposition (model-based methods), and 2) "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 2)" about deep learning models (data-based methods).

</details>


### [316] [Efficient Scene-Text Refinement via Pixel-Space Grouped Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18284)
*Yaoli Liu,Guo-Hua Wang,Yiyang Chen,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixText提出一种在像素空间进行分组生成的局部文本细化方法，仅改写目标文本区域并保持未编辑内容，结合四步MeanFlow实现高效生成；在新建的Text Refinement Bench上显著优于VAE与整图编辑基线，在可读性与保真度上均达到SOTA，并具极低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像的整体结构已较好，但小字与复杂字符常出现笔画丢失、错别字与颜色漂移，影响可读性与应用。现有VAE重建会损失细粒度笔画，整图编辑又会引入背景漂移。因此需要一种既精确纠正文本、又严格保留非目标区域且高效的局部细化方法与评测基准。

Method: 将场景文本细化表述为像素空间的“分组图像生成”：在原始图像条件下，仅对指定目标区域进行生成，未编辑区域通过条件保持；使用MeanFlow进行四步快速生成，减少计算；并提出Text Refinement Bench评测框架，分别考察文本纠正（glyph pass、目标字符OCR准确率）与图像保留。

Result: 在Text Refinement Bench上，PixText获得83.06%的glyph pass与94.66%的目标字符OCR精确度，显著超过最强基线的36.77%与52.31%。其四步MeanFlow变体在不到1 GPU-秒/图的摊销成本下仍保持81.84%的通过率。

Conclusion: PixText在保持图像未编辑内容的同时高精度纠正局部文本，并以极低步数与计算开销实现高效率；新基准验证其在可读性与保真度上的显著领先，适合用于生成图像的文本修复与纠错场景。

Abstract: Generated images often have coherent layouts and visual content, yet local errors in small text and complex characters still limit their readability and practical use. Scene-text refinement requires accuracy, preservation, and efficiency. Existing methods based on variational autoencoders (VAEs) can lose fine strokes during reconstruction, while full-image editing can shift background colors. In this work, we introduce PixText, a model that formulates local text refinement as grouped image generation in pixel space. Conditioned on the original image, PixText generates only the target regions, preserves unedited content, and uses MeanFlow for four-step generation. We further introduce Text Refinement Bench to evaluate text correction and image preservation in generated images. On this benchmark, PixText achieves 83.06% glyph pass and 94.66% exact target-character OCR accuracy, versus 36.77% and 52.31% for the strongest baseline. Its four-step MeanFlow variant retains an 81.84% pass rate with less than one GPU-second of amortized generation cost per image.

</details>


### [317] [LongForcing: Scaling Temporal Manifold Matching for Long-Horizon World Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18289)
*Ziyu Zhu,Zhaoxu Sun,Fan Jiang,Mengchao Wang,Junfeng Ni,Ting-Bing Xu,Zhicheng Liu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongForcing：在不改学生模型结构和推理开销的前提下，将教师监督从短窗扩展到长时域，显著缓解自回归视频扩散在超出训练窗时的漂移与身份崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散在长时滚动时，因训练监督仅覆盖短窗而产生监督-时域缺口，导致质量逐步恶化、运动不一致、对象身份丢失；匹配短窗边缘分布并不能保证长时行为正确，需要能暴露延迟失败的长轨迹监督。

Method: 提出Temporal Manifold Matching at scale思路，并据此引入LongForcing蒸馏：保留短窗的teacher-forcing与因果ODE蒸馏阶段；在最终分布匹配阶段，用长时、双向的教师替代短窗实分教师，对学生的长自回滚进行标准分布匹配监督；无需修改学生架构或增加推理成本。

Result: 在文本生成视频与交互式动作条件世界模型上，VBench Quality达85.52；WorldRoamBench 60s动作得分在第一人称消融中提升23.9分；定性压力测试可进行长达24小时的交互滚动，远超训练时域。

Conclusion: 沿时间轴扩展教师监督能有效缓解长时漂移，是迈向长时域世界模型的务实路径；LongForcing在不增推理代价下提升稳定性与一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have emerged as a foundation for long-horizon world rollout. However, when extended far beyond their training windows, these models suffer from progressive drift: visual quality deteriorates, motion becomes inconsistent, and object identity gradually collapses as generated frames are recursively reused as context. We study a supervision-horizon gap: short-horizon distillation leaves later rollout states without direct supervision. We show that matching short-window marginals need not determine long-horizon behavior, while longer on-policy trajectories expose delayed failures to training. This motivates temporal manifold matching at scale for long-horizon world models. Building on this insight, we introduce LongForcing, a distillation approach that scales teacher supervision to long student self-rollouts. LongForcing preserves the short-horizon teacher-forcing and causal ODE-distillation stages, while replacing the realscore teacher in the final distribution-matching stage with an extended-horizon bidirectional teacher. The long-horizon teacher supervises extended student self-rollouts using the standard distribution-matching formulation, without modifying the student architecture or increasing inference cost. We evaluate LongForcing on text-to-video generation and interactive action-conditioned world models, achieving a VBench Quality score of 85.52 and improving WorldRoamBench 60s action score by 23.9 points in first-person ablations. Qualitative stress tests further sample interactive rollouts up to 24 hours, far beyond the training horizon. These results suggest that scaling supervision along the temporal axis is a practical step toward long-horizon world models. The project website and code are at https://github.com/Fantasy-AMAP/LongForcing.

</details>


### [318] [Unsupervised and Supervised Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18291)
*Thierry Bouwmans,Anastasia Zakharova,Belmar Garcia,Marwa Chendeb El Rai,Meghna Kapoor,Badri Narayan Subudhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了深度学习在红外小目标检测中的方法与进展，比较不同模型范式、适用场景与评测方式，并给出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于低秩稀疏分解与无监督方法在复杂背景、低信噪比、小尺度目标场景下性能受限；近年来监督式深度学习（CNN、GAN、Transformer、MoE、图/超图神经网络）在可见光任务中表现出色，亟需系统性评估其在红外小目标检测（IRSTD）中的适配性、优势与不足，建立统一分类、对比与评测框架。

Method: 对现有文献进行系统调研与分类：按模型范式（CNN、GAN、Transformer、MoE、图/超图网络等）与策略（例如分割/检测、单帧/多帧、监督/弱监督等）进行归纳；按其宣称能处理的关键情景（杂波背景、低对比度、极小目标、云层/海杂波、噪声干扰、伪目标等）进行讨论；梳理并呈现特定的性能评估体系与指标。

Result: 给出了深度学习方法在IRSTD上的整体性能与适用性概览，明确了各类模型在不同复杂场景下的长短板与代表性成果，并汇总了评测实践与对比结论。总体上，深度学习方法已显著超越传统无监督与低秩稀疏分解方法。

Conclusion: 深度学习已成为IRSTD主流方向，但在小样本泛化、真实复杂背景鲁棒性、跨域迁移、实时性与可解释性方面仍有挑战。未来可探索：更强的时空建模与多模态融合、弱/半监督与自监督学习、开放集与小样本学习、轻量化与边缘部署、物理先验与可解释建模、统一基准与规范化评测。

Abstract: Since the rise of supervised deep learning modelet dets, researchers has attempt to solve infrared small targection using various models like conventional neural networks, GANS, Transformers, Mixture of Experts and recent graph/hypergraph neural networks. Moreover, these deep neural networks methods surpassed the traditional unsupervised models and the low-rank and sparse decomposition unsupervised models reviewed in "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1). In this context, the aim of this paper is to provide a complete survey of the deep learning models in infrared small target detection. First, we categorize the different approaches found in the literature in terms of the modeling models of strategies. Then, we have discussed them in terms of the critical situations that they claim to handle. Furthermore, we present the specific performance evaluation. Then, we conclude with several promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [319] [Robust Logical Anomaly Detection under Capture Shifts via Synthetic Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18295)
*Ufuk Efe,Alptekin Temizel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种不依赖“与正常样本对比”的异常检测框架，以分类器直接判别是否为逻辑异常；在存在拍摄条件扰动时显著更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于“距正常分布”的方法在标准数据上AUROC很高，但对拍摄条件变化（亮度降低、缩放等）鲁棒性未经充分检验，表现大幅下滑，暴露出对采集条件敏感的问题。

Method: 从产品的简要文本描述出发，用语言模型自动生成潜在异常类型与图像编辑指令；用图像编辑器在正常图上合成异常；再训练一个分类器：一方面学习区分这些合成异常，另一方面自动挖掘正常数据的子模态并与异常分离。该分类器在推理时不再与正常参考库比对，直接输出分数。

Result: 在MVTec LOCO等基准上：干净图像AUROC=94.9%（略低于CSAD 97.7%、SALAD 96.4%）；在异质扰动协议下AUROC=94.1%，显著优于参考法（CSAD降至68.1%，SALAD至62.3%，其它方法普遍下降9.7–34.1点）。即便训练未包含亮度或缩小增广，扰动下仍达92.5%（干净93.2%）。

Conclusion: 仅凭干净基准的高分可能掩盖对拍摄条件的脆弱性；将“正常样本”与“基于规则的异常”共同用于学习决策、并在推理时不依赖正常参考，可显著提升对采集条件变化的稳健性。

Abstract: Detectors of logical anomalies, such as missing, extra or wrongly combined parts, usually score a test image by its distance from normal (good) images. They achieve near-perfect image-level AUROC on standard industrial anomaly detection benchmarks such as MVTec LOCO, but their robustness to changes in capture conditions remains largely unexamined. Under a heterogeneous perturbation protocol in which each test image is randomly kept unaltered or subjected to a brightness reduction or a zoom-out transformation, the normal-reference methods we tested lose 9.7 to 34.1 AUROC points, and two of the strongest, CSAD and SALAD, fall from 97.7% and 96.4% to 68.1% and 62.3%. We propose a framework in which a classifier, trained on normal images and on synthetic anomalies, scores a test image without comparing it with normal data. From a short description of each product, a language model derives anomaly types and editing instructions, an image editor applies them to normal images, and the classifier learns to separate the edited images from automatically discovered normal sub-modes. With its full training recipe, the framework reaches 94.9% AUROC on clean images, 1.5 to 2.8 points below SALAD and CSAD, and 94.1% under the perturbation protocol. Trained without brightness or zoom-out augmentation, so that neither test perturbation occurs in training, it still reaches 92.5% (93.2% clean). These results suggest that clean benchmark scores can hide a strong sensitivity to capture conditions, and that learning the decision from both normal images and rule violations may reduce it.

</details>


### [320] [AffectWorld: Benchmarking Emotion-Driven Future World Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18305)
*Changhao Song,Yacong Chen,Hui Gao,Yazhou Zhang,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AffectWorld是一个评测“情绪→行动→世界状态”链条的基准，用多任务与多指标系统性测试世界模型是否能预测情绪驱动的未来变化，并显示现有SOTA模型在情绪敏感与因果一致性上存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评测多聚焦物理合理性与感知质量，忽略了人类情绪对行动与世界演化的影响；需要一个能系统刻画情绪—行为—后果因果链的基准，以推动模型理解和预测社会物理混合场景中的未来。

Method: 构建AffectWorld数据集（1,498段日常视频，含情绪、行动倾向与原子级世界状态约束标注），并提供233段反事实子集（固定场景与事件，仅改变主体情绪）。设计三类任务：世界状态一致性、情绪—行动一致性、因果一致性；定义8个自动与VLM/LLM人校准结合的指标，另含一项反事实指标，评估在情绪干预下的行动与后果变化。

Result: 对5个SOTA世界模型（如Cosmos3）的评测显示：世界状态变化得分仅0.100–0.280，反事实情绪敏感度0.027–0.070（0–1尺度），说明模型难以生成情绪驱动行动的后果，也难以在情绪干预下调整未来预测。

Conclusion: AffectWorld填补了情绪驱动世界演化评测的空白，为发展能将情绪纳入物理与社会状态预测的世界模型提供测试平台；当前模型在情绪因果链建模上仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: The world encompasses both physical environments and human actors. Predicting its evolution requires understanding both physical laws and how emotions shape human actions. However, existing world model benchmarks primarily assess physical plausibility and perceptual quality, leaving emotion-driven world evolution largely untested.We introduce AffectWorld, a benchmark that evaluates the chain Emotion-Action-World State through three tasks: World-State Consistency, Emotion-Action Consistency, and Causal Consistency. It comprises 1,498 everyday video samples annotated with plausible emotions, action tendencies, and atomic world-state constraints. A counterfactual subset of 233 samples tests whether changing an agent's emotion under fixed scene and event conditions yields corresponding changes in actions and consequences. We define eight metrics across the three tasks and an additional counterfactual metric, combining automatic visual assessment with human-calibrated VLM/LLM judgments. Evaluations of five SoTA world models (e.g., Cosmos3) required world-state change scores of 0.100-0.280 and counterfactual emotion sensitivity scores of 0.027-0.070 on a [0, 1] scale. These results reveal substantial gaps in generating the consequences of emotion-driven actions and adapting future predictions to emotional interventions. AffectWorld provides a testbed for advancing world models that capture how emotions shape future physical and social states.

</details>


### [321] [Omni-RS: Unifying Multi-Task Understanding and Generation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18323)
*Pan Yin. Shuai Yang,Yuanhao Liu,Datao Tang,Jialin Li,Hao Wang,Pei Liu,Kaiyu Li,Cong Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Omni-RS统一框架，覆盖4种遥感理解与5种生成任务，通过“两阶段场景引导训练”和“细粒度视觉条件”实现强理解与多样生成，并构建统一多任务生成数据集UniGen，实验在理解与生成上均达SOTA或接近SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实遥感应用常需对同一地理场景既能理解（分析）又能生成（合成/重建/编辑），但现有方法多各自为政：理解或生成单一任务，或统一模型仅支持文生图、难以处理参考图等多样生成需求。因此需要一个能同时胜任多理解与多生成任务且可共享知识的统一框架。

Method: 1) 场景引导两阶段训练：先将多模态大语言模型(MLLM)适配到遥感理解任务；再用其隐状态作为条件，引导扩散Transformer进行图像生成，实现从理解到生成的知识迁移。2) 构建UniGen数据集：将异构遥感数据（文本、图像、掩膜、变化对、等）统一到通用格式以支持多生成任务的联合训练。3) 细致视觉条件：用VAE编码参考视觉条件（如参考图/掩膜/布局等）注入到生成过程，扩展到需参考图像的条件生成与编辑任务。

Result: Omni-RS在四个理解任务上取得优异表现；在多个生成任务（含文本生成、参考引导、编辑等）上获得出色结果，优于或可比现有方法，显示统一训练与视觉条件设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段场景引导和统一数据组织，Omni-RS实现理解与生成的深度融合，显著提升多任务遥感能力；详细视觉条件扩展了任务覆盖面。该框架为通用遥感智能体提供可行路径。

Abstract: Practical remote sensing applications often require both understanding and generation capabilities for the same geographic scene. However, most existing methods address either understanding or generation in isolation, while existing unified remote sensing models' generative ability is limited to text-to-image. To bridge this gap, we propose Omni-RS, a unified framework supporting four remote sensing understanding tasks and five generation tasks. Specifically, we introduce the "Scene-Guided Two-Stage Training" strategy, which first adapts a multimodal large language model to remote sensing understanding and then uses its hidden states to guide a diffusion transformer for image generation. To enable joint learning across diverse generation tasks, we construct UniGen, a unified multi-task generation dataset, by organizing heterogeneous remote sensing data into a unified format. Furthermore, we introduce "Detailed Visual Conditioning", which uses visual conditions encoded by a VAE to guide the generation process, thereby supporting additional generation tasks that require reference images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Omni-RS performs exceptionally well in understanding tasks and has achieved outstanding results in a variety of generative tasks.

</details>


### [322] [ShoeFit: A New Dataset and Dual-image-stream DiT Framework for Virtual Footwear Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18325)
*Yuhan Li,Zhiyu Jin,Yifan Tong,Wenxiang Shang,Benlei Cui,Xuanhong Chen,Hangcheng Zhu,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVShoes数据集与ShoeFit模型，解决鞋类虚拟试穿中的数据稀缺、视角错位与背景致色问题，显著提升真实产品鞋在复杂场景下的渲染保真与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟试穿研究多聚焦服装，鞋类因难以获得匹配数据、视角与脚姿势错位、材质与环境光交互导致颜色污染而欠研究，迫切需要数据与方法共同推进。

Method: (1) 构建MVShoes：包含7305个多视角鞋—人体图像三元组及精标注，覆盖多类别与复杂场景；(2) 设计ShoeFit：双分支DiT（扩散Transformer）架构，采用多视图条件与3D旋转位置编码缓解视角错位；提出LayeredRefAttention，用背景特征调制鞋部latent，削弱背景致色；通过解耦的去噪与条件分支保持纹理细节并去除环境干扰。

Result: 在定量与定性评测中，相较现有方法大幅提升渲染保真度与在真实商品鞋复杂场景下的稳健性，树立高保真鞋类试穿新基准。

Conclusion: 数据与模型双管齐下：MVShoes为训练与评测提供基础，ShoeFit以多视图与解耦注意力机制有效对齐视角并抑制背景颜色污染，实现在保持纹理细节的同时获得3D一致、逼真的鞋类试穿效果。

Abstract: Virtual footwear try-on (VFTON), a critical yet underexplored area in virtual try-on (VTON), aims to synthesize faithful try-on results given diverse footwear and model images while maintaining 3D consistency and texture authenticity. Unlike conventional garment-focused VTON methods, VFTON presents unique challenges due to (1) Data Scarcity, which arises from the difficulty of perfectly matching product shoes with models wearing the identical ones, (2) Viewpoint Misalignment, where the target foot pose and source shoe views are always misaligned, leading to incomplete texture information and detail distortion, and (3) Background-induced Color Distortion, where complex material of footwear interacts with environmental lighting, causing unintended color contamination. To address these challenges, we introduce MVShoes, a multi-view shoe try-on dataset consisting of 7305 well-annotated image triplets, covering diverse footwear categories and challenging try-on scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a dual-stream DiT architecture, ShoeFit, designed to mitigate viewpoint misalignment through Multi-View Conditioning with 3D Rotary Position Embedding, and alleviate background-induced distortion using the LayeredRefAttention which leverages background features to modulate footwear latents. The proposed framework effectively decouples shoe appearance from environmental interferences while preserving high-quality texture detail through decoupled denoising and conditioning branches. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method substantially improves rendering fidelity and robustness under challenging real-world product shoes, establishing a new benchmark in high-fidelity footwear try-on synthesis.

</details>


### [323] [CityCode: Building Agent-Operable 3D Cities from Sparse Aerial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18342)
*Jiahui Sun,Ran Bi,Yifei Liu,Xiaolong Zhou,Yishuo Xu,Yuanqi Li,Congcong Zhu,Lei Yang,Yanwen Guo,Zhihang Zhong,Hongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CityCode与Air2Code，从单张低空航拍RGB图+相机参数重建可持久更新的参数化3D城市，实现度量尺度建筑与显式道路几何/连通性，并支持长期任务链中的查询、编辑与历史回溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为城市空间智能提供可被智能体测量、解释、修改的3D城市，需要轻量获取与可持续一致更新的代码化城市状态，避免重复高成本稠密重建，同时保证度量精度、拓扑一致性与可操作性。

Method: 1) 定义新任务：从每个区域一张低空航拍图像+已知内外参，生成大规模、实例级、参数化3D城市（含度量建筑、显式道路几何与连通性）。2) 提出CityCode表示：以持久实体ID统一度量几何、城市拓扑、语义与3D资产，并引入依赖感知的版本化执行，以验证更新与历史恢复。3) 提出Air2Code流程：结合视觉结构提取与改造的度量深度估计；在遮挡与覆盖不全时，以已恢复结构与外观引导生成式补全与结构感知3D生成（建筑与背景）。

Result: 在CitySample-Aerial与UrbanScene3D上，相比基线实现更准确的度量结构恢复与更高的视觉保真度。通过50条、每条50步的任务链验证，CityCode支持持续查询、编辑、规划与历史恢复。

Conclusion: CityCode+Air2Code可将稀疏航拍观测转化为可持久、可操作的城市数字孪生，实现长期城市空间智能任务中的高保真度量重建与可演化状态管理。

Abstract: Urban spatial intelligence calls for 3D cities that agents can measure, interpret, and modify across successive tasks. Maintaining such cities as persistent, code-based states requires lightweight acquisition and support for consistent state updates without repeated dense reconstruction. We therefore formulate a new setting for grounded urban world construction: generating large-scale, instance-level parameterized 3D cities from one low-altitude aerial RGB image per region and known camera parameters, while recovering metrically scaled buildings and explicit road geometry and connectivity. We introduce CityCode, an agent-operable representation that unifies metric geometry, urban topology, visual semantics, and 3D assets through persistent entity identities, with dependency-aware versioned execution for validated updates and historical restoration. We further develop Air2Code to construct this representation by combining visual structure extraction with adapted metric depth estimation. To address occlusion and incomplete coverage, recovered structure and observed appearance guide generative completion and subsequent structure-aware 3D generation for buildings and backgrounds. Experiments on CitySample-Aerial and UrbanScene3D demonstrate substantially more accurate metric structure recovery and superior visual fidelity to the target cities compared with the evaluated baselines. Across 50 task chains of 50 steps each, CityCode supports continued querying, editing, planning, and historical restoration over evolving city states. Together, CityCode and Air2Code turn sparse aerial observations into persistent, agent-operable urban worlds for long-horizon urban spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [324] [Urbex: Agentic Spatial Grounding for City-Scale 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18356)
*Yu Wang,Rui Dai,Wei Chen,Yi Wang,Bo Dang,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu,Yansheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Urbex将城市级3D指代定位从“候选实例排序”转变为“主动空间搜索”，以交互式多视图环境+VLM代理+强化学习实现更快更准、免候选框的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统城市级3D grounding依赖预先生成的大量候选实例并进行排序，随着场景规模扩大，计算与内存开销陡增且对标注框依赖强，泛化与效率受限。需要一种能直接在大规模户外场景中高效搜索并定位目标、减少先验依赖的方法。

Method: 构建交互式多视图城市环境，允许VLM代理执行工具使用：查询地标、放大局部、渲染斜视角、提交边界框；将grounding表述为主动空间搜索。以CityRefer为训练场，设计平滑定位奖励与轻量塑形奖励，采用强化学习优化工具使用策略，实现无需GT候选框的端到端决策。

Result: 在CityRefer上，Urbex在Hit@1与IoU均达SOTA；无需真值候选框即可完成指代定位，且推理速度显著快于最强城市级排序基线；在STPLS3D-Refer零样本评测中展现更好的跨场景鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主动搜索式、代理驱动的多视图交互框架能替代候选排序范式，在城市级3D grounding中同时提升精度、速度与泛化；强化学习与精心设计的奖励对高效探索与稳健定位至关重要。

Abstract: City-scale 3D grounding is commonly formulated as candidate ranking, which requires precomputed instance proposals and becomes inefficient in large outdoor scenes. We propose Urbex, an agentic vision-language framework that instead casts grounding as active spatial search. Urbex represents a city-scale 3D scene as an interactive multi-view environment, allowing a VLM agent to query landmarks, zoom into local regions, render oblique views, and commit a grounded bounding box. We optimize this tool-use policy with reinforcement learning on CityRefer, using smooth localization rewards and lightweight shaping rewards for efficient exploration. Experiments show that Urbex achieves the best Hit@1 and IoU on CityRefer, supports candidate-free grounding without ground-truth boxes, and is substantially faster than the strongest city-scale ranking baseline. Zero-shot results on STPLS3D-Refer further indicate improved cross-scene robustness.

</details>


### [325] [Hallucination-aware Faithful Reasoning in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18369)
*Yi Liu,Jing Zhang,Xiang Feng,Xiaoyu Tian,Haonan Guo,Di Wang,Zengmao Wang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FaithRS：一种面向遥感MLLM的“去幻觉”自反思训练框架；构建SFHD-43K幻觉标注集与检测器，利用安全门控与纠错rollout，将诊断反馈融入训练，并用复合奖励联合优化正确性、忠实性与安全性；在多基准上带来最高+11.64分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在遥感推理中常出现视觉不支撑的推理（幻觉），且主流后训练多只对最终答案优化，缺乏对中间推理链幻觉的识别与纠偏监督，导致答案对图像证据不忠实。

Method: 1) 构建SFHD-43K：含细粒度、结构化的幻觉类型/成因/证据标注；训练幻觉检测器。2) FaithRS：将检测器的诊断反馈接入“安全检测器引导的纠错rollout”框架：安全门控判定是否及如何修订；在修订中引用检测到的证据与成因进行定向更正。3) 训练以复合奖励驱动，联合优化答案准确性、推理忠实性、减少幻觉、格式合规与修订安全。4) 以Qwen3.5(0.8B/4B/9B)为骨干进行实验与评估。

Result: 在四个遥感基准上，所有规模的模型均有一致提升，最高提升达11.64分；表明该框架在减少幻觉、提升答案正确性与推理一致性方面有效。

Conclusion: 面向遥感的FaithRS通过“检测—诊断—门控—纠错—复合奖励”闭环，显著缓解MLLM视觉不忠实问题；该思路可推广到其他多模态场景以提升推理可信度与安全性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities for remote sensing reasoning, yet they frequently generate visually unsupported reasoning that contradicts the visual evidence. Existing post-training methods optimize primarily for final-answer correctness, providing limited supervision for identifying and correcting hallucinations that arise during the reasoning process. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{FaithRS}, a hallucination-aware self-reflection framework for faithful reasoning in remote sensing. We first construct \textbf{SFHD-43K}, a dataset of 43K structured, fine-grained hallucination annotations, and train a detector to identify hallucination types, underlying causes, and supporting visual evidence. FaithRS then incorporates this diagnostic feedback into a Safe Detector-Guided Corrective Rollout framework, where a safety-aware gate selectively determines whether and how to revise the response. Training is driven by a composite reward that jointly optimizes answer accuracy, reasoning faithfulness, hallucination reduction, format compliance, and revision safety. Experiments with Qwen3.5 backbones (0.8B, 4B, and 9B) show consistent improvements across four remote sensing benchmarks, with gains of up to 11.64 points.

</details>


### [326] [SchemaPose: RGB-Based Category-Level Pose Estimation with Parametric Category Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18374)
*Shuang Wu,Jiude Wei,Cewu Lu,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SchemaPose：一种以范式(schema)为指导的RGB单目类别级9D位姿估计框架，利用参数化范式显式建模类别共性与实例差异，在端到端查询式框架中用推断的范式变量指导位姿与尺寸预测，在合成与真实数据上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级9D位姿（3D平移、3D旋转、3D尺寸）在仅RGB条件下需对未见实例泛化，并同时分辨类别共性与实例结构差异；现有方法因缺少几何约束，外观信号将姿态线索、类别共性与实例差异纠缠，难以学到与类别级目标对齐的表示。

Method: 提出SchemaPose：在估计流水线中引入显式参数化“范式”作为类别级先验，联合建模类别层面的规律与结构化的实例变化；将推断出的范式变量作为条件，引导位姿相关预测；以端到端的query-based（类似DETR范式）框架实现。同时该表述自然支持模板实例化、合成标注与位姿学习的一体化。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的实验显示，SchemaPose在仅RGB的类别级9D位姿估计上达到当前最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 显式的范式引导能在RGB-only类别级位姿任务中有效解纠缠类别共性与实例差异，提升泛化与精度，并为模板实例化与数据合成提供统一框架。

Abstract: RGB-based category-level 9D pose estimation remains highly challenging because a monocular system must generalize across unseen object instances while jointly inferring 3D translation, 3D rotation, and 3D size. Unlike instance-level pose estimation, the category-level setting requires the network to learn both what is shared across objects within a category and how individual instances vary in structure. This challenge is amplified in the RGB-only setting, where pose cues, category-level commonality, and instance-specific variation are entangled in appearance without direct geometric constraints, making it difficult for existing frameworks to learn a representation that is truly aligned with the category-level estimation objective. To address this problem, we propose SchemaPose, a schema-guided framework for RGB-based category-level pose estimation. SchemaPose equips the estimation pipeline with an explicit parametric schema for modeling category-level regularities together with structured instance variation, and uses the inferred schema variables to guide pose-related prediction in an end-to-end query-based framework. The same formulation also supports template instantiation, synthetic annotation, and pose learning within a unified framework. Experiments on synthetic and real data show that SchemaPose achieves state-of-the-art performance among RGB-based methods for category-level 9D pose estimation.

</details>


### [327] [Noise2Match: Self-Supervised Low-Dose CT Denoising via Adaptive Inter-Slice Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18379)
*Langrui Zhou,Guang Li,Xinyu Huang,Ziteng Zhou,Zuyang Huang,Hongming Shan,Shouhua Luo,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Noise2Match：一种基于自适应切片间匹配的自监督LDCT/CBCT去噪方法，通过学习切片间标签迁移并对去噪输出做校正，使训练更接近有监督效果，实验证明优于现有自监督图像域方法，适用性更广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式CNN去噪需配对干净数据，难以获取；经典自监督方法假设像素级独立噪声，不适用于LDCT中相关噪声。利用相邻切片的自监督方法又受解剖变化导致的非刚性错位影响，难以满足Noise2Noise所需的“等均值”条件，导致训练偏差。

Method: 提出Noise2Match：引入切片间匹配网络，显式建模相邻切片间的“标签迁移”（非刚性对齐/映射）；在此基础上对主去噪器的输出施加校正，使相邻切片在统计上满足等均值条件，从而可以用相邻低剂量切片进行自监督训练；理论上证明在理想条件下等价于用成对噪声-干净图像的监督训练。此外，该校正过程还能降低切片间噪声相关性，从而扩展到CBCT。

Result: 在多组LDCT与CBCT实验中，Noise2Match整体优于现有图像域自监督去噪方法，表现出更强的泛化性与实用价值；并首次在低剂量CBCT图像域自监督去噪中取得有效结果。

Conclusion: 自适应切片间匹配与输出校正可弥补相邻切片训练的错位与噪声相关问题，使自监督LDCT/CBCT去噪接近监督上限，具有更广泛应用前景。

Abstract: With the rapid development of deep learning, supervised convolutional neural network methods have greatly improved the performance of low-dose computed tomography (CT) denoising. However, they require paired noisy-clean training data, which is difficult to obtain in practice. Some classic self-supervised denoising methods rely on the assumption of pixel-wise independent noise, making them ineffective for correlated noise in low-dose CT (LDCT) images. Considering the high similarity and weaker noise correlation between adjacent CT slices, several recently proposed self-supervised methods use adjacent LDCT slices for training. Nevertheless, these approaches fail to effectively account for the non-rigid misalignment between slices caused by anatomical variation, hindering them from satisfying the equal-mean condition required by the classic Noise2Noise framework. In this paper, we propose Noise2Match, a self-supervised LDCT denoising method based on adaptive inter-slice matching. Noise2Match introduces an inter-slice matching network to model the label transition process between adjacent slices, and then applies correction to the denoiser's outputs to better satisfy the equal-mean condition. We theoretically prove that, under idealized conditions, training Noise2Match using adjacent LDCT slices is equivalent to supervised training with paired noisy-clean images. We further find that Noise2Match can also effectively perform low-dose conebeam CT (CBCT) denoising, which could be attributed to its correction operation that reduces inter-slice noise correlation. To the best of our knowledge, Noise2Match is the first effective image-domain self-supervised method for low-dose CBCT. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Noise2Match outperforms existing image-domain selfsupervised methods, offering broader applicability and higher practical value.

</details>


### [328] [ReDiS: Reasoning-Diagnosis Self-Evolution for Multimodal Reasoning in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18388)
*Yi Liu,Wentao Jiang,Shuo Ni,Zhiming Luo,Di Wang,Zengmao Wang,Jing Zhang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDiS框架，用“推理者-诊断者”双专家自进化与失败触发恢复机制，提升遥感多模态大模型在细粒度可视化定位与多步推理任务上的可靠性与准确率，在多项基准和不同模型规模上显著超越Qwen3.5骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在遥感理解中：多步推理与细粒度视觉对齐困难；依赖昂贵的预构造推理轨迹或自生成一致性信号，容易放大共性错误、监督不可靠。需要一种既能探索多样推理、又能稳健评估并提供可信学习信号的方案。

Method: 提出Reasoning–Diagnosis Self-Evolution（ReDiS）：从共享多模态骨干派生两位专家——Reasoner负责生成多样“证据—答案”候选；Diagnoser对整组候选进行联合评估，给出带可信度的反馈；二者联动自进化，Reasoner暴露新分布与错误模式，Diagnoser随之自适应并重塑后续学习信号。为应对“整组全错、相对优势消失”的情形，引入Failure-Triggered Recovery（FTR），以最小结果级别纠偏而不强加中间推理轨迹。

Result: 在LHRS-Bench、MME-RS、CHOICE上，基于Qwen3.5的0.8B/2B/4B规模均有一致提升：0.8B平均准确率提升6.47个百分点；4B版本在对比的遥感MLLM中取得最高平均性能。

Conclusion: 将推理探索与候选诊断分离并联动演化，可在无需昂贵外部轨迹与避免自一致性偏误的前提下，显著增强遥感MLLM的可解释、可信推理能力；FTR机制有效覆盖全错场景，整体带来稳定跨规模增益。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for remote sensing understanding, yet reliable reasoning remains challenging when tasks require fine-grained visual grounding and multi-step inference. Existing approaches typically rely on either costly pre-constructed reasoning trajectories or self-generated signals such as response consistency, which can reinforce shared errors and provide unreliable supervision. We introduce \textbf{Reasoning--Diagnosis Self-Evolution (ReDiS)}, a framework that separates reasoning exploration from candidate evaluation while allowing the two processes to evolve jointly. ReDiS instantiates two role-specific experts from a shared multimodal backbone: a \textbf{Reasoner} that explores diverse visual evidence--answer candidates and a \textbf{Diagnoser} that jointly evaluates the complete candidate set and provides credibility-aware feedback. As the Reasoner evolves, it exposes new candidate distributions and failure modes; the Diagnoser adapts to these changes and, in turn, reshapes the learning signal for subsequent exploration. To handle groups in which all sampled answers are incorrect and group-relative advantages vanish, we further introduce \textbf{Failure-Triggered Recovery (FTR)}, which provides minimal outcome-level correction without prescribing intermediate reasoning trajectories. Experiments on LHRS-Bench, MME-RS, and CHOICE show consistent improvements over Qwen3.5 backbones at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B scales. ReDiS improves category-averaged accuracy by 6.47 percentage points at 0.8B, while ReDiS-4B achieves the highest average performance among the compared remote sensing MLLMs.

</details>


### [329] [Bifocal: Training-Free Mixed Granularity Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18399)
*Peiran Yin,Hao Wu,Zihao Yang,Fengyu Zhou,Yijuan Liang,Haozhe Hu,Xudong Wang,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bifocal是一种针对多模态大模型的视觉token剪枝方法，在仅保留约四分之一视觉token的情况下，显著缓解现有方法在精细任务（如OCR与视觉指向/定位）上的性能崩塌，同时在多模型、多能力组与多预算设置下保持领先，并在各能力组均保留>90%的未剪枝性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在常见VQA指标上看似有效，但在OCR、视觉定位等精细粒度任务上出现严重退化。根因在于：1）单一重要性排序忽视不同任务所需的互补证据；2）稀疏地保留原生patch破坏图像内容与空间连续性。需要一种能兼顾查询相关与图像内在证据、并维护空间结构的剪枝框架。

Method: 提出Bifocal：训练-free、两阶段、混合粒度剪枝。阶段一（LLM之前）：为查询相关证据与图像内在证据分别分配预算，并提供整图的池化粗粒度表示；通过“软合取”的词-区匹配与经范数去偏的图像显著性平衡，选择查询相关区域；对图像内在证据采用最大化表征覆盖度的选择策略。阶段二（LLM内部）：依据密度校正的文-图注意力进一步细剪。整体以约1/4原始视觉token实现混合粒度保留与空间完整性维护。

Result: 在三种MLLM家族、五类能力组与九种模型-预算组合上，Bifocal均排名平均第一；在每个能力组与每个模型上均能在约25%视觉token预算下保留>90%的未剪枝性能，且是唯一达成该一致性结果的方法。

Conclusion: 单一排名+稀疏patch保留的主流范式难以兼顾多任务证据需求与空间结构。Bifocal通过分预算的混合粒度与两阶段剪枝，有效维持查询与内在证据的互补性与空间完整性，在低token预算下实现稳健而广泛的性能保持。

Abstract: Visual token pruning for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) appears highly effective, with existing methods retaining over 95% of unpruned performance on common VQA benchmarks using a quarter of the visual tokens or fewer.
